# Targum Jonathan on Exodus

תרגום יונתן על שמות

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus)

תרגום מרכזי על ספרי הנביאים שנקרא בציבור בבית הכנסת בתקופת התלמוד, ועדיין נקרא היום בקהילות של יהודי תימן.

Primary Targum on the books of the Prophets, read publicly in synagogues in talmudic times and still today by Yemenite Jews.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Targum/Targum Jonathan/Torah

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Targum Jonathan on Exodus (Segments: 1210, License: Public Domain)

Version (En): The Targum of Jonathan ben Uzziel, trans. J. W. Etheridge, London, 1862 (Segments: 1205)


## Chapter 1

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:1)


וְאִילֵין שְׁמָהַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל דְעָלוּ לְמִצְרַיִם עִם יַעֲקב גְבַר עִם אֱנַשׁ בֵּיתֵיהּ עָלוּ


AND these are the names of the sons of Israel who went into Mizraim with Jakob, each with the men of his house entered in:


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:2)


רְאוּבֵן שִׁמְעוֹן לֵוִי וִיהוּדָה


Reuben, Shimeon, Levi, and Jehudah;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:3)


יִשָשׁכָר זְבוּלֻן וּבִנְיָמִין


Issakar, Zebulon, and Benjamin;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:4)


דָן וְנַפְתָּלִי גָד וְאָשֵׁר


Dan and Naphtali; Gad and Asher.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:5)


וַהֲוָה סְכוּם כָּל נַפְשָׁתָא נַפְקֵי יַרְכָא דְיַעֲקב שׁוּבְעִין נַפְשָׁתָא עִם יוֹסֵף וּבְנוֹהִי דַהֲוָה בְּמִצְרַיִם


And the number of all the souls coming from the thigh of Jakob, seventy souls, with Joseph and his sons, who were in Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:6)


וּמִית יוֹסֵף וּבַתְרֵיהּ מִיתוּ כָּל אֲחוֹהִי וְכָל דָרָא הַהוּא


And Joseph died, and after him died all his brethren, and all that generation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:7)


וּבְנוֹי דְיִשְרָאֵל נְפִישׁוּ וְאִתְיְלִידוּ וּסְגוּ וְאִתְקִיפוּן לַחֲדָא לַחֲדָא וְאִתְמְלִיאַת אַרְעָא מִנְהוֹן


And the souls of Israel increased, and multiplied children, and became strong, and prevailed greatly, and the land was filled with them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:8)


וְקָם מִלִיךְ חָדַת כְּמִין שֵׁירוּיָא עַל מִצְרַיִם דְלָא חֲכִּים יַת יוֹסֵף וְלָא הֲלִיךְ בְּנִימוּסוֹי


And there arose a new king (other) than he who was formerly over Mizraim, who took no knowledge of Joseph, and walked not in his laws.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:9)


וַאֲמַר לְעַמֵיהּ הָא עַמָא בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל סַגִיאִין וְתַקְפִין יַתִּיר מִינָן


And he said to his people, Behold now, the people of the house of Israel are many, and are stronger than we.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:10)


אִיתוֹן כְּדוֹן נִתְיַעַט עֲלֵיהוֹן בְּהִלֵן דִינִין נַזְעַר יַתְהוֹן קֳדָם עַד לָא יִסְגוֹן וְיֶהֱוֵי אֲרוּם יָתָן סִדְרֵי קְרָבָא וְיִתּוֹסְפוּן לְחוֹד הִינוּן עַל סַנְאֵינָן וִישֵׁיצוּן יָתָנָא וְלָא יְשַׁיְירוּן מִינָן אוּף לָא חָד וּמִן בָּתַר כְּדֵין יִפְקוּן לְהוֹן מִן אַרְעָא


Come, let us take counsel against them in these matters, to diminish them that they multiply not, so as that, should war be arrayed against us, they be not added to our adversaries, and destroy us that not one of us be left, and they afterward go forth from the land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:11)


וְשַׁווּן עֲלֵיהוֹן רַבְרְבָנִין מְפַלְחָנִין מִן בִּגְלַל לְמִצְעֲרָא יַתְהוֹן בְּשִׁעֲבּוּדְהוֹן וּבִנְיַין קוּרְיַין תְלִילִין לְשׁוּם בֵּית אוֹצְרוֹי דְפַרְעה יַת טָאנֵיס וְיַת פִּילוּסִין


And they set over them work-masters to afflict them in their servitude; and they builded walled cities to become Pharoh's treasure-places, Tanis and Pilusin.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:12)


וְהֵיכְמָא דִמְעַנְיַין לְהוֹן הֵיכְדֵין הֲווֹן סָגַן וְהֵיכְדַין הֲווֹן תַּקְפִין וְאִתְיַעֲקוּ מִצְרָאֵי בְּחַיֵיהוֹן מִן קֳדָם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


But as much as they depressed them, so much they multiplied, and so much they prevailed, and the Mizraee were troubled in their lives before the sons of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:13)


וְשַׁעֲבִּידוּ מִצְרָאֵי יַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל בְּקַשְׁיוּ


And the Mizraee enslaved the sons of Israel,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:14)


וְאַמְרָרוּ יַת חַיֵיהוֹן בְּפוּלְחָנָא קַשְׁיָא בְּטִינָא וּבְלִיבְנִין וּבְכָל פּוּלְחָנָא בְּאַנְפֵּי בָרָא יַת כָּל פּוּלְחַנְהוֹן הֲווֹן מְפַלְחִין בְּהוֹן בְּקַשְׁיוּ


and made their lives bitter by hard service in clay and bricks, and all the labour of the face of the field; and in all the work which they made them do was hardness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:15)


וַאֲמַר פַּרְעה דָמָךְ הֲוָה חָמֵי בְּחֵילְמֵיהּ וְהָא כָּל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם קַיְימָא בְּכַף מוֹדָנָא חֲדָא וְטַלְיָא בַּר אִימְרָתָא בְּכַף מוֹדָנָא חֲדָא וַהֲוַת כַּרְעָא כַּף מוֹדָנָא מְטַלְיָא בְּגַוָה מִן יַד שָׁדַר וּקְרָא כָּל חַרְשֵׁי מִצְרַיִם וְתָנֵי לְהוֹן יַת חוּלְמֵיהּ מִן יַד פַּתְחִין פּוּמְהוֹן יֵינִיס וְיִמְבְּרֵס רֵישֵׁי חַרְשַׁיָא וְאָמְרִין לְפַרְעה בִּיר חַד עָתִיד לְמֶהֱוֵי מִתְיְלִיד בִּכְנִישַׁתְהוֹן דְיִשְרָאֵל דְעַל יְדוֹ עֲתִידָא לִמְחַרְבָא כָּל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם וּבְגִין כֵּן אִתְיַעַט פַּרְעה מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרַיִם לְחַיָיתָא יְהוֹדַיְיתָא דִשְׁמָא דְחָדָא שִׁפְרָה הִיא יוֹכֶבֶד וּשְׁמָא דְתִּנְיֵיתָא פּוּעָה הִיא מִרְיָם בְּרַתָּה


And Pharoh told that he, being asleep, had seen in his dream, and, behold, all the land of Mizraim was placed in one scale of a balance, and a lamb, the young of a sheep, was ill the other scale; and the scale with the lamb in it overweighed. Forthwith he sent and called all the magicians of Mizraim, and imparted to them his dream. Immediately Jannis and Jambres, the chief of the magicians, opened their mouth and answered Pharoh,?A certain child is about to be born in the congregation of Israel, by whose hand will be destruc-tion to all the land of Mizraim. Therefore did Pharoh, king of Mizraim, give counsel to the Jehudith midwives, the name of one of whom was Shifra, who is Jokeved, and the name of the other Puvah, who is Miriam her daughter.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:16)


וַאֲמַר כַּד תֶּהֱוְיַין מוֹלְדָן יַת יְהוֹדַיְיתָא וְתִיסְתְּכִין עַלוֹי מַתְבְּרָא אִין בִּיר דְכַר הוּא וְתִקְטְלוּן יָתֵיהּ וְאִין בְּרַתָּא נוּקְבָא הִיא וְתִתְקַיֵים


And he said, When you attend Jehudith women, and see them bear, if it be a male child, you shall kill him; but if a daughter, you may let her live.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:17)


דְחִילָא חַיְיתָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וְלָא עָבָדוּ הֵיכְמָא דְמַלֵל עִמְהוֹן מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרַיִם וְקַיָימָא יַת בְּנַיָיא


But the midwives feared before the Lord, and would not do according to what the king of Mizraim had said to them, but they saved the children.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:18)


וּקְרָא מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרַיִם לְחַיָיתָא וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן לְמָה דֵין עֲבַדְתּוּן יַת פִּתְגָמָא הָדֵין וּקְיַימְתּוּן יַת בְּנַיָא


And the king of Mizraim called the midwives, and said to them, Why have you done this thing, and have saved the children?


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:19)


וַאֲמָרַן חַיְיתָא לְפַרְעה אֲרוּם לָא כִנְשַׁיָא מִצְרַיְתָא יְהוּדַיְיתָא אֲרוּם זְרִיזִין וְחַכִּימִין בְּדַעֲתֵּיהֶן הִינוּן קֳדָם עַד לָא תֵיתֵי לְוַותְהוֹן חַיְיתָא הִינִין תַּלְיַין עֵינֵיהֶן בִּצְלוֹי מַצְלָן וּבַעֲיָין רַחֲמִין מִן קֳדָם אֲבוּהֶן דְבִשְׁמַיָא וְהוּא שָמַע בְּקַל צְלוֹתְהוֹן מִן יַד הִינִין מִתְעַנְיַין וִילִידַן וּפָרְקַן בִּשְׁלָם


And the mid-wives said to Pharoh, The Jehudith women are not as the Mizraite, for they are sturdy (or, courageous) and wise-minded: before the midwife cometh to them they lift up their eyes in prayer, supplicating mercy before their Father who is in heaven, who heareth the voice of their prayer, and at once they are heard, and bring forth, and are delivered in peace.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:20)


וְאוֹטִיב יְיָ לְחַיְיתָא וּסְגוֹ עַמָא וּתְקִיפוּ לַחֲדָא


And the Lord did good to the midwives, and the people multiplied and prevailed greatly.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:21)


וַהֲוָה כַּד דְחִילָא חַיְתָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וּקְנוּ לְהוֹן שׁוּם טַב לְדָרַיָא וּבְנָא לְהוֹן מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ בֵּית מַלְכוּתָא וּבֵית כְּהוּנְתָּא רַבְּתָא


And forasmuch as the midwives feared before the Lord, they obtained for themselves a good name unto the ages; and the Word of the Lord up-builded for them a royal house, even the house of the high priesthood.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 1:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/1:22)


וְכַד חָמָא פַּרְעה כְּדֵין פַּקֵיד לְכָל עַמֵיהּ לְמֵימָר כָּל בִּיר דְכַר דְאִתְלִיד לִיהוּדָאֵי בְּנַהֲרָא תִּטְלוֹקְנֵיהּ וְכָל בְּרַתָּא תְּקַיְימוּן


But when Pharoh saw this, he commanded all his people, saying, Every male child that is born to the Jehudaee you shall cast into the river; but every daughter you may spare.



## Chapter 2

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/2:1)


וַאֲזַל עַמְרָם גַבְרָא דְמִשֵׁבֶט לֵוִי וְאוֹתִיב בְּכִילְתָא וְגִינוּנָא דְהִלוּלָא יַת יוֹכֶבֶד אִנְתְּתֵיהּ דְתָרִיךְ מִן קֳדָם גְזֵירְתָּא דְפַרְעה וַהֲוַת בְּרַת מֵאָה וּתְלָתִין שְׁנִין כַּד אֲהַדְרָהּ לְוָותֵיהּ וְאִתְעֲבֵיד לָהּ נִיסָא וְהַדְרַת לְעַלֵימוֹתָא כְּמָא דַהֲוַת כַּד הִיא זְעֵירְתָּא מִקְרְיָיא בְּרַת לֵוִי


And Amram, a man of the tribe of Levi, went and returned to live in marriage with Jokeved his wife, whom he had put away on account of the decree of Pharoh. And she was the daughter of a hundred and thirty years when he returned to her; but a miracle was wrought in her, and she returned unto youth as she was, when in her minority she was called the daughter of Levi.

And the woman conceived and bare a son at the end of six months; and she saw him to be a child of steadfastness, (or, of steadfast life,) and hid him three months, which made the number nine.

But she could conceal him no longer, for the Mizraee had become aware of him. And she took an ark of papyrus, (tunes,) and coated it with bitumen and pitch, and placed the child within it, and laid him among the reeds on the bank of the river.

and Mosheh turned, and considered in the wisdom of his mind, and understood that in no generation would there arise a proselyte from that Mizraite man, and that none of his children's children would ever be converted; and he smote the Mizraite, and buried him in the sand.

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 2:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/2:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/2:21)


וְכַד חַכִּים רְעוּאֵל דְעָרַק משֶׁה מִן קֳדָם פַרְעה טָלַק יָתֵיהּ לְגוּבָא וַהֲוַת צִפּוֹרָה בְּרַתֵּיהּ דִבְרֵיהּ מְפַרְנָסָא יָתֵיהּ בְּסִיתְרָא בִּזְמַן עִשַרְתִּי שְׁנִין וּלְסוֹף עִשַרְתִּי שְׁנִין אַפְקֵיהּ מִן גוּבָא וְעַל משֶׁה בְּגוֹי גִינוּנִיתָא דִרְעוּאֵל וַהֲוָה מוֹדֵי וּמַצְלֵי קֳדָם יְיָ דַעֲבֵד עִמֵיהּ נִיסִין וּגְבוּרַן וְאִיסְתְּכִיַית חוּטְרָא דְאִיתְבְּרִאַת בֵּינֵי שִׁמְשְׁתָא וְחַקְיָין וּמְפָרַשׁ עֲלָהּ שְׁמָא רַבָּא וְיַקִירָא דְבֵיהּ עָתִיד לְמֶעֱבַד יַת תִּמְהַיָא בְּמִצְרַיִם וּבֵיהּ עָתִיד לְמִבְזַע יַת יַמָא דְסוּף וּלְהַנְפָקָא מוֹי מִן כֵּיפָא וַהֲוָה דָעִיץ בְּגוֹ גִינוּנִיתָא וּמִן יַד אוֹשִׁיט יְדֵיהּ וְנַסְבֵיהּ הָא בְּכֵין צָבֵי משֶׁה לְמִתַּב עִם גַבְרָא וִיהַב יַת צִפּוֹרָה בְּרַת בְּרַתֵּיהּ לְמשֶׁה


But when Reuel knew that Mosheh had fled from before Pharoh he cast him into a pit; but Zipporah, the daughter of his son, maintained him with food, secretly, for the time of ten years; and at the end of ten years brought him out of the pit. And Mosheh went into the bedchamber of Reuel, and gave thanks and prayed before the Lord, who by him would work miracles and mighty acts. And there was shown to him the Rod which was created between the evenings, and on which was engraven and set forth the Great and Glorious Name, with which he was to do the wonders in Mizraim, and to divide the sea of Suph, and to bring, forth water from the rock. And it was infixed in the midst of the chamber, and he stretched forth his hand at once and took it. Then, behold, Mosheh was willing to dwell with the man, and he gave Zipporah, the daughter of his son, to Mosheh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 2:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/2:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/2:22)


וִילֵידַת בִּיר דְכַר וּקְרָא שְׁמֵיהּ גֵרְשׁוֹם אֲרוּם אָמַר דַיָר הֲוֵיתִי בְּאַרְעָא נוּכְרַיָיא דְלָא דִידִי


And she bare him a male child, and he called his name Gershom, Because, said he, a sojourner have I been in a strange land which is not mine.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 2:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/2:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/2:23)


וַהֲוָה בְּיוֹמַיָא סְגִיאַיָיא הָאִינוּן וְאִתְכְּתַשׁ מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרָיִם וּפַקֵיד לְקַטָלָא בּוּכְרַיָיא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל בְגִין לְמִסְחֵי בְּאַדְמֵיהוֹן וְאִתְאַנָחוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מִן פּוּלְחָנָא דַהֲוָה קַשְׁיָא עֲלֵיהוֹן וּזְעָקוּ וּסְלֵיקַת קְבֵילְתְּהוֹן לִשְׁמֵי מְרוֹמָא דַיְיָ וַאֲמַר בְּמֵימְרֵיהּ לְמִפְרוֹקִינוּן מִן פּוּלְחָנָא


And it was after many of those days that the king of Mizraim was struck (with disease), and he commanded to kill the firstborn of the sons of Israel, that he might bathe himself in their blood. And the sons of Israel groaned with the labour that was hard upon them; and they cried, and their cry ascended to the high heavens of the Lord. And He spake in His Word to deliver them from the travail.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 2:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/2:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/2:24)


וּשְׁמִיעַ קֳדָם יְיָ קְבֵילְתְּהוֹן וּדְכִיר קֳדָם יְיָ יַת קְיָימֵיהּ דְקַיֵים עִם אַבְרָהָם וְעִם יִצְחָק וְעִם יַעֲקב


And their cry was heard before the Lord, and before the Lord was the covenant remembered which He had covenanted with Abraham, with Izhak, and with Jakob.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 2:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/2:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/2:25)


וַחֲמָא יְיָ צַעַר שִׁעֲבּוּדְהוֹן דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וּגְלֵי קֳדָמוֹי יַת תִּיוֹבְתָּא דְעָבָדוּ בְּטוּמְרָא דְלָא יָדְעוּ אַנַשׁ בְּחַבְרֵיהּ


And the Lord looked upon the affliction of the bondage of the sons of Israel; and the repentance was revealed before Him which they exercised in concealment, so as that no man knew that of his companion.



## Chapter 3

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:1)


וּמשֶׁה הַוָה רָעֵי יַת עָנַא דְיִתְרוֹ חָמוֹי רַבָּא דְמִדְיָן וּדְבַר יַת עָנָא לַאֲתַר שְׁפַר רַעֲיָא לַאֲחוֹרֵי מַדְבְּרָא וְאָתָא לְטַוְורָא דְאִתְגְלֵי עֲלוֹי יְקָרָא דַיְיָ לְחוֹרֵב


But Mosheh was keeping the flock of Jethro his father-in-law, the rabba of Midian; and he had led the flock to a pleasant place of pasturage which is behind the desert, and had come to the mountain on which was revealed the glory of the Lord, even Horeb.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:2)


וְאִתְגְלֵי זַגְנוּגָאֵל מַלְאָכָא דַיְיָ לֵיהּ בְּלַהֲבֵי אֵישָׁתָא מִגוֹ סַנְיָא וַחֲמָא וְהָא סַנְיָא מַשְׁרִיב בְּאֵישָׁתָא וְסַנְיָא לֵיתוֹ יַקִיד וּמִתְאָכֵל בְּנוּרָא


And Zagnugael, the angel of the Lord, appeared to him in a flame of fire in the midst of the bush. And he gazed, and, behold, the bush burned with fire, yet the bush was neither burned nor consumed with fire.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:3)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה אִתְפְּנֵי כְדוּן וְאֵיחְמֵי יַת חֶזְוָונָא רַבָּא הָדֵין מַדֵין לָא שְׁרִיב סַנְיָא


And Mosheh said, I will turn aside now and consider this great sight, why the bush is not burned.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:4)


וּגְלֵי קֳדָם יְיָ אֲרוּם אִיתְפְּנֵי לְמֵיחְמֵי וּקְרָא לֵיהּ יְיָ מִגוֹ סַנְיָא וַאֲמַר משֶׁה משֶׁה וַאֲמַר הָא אֲנָא


And when it was seen before the Lord that he turned to look, the Lord called to him from the midst of the bush and said, Mosheh, Mosheh ! And he said, Behold me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:5)


וַאֲמַר לָא תִקְרַב הַלְכָא שְׁלוּף סֵינָךְ מֵעַל רִגְלָךְ אֲרוּם אַתְרָא דְאַנְתְּ קָאִים עֲלוֹי אָתַר קַדִישׁ הוּא וְעָלוֹי אַנְתְּ עָתִיד לְקַבָּלָא אוֹרַיְיתָא לְמַלְפָא יָתָהּ לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


And He said, Approach not hither, take the shoe from thy feet, for the place on which thou standest is a holy place; and upon it thou art to receive the Law, to teach it to the sons of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:6)


וַאֲמַר אֲנָא הוּא אֱלָהֵיהּ דְאָבוּךְ אֱלָהֵיהּ דְאַבְרָהָם אֱלָהֵיהּ דְיִצְחָק וֵאלָהֵיהּ דְיַעֲקב וּכְבָשִׁינוּן משֶׁה לְאַנְפּוֹי אֲרוּם הֲוָה דָחִיל מִלְמִסְתְּכֵי בְּצֵית אִיקַר שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ


And He said, I am the God of thy father, the God of Abraham, the God of Izhak, and the God of Jakob. And Mosheh covered his face; for he was afraid to look upon the height of the glory of the Shekinah of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:7)


וַאֲמַר מִיגְלֵי גְלֵי קֳדָמַי יַת סִיגוּף עַמִי דִבְמִצְרַיִם וְיַת קְבֵלְתְּהוֹן שְׁמִיעַ קֳדָמַי מִן קֳדָם מְשַׁעֲבְּדֵיהוֹן אֲרוּם גְלֵי קֳדָמַי כֵּיבֵיהוֹן


And He said, The oppression of My people who are in Mizraim is verily manifest before Me, and heard before Me is their cry on account of them who hold them in bondage; for their affliction is known before Me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:8)


וְאִיתְגְלֵיתִי יוֹמָא דֵין עֲלָךְ בְּגִין דִי בְמֵימְרִי לְשֵׁיזְבוּתְהוֹן מִן יְדָא דְמִצְרָאֵי וּלְאַסָקוּתְהוֹן מִן אַרְעָא הַהִיא מְסָאֲבָתָא לְאַרְעָא טַבְתָא וּפִתְיָא בִּתְחוּמִין לְאַרְעָא עַבְדָא חֲלַב וּדְבָשׁ לַאֲתַר דְדַיְירִין תַמָן כְּנַעֲנָאֵי וְחִיתָּאֵי וֶאֱמוֹרָאֵי וּפְרִיזָאֵי וְחִיוָואֵי וִיבוּסָאֵי


And I have revealed Myself to thee this day, that by My Word they may be delivered from the hand of the Mizraee, to bring them up out of the unclean land, unto a good land, and large in its boundaries, a land yielding milk and honey, unto the place where dwell the Kenaanaee, and the Hittaee, and the Amoraee, and the Pherizaee, and the Hivaee, and the Jebusaee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:9)


וּכְדוֹן הָא קְבֵילַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל סְלִיקַת לְקָדָמָי וּלְחוֹד גְלֵי קֳדָמָי יַת דוֹחֲקָא דְמִצְרָאֵי דַחֲקִין יַתְהוֹן


And now, behold, the cry of the sons of Israel cometh up before Me, and the bruising of the Mizraee wherewith they bruise them is also revealed before Me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:10)


וּכְדוֹן אִיתָא וְאַשַׁדְרִינָךְ לְוַת פַּרְעה וְאַפֵּיק יַת עַמִי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מִמְצְרָיִם


And now, come, and I will send thee unto Pharoh, and thou shalt bring forth My people, the sons of Israel, out of Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:11)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה קֳדָם יְיָ מַאן אֲנָא אֲרוּם אֵזִיל לְוַת פַּרְעה וַאֲרוּם אַפֵּיק יַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מִמִצְרָיִם


And Mosheh said before the Lord, Who am I, that I should go to Pharoh, and bring forth the sons of Israel out of Mizraim?


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:12)


וַאֲמַר אֲרוּם יְהֵי מֵימְרִי בְּסַעְדָךְ וְדֵין לָךְ סִימָנָא דַאֲנָא שַׁדְרָתָךְ בְּהַנְפּוּקָתָךְ יַת עַמָא מִמִצְרַיִם תְּפַלְחוּן קֳדָם יְיָ דִתְקַבְּלוּן יַת אוֹרַיְיתִי עַל טַוְורָא הָדֵין


But He said, Therefore My Word shall be for thy help; and this shall be the sign to thee that I have sent thee: when thou hast, brought the people forth from Mizraim, ye shall worship before the Lord, because ye shall have received the Law upon this mountain.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:13)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה קֳדָם יְיָ אֲנָא אֵזִיל לְוַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְאֵמַר לְהוֹן יְיָ אֱלָהָא דַאֲבָהַתְכוֹן שַׁדְרַנִי לְוַתְכוֹן וְיֵימְרוּן לִי מַה שְׁמֵיהּ מַה אֵימַר לְהוֹן


And Mosheh said before the Lord, Behold, I will go to the sons of Israel, and say to them, The Lord God of your fathers hath sent me to you: and they will say to me, What is His Name ? What shall I say to them?


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:14)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה דֵין דְאָמַר וַהֲוָה עַלְמָא אָמַר וַהֲוָה כּוּלָא וַאֲמַר כִּדְנָא תֵּימַר לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל אֲנָא הוּא דְהַוֵינָא וְעָתִיד לְמֶהֱוֵי שַׁדְרַנִי לְוַותְכוֹן


And the Lord said unto Mosheh, He who spake, and the world was; who spake, and all things were. And He said, This thou shalt say to the sons of Israel, I AM HE WHO IS, AND WHO WILL BE, hath sent me unto you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:15)


וַאֲמַר תּוּב יְיָ לְמשֶׁה כִּדְנַן תֵּימַר לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל אֱלָהָא דְאַבְהַתְכוֹן אֱלָהֵיהּ דְאַבְרָהָם אֱלָהֵיהּ דְיִצְחָק וֵאלָהֵיהּ דְיַעֲקב שַׁדְרַנִי לְוַותְכוֹן דֵין הוּא שְׁמִי לְעָלַם וְדֵין דוּכְרָנִי לְכָל דַר וָדַר


And the Lord said again unto Mosheh, Thus shalt thou speak to the sons of Israel, The God of your fathers, the God of Abraham, the God of Izhak, and the God of Jakob, hath sent me unto you. This is His Name for ever, and this is His Memorial to every generation and generation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:16)


אִיזֵל וְתִכְנוֹש יַת סָבֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְתֵימַר יְיָ אֱלָהָא דְאַבְהַתְכוֹן אִתְגְלֵי לִי אֱלָהֵיהּ דְאַבְרָהָם יִצְחָק וְיַעֲקב לְמֵימַר מִידְכַּר דְכִירְנָא יַתְכוֹן וְיַת עוּלְבָנָא דְאִיתְעֲבִיד לְכוֹן בְּמִצְרַיִם


Go, and assemble the elders of Israel, and say to them, The Lord God of your fathers hath appeared unto me, the God of Abraham, Izhak, and Jakob, saying, Remembering, I have remembered you, and the injury that is done you in Mizraim;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:17)


וַאֲמָרִית בְּמֵימְרִי אַפִּיק יַתְכוֹן מִסִיגוּף מִצְרָאֵי לְאַרַע כְּנַעֲנָאֵי וְחִיתָּאֵי וְאִימוֹרָאֵי וּפְרִיזָאֵי וְחִיוָאֵי וִיבוּסָאֵי לְאַרְעָא עַבְדָא חֲלָב וּדְבָשׁ


and I have said in My Word, I will bring you up out from the oppression of the Mizraee into the land of the Kenaanaee, and Hittaee, and Amoraee, and Pherizaee, and Hivaee, and Jebusaee, to the land that yieldeth milk and honey.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:18)


וִיקַבְּלוּן מִינָךְ וְתֵיעוּל אַנְתְּ וְסָבֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְוַת מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרַיִם וְתֵימְרוּן לֵיהּ יְיָ אֱלָהָא דִיהוּדָאֵי אִתְקְרֵי עֲלָנָא וּכְדוּן נֵזֵיל כְּדוּן מַהֲלָךְ תְּלָתָא יוֹמִין בְּמַדְבְּרָא וּנְדַבַּח קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא


And they will hearken to thee: and thou and the elders of Israel shall go to the king of Mizraim and say to him, The Lord God of the Jehudaee hath called us; and now let us go a journey of three days into the wilderness, to sacrifice before the Lord our God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:19)


וַאֲנָא קֳדָמַי גְלֵי אֲרוּם לָא יִשְׁבּוֹק יַתְכוֹן מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרַיִם לְמֵיזַל וְלָא מִן דְחֵילֵיהּ תַּקִיף אֱלָהֵין בְּגִין דִבְמֵימְרִי לְאוֹכְחוֹתֵיהּ בְּמַכְתָּשִׁין בִּישִׁין


But it is manifest before Me that the king of Mizraim will not let you go, (no,) not from fear of Him who is Mighty, until that by My Word he shall have been punished with evil plagues.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:20)


וְתִתְעַכְּבוּן תַּמָן עַד דְאֵשַׁדַר יַת מְחַת גְבוּרְתִּי וְאִמְחֵי יַת מִצְרָאֵי בְּכָל פְּרִשְׁוָותִי דְאֶעְבַּד בֵּינֵיהוֹן וּמִבָּתַר כֵּן יִפְטוֹר יַתְכוֹן


And ye will be hindered there until I have sent forth the stroke of My power, and have smitten Mizraee with all My wonders, that I will do among them; and afterward he will release you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:21)


וְאֶתֵּן יַת עַמָא הָדֵין לְרַחֲמִין בְּעֵינֵי מִצְרָאֵי וִיהֵי אֲרוּם תַּחֲכוּן מִן תַּמָן פְּרוּקִין לָא תַהֲכוּן רֵיקָנִין


And I will give this people grace in the eyes of the Mizraee; and it shall be that when ye go free from thence, ye shall not go empty.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 3:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/3:22)


וְתִשְׁאַל אִתְּתָא מִן שְׁבַבְתָּא וּמִן קְרִיבַת כּוֹתְלֵי בֵיתָהּ מָנִין דִכְסַף וּמָנִין דִדְהַב וּלְבוּשִׁין וּתְעַטְרוּן עַל בָּנֵיכוֹן וְעַל בְּנָתֵיכוֹן וּתְרוֹקִינוּן יַת מִצְרָאֵי


But a woman shall ask of her neighbour, and from those next to the wall of her house, vessels of silver, and vessels of gold, and vestments; and you shall set them as crowns upon your sons and your daughters, and make the Mizraee empty.



## Chapter 4

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:1)


וְאָתֵיב משֶׁה וַאֲמַר וְהָא לָא יְהֵימְנוּן לִי וְלָא יְקַבְּלוּן מִנִי אֲרוּם יֵימְרוּן לָא אִיתְגְלֵי לָךְ יְיָ


And Mosheh answered and said, But, behold, they will not believe me, nor hearken to me; for they will say, The Lord hath not appeared to thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:2)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ יְיָ מַה דִין בִּידָךְ וַאֲמַר חוּטְרָא


And the Lord said to him, What is that in thy hand ? And he said, The rod.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:3)


וַאֲמַר טְלוֹק יָתֵיהּ לְאַרְעָא וְטַלְקֵיהּ לְאַרְעָא וַהֲוָה לְחִוְיָא וַעֲרַק משֶׁה מִן קֳדָמוֹי


And He said, Cast it on the ground; and he cast it to the ground, and it became a serpent; and Mosheh fled from before it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:4)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶה אוֹשִׁיט יְדָךְ וְאֵיחוּד בְּקוּטְנֵיהּ וְאוֹשִׁיט יְדֵיהּ וְאַתְקֵיף בֵּיהּ וַהֲוָה לְחוּטְרָא בִּידֵיהּ


And the Lord said to Mosheh, Stretch forth thy hand and seize (it) by its tail. And he stretched forth his hand and grasped it, and it became the rod in his hand:


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:5)


מִן בִּגְלַל דִיהֵמְנוּן אֲרוּם אִתְגְלֵי לָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָא דְאַבָהַתְכוֹן אֱלָהֵיהּ דְאַבְרָהָם אֱלָהֵיהּ דְיִצְחָק וֵאלָהֵיהּ דְיַעֲקב


In order that they may believe that the Lord God of their fathers, the God of Abraham, the God of Izhak, and the God of Jakob, hath revealed Himself to thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:6)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לֵיהּ תּוּב אָעֵל כְּדוֹן יְדָךְ בְּחוּבָךְ וְאָעֵיל יָתֵיהּ בְּגוֹ חוּבֵיהּ וְהַנְפָּקָהּ וְהָא יְדֵיהּ סְגִירְתָּא מְחַוְורָא הִיא כְּתַלְגָא


And the Lord said to him again, Put now thy hand within thy breast (Choba); and he put it within his breast, and withdrew it, and, behold, his hand was leprous, it was white as snow.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:7)


וַאֲמַר אָתֵיב יְדָךְ לְעִיטְפָךְ וְאָתֵיב יְדֵיהּ לְחוּבֵיהּ וְהַנְפָּקָהּ מִן גוֹי חוּבֵיהּ וְתָבַת לְמֶהֱוֵי בַרְיָא הֵי כְּבִישְרֵיהּ


And He said, Return thy hand into thy bosom (Aitaph); and he returned his hand to his breast, and withdrew it from his breast, and it had become clean as his flesh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:8)


וִיהֵי אִם לָא יְהֵימְנוּן לָךְ וְלָא יְקַבְּלוּן לְקַל אָתָא קַדְמָאָה וִיהֵמְנוּן לְקַל אָתָא בַּתְרָאָה


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:9)


וִיהֵי אִין לָא יְהֵימְנוּן אוּף לִתְרֵין אָתַיָא הָאִילֵן וְלָא יְקַבְּלוּן מִינָךְ וְתִסַב מִן מוֹי דִבְנַהֲרָא וְתֵשׁוֹד לְיַבֶּשְׁתָּא וִיהוֹן מוֹי דְתִיסַב מִן נַהֲרָא וִיהוֹן לִדְמָא בְּיַבֶּשְׁתָּא


And if they believe not these two signs, nor receive from thee, thou shalt take of the water of the river and pour it on the ground, and the water that thou shalt take from the river shall become blood upon the ground.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:10)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה קֳדָם יְיָ בְּבָעוּ יְיָ לָא גְבַר דַבְּרָן אֲנָא אוּף מֵאִתְמְלֵי אוּף מִן לְקָדָמוֹי אוּף מִן שַׁעְתָּא דְמַלֵילְתָּא עִם עַבְדָךְ אֲרוּם חֲגַר פּוּם וַחֲגַר מַמְלֵל אֲנָא


And Mosheh said before the Lord, O Lord, I pray: I am not a man of words, nor ever have been before that Thou didst speak with Thy servant; for I am of a staggering * mouth and staggering speech. *Or, lame


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:11)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לֵיהּ מַאן הוּא דְשַׁוֵי מַמְלֵל פּוּמָא בְּפוּם אָדָם קַדְמָאִי אוֹ מַאן שַׁוִי אַלִימָא אוֹ חַרְשָׁא אוֹ פִּתִיחָא אוֹ סַמְיָא הֲלָא אֲנָא יְיָ


And the Lord said, Who is he who first put the language of the mouth into the mouth of man? or who hath appointed the dumb or the deaf, the open-seeing or the blind, but I the Lord?


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:12)


וּכְדוֹן אִיזֵל וַאֲנָא בְּמֵימְרִי אֱהֵא עִם מַמְלֵל פּוּמָךְ וַאֲלִיף יָתָךְ מַה דִתְמַלֵיל


And now go, and I by My Word will be with the speaking of thy mouth, and will teach thee what thou shalt say.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:13)


וַאֲמַר בְּבָעוּ בְּרַחֲמִין מִן קֳדָמָךְ יְיָ שְׁלַח כְּדוֹן שְׁלִיחוּתָךְ בְּיַד פִּנְחָס דְחָמֵי לְמִשְׁתַּלְחָא בְּסוֹף יוֹמַיָא


And he said, I pray for mercy before the Lord. Send now Thy sending by the hand of Phinehas, by whom it is to be sent at the end of the days.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:14)


וּתְקֵיף רוּגְזָא דַיְיָ בְּמשֶׁה וַאֲמַר הֲלָא אַהֲרן אָחוּךְ לֵיוָאֵי גְלֵי קֳדָמַי אֲרוּם מַלָלָא יְמַלֵיל הוּא וְאוּף הָא הוּא נָפֵיק לִקְדָמוּתָךְ וְיַחֲמִינָךְ וִיחֲדֵי בְּלִבֵּיהּ


And the anger of the Lord was kindled against Mosheh, and He said, Is it not manifest before Me that Aharon thy brother speaking can speak? And, behold, also, he cometh forth to meet thee, and will see thee and rejoice in his heart.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:15)


וּתְמַלֵל עִמֵיהּ וּתְשַׁוֵי יַת פִּתְגָמַיָא בְּפוּמֵיהּ וּמֵימְרִי יְהֵי עִם מֵימַר פּוּמָךְ וְעִם מֵימַר פּוּמֵיהּ וְאַלִיף יַתְכוֹן יַת מַה דְתַעַבְּדוּן


And thou shalt speak with him, and put the matter in his mouth, and My Word shall be with the word of thy mouth, and with the word of his mouth, and I will instruct you what you are to do.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:16)


וִימַלֵיל הוּא לָךְ עִם עַמָא וִיהֵי הוּא יֶהֱוֵי לָךְ לִמְתֻּרְגְמִין וְאַנְתְּ תֶּהֱוֵי לֵיהּ לְרַב תְּבוֹעַ אוּלְפַן מִן קֳדָם יְיָ


And he shall speak for thee with the people, and be to thee an interpreter, and thou to him the principal, seeking instruction from before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:17)


וְיַת חוּטְרָא הָדֵין תִּיסַב בִּידָךְ דִתַעֲבֵיד בֵּיהּ יַת אָתַיָיא


And this rod take thou in thy hand to work therewith the signs.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:18)


וַאֲזַל משֶׁה וְתַב לְוַת יִתְרוֹ חָמוֹי וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ אֵיזִיל כְּדוּן לְוַת אַחַי דִבְמִצְרַיִם וְאֵיחְמֵי הַעַד כְּדוּן אִינוּן קַיְימִין וַאֲמַר יִתְרוֹ לְמשֶׁה אֵיזֵיל לִשְׁלָם


And Mosheh went, and returned unto Jethro his father in law, and said, I will now go to my brethren who are in Mizraim, to see how they now live. And Jethro said to Mosheh, Go in peace.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:19)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה בְּמִדְיָן אִיזֵיל תּוּב לְמִצְרַיִם אֲרוּם אִתְרוֹקְנוּ וְנַחֲתוּ מִנִכְסֵיהוֹן וְהָא אִינוּן חֲשִׁיבוּן כְּמֵתַיָא כָּל גוּבְרַיָא דַהֲווֹ תַּבְעִין יַת נַפְשָׁךְ לְמִיסַב יָתָהּ


And the Lord said unto Mosheh in Midian, Go, return to Mizraim; for they have come to nought, and gone down from their possessions; behold, all the men who sought to take thy life are reckoned as the dead.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:20)


וּדְבַר משֶׁה יַת אִינְתְּתֵיהּ וְיַת בְּנוֹי וְאַרְכְּבִינוּן עַל חַמְרָא וְתַב לְאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם וּנְסֵיב משֶׁה יַת חוּטְרָא דְנָסַב מִן גִנוּנִיתָא דְחָמוֹי וְהוּא מִסַפִּיר כּוּרְסֵי יְקָרָא מַתְקְלֵיהּ אַרְבְּעִין סְאִין וְעִילוֹ חָקוּק וּמְפָרֵשׁ שְׁמָא רַבָּא וְיַקִירָא וּבֵיהּ אִתְעֲבִידוּ נִיסִין מִן קֳדָם יְיָ בִּידֵיהּ


And Mosheh took his wife and his sons, and made them ride on the ass, and returned to the land of Mizraim. And Mosheh took the rod which he had brought away from the chamber of his father-in-law; and it was from the sapphire Throne of glory, in weight forty sein; and upon it was engraven and set forth the Great and Glorious Name by which the signs should be wrought before the Lord by his hand.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:21)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה בִּמְהָכָךְ לְמֵיתוּב לְמִצְרַיִם חֲמִי כָּל תִּמְהַיָא דְשַׁוִיתִי בִידָךְ וְתַעֲבִידִינוּן קֳדָם פַּרְעה וַאֲנָא אַתְקֵיף יַת יִצְרָא דְלִבֵּיהּ וְלָא יִפְטוֹר יַת עַמָא


And the Lord said to Mosheh, In going to return into Mizraim, consider all the miracles that I have put in thy hand, and do them before Pharoh: and I will make obstinate the disposition (passion) of his heart, and he will not deliver the people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:22)


וְתֵימַר לְפַרְעה כִּדְנָא אָמַר יְיָ בְּרִי בּוּכְרִי יִשְרָאֵל


And thou shalt say to Pharoh, Thus saith the Lord, Israel is My first-born son;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:23)


וַאֲמָרִית לָךְ פְּטוֹר יַת בְּרִי וְיִפְלַח קֳדָמַי וּמְסָרֵב אַנְתְּ לְמִפְטְרֵיהּ הָא אֲנָא קָטֵיל יַת בְּרָךְ בּוּכְרָךְ


and to thee I say, Let My son go free, that he may worship before Me; and if thou refuse to let him go, behold, I will slay thy first-born son.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:24)


וַהֲוָה בְאוֹרְחָה בְּבֵית מִבְתּוֹתָא וְעַרַע בֵּיהּ מַלְאָכָא דַיְיָ וּבְעָא לְמִקְטְלֵיהּ מִן בִּגְלַל גֵרְשׁוֹם בְּרֵיהּ דְלָא הֲוָה גְזִיר עַל עֵיסַק יִתְרוֹ חָמוֹי דְלָא שַׁבְקֵיהּ לְמִגְזְרֵיהּ בְּרַם אֱלִיעֶזֶר הֲוָה גָזַר בִּתְּנָאָה דְאַתְנִיוּ תַּרְוֵיהוֹן


But it was on the way, in the place of lodging that the angel of the Lord met him, and sought to kill him, because Gershom his son had not been circumcised, inasmuch as Jethro his father-in-law had not permitted him to circumcise him: but Eliezer had been circumcised, by an agreement between them two.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:25)


וּנְסִיבַת צִפּוֹרָה טִינְרָא וּגְזָרַת יַת עוּרְלַת גֵרְשׁוֹם בְרָהּ וְאַקְרִיבַת יַת גְזֵירַת מָהוּלְתָּא לְרַגְלוֹי דְמַלְאָךְ חַבָּלָא וַאֲמָרַת חַתְנָא בְּעָא לְמִיגְזוֹר וְחָמוֹי עַכִּיב וּכְדוֹן אֲדָם גְזוּרְתָּא הָדֵין יְכַפֵּר עַל חַתְנָא דִילִי


And Zipporah took a stone, and circumcised the foreskin of Gershom her son, and brought the severed part to the feet of the angel, the Destroyer, and said, The husband sought to circumcise, but the father-in-law obstructed him; and now let this blood of the circumcision atone for my husband.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:26)


וּפְסַק מַלְאָךְ חַבָּלָא מִנֵיהּ בְּכֵן שַׁבְּחַת צִפּוֹרָה וַאֲמַרַת מַה חָבִיב הוּא אֲדָם גְזוּרְתָּא הָדֵין דְשֵׁזִיב יַת חֲתָנָא מִן יְדוֹי דְמַלְאָךְ חֲבָּלָא


And the destroying angel desisted from him, so that Zipporah gave thanks, and said, How lovely is the blood of this circumcision that hath delivered my husband from the angel of destruction!


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:27)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְאַהֲרן אִיזֵיל לְקַדְמוּת משֶׁה לְמַדְבְּרָא וַאֲזַל וְאַרְעֵיהּ בְּטַוְורָא דְאִתְגְלֵי עֲלוֹי יְקָרָא דַיְיָ וּנְשֵׁיק לֵיהּ


And the Lord had said to Aharon, Go and meet Mosheh in the desert. And he came and met him at the mountain where was revealed the glory of the Lord, and he embraced him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:28)


וּתְנֵי משֶׁה לְאַהֲרן יַת פִּתְגָמָא הָאִלֵין דְשַׁלְחֵיהּ וְיַת כָּל אָתַיָא דְפַקְדֵיהּ לְמֶעֱבַד


And Mosheh delivered to Aharon all these words with which he had sent him, and all the signs that he had instructed him to work.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:29)


וַאֲזַל משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן וּכְנָשׁוּ יַת כָּל סָבֵי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


And Mosheh and Aharon went, and gathered together all the elders of the sons of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:30)


וּמַלֵיל אַהֲרן יַת פִּתְגָמַיָא דְמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה וְעָבַד אָתַיָא לְעֵינֵי עַמָא


And Aharon spake all the words which the Lord had spoken with Mosheh, and did the signs in the eyes of the people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 4:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/4:31)


וְהֵימִין עַמָא וּשְׁמָעוּ אֲרוּם דְכִיר יְיָ יַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וַאֲרוּם גְלֵי קֳדָמוֹי שִׁעְבּוּדְהוֹן וּגְחָנַן וּסְגִידוּ


And the people believed, and heard that the Lord had remembered the sons of Israel, and that their bondage was manifest before Him; and they bowed themselves and worshipped.



## Chapter 5

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:1)


וּבָתַר כְּדֵין עָלוּ משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן וַאֲמָרוּ לְפַרְעה כִּדְנָא אָמַר יְיָ אֱלָהָא דְיִשְרָאֵל פְּטוֹר יַת עַמִי וְיַעַבְדוּן לִי חַגָא בְּמַדְבְּרָא


And after this Mosheh and Aharon went in unto Pharoh, and said, Thus saith the Lord, the God of Israel: Release My people, that they may make unto Me a festival in the wilderness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:2)


וַאֲמַר פַּרְעה שְׁמָא דַיְיָ לָא אִיתְגְלֵי לִי דַאֲקַבֵּל בְּמֵימְרֵיהּ לְמִפְטוֹר יַת יִשְרָאֵל לָא אַשְׁכָּחִית בְּסֵפֶר מַלְאָכַיָא כְּתִיב יַת שְׁמָא דַיְיָ מִינֵהּ לֵית אֲנָא דָחִיל וְאוּף יַת יִשְרָאֵל לָא אִיפְטוֹר


And Pharoh said, The name of the Lord is not made known to me, that I should receive His word to release Israel. I have not found written in the Book of the Angels the name of the Lord. Of Him I am not afraid, neither will I release Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:3)


וַאֲמָרוּ אֱלָהָא דִיהוּדָאֵי אִיתְקְרִי שְׁמֵיהּ עֲלָנָא נְטַיֵיל כְּדוֹן מַהֲלָךְ תְּלָתָא יוֹמִין בְּמַדְבְּרָא וּנְדַבַּח נִיכְסַת חַגָא קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהִין דִלְמָא יֶאֱרַע יָתָן בְּמוֹתָא אוֹ בְּקַטְלָא


And they said, The Name of the God of the Jehudaee is invoked by (or upon) us. We will go, then, to proceed three days into the desert, and offer the sacrifices of a festival before the Lord God, that death and slaughter befall us not.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:4)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרַיִם לְמָא משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן תְּבַטְלוּן יַת עַמָא מֵעֲבִידְתְּהוֹן אִיזִילוּ לְפוּלְחַנְכוֹן


And the king of Mizraim said to Mosheh and Aharon, Why do you make the people cease from their labours? Go to your work.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:5)


וַאֲמַר פַּרְעה הָא סַגִין הִינוּן כְּדוּן עַמָא דְאַרְעָא דְאַתּוּן מְבַטְלִין יַתְהוֹן מִפּוּלְחַנְהוֹן


And Pharoh said, Behold, this people of the land are many, whom you would stop from their service.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:6)


וּפַקֵיד פַּרְעה בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא יַת שׁוּלְטָנַיָא דְעַמָא וְיַת סַרְכוֹי לְמֵימָר


And Pharoh that day commanded the officers of the people and their exactors, saying,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:7)


לָא תוֹסְפוּן לְמִיתַּן תִּיבְנָא לְעַמָא לְמִירְמֵי לִבְנַיָא הֵי כְמֵאִיתְמְלֵי וְהֵי כְמִלְקָדָמוֹי הִינוּן יֵזְלוּן וִיגַבְּבוּן לְהוֹן תִּבְנָא


You shall no more give straw to the people to cast bricks as heretofore; let them go and collect straw for themselves;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:8)


וְיַת סְכוּם לִבְנַיָא דְהִינוּן עָבְדִין מֵאִיתְמְלֵי וּמִדְקָדָמוֹי תְּמַנוּן עֲלֵיהוֹן לָא תִמְנְעוּן מִנֵיהּ אֲרוּם בַּטְלָנִין הִינוּן בְּגִין כֵּן הִינוּן צַוְוחִין לְמֵימַר נֵיזִיל נְדַבַּח נִיכְסַת חַגָא קֳדָם אֱלָהָנָא


but the (same) number of bricks which they have heretofore made ye shall lay upon them, and not diminish from it, because they are idle; therefore they clamour, saying, Let us go to offer the sacrifice of a festival before our God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:9)


תִּתְקַף פּוּלְחָנָא עִלוֹי גוּבְרַיָא וְיִתְעַסְקוּן בָּה וְלָא יִתְרַחֲצוּן עַל מִילֵי שִׁקְרָא


Make their work strenuous upon the men, that they may be occupied with it, and not be setting their hopes upon lying words.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:10)


וּנְפָקוּ שׁוּלְטָנֵי עַמָא וְסַרְכוֹי וַאֲמָרוּ לְעַמָא כִּדְנָא אֲמַר פַּרְעה לֵית אֲנָא יָהִיב לְכוֹן תִּיבְנָא


And the officers and exactors of the people went forth, and said to the people, Thus saith Pharoh, I will not give you straw;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:11)


אַתּוּן אִיזִילוּ סָבוּ לְכוֹן תִּיבְנָא מִן אָתַר דְתִשְׁכְּחוּן אֲרוּם לָא מִתְמְנַע מִפּוּלְחַנְכוֹן מִדָעַם


you must go and take straw wherever you can find it; for your work will not in anywise be diminished.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:12)


וְאִתְבַּדַר עַמָא בְכָל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם לְגַבְבָא גִילֵי לְתִיבְנָא


And the people were scattered abroad in all the land of Mizraim to gather stubble for the straw.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/5:13)


וְשׁוּלְטָנַיָא דַחֲקִין לְמֵימָר אַשְׁלִימוּ עֲבִידַתְכוֹן פִּתְגָם יוֹם בְּיוֹמֵיהּ הֵיכְמָא דַהֲוֵיתוּן עָבְדִין כַּד הֲוָה יָהֵיב לְכוֹן תִּיבְנָא


But the officers were pressing, saying, Fulfil your work day by day, as you did when the straw was given you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 5:14

and they said to them, Our affliction is manifest before the Lord, but our punishment is from you who have made our smell offensive before Pharoh and his servants; for you have occasioned a sword to be put into their hand to kill us.

From the hour that I went in unto Pharoh to speak in Thy name, this people hath suffered evil, and delivering Thou hast not delivered them.



## Chapter 6

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 6:1

And I was revealed unto Abraham, and to Izhak, and to Jakob, as EI-Shaddai; but My Name Ye-ya, as it discovereth My Glory,* was not known to them. *Or, in the face of My Shekinah

And now cometh before Me the groaning of the sons of Israel, because the Mizraee do enslave them; and I remember My covenant.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/6:6)

Therefore say to the sons of Israel, I am the Lord; and I will bring you forth from the oppressive bondage of the Mizraee, and will deliver you from your servitude, and save you with an uplifted arm, and by great judgments.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/6:7)

And I will bring you nigh before Me to be a people, and I will be a God unto you, and you shall know that I am the Lord your God who hath led you forth from the hard service of the Mizraee.

And Mosheh spake according to this to the sons of Israel; but they received not from Mosheh, through anxiety of spirit, and from the strange and hard service which was upon their hands.

And the Lord spake with Mosheh and with Aharon, and gave them admonition for the sons of Israel, and sent them to Pharoh, king of Mizraim, to send forth the children of Israel from the land of Mizraim.

And the sons of Shimeon, Jemuel, and Jamin, and Ohad, and Jakin, and Sochar, and Shaul (he is Zimri, who yielded himself unto fornication, as among the Kenaanaee); these are the race of Shimeon.

וְאִלֵין שְׁמָהַת בְּנוֹי דְלֵוִי לְיִיחוּסֵיהוֹן גֵרְשׁוֹן וּקְהָת וּמְרָרִי וּשְׁנֵי חַיוֹי דְלֵוִי מְאָה וּתְלָתִין וּשְׁבַע שְׁנִין חַיָיא עַד דְחָמָא יַת משֶׁה וְיַת אַהֲרן פְּרִיקַיָיא דְיִשְרָאֵל

And the sons of Gershon, Libni and Shemei, according to their generations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 6:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/6:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/6:18)

And the sons of Kehath, Amram, and Jitshar, and Hebron, and Uzziel. And the years of the life of Kehath the saint, a hundred and thirty and three years. He lived to see Phinehas, who is Elijah, the Great Priest, who is to be sent to the captivity of Israel at the end of the days.

-these are they who spake with Pharoh, king of Mizraim, that he should send out the sons of Israel from Mizraim; it is Mosheh the prophet, and Aharon the priest.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/6:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/6:28)

And it was in the day when the Lord spake with Mosheh in the land of Mizraim, that Aharon gave a listening ear, and heard what He spake with him.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/6:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/6:29)

And the Lord spake with Mosheh, and said to him, I am the Lord. Say to Pharoh, king of Mizraim all that I tell thee.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/6:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/6:30)

And Mosheh said before the Lord, Behold, I am difficult in speaking; how then will Pharoh hearken to me?



## Chapter 7

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:1)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה לְמָא אַנְתְּ מִסְתְּפֵי חָמֵי דִכְבַר שַׁוִית יָתָךְ דְחִילָא לְפַרְעה כְּאִילוּ אֱלָהָא דִילֵיהּ וְאַהֲרן אָחוּךְ יְהֵא נְבִיָא דִילָךְ


But the Lord said to Mosheh, Wherefore art thou fearful? Behold, I have set thee a terror to Pharoh, as if thou wast his God, and Aharon thy brother shall be thy prophet.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:2)


אַנְתְּ תְּמַלֵיל לְאַהֲרן יַת כָּל דְאִיפַקְדִינָךְ וְאַהֲרן אָחוּךְ יְמַלֵיל לְפַרְעה וְיִפְטוֹר יַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מֵאַרְעֵיהּ


Thou shalt speak to Aharon that which I command thee, and Aharon thy brother shall speak to Pharoh, that he release the sons of Israel from his land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:3)


וַאֲנָא אַקְשֵׁי יַת יִצְרָא דְלִבָּא דְפַרְעה וְאַסְגֵי יַת אַתְוָותַי וְיַת תִמְהַיַי בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם


But I will harden the disposition of Pharoh's heart to multiply My signs and My wonders in the land of Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:4)


וְלָא יְקַבֵּל מִנְכוֹן פַּרְעה וְאִיגָרֵי בְּהוֹן גִירִין דִקְטוֹל וְאֶתֵּן מְחַת גְבוּרַת יְדִי בְּמִצְרָיִם וְאַפֵּיק יַת עַמִי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל פְּרִיקִין מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם בְּדִינִין רַבְרְבִין


Nor will Pharoh hearken to you. But I will shoot among them the arrows of death, and inflict the plagues of My mighty hand upon Mizraim, and will bring out the sons of Israel free from among them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:5)


וְיִנְדְעוּן מִצְרָאֵי אֲרוּם אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ כַּד אָרִים יַת מְחַת גְבוּרְתִּי עַל מִצְרָיִם וְאַנְפֵּיק יַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל פְּרִיקִין מִבֵּינֵיהוֹן


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:6)


וַעֲבַד משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן הֵיכְמָא דְפַקֵיד יְיָ יַתְהוֹן הֵיכְדֵין עָבָדוּ


And Mosheh and Aharon did as the Lord commanded them, even so did they.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:7)


וּמשֶׁה בַּר תַּמְנָן שְׁנִין וְאַהֲרן בַּר תַּמְנָן וּתְלַת שְׁנִין בְּמַלָלוּתְהוֹן עִם פַּרְעה


And Mosheh was the son of eighty years, and Aharon the son of eighty and three years, at their speaking with Pharoh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:8)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה וּלְאַהֲרן לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake to Mosheh and to Aharon, saying,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:9)


אֲרוּם יְמַלֵיל עִמְכוֹן פַּרְעה לְמֵימַר הָבוּ לְכוֹן תִּימְהָא וְתֵימַר לְאַהֲרן סַב יַת חוּטְרָךְ וּטְלוֹק יָתֵיהּ קֳדָם פַּרְעה יְהֵי לְחִיוִי חוּרְמַן אֲרוּם עֲתִידִין כָּל דַיְירֵי אַרְעָא לְמִשְׁמַע קַל צְוָוחַתְהוֹן דְמִצְרַיִם בְּתַבְרוּתִי יַתְהוֹן הֵיכְמָא דִשְׁמָעַן כָל בִּרְיָיתָא יַת קַל צְוָוחַת חִוְיָא כַּד אִיתְעַרְטִיל מִן שֵׁירוּיָא


When Pharoh talketh with you, saying, Give us a miracle, thou shalt say to Aharon, Take thy rod, and cast it down before Pharoh, and it shall become a basilisk-serpent; for all the inhabiters of the earth shall hear the voice of the shriek of Mizraim when I shatter them, as all the creatures heard the shriek of the serpent when made naked at the beginning.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:10)


וְאָעֵל משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן לְוַת פַּרְעה וְעָבָדוּ הֵיכְדֵין הֵיכְמָא דְפַקֵיד יְיָ וּטְלַק אַהֲרן יַת חוּטְרֵיהּ קֳדָם מֵיחְמֵי פַּרְעה וְקָדָם מֵיחְמֵי עַבְדוֹי וַהֲוָה לְחוּרְמָנָא


And Mosheh and Aharon went in unto Pharoh, and did as the Lord had commanded. And Aharon threw down the rod before the sight of Pharoh, and before the sight of his servants, and it became a basilisk.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:11)


וּקְרָא לְחוֹד פַּרְעה לְחַכִּימַיָא וּלְחַרְשַׁיָא וַעֲבָדוּ לְחוֹד הִינוּן יֵנִיס וְיִמְבְּרֵיס חַרְשִׁין דִבְמִצְרַיִם בְּלַחֲשֵׁי קוּסְמֵיהוֹן הֵיכְדֵין


But Pharoh called the hachems and magicians; and they also, Janis and Jamberes, magicians of Mizraim, did the same by their burnings of divination.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:12)


וּטְלָקוּן אֵינַשׁ חוּטְרֵיהּ וַהֲווֹ לְחָרְמָנִין וּמִן יַד אִתְהֲפִּיכוּ לְמֶהֱוֵי כְּמִן שֵׁירוּיָא וּבְלַע חוּטְרָא דְאַהֲרן יַת חוּטְרֵיהוֹן


They threw down each man his rod, and they became basilisks; but were forthwith changed to be what they were at first; and the rod of Aharon swallowed up their rods.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:13)


וְאִיתְקַף יִצְרָא דְלִבָּא דְפַרְעה וְלָא קַבֵּיל מִנְהוֹן הֵיכְמָא דְמַלֵל יְיָ


And the disposition Pharoh's heart was hardened, and he would not hearken to them, as the Lord had said.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:14)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אִיתְקַף יִצְרָא דְלִבָּא דְפַרְעה מְסָרֵב לְמִפְטוֹר יַת עַמָא


And the Lord said to Mosheh, The disposition of Pharoh's heart is obdurated in refusing to release the people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 7:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:15)


אִיזֵיל לְוַת פַּרְעה בְּצַפְרָא הָא נָפֵיק לְמִנְטוֹר קוּסְמִין עִילַוֵי מַיָא הֵי כְּאַמְגוֹשָׁא וְתִתְעַתַּד לְקָדָמוּתֵיהּ עַל גֵיף נַהֲרָא וּבְרַם חוּטְרָא דְאַהֲרן דְאִתְהֲפִיךְ לְחִיוֵי תִּיסֵב בִּידָךְ


Go unto Pharoh in the morning: behold, he cometh forth to observe divinations at the water as a magician; so shalt thou prepare thee to meet him on the bank of the river, and Aharon's rod that was changed to be a serpent thou shalt take in thy hand.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 7:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:16)

And thou shalt say to him, The Lord God of the Jehudaee hath sent me unto thee, saying Release My people, that they may serve Me in the desert; and, behold, as yet thou bast not hearkened.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:17)

Thus saith the Lord: By this sign thou shalt know that I am the Lord. Behold, with the rod that is in my hand, I will smite the waters of the river, and they shall be changed into blood.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/7:19)

## Chapter 8

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:1)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אֵימַר לְאַהֲרן אֲרֵים יַת יְדָךְ בְּחוּטְרָא עַל נַהֲרָא עַל פַּצִידַיָיא וְעַל שִׁיקַיָיא וְאַסֵיק יַת עוּרְדְעָנַיָא עַל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם


And the Lord said to Mosheh, Lift up thy hand with thy rod over the rivers, over the trenches, and over the canals, and I will bring up the frogs upon the land of Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:2)


וַאֲרֵים אַהֲרן יַת יְדֵיהּ עַל מַיָא דְמִצְרַיִם וּסְלִיקַת מְחַת עוּרְדְעָנַיָא וַחֲפַת יַת אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם בְּרַם משֶׁה לָא אַלְקֵי יַת מַיָא לָא בְּאַדְמָא וְלָא בְּעוּרְדְעָנַיָא מִן בִּגְלַל דַהֲוַת לֵיהּ בְּהוֹן שֵׁיזְבוּתָא בִּזְמַן דְטָלְקַת יָתֵיהּ אִמֵיהּ בְּנַהֲרָא


And Aharon uplifted his hand over the waters of Mizraim, and the plague of frogs came up and covered the land of Mizraim. But Mosheh (himself) did not smite the waters, either with the blood or with the frogs because through them (the waters of the Nile) he had (found) safety the time that his mother laid him in the river.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:3)


וְעָבָדוּ הֵיכְדֵין אִיסְטַגְנִינָן בְּלַחְשֵׁיהוֹן וְאַסִיקוּ יַת עוּרְדְעָנַיָא עַל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם


And the astrologers did likewise by their burnings, and brought up frogs upon the land of Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:4)


וּקְרָא פַּרְעה לְמשֶׁה וּלְאַהֲרן וַאֲמַר צְלוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ וְיַעֲדֵי עוּרְדְעָנַיָא מִינִי וּמִן עַמָא וְאֶפְטוֹר יַת עַמָא וְיִדְבְּחוּן נִיכְסַת חַגָא קֳדָם יְיָ


And Pharoh called to Mosheh and to Aharon, saying, Pray before the Lord, that He may remove the frogs from me and from my people; and I will release the people to offer the sacrifices of a feast before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:5)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְפַרְעה שַׁבַּח בְּגִינִי לְאֵימַת דְאַנְתְּ בָּעֵי אַיְצַלֵי עֲלָךְ וְעַל עַבְדָךְ וְעַל עַמָךְ לְשֵׁיצָאָה עוּרְדְעָנַיָא מִינָךְ וּמִבָּתָּךְ לְחוֹד מָה דִבְנַהֲרָא יִשְׁתַּיְירוּן


And Mosheh said to Pharoh, Glorify thyself on account of me. At what time dost thou request that I should pray for thee, and for thy servants, and for thy people, that the frogs may be destroyed from thee and from thy house, and be left only in the river?


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:6)


וַאֲמֵר לְמָחָר וַאֲמַר כְּפִתְגָמָךְ מִן בִּגְלַל דְתִנְדַע אֲרוּם לֵית כַּיְיָ אֱלָהָנָא


And he said, Tomorrow. And he said, According to thy word: that thou mayest know that there is none like the Lord our God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:7)


וְיַעֲדוּן עוּרְדְעָנַיָא מִינָךְ וּמִבָּתָּךְ וּמִן עַבְדָךְ וּמִן עַמָךְ לְחוֹד מַה דִבְנַהֲרָא יִשְׁתַּיְירוּן


And the frogs shall depart from thee, and from thy house, and from thy servants, and from thy people; and those only that are in the river shall remain.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:8)

And Mosheh and Aharon went out from Pharoh, and Mosheh prayed before the Lord respecting the frogs, as he had proposed to Pharoh.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:9)


וַעֲבַד יְיָ כְּפִתְגָמָא דְמשֶׁה וּמִיתוּ עוּרְדְעָנַיָא מִן בָּתַּיָא וּמִן דַרְתָּא וּמִן חַקְלָא


And the Lord did according to the word of Mosheh; and the frogs died from the houses and from the courts and from the field,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:10)


וּכְנָשׁוּ יַתְהוֹן כַּרְוַון כַּרְוַון וּסְרִיַית אַרְעָא


and they collected them in heaps and heaps, and the land was corrupted.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:11)


וַחֲמָא פַרְעה אֲרוּם הֲוָה רְוַוחְתָּא לְעַקְתֵיהּ וִיקַר יַת לִבֵּיהּ וְלָא קַבֵּיל מִנְהוֹן הֵיכְמָא דְמַלֵל יְיָ


And Pharoh saw that he was refreshed from his molestation, but hardened his heart, and would not hearken to them, as the Lord had said.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:12)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אֵימַר לְאַהֲרן אֲרֵים יַת חוּטְרָךְ וּמְחֵי יַת עַפְרָא דְאַרְעָא וִיהֵי לְקַלְמֵי בְּכָל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם בְּרַם עַל יְדָךְ לֵית אֶפְשַׁר לְמִלְקֵי אַרְעָא דְבָהּ הֲוָת לָךְ שֵׁיזְבוּתָא כַּד קָטַלְתָּא יַת מִצְרָאֵי וְקַבִּילְתֵּיהּ


And the Lord said to Mosheh, Speak unto Aharon, Lift up thy rod and smite the dust of the earth, and it shall become venomous insects in all the land of Mizraim. But it shall not be by thee that the ground shall be smitten, because therein for thee was (the means of) safety when thou hadst slain the Mizraite and it received him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:13)


וַעֲבָדוּ הֵיכְדֵין וַאֲרֵים אַהֲרן יַת יְדֵיהּ בְּחוּטְרֵיהּ וּמְחָא יַת עַפְרָא דְאַרְעָא וַהֲוַת מְחַת קַלְמֵי בְּבִשְרָא דְאֵינָשָׁא וְדִבְעִירָא כָל עַפְרָא דְאַרְעָא אִיתְהֲפִּיךְ לְמֶהֱוֵי קַלְמֵי בְּכָל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם


And they did so, and Aharon lifted up his hand with his rod, and smote the dust of the ground, and it became a plague of venomous insects upon the flesh of men and of cattle; all the dust of the earth was changed to become insects, in all the land of Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:14)


וַעֲבָדוּ הֵיכְדֵין אִסְטַגְנִינַיָא חַרְשְׁיוּתָא בְּלַחֲשֵׁהוֹן לְאַנְפָּקָא יַת קַלְמִין וְלָא יָכִילוּ וַהֲוַת מְחַת קַלְמֵי שַׁלְטָא בְּאֵינָשָׁא וּבִבְעִירָא


And the astrologers wrought with their burnings to bring forth the insects, but were not able; and the plague of insects prevailed upon men and upon cattle.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:15)


וַאֲמָרוּ אִיסְטַגְנִינֵי פַּרְעה לָא מִן כּחַ גְבוּרַת משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן הִיא אֱלָהֵן מְחָא מִשְׁתַּלְחָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ הִיא וְאִתְקַף יִצְרָא דְלִבָּא דְפַרְעה וְלָא קַבֵּיל מִנְהוֹן הֵיכְמָא דְמַלֵיל יְיָ


And the astrologers said to Pharoh, This is not by the power or strength of Mosheh and Aharon; but this is a plague sent from before the Lord. Yet the design of Pharoh's heart was strengthened, and he would not hearken to them, as the Lord had said.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:16)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אַקְדֵם בְּצַפְרָא וְתִתְעַתַּד קֳדָם פַּרְעה הָא נָפִיק לְמִנְטַר קוּסְמִין עִילוֹי מַיָא הֵי כְּאַמְגוּשָׁא וְתֵימַר לֵיהּ כִּדְנָא אָמַר יְיָ פְּטוֹר יַת עַמִי וְיִפְלְחוּן קֳדָמַי


And the Lord spake to Mosheh, Arise in the morning, and stand before Pharoh: behold, be goeth forth to observe divinations at the water, as a magician; and thou shalt say to him, Thus saith the Lord, Emancipate My people, that they may worship before Me;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:17)


אֲרוּם אִין לֵיתָךְ מִפְטַר יַת עַמִי הָאֲנָא מְגָרֵי בָךְ וּבְעַבְדָךְ וּבְעַמָךְ וּבְבֵיתָךְ יַת עִירְבּוּב חֵיוַת בָּרָא וְיִתְמְלוּן בָּתֵּי מִצְרָאֵי עִירְבּוּב חֵיוַת בָּרָא וְאוּף יַת אַרְעָא דְהִינוּן עֲלָהּ


but if thou wilt not set My people free, behold, I will stir up among thee, and thy servants, and thy people, and thy house, a mixed multitude of wild beasts and the houses of the Mizraee shall be filled with a swarm of wild beasts, and they shall be upon the land also.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:18)


וְאֶעֱבֵיד פִּלְאִין בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא עִם אַרְעָא דְגשֶׁן דְעַמִי שְׁרֵי עֲלָהּ בְּדִיל דְלָא לְמֶהֱוֵי תַמָן עִירְבּוּב חֵיוַת בָּרָא מִן בִּגְלַל דְתִנְדַע אַרוּם אֲנָא יְיָ שַׁלִיט בְּגוֹ אַרְעָא


And I will do wonders that day in the land of Goshen where My people dwell, that there no swarms of wild beasts shall be; - that thou mayest know that I the Lord am the Ruler in the midst of the land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:19)


וַאֲשַׁוֵי פּוּרְקַן לְעַמִי וְעַל עַמָךְ אַיְיתֵי מָחָא לְעִידַן מָחָר יְהֵי אָתָא הָדֵין


And I will appoint redemption for My people, and upon thy people will I lay the plague: tomorrow this sign shall be.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:20)


וְעָבַד יְיָ כֵּן וְאַיְיתֵי עִירְבּוּב חֵיוַת בָּרָא תַקִיף לְבֵית פַּרְעה וּלְבֵית עַבְדוֹי וּבְכָל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם אִיתְחַבִּילוּ יַתְבֵי אַרְעָא מִן קֳדָם עִירְבּוּב חֵיוַת בָּרָא


And the Lord did so; and sent the mixed multitude of wild beasts in strength to the house of Pharoh, and to the house of his servants and in all the land of Mizraim the inhabitants of the land were devastated from the swarm of wild beasts.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:21)


וּקְרָא פַרְעה לְמשֶׁה וּלְאַהֲרן וַאֲמַר אִיזִילוּ פְּלָחוּ נִכְסַת חַגָא קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן בְּאַרְעָא הָדָא


And Pharoh called to Mosheh and to Aharon., saying, Go, worship with festival sacrifices before the Lord your God in this land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:22)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לָא תַקִין לְמֶעֱבַד כֵּן אֲרוּם אִמְרַיָא דְהִינוּן טַעֲוַותְהוֹן דְמִצְרָאֵי מִנְהוֹן נִיסַב וְנִקְרְבָא קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהֵן הָא אִין מְקַרְבִין אֲנָן יַת טַעֲוַותְהוֹן דְמִצְרָאֵי קֳדָמֵיהוֹן הוּא מִן דִינָא הוּא לְאַטָלָא יָתָן בְּאַבְנִין


But Mosheh said, It will not be right to do so; because we shall take sheep, which are the abomination of the Mizraee, and offer them before the Lord our God. Behold if we offer the abomination of the Mizraee before them, they would stone us with stones as an act of justice.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:23)


מַהֲלַךְ תְּלָתָא יוֹמִין נְטַיֵיל בְּמַדְבְּרָא וּנְדַבַּח נִיכְסַת חַגָא קֳדָם אֱלָהָנָא הֵיכְמָא דְיֵימַר לָנָא


We will go three days journey into the wilderness to offer the festival sacrifices before our God, as He hath bidden us.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:24)


וַאֲמַר פַּרְעה אֲנָא אֶפְטוֹר יַתְכוֹן וְתִדְבְּחוּן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן בְּמַדְבְּרָא לְחוֹד אַרְחָקָא לָא תַרְחִיקוּן לְטַיְילָא צְלוֹ עָלָי


And Pharoh said, I will release you to sacrifice before the Lord your God in the wilderness, only you shall not go to a greater distance. Pray, (too,) for me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:25)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה הָא אֲנָא נָפִיק מִלְוָתָךְ וְאֵיצַלֵי קֳדָם יְיָ וְיַעְדֵי עִרְבּוּב חֵיוַת בָּרָא מִן פַּרְעה וּמִן עַמֵיהּ לִמְחַר לְחוֹד לָא יוֹסֵף פַּרְעה לִמְשַׁקְרָא בְּדִיל דְלָא לְמִפְטוֹר יַת עַמָא לִמְדַבְּחָא נִכְסַת חַגָא קֳדָם יְיָ


And Mosheh said, I will go forth from thee, and pray before the Lord to remove the swarm of wild beasts from Pharoh, and from his servants, and from his people, tomorrow; only let not Pharoh add to deceive, in not releasing the people to offer the festal sacrifices before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:26)


וּנְפַק משֶׁה מִלְוַת פַּרְעה וְצַלִי קֳדָם יְיָ


And Mosheh went out from Pharoh, and prayed before the Lord;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:27)


וַעֲבַד יְיָ כְּפִתְגַם בְּעוּתָא דְמשֶׁה וְאַעֲדֵי עִירְבּוּב חֵיוַת בָּרָא מִפַּרְעה וּמֵעַבְדוֹי וּמֵעַמֵיהּ לָא אִשְׁתְּאַר חָד


and the Lord did according to the word of the prayer of Mosheh, and removed the swarm of wild beasts from Pharoh, and from his servants, and from his people; not one was left.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 8:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/8:28)


וִיקַר פַּרְעה יַת יִצְרָא דְלִבֵּיהּ אוּף בְּזִמְנָא הָדָא וְלָא פְּטַר יַת עַמָא


Yet did Pharoh strengthen the design of his heart this time also, and released not the people.



## Chapter 9

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:1)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה עוּל לְוַת פַּרְעה וּתְמַלֵיל עִמֵיהּ כִּדְנַן אָמַר יְיָ אֱלָהָא דִיהוּדָאֵי פְּטוֹר יַת עַמִי וְיִפְלְחוּן קֳדָמַי


And the Lord said to Mosheh, Go in to Pharoh, and say to him, Thus saith the Lord, the God of the Jehudaee, Emancipate My people, that they may worship before Me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:2)


אֲרוּם אִין מְסָרֵב אַנְתְּ לְמִפְטוֹר וְעַד כְּדוֹן אַנְתְּ מַתְקִיף בְּהוֹן


But if thou refuse to release, and hitherto thou hast constrained them,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:3)
