הָא מְחַת יְדָא דַיְיָ הַוְיָא כְּעַן כַּד לָא הֲוַת לְמֶהֱוֵי בִּבְעִירָךְ דִבְחַקְלָא בְּסוּסְוָותָא בְּחַמְרֵי בִּגְמַלֵי בְּתוֹרֵי וּבְעָנָא מוֹתָא תַקִיף לַחֲדָא


behold, the stroke of the Lord's hand shall be as it hath not been yet, upon thy cattle that are in the field, upon the horses, and upon the asses, upon the camels, oxen, and sheep, with a very mighty death.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:4)


וְיַעֲבֵד יְיָ פִּלְאִין בֵּין גֵיתֵי דְיִשְרָאֵל וּבֵין גֵיתֵי דְמִצְרָאֵי וְלָא יְמוּת מִכָּל לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מִדָעַם


And the Lord will work wonders between the flocks of Israel and the flocks of the Mizraee, that not any of those which belong to the sons of Israel shall die.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:5)


וּקְבַע יְיָ זִמְנָא לְמֵימָר מְחָר יַעֲבֵיד יְיָ יַת פִּתְגָמָא הָדֵין בְּאַרְעָא


And the Lord set a time, saying, Tomorrow will the Lord do this thing in the land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:6)


וַעֲבַד יְיָ יַת פִּתְגָמָא הָדֵין לְיוֹם חֲרָן וּמִית כָּל בְּעִירָא דְמִצְרָאֵי וּמִבְּעִירָא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לָא מִית חָד


And the Lord did that thing the day after, and all the cattle of the Mizraee died; but of the cattle of the sons of Israel died not one.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:7)


וְשָׁדַר פַּרְעה פּוּלִין לְמֶחֱמֵי וְהָא לָא מִית מִבְּעִירָא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל עַד חָד וְאִתְיַיקַר יִצְרָא דְלִבָּא דְפַרְעה וְלָא פְטַר יַת עַמָא


And Pharoh sent certain to look; and, behold, not one of the cattle of the sons of Israel had died, not even one. But the disposition of Pharoh, heart was aggravated, and he would not release the people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:8)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה וּלְאַהֲרן סִיבוּ לְכוֹן מָלֵי חוּפְנֵיכוֹן קְטַם דְקִיק מִן אַתּוּנָא וְיִדְרְקִינֵיהּ משֶׁה לְצֵית שְׁמַיָא לְמֶיחֱמֵי פַרְעה


And the Lord said to Mosheh and to Aharon, Take with you hands-full of fine ashes from the furnace, and let Mosheh sprinkle them towards the height of the heavens in the sight of Pharoh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:9)


וִיהֵי לְאַבְקָא עַל כָּל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם וִיהֵי עַל אֱנָשָׁא וְעַל בְּעִירָא לִשְׁחִין סַגִי שַׁלְפוּקִין בְּכָל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם


And the dust shall be upon all the land of Mizraim, upon man and upon beast, for a boil, producing tumours in all the land of Mizraim


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:10)


וּנְסִיבוּ יַת קִיטְמָא דְאַתּוּנָא וְקָמוּ לִקְדָם פַּרְעה וּדְרַק יָתֵיהּ משֶׁה לְצֵית שְׁמַיָא וַהֲוָה שְׁחִין שַׁלְפוּקִין סַגִי בְּאֵינָשָׁא וּבִבְעִירָא


And they took ashes of the furnace, and arose to meet Pharoh; and Mosheh sprinkled them towards the height of the heavens; and there came a boil multiplying tumours upon man and beast.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:11)


וְלָא יָכִילוּ אִיסְטַגְנִינַיָא לְמֵיקַם קֳדָם משֶׁה מִן קֳדָם שִׁיחְנָא אֲרוּם הֲוָה מְחַת שִׁיחְנָא בְּאִיסְטַגְנִינַיָא וּבְכָל מִצְרָאֵי


And the astrologers could not stand before Mosheh, on account of the boil; for the plague of the boil was upon the astrologers, and upon all the Mizraee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:12)


וּתְקֵיף יְיָ יַת יִצְרָא דְלִבָּא דְפַרְעה וְלָא קַבֵּיל מִנְהוֹן הֵיכְמָא דְמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה


And the Lord hardened the design of Pharoh's heart, and he would not hearken to them, as the Lord had said to Mosheh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:13)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אַקְדֵים בְּצַפְרָא וְתִתְעַתַּד קֳדָם פַּרְעה וְתֵימַר לֵיהּ כִּדְנָא אָמַר יְיָ אֱלָהָא דִיהוּדָאֵי פְּטוֹר יַת עַמִי וְיִפְלְחוּן קֳדָמַי


And the Lord said to Mosheh, Arise in the morning, and place thyself before Pharoh, and say to him, Thus saith the Lord, the God of the Jehudaee, Emancipate My people, that they may worship before Me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:14)


אַרוּם בְּזִימְנָא הָדָא אֲנָא שַׁלַח מַחְתָּא לָךְ מִן שְׁמַיָא וְתָתֵיב יַת כָּל מַחֲתַי דִמְחִיתָךְ לְלִבָּךְ וּבְעַבְדָךְ וּבְעַמָךְ דְמִן קֳדָמַי הַוָון מִשְׁתַּלְחוּן וְלָא מִן חַרְשְׁיוּתָא דִבְנֵי נְשָׁא בְּגִין דְתִנְדַע אֲרוּם לֵית דְדָמַי לִי בְכָל אַרְעָא


For at this time I will send upon thee a plague from the heavens, and all My plagues Wherewith I have plagued thee thou wilt cause to return upon thy heart, and upon thy servants, and upon thy people, (plagues) which have been sent from before Me, and not from the magic of the sons of men, that thou mayest know that there is none like Me in all the earth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:15)


אֲרוּם כְּדוֹן שַׁדָרִית יַת מְחַת גְבוּרְתִּי מִן דִינָא הוּא דְמָחִיתִי יָתָךְ וְיַת עַמָךְ בְּמוֹתָא וְאִישְׁתֵּצִית מִן אַרְעָא


Now could I send the plague of My strength by judgment (or, with justice) to strike thee and thy people with death, and destroy thee from the earth;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:16)


וּבְרַם בְּקוּשְׁטָא לָא מִן בִּגְלַל דִי נְטַיְיבָא לָךְ קְיַימְתִּיךְ אֶלָא מִן בִּגְלַל לִמְחַזְיָךְ יַת חֵילִי וּמִן בִּגְלַל דְתִתְגֵי שְׁמִי קַדִישָׁא בְּכָל אַרְעָא


but verily I have spared thee alive, not that I may benefit thee, but that My power may be made manifest to thee, and that My Holy Name may be made known in all the earth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:17)


עַד כְּדוֹן אַנְתְּ מִתְרַבְרַב בְּעַמִי בְּדִיל דְלָא לְמִפְטְרִינוּן


Hitherto hast thou tyrannized over My people, instead of releasing them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:18)


הָאֲנָא מָחִית בְּעִידַן הָדֵין לְמָחָר מִן אוֹצְרֵי שְׁמַיָא בַּרְדָא תַּקִיף לַחֲדָא דְלָא הֲוֵי דִכְוָותָא בְּמִצְרַיִם מִן יוֹמָא דְאִשְתַּכְלָלוּ אַנְשָׁהָא וְעַד כְּדוֹן


Behold, at this time tomorrow I will cause to come down from the treasures of the heavens a mighty hall, the like of which hath never been in Mizraim since the day when men were settled upon it until now.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:19)


וּכְדוּן שְׁדַר כְּנוֹשׁ יַת גֵיתָךְ וְיַת כָּל דִילָךְ בְּחַקְלָא כָּל אֵינָשָׁא וּבְעִירָא דְאִשְׁתַּכַּח בְּחַקְלָא וְלָא יִתְכְּנוֹשׁ לְבֵיתָא וְיֵיחוֹת עִילַוֵהוֹן בַּרְדָא וִימוּתוּן


But now send, gather together thy flocks, and all that thou hast in the field (for) upon all men and cattle that are found in the field, and not gathered together within the house, will the hail come down, and they will die.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:20)


אִיוֹב דַהֲוָה דָחִיל מִפִּתְגָמָא דַיְיָ מֵעַבְדוֹי דְפַרְעה כְּנַש יַת עַבְדוֹי וְיַת גֵיתוֹי לְגוֹ בֵיתָא


Hiob, (Job,) who reverenced the word of the Lord, among the servants of Pharoh, gathered together his servants and his flocks within the house.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:21)


וּבִלְעָם דְלָא שַׁוֵיהּ לִבֵּיהּ לְפִתְגָמָא דַיְיָ וּשְׁבַק יַת עַבְדוֹי וְיַת גֵיתוֹי בְּחַקְלָא


But Bileam, who did not set his heart upon the word of the Lord, left his servants and his flocks in the field.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:22)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אֲרֵים יַת יְדָךְ עַל צֵית שְׁמַיָא וִיהִי בַרְדָא בְּכָל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם עַל אֱנָשָׁא וְעַל בְּעִירָא וְעַל כָּל עִיסְבָּא דְחַקְלָא בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם


And the Lord said to Mosheh, Uplift thy hand towards the height of the heavens, and there shall be hail on all the land of Mizraim, upon men, and upon beasts, and upon every herb of the field in the land of Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:23)


וַאֲרֵים משֶׁה יַת חוּטְרֵיהּ עַל צֵית שְׁמַיָא וַיְיָ יְהַב קָלִין וּבַרְדִין וּמְצַלְהֲבָא אֵישָׁתָא עַל אַרְעָא וְאָחִית יְיָ בַּרְדָא עַל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם


And Mosheh lifted up his rod toward the height of the heavens, and the Lord gave forth thunders and hailstones with flaming, fire upon the ground; the Lord made the hail descend upon the land of Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:24)


וַהֲוָה בַרְדָא וְאֵישָׁתָא מִתְקַפְּצָא בְּגוֹ בַרְדָא תַּקִיף לַחֲדָא דְלָא הֲוָה דִכְוָותֵיהּ בְּכָל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם מִן עִידָן דְהֲוַת לְאוּמָא וּמַלְכוּ


And there was hail, and fire darting among the hail with exceeding force: unto it had never been the like in all the land of Mizraim ever since it was a nation and a kingdom.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:25)


וּמְחָא בַרְדָא בְּכָל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם יַת כָּל דִבְחַקְלָא מֵאֵינָשָׁא וְעַד בְּעִירָא וְיַת כָּל עִסְבָּא דְחַקְלָא מְחָא בַרְדָא וְיַת כָּל אִילָנָא דְחַקְלָא תְּבִיר וְשַׁרִישׁ


And the hail smote in all the land of Mizraim whatsoever was in the field, of men and of cattle, and all the herbage of the field the hail smote, and every tree of the field it shattered and uprooted.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:26)


לְחוֹד בְּאַרְעָא דְגשֶׁן דְתַמָן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לָא הֲוָה בַרְדָא


Only in the land of Goshen, where the children of Israel were, there was no hail.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:27)


וְשַׁדַר פַּרְעה פּוּלִין לְמִקְרֵי לְמשֶׁה וּלְאַהֲרן וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן חָבִית בְּזִמְנָא הָדָא יְדָעִית דַיְיָ הוּא אֱלָהָא זַכָּאָה וּבְרַם אֲנָא וְעַמִי חַיָיבִין בְּכָל מַחְתָא וּמַחְתָא


And Pharoh sent certain to call Mosheh and Aharon; and he said to them, This time I have sinned. I know that the Lord is a righteous God, and that I and my people have deserved every one of these plagues.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:28)


צְלוֹי קֳדָם יְיָ וִיהִי סַגִי קָדָמוֹי מִלְמֶהֱוֵי קָלִין דִלְוָוט מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וּבַרְדָן וְאֶפְטוֹר יַתְכוֹן וְלָא תוֹסְפוּן לְאִתְעַכָּבָא


Intercede before the Lord, that with Him it may be enough, and there may be no more maledictory thunders nor hail from the presence of the Lord; and I will release you, and no longer hinder.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:29)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ משֶׁה כְּמִפְקִי סְמִיךְ לְקַרְתָּא אֶפְרוֹשׁ יַת יְדַי בִּצְלוֹי קֳדָם יְיָ קָלַיָיא יִתְמַנְעוּן וּבַרְדָא לָא יְהִי תוּב מִן בִּגְלַל דְתִנְדַע אֲרוּם דַיְיָ הִיא אַרְעָא


And Mosheh said to him, When I have gone out from thee into the city, I will outspread my hands in prayer before the Lord, and the thunders shall cease, and there shall be no more hail; that you may know that the earth is the Lord's.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:30)


וְאַנְתְּ וְעַבְדָךְ חַכִּימִית אֲרוּם עַד לָא תִפְטְרוּן עַמָא תְּהוֹן דַחֲלִין מִן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלהִים


But I know that thou and thy servants release the people, they will have to be afraid before the Lord God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:31)


וְכִתְּנָא וְסַרְתָּא לָקוּן אֲרוּם סַרְתָּא הֲוָות כְּסִירָא וְכִיתְנָא עָבַד פּוּקְלִין


And the flax and the barley were beaten down, because the barley was in the ear, and the flax was making pods.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:32)


וְחִיטַיָא וְכוּנְתַּיָא לָא לָקוּן אֲרוּם לְקִישָׁן הִינוּן


But the wheat and the spelt were not smitten, because they are later.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:33)


וּנְפַק משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן מִלְוַת פַּרְעה סְמִיךְ לְקַרְתָּא וּפְרַס יְדוֹי בִּצְלוֹי קֳדָם יְיָ וְאִתְמְנָעוּ קָלִין דִלְוָוט וּבַרְדָא וּמִטְרָא דַהֲוָה נָחִית לָא מָטָא עַל אַרְעָא


And Mosheh and Aharon went out from Pharoh to the suburb, and he stretched out his hands in prayer before the Lord, and the thunders of the curse were withheld, and the hail and rain that were descending came not on the earth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:34)


וַחֲמָא פַּרְעה אֲרוּם אִתְמְנַע מִטְרָא וּבַרְדָא וְקָלַיָיא דִלְוָוט פָּסְקוּ וְאוֹסִיף לְמֶחֱטֵי וִיקָרֵיהּ לְיִצְרָא דְלִבֵּיהּ הוּא וְעַבְדוֹי


And Pharoh saw that the rain and hail and the thunders of the curse had ended, and he added to sin, and made strong the design of his heart, both he and his servants.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 9:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/9:35)


וְאִיתְקַף יִצְרָא דְלִבָּא דְפַרְעה וְלָא פְטַר יַת בְּנֵי יִשְׁרָאֵל הֵיכְמָא דְמַלֵיל יְיָ בִּידָא דְמשֶׁה


And Pharoh's heart was made obstinate, and he would not release the children of Israel, as the Lord had said through Mosheh.



## Chapter 10

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:1)


ויאמר וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה עוּל לְוַת פַּרְעה אֲרוּם אֲנָא יַקִּירִית יִצְרָא דְלִבֵּיהּ וְיִצְרָא דְלִבְּהוֹן דְעַבְדוֹי מִן בִּגְלַל לְשַׁוָואָה אַתְוָותַי אִלֵין בֵּינֵיהוֹן


And the Lord spake to Mosheh, Go in unto Pharoh; for I have made strong the design of his heart, and the design of the heart of his servants, to set these My signs among them;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:2)


וּמִן בִּגְלַל דְתַתְנֵי בְּמִשְׁמְעֵי בְּרָךְ וּבַר בְּרָךְ יַת נִיסִין דַעֲבָדִית בְּמִצְרַיִם וְיַת אַתְוָותַי דְשַׁוֵּיתִי בְּהוֹן וְתִנְדְּעוּן אֲרוּם אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ


and that in the hearing of thy sons and of thy children's children may be told the wonders I have done in Mizraim, and the signs that I set among them, that ye may know that I am the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:3)


וְאָעַל משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן לְוַת פַּרְעה וַאֲמָרוּ לֵיהּ כִּדְנָא אָמַר יְיָ אֱלָהָא דְיִשְרָאֵל עַד אֵימַת מְסָרֵב אַנְתְּ (מִן) לְמִתְכְּנָעָא מִן קֳדָמַי פְּטוֹר יַת עַמִּי וְיִפְלְחוּן קֳדָמַי


And Mosheh and Aharon went in unto Pharoh, and said to him, Thus saith the Lord, the God of Israel, How long wilt thou refuse to humble thyself before Me? Let My people go, that they may worship before Me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:4)


אֲרוּם אִין יֵשׁ מְסָרֵב אַנְתְּ לְמִפְטוֹר יַת עַמִּי הָא אֲנָא מַיְיתֵי מְחַר גּוֹבָא בִּתְחוּמָךְ


But if thou refuse to let My people go, behold, tomorrow I bring the locust upon thy borders,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:5)


וְיַחֲפֵי יַת חֶזְוָונָא דְאַרְעָא וְלָא יֶהֱוֵי יָכִיל לְמֶחֲמֵי יַת אַרְעָא וִישֵׁיצֵי יַת שְׁאָר שֵׁיזְבוּתָא דְּאִישְׁתָּאֲרַת לְכוֹן מִן בַּרְדָא וִישֵׁיצֵי יַת כָּל אִילָנָא דְיִצְמַח לְכוֹן מִן חַקְלָא


and they shall cover the face of the ground, so that it will be impossible to see the ground, and shall destroy the remainder that was spared to you from the hail, and destroy every tree which groweth for you out of the field.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:6)


וְיִתְמְלוּן בָּתָּךְ וּבָתֵּי כָּל עַבְדָךְ וּבָתֵּי כָל מִצְרָאֵי דְלָא חָמוּן אַבְהָתָךְ וְאַבְהַת אַבְהָתָךְ מִן יוֹם מֶהֱוֵיהוֹן עַל אַרְעָא עַד יוֹמָא הָדֵין וְאִתְפְּנֵי וּנְפַק מִלְוַת פַּרְעה


And they shall fill thy house, and the houses of all thy servants, and the houses of the Mizraee, (the like of) which neither thy fathers nor thy forefathers have seen since the day that they were upon the earth unto this day. And he turned and went out from Pharoh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:7)


וְאָמָרוּ עַבְדֵי פַּרְעה עַד אֵימַת יְהֵי דֵין גַּבְרָא לָנָא לְתַקְלָא פְּטוֹר יַת גַּבְרַיָא וְיִפְלְחוּן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָהוֹן הַעַד כְּדוֹן לָא חַכִּימְתָּ אֲרוּם עַל יְדוֹי עֲתִידָא לְמוֹבְדָא אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם


And the servants of Pharoh said, How long shall this man be a stumbling-block to us? Let the men be released, that they may worship before the Lord their God. Art thou not aware that by His hand it will be that the land of Mizraim shall be destroyed?


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:8)


וּפַקֵּיד לְאִיתּוֹתָבָא יַת משֶׁה וְיַת אַהֲרן לְוַת פַּרְעה וַאֲמַר לְהוֹם אִיזִילוּ פְּלָחוּ קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן מָן וּמָן הִינוּן דְּאָזְלִין


And he commanded to bring back Mosheh and Aharon to Pharoh, and said to them, Go, worship before the Lord your God: but who are they that are to go?


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:9)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה בְּטַלְיָינָא וּבְסָבָנָא נֵיזִיל בִּבְנָנָא וּבִבְרַתָּנָא בְּעַנָנָא וּבְתוֹרָנָא נֵיזִיל אֲרוּם חַגָא קֳדָם יְיָ לָנָא


And Mosheh said, With our children and with our old men will we go; with our sons and with our daughters we will go; with our sheep and with our oxen we will go; for we have a solemn feast before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:10)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן יְהִי כְדֵין מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ בְּסַעַדְכוֹן כְּמָא דְאֶפְטוֹר יַתְכוֹן וְיַת טַפְלְכוֹן חָמוּן אֲרוּם לְתַקָּלָא בִּישָׁא הִיא לְכוֹן לִקְבֵיל אַפֵּיכוֹן בְּאוֹרְחֲכוֹן דִּתְהַלְכוֹן עַד זְמַן דִּי תִמְטוּן לְבֵית אֲתַר מַשְׁרוּיֵיכוֹן


And he said to them, So may the Word of the Lord be a help to you: (but) how can I release (both) you and your children? The evil offence is in the look of your faces: (you think to go onward) in the way that you would walk, till the time that you shall have come to the house of the place of your habitation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:11)


לָא כְמָא דְאַתּוּן סַבְרִין אֱלָהֵן אִיזִילוּ כְּדוֹן גּוּבְרַיָא וּפָלְחוּ קֳדָם יְיָ אֲרוּם יָתֵיהּ אַתּוּן בָּעָן וְתָרִיךְ יַתְהוֹן מִלְוַת אַפֵּי פַּרְעה


(It shall be) not so as ye devise; but the men only shall go and worship before the Lord; for that it was which ye demanded. And he drave them out from before the face of Pharoh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:12)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אֲרֵים יַת יְדָךְ עַל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם בְּדִיל גּוֹבָא וְיַיסֵק עַל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם וִישֵׁיצֵי יַת כָּל עִיסְבָּא דְאַרְעָא יַת כָּל מַה דִּי שַׁיֵּיר בַּרְדָא


And the Lord spake to Mosheh, Lift up thy hand over the land of Mizraim for the locust, that he may come up over the land of Mizraim, and destroy every herb of the earth, whatsoever the hail hath left.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:13)


וַאֲרֵים משֶׁה יַת חוּטְרֵיהּ עַל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם וַיְיָ דְבַר רוּחַ קִידוּמָא בְּאַרְעָא כָּל יוֹמָא הַהוּא וְכָל לֵילְיָא צַפְרָא הֲוָה וְרוּחַ קִידוּמָא נָטַל יַת גּוֹבָא


And Mosheh lifted up his rod over the land of Mizraim, and the Lord brought an east wind upon the country all that day and all the night; and in the morning the east wind bare the locust.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:14)


וּסְלֵיק גּוֹבָא עַל כָּל אַרְעָא דְּמִצְרָיִם וּשְׁרָא בְּכָל תְּחוּם מִצְרָיִם תַּקִּיף לַחֲדָא קֳדָמוֹי לָא הֲוָה כְדֵין קַשְׁיָין גּוֹבָא דִכְוָותֵיהּ וּבַתְרוֹי לָא עָתִיד דִּיהִי כֵן


And the locust came up over all the land of Mizraim, and settled in all the limits of Mizraim exceedingly strong. Before him there had been no locust so hard, nor will there be like him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:15)


וַחֲפָא יַת חֶזְוָונָא דְכָל אַרְעָא עַד דִּי חֲשׁוֹכַת אַרְעָא וִישֵׁיצֵי יַת כָּל עִיסְבָּא דְאַרְעָא וְיַת כָּל פֵּירֵי אִילָנָא דִי שַׁיֵּיר בַּרְדָא וְלָא אִשְׁתַּיֵּיר כָּל יְרוֹק בְּאִילָנָא וּבְעִיסְבָּא דְחַקְלָא בְּכָל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם


And he covered the face of all the land, until the land was darkened, and every herb of the ground was consumed, and all the fruit of the tree that the hail had left; and nothing green of tree or herb of the field was left in all the land of Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:16)


וְאוֹחֵי פַּרְעה וּשְׁדַר פּוּלִין לְמִקְרֵי לְמשֶׁה וּלְאַהֲרן וַאֲמַר חָבִית קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן וּלְכוֹן


And Pharoh made haste, and sent certain to call Mosheh and Aharon. And he said, I have sinned before the Lord your God and against you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:17)


וּכְדוּן שְׁבוֹק כְּדוּן חוֹבִי לְחוֹד זִמְנָא הָדָא וּצְלוּ קֳדָם יְיָ וְיַעֲדֵי מִינִי לְחוֹד יַת מוֹתָא הָדֵין


But now, pardon my sin only this once, and pray before the Lord, that He would only remove from me this death.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:18)


וּנְפַק מִלְוַת פַּרְעה וְצַלִּי קֳדָם יְיָ


And he went out from Pharoh, and prayed before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:19)


וַהֲפַךְ יְיָ יַת רוּחָא מִמְעַרְבָא תַּקִּיף לַחֲדָא וּנְטַל יַת גּוֹבָא וְטַלְקֵיהּ לְיַמָּא דְסוּף לָא אִשְׁתְּיַיר גּוֹבָא חָד בְּכָל תְּחוּם מִצְרַיִם וַאֲפִילּוּ מַה דְּמַלְחוּן בְּמָנַיָיא לִצְרוֹךְ מֵיכַלְהוֹן נָשָא הִינוּן רוּחַ מַעֲרְבָא וַאֲזָלוּ


And the Lord turned a wind from the west of exceeding strength, and it carried away the locust, and bare him to the sea of Suph: there was not one locust left in all the borders of Mizraim. And even such as had been salted in vessels for needed food, those, too, the western wind bare away, and they went.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:20)


וּתְקֵיף יְיָ יַת יִצְרָא דְלִיבָּא דְפַרְעה וְלָא פְּטַר יַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


But the Lord strengthened the design of Pharoh's heart, and he would not release the children of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:21)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אֲרֵים יַת יְדָךְ עַל צֵית שְׁמַיָא וִיהִי חֲשׁוֹכָא עַל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם בִּקְרִיצְתָּא וְיַעֲדֵי בְּקַדְמֵתָא חֲשׁוֹךְ לֵילְיָא


And the Lord said to Mosheh, Lift up thy hand towards the height of the heavens, and there shall be darkness over all the land of Mizraim, in the morning, at the passing away of the first darkness of the night.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:22)


וַאֲרֵים משֶׁה יַת יְדֵיהּ עַל צֵית שְׁמַיָא וַהֲוָה חֲשׁוֹךְ דִּקְבֵיל בְּכָל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם תְּלָתָא יוֹמִין


And Mosheh stretched out his hand towards the height of the heavens, and there was dark darkness in all the land of Mizraim three days.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:23)


לָא חָמוּן גְּבַר יַת אָחוֹי וְלָא קָמוּן אֱנַשׁ מֵאַתְרֵיהּ תְּלָתָא יוֹמִין וּלְכָל בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל הֲוָה נְהוֹרָא לְמִקְבּוֹר רַשִׁיעַיָא דִי בֵינֵיהוֹן דְּמִיתַן וְזַכָּאַיָא לְמֶעֱסוֹק בְּמִצְוָתָא בְּמוֹתְבָנֵיהוֹן


No man saw his brother, and none arose from his place three days. But among all the sons of Israel there was light, that the wicked among them who died might be buried, and that the righteous might be occupied with the precepts of the law in their dwellings.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:24)


וּבְסוֹף תְּלָתָא יוֹמִין קָרָא פַּרְעה לְמשֶׁה וַאֲמַר זִילוּ פְלָחוּ קֳדָם יְיָ לְחוֹד עַנְכוֹן וְתוֹרֵיכוֹן יְקוּם גַּבִּי אוּף טַפְלְכוֹן יֵזִיל עִמְכוֹן


And at the end of three days Pharoh called Mosheh, and said, Go, worship before the lord; only your sheep and your oxen shall abide with me: your children also may go with you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:25)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה אוּף אַנְתְּ תִּתֵּן בִּידָנָא נִכְסַת קוּדְשִׁין וְעַלְוָון וְנַעֲבֵיד קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא


But Mosheh said, Thou must also give into our hands holy oblations and burnt offerings, that we may perform service before the Lord our God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:26)


וְאוּף גֵּיתָנָא יֵזִיל עִמָּנָא לָא תִשְׁתַּיֵּיר מִנְהוֹן פַּרְסָתָא חָדָא אֲרוּם מִנְהוֹן נִסַּב לְמִפְלַח קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָן וַאֲנַחְנָא אִין נַשְׁבִּיקִינוּן לֵית אֲנַן יַדְעִין מִמָא נִפְלַח קֳדָם יְיָ עַד מֵיתָנָא לְתַמָּן


Our flocks, moreover, must go with us; not one hoof of them shall remain; for from them we are to take, to do service before the Lord our God. We cannot leave them; for we know not (as yet) in what manner we are to worship before the Lord, until we come thither.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:27)


וּתְקֵיף יְיָ יַת יִצְרָא דְלִבָּא דְפַרְעה וְלָא צְבָא לְמִפְטְרִינוּן


But the Lord made strong the design of Pharoh's heart, and he would not release them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:28)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ פַּרְעה אִיזֵל מֵעִילַוָוי אִיזְדַהַר לָךְ לָא תוֹסִיף לְמֶחֱמֵי סְבַר אַפַּי לְמַלָלָא קֳדָמַי חֲדָא מִן מִלַיָא קַשְׁיָיתָא כְּאִלֵּין אֲרוּם בְּיוֹמָא דְאַתְּ חָמֵי סְבַר אַפַּי יִתְקוֹף רוּגְזִי בָךְ וְאֶמְסוֹר יָתָךְ בְּיַד בְּנֵי נְשָׁא אִילֵין דַּהֲווֹ תַּבְעִין נַפְשָׁךְ לְמִיסַב יָתָהּ


And Pharoh said to him, Go from me. Beware that thou add not to see my face to speak before me one of these words that are so hard: for in the day that thou seest my face, my anger will grow strong against thee, and I will deliver thee into the hands of the men who seek thy life to take it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 10:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/10:29)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה יָאוּת מַלֵּילְתָּא אֲנָא עַד דַּהֲוֵינָא יָתִיב בְּמִדְיָן אִיתְאֲמַר לִי בְּמֵימַר מִן קֳדָם יְיָ דְגוּבְרַיָא דְבָעוּ לְמִקְטְלִי נַחֲתוּ מִנִּכְסֵיהוֹן וְהִינוּן חֲשִׁיבִין כְּמִיתַיָא וְלָא מִן סוּף דַּהֲוָה עֲלָךְ רַחֲמִין הֲוֵינָא מְצַלֵּי וּמַחְתָא הֲוָה מִתְכַּלְיָא מִינָךְ וּכְדוֹן לָא אוֹסִיף תּוּב לְמֶחֱמֵי סְבַר אַפָּךְ


And Mosheh said, Thou hast spoken fairly. While I was dwelling in Midian, it was told me in a word from before the Lord, that the men who had sought to kill me had fallen from their means, and were reckoned with the dead. At the end there will be no mercy upon thee; but I will pray, and the plague shall be restrained from thee. And now I will see thy face no more.



## Chapter 11

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/11:1)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה תּוּב מַכְתַּשׁ חַד אֲנָא מַיְיתִי עֲלוֹי פַּרְעה וְעִילוֹי מִצְרָאֵי דְקָשֵׁי עֲלֵיהוֹן מִכֻּלְהוֹן וּמִבָּתַר כְּדֵין יִפְטוֹר יַתְכוֹן מִכָּא בְּמִפְטְרֵיהּ גְּמִירָא יְהֵי לֵיהּ מַטְרֵד יִטְרוֹד יַתְכוֹן מִכָּא


And the Lord spake unto Mosheh, Yet one stroke will I bring upon Pharoh and upon the Mizraee, which shall be greater than all, and afterward will he send you hence: when he releases, there shall be to himself an end: driving, he will drive you forth from hence.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/11:2)


מַלֵּיל כְּדוֹן בְּמִשְׁמָעֵיהוֹן דְּעַמָּא וִישַׁיְילוּן גְּבַר מִן רַחֲמֵיהּ מִצְרָאֵי וְאִתְּתָא מִן רְחִימָא מִצְרֵיתָא מָאנִין דִּכְסַף וּמָאנִין דִּדְהַב


Speak now in the hearing of the people, That every man shall demand from his Mizraite friend, and every woman of her Mizraite friend, vessels of silver and vessels of gold.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/11:3)


וִיהַב יְיָ יַת עַמָּא לְרַחֲמִין קֳדָם מִצְרָאֵי אוּף גַּבְרָא משֶׁה רַב לַחֲדָא בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם קֳדָם עַבְדֵי פַרְעה וּקְדָם עַמָּא


And the Lord gave the people favour before the Mizraee; also the man Mosheh was very great in the land of Mizraim before the servants of Pharoh and before his people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/11:4)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְפַרְעה כִּדְנָא אָמַר יְיָ לֵילְיָא חוֹרַן בְּשַׁעְתָּא דָא אֲנָא מִתְגְּלֵי בְּגוֹ מִצְרָאֵי


And Mosheh spake (or, had spoken) to Pharoh, Thus saith the Lord, At this hour of the following night will I be revealed in the midst of the Mizraee,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/11:5)


וִימוּת כָּל בּוּכְרָא בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם מִבִּיר בּוּכְרָא דְפַרְעה דְּעָתִיד דְּיֵיתִיב עַל כּוּרְסַיָא מַלְכוּתֵיהּ עַד בּוּכְרָא דְאַמְתָא בִּיצִירְתָּא דִבְמִצְרַיִם דְּמִתְיְלִיד לָהּ כַּד הִיא טַחְנָא אֲחוֹרֵי רֵיחַיָא וְכָל בּוּכְרָא דִבְעִירָא


and every firstborn in the land of Mizraim shall die: from the firstborn of Pharoh who should sit upon the throne of his kingdom, unto the firstborn son of the humblest mother in Mizraim who grindeth behind the mills, and all the firstborn of cattle.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/11:6)


וּתְהֵי צְוַוחְתָּא רַבְּתָא בְּכָל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם דְּדִכְוָותֵיהּ לֵילְיָא לָא הֲוָת בֵּיהּ מַחְתָּא כְּדָא וְדִכְוָותֵיהּ לֵילְיָא לָא תוֹסֵיף לְמֶהֱוֵי מַחְתָּא כְּדָא


And there will be a great cry in all the land of Mizraim, because like the plague of this night there hath not been, and like the plague of this night there never will be one.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/11:7)


וּלְכָל בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לָא יְהַנְזַק כַּלְבָּא בְּלִשְׁנֵיהּ לְמִנְבַּח לְמֵאֱנָשָׁא וְעַד בְּעִירָא מִן בִּגְלַל דְּתִנְדְּעוּן דְּיַפְרֵישׁ יְיָ בֵּין מִצְרָאֵי וּבֵינֵי יִשְרָאֵל


But any of the children of Israel a dog shall not harm by lifting up his tongue against either man or beast ; that they may know that the Lord maketh distinction between the Mizraites and the sons of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 11:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/11:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/11:8)


וְיַחְתוּן כָּל עַבְדָּךְ אִלֵּין לְוָותִי וְיִבְעוּן מָטוּ מִינִי לְמֵימַר פּוּק אַנְתְּ וְכָל עַמָּא דְּעִמָּךְ וּמִבָּתַר כְּדֵין אֶפּוֹק וּנְפַק מִלְוַת פַּרְעה בִּתְקוֹף רְגַז


And thou shalt send down all thy servants to me, coming and beseeching me, saying, Go forth, thou and all the people who are with thee; and afterwards I will go. And he went out from Pharoh in great anger.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 11:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/11:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/11:9)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה לָא יְקַבֵּיל מִנְכוֹן פַּרְעה מִן בִּגְלַל לְאַסְגָאָה תִּמְהַיַי בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם


But the Lord said to Mosheh, Pharoh will not hearken to you ; that I may multiply My wonders in the land of Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 11:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/11:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/11:10)


וּמשֶׁה וְאַהֲרן עֲבָדוּ יַת כָּל תִּמְהַיָא הָאִילֵין קֳדָם פַּרְעה וּתְקֵיף יְיָ יַת יִצְרָא דְלִבָּא דְפַרְעה וְלָא פְטַר יַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מֵאַרְעֵיהּ


And Mosheh and Aharon did all these wonders before Pharoh; and the Lord strengthened the design of Pharoh's heart, and he would not release the sons of Israel from his land.



## Chapter 12

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:1)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה וּלְאַהֲרן בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spoke to Mosheh and to Aharon in the land of Mizraim, saying,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:2)


יַרְחָא הָדֵין לְכוֹן לְמִקְבְּעֵיהּ רֵישׁ יַרְחַיָּיא וּמִנֵּיהּ תֵּשְׁרוּן לְמִמְנֵי חַגַיָא וְזִמְנַיָא וּתְקוּפְתָּא קַדְמָאֵי הוּא לְכוֹן לְמִנְיַן יַרְחֵי שַׁתָּא


This month is ordained to be to you the beginning of the months; and from it you shall begin to number for festivals, and times, and cycles; it shall be to you the first of the number of the months of the year.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:3)


מַלִילוּ עִם כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְמֵימַר בְּעַשְרָא לְיַרְחָא הָדֵין זִמְנֵיהּ קָבִיעַ בַּהֲדָא זִימְנָא וְלָא לְדָרַיָא וְיִסְבוּן לְהוֹן אִימַר לְבֵית גְּנִיסְתָּא וְאִין סַגִּיאִין מִמִּנְיָנָא יִסְבוּן אִימְרָא לְבֵיתָא


Speak to all the congregation of the children of Israel, saying, In the tenth of this month, whose time is appointed for this time (occasion), and not for (coming) generations, they shall take to them a lamb for the house of a family, and, if many in number, they shall take a lamb for a house:


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:4)


וְאִין זְעִירִין אִינְשֵׁי בֵיתָא מִמִּנְיַן עַשְרָא כְּמִיסַת לְמֵיכוּל אִימְרָא וְיִסַב הוּא וְשִׁיבְבֵיהּ דִּקָרֵיב לְבֵיתֵיהּ בִּסְכוּם נַפְשָׁתָא גְּבַר לְפוּם מֵיסַב מֵיכְלֵיהּ תִּיכְסוּן יַת אִימְרָא


but if the men of the house are fewer than ten in number, in proportion to a sufficient number to eat the lamb, he and his neighbour who is nearest to his house shall take according to the number of souls: each man according to the sufficiency of his eating shall be counted for the lamb.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:5)


אִימַר שְׁלִים דְּכַר בַּר שַׁתָּא יְהֵי לְכוֹן מִן אִימְרַיָא וּמִן בְּנֵי עִזַּיָא תִּסְבוּן


The lamb shall be perfect, a male, the son of a year he shall be to you; from the sheep or from the young goats ye may take.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:6)


וִיהֵי לְכוֹן קָטִיר וְנָטִיר עַד אַרְבֵּיסַר יוֹמָא לְיַרְחָא הָדֵין דְּתִנְדְּעוּן דְּלֵיתֵיכוֹן מִסְתַּפְיַן מִמִּצְרָאֵי דְחַמְיָין יָתֵיהּ וְיִכְסוֹן יָתֵיהּ כְּהִילְכָתָא כָּל קְהַל כְּנִישְׁתָּא דְיִשְרָאֵל בֵּינֵי שִׁמְשָׁתָא


And it shall be bound and reserved for you until the fourteenth day of this month, that you may not know the fear of the Mizraee when they see it; and ye shall kill him according to the rite of all to congregation of the assembly of Israel, between the suns.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:7)


וְיִסְבוּן מִן דְּמָא וְיִתְּנוּן עַל תְּרֵין סִיפַיָּיא וְעִילוֹי מִסְקוּפָא עִלָּאָה מִלְּבַּר עַל בָּתַּיָא דְיֵיכְלוּן יָתֵיהּ וּדְדַמְכוּן בְּהוֹן


And you shall take of the blood and set it upon the two posts and upon the upper board outside of the houses in which you eat and sleep.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:8)


וְיֵיכְלוּן יַת בִּישְרָא בְּלֵילְיָא הָדֵין דַּחֲמֵיסַר בְּנִיסַן עַד פַּלְגוּתֵיהּ דְּלֵילְיָא טְוֵי נוּר וּפַטִּיר עַל תַּמְכָא וְעוּלְשִׁין יֵיכְלוּנֵיהּ


And you shall eat the flesh on that night, the fifteenth of Nisan, until the dividing of the night roasted with fire, without leaven, with horehound and lettuce shall you eat it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:9)


לָא תֵיכְלוּן מִנֵיהּ כַּד חַי וְלָא כַּד בַּשְׁלָא בְּחַמְרָא וּמִשְׁחָא וְשַׁקְיָינֵי וְלָא מְבֻשָּׁל בְּמַיָא אֱלָהֵן טְוֵי נוּר עִם רֵישֵׁיהּ עִם רַגְלוֹי וְעִם גַּוֵּיהּ


Eat not of it while living, neither boiled in wine, or oil, or other fluids, neither boiled in water, but roasted with fire, with its head, and its feet, and its inwards.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:10)


וְלָא תְשַׁיְירוּן מִנֵּיהּ עַד צַפְרָא וּדְאִשְׁתַּיֵּיר מִנֵּיהּ עַד צַפְרָא תַּצְנְעִנֵּיהּ וּבְאוֹרְתָּא דְּשִׁתְּסַר בְּנוּרָא תוֹקְדוּן דְּלֵית אֶפְשַׁר לְמִיתוֹקְדָא מוֹתַר נִיכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא בְּיוֹמָא טָבָא


Nor shall any be left of it till the morning; but what may remain of it in the morning you shall cover over, and in the daylight of the sixteenth day burn with fire; for you may not burn the residue of a holy oblation on the feast day.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:11)


וּכְדָא הִילְכָתָא תֵיכְלוּן יָתֵיהּ בְּזִמְנָא דָא וְלָא לְדָרַיָא חַרְצֵיכוֹן יְהוֹן מְזַרְזִין מְסַנֵיכוֹן בְּרִיגְלֵיכוֹן וְחוּטְרֵיכוֹן בִּידֵיכוֹן וְתֵיכְלוּן יָתֵיהּ בִּבְהִילוּ דִשְׁכִינַת מָארֵי עַלְמָא מְטוּל דְּחַיְיסָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ לְכוֹן הוּא


And according to this manner you shall eat it, this time, but not in (other) generations: your loins shall be girded, your shoes on your feet, and your staves in your hands; and you shall eat in the fear of the majesty of the Lord of the world; because mercy hath been shown to you from before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:12)


וְאִתְגְּלֵי בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם בִּשְׁכִינַת יְקָרִי בְּלֵילְיָא הָדֵין וְעִמִּי תִּשְׁעִין אַלְפִין רִבְוָון מַלְאָכִין מְחַבְּלִין וְאֶקְטוֹל כָּל בּוּכְרָא בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם מֵאֱנָשָׁא וְעַד בְּעִירָא וּבְכָל טַעֲוַות מִצְרָאֵי אֶעֱבֵיד אַרְבַּע דִינִין טַעֲוַות מַתְּכָא מִתְרַכְּכִין טַעֲוַות אַבְנָא מִתְגַּדְעִין טַעֲוַות פַּחֲרָא מִתְעַבְּדִין בְּקִיקִין טַעֲוַות אָעָא מִתְעַבְּדִין קְטַם דְּיִנְדְּעוּן מִצְרָאֵי אֲרוּם אֲנָא יְיָ


And I will be revealed in the land of Mizraim in the majesty of My glory this night, and with Me ninety thousand myriads of destroying angels; and I will slay all the firstborn in the land of Mizraim, of man and of beast, and against all the idols of the Mizraee I will execute four judgments: the molten idols shall be melted, the idols of stone be broken, the idols of clay shall he shattered, and the idols of wood be made dust, that the Mizraee may know that I am the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:13)


וִיהֵי דַּם נִכְסַת פִּסְחָא וּגְזֵרַת מְהוּלְתָּא מְעַרַב לְכוֹן לְמֶעֱבַד מִנֵּיהּ אָת עַל בָּתַּיָא דְּאַתּוּן שַׁרְיָן תַּמָּן וְאֶחֱמֵי יַת זְכוּת דְּמָא וְאֵיחוּס עֲלֵיכוֹן וְלָא יִשְׁלוֹט בְּכוֹן מַלְאָךְ מוֹתָא דְּאִתְיַהַב לֵיהּ רְשׁוּתָא לִמְחַבְּלָא בְּמִקְטְלִי בְּאַרְעָא דְּמִצְרָיִם


And the blood of the paschal oblation, (like) the matter of circumcision, shall be a bail for you, to become a sign upon the houses where you dwell; and I will look upon the worth of the blood, and will spare you; and the angel of death, to whom is given the power to destroy, shall have no dominion over you in the slaughter of the Mizraee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:14)


וִיהֵי יוֹמָא הָדֵין לְכוֹן לְדוּכְרָנָא וּתְחַגּוּן יָתֵיהּ חַגָּא קֳדָם יְיָ לְדָרֵיכוֹן קְיַים עֲלָם תַּחֲגוּנֵיהּ


And this day shall be to you for a memorial, and you shall celebrate it a festival before the Lord in your generations; by a perpetual statute shall you solemnize it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:15)


שַׁבְעַת יוֹמִין פַּטִּירָא תֵיכְלוּן בְּרַם מִפַּלְגוּת יוֹמָא דְמִיקַמֵּי חַגָּא תְבַטְּלוּן חָמִיר מִבָּתֵּיכוֹן אֲרוּם כָּל דְּיֵיכוּל חָמִיעַ וְיִשְׁתֵּיצֵי אֵינָשָׁא הַהוּא מִיִּשְרָאֵל לְמִיּוֹמָא קַדְמָאָה דְחַגָּא וְעַד יוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה


Seven days you shall eat unleavened bread: in the dividing of the day which precedes the feast you shall put away leaven from your houses; for whosoever eateth what is leavened, from the first day of the feast until the seventh day, that man shall be destroyed from Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:16)


וּבְיוֹמָא קַדְמָאָה מְעָרַע קַדִּישׁ וּבְיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה מְעָרַע קַדִּישׁ יְהֵי לְכוֹן כָּל עֲבִידְתָּא לָא יִתְעֲבֵיד בְּהוֹן לְחוֹד מִן דְּיִתְעֲבֵד לְמֵיכַל כָּל נֶפֶשׁ אִיהוּ בִּלְחוֹדוֹי יִתְעֲבֵיד לְכוֹן


And on the first day there shall be a holy congregation, and on the seventh day there shall be to you a holy congregation. No work shall be done among you, only that which must be done for every one's eating may be done by you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:17)


וְתִטְרוּן יַת לִישָׁתָא דְּפַטִּירֵי אֲרוּם בִּכְרַן יוֹמָא הָדֵין הַנְפֵּיק יְיָ יַת חֵילֵיכוֹן פְּרִיקִין מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם וְתִטְרוּן יַת יוֹמָא הָדֵין לְדָרֵיכוֹן קְיַם עֲלָם


And you shall observe the feast of the unleavened bread, because in this same day the Lord will bring out your hosts free from the land of Mizraim; and you shall observe this day in your generations, a statute for ever.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:18)


בְּנִיסָן בְּאַרְבֵּיסַר יוֹמִין לְיַרְחָא תִּכְסוּן יַת פִּסְחָא וּבְרַמְשָׁא דְּחַמְסַר תֵּיכְלוּן פַּטִּירֵי עַד יוֹמָא דְעַשְרִין וְחַד לְיַרְחָא בְּרַמְשָׁא דְעַשְרִין וּתְרֵין תֵּיכְלוּן חָמִיעַ


In Nisan, on the fourteenth day of the month, you shall kill the passover, and at evening on the fifteenth you shall eat unleavened bread until the twenty-first of the month. On the evening of the twenty-second you may eat leavened bread.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:19)


שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין חָמִיר לָא יִשְׁתְּכַח בְּבָתֵּיכוֹן אֲרוּם כָּל מַאן דְּיֵיכוּל מַחְמְעָא וְיִשְׁתֵּיצֵי בַּר נְשָׁא הַהוּא מִכְּנִשְׁתָּא דְיִשְרָאֵל בְּדִיוֹרֵי וּבְיַצִּיבֵי דְאַרְעָא


For seven days leaven shall not be found in your houses; for whosoever eateth of leaven, that man shall perish from the congregation of Israel, whether he be a stranger or home-bred in the land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:20)


כָּל עַרְבִיבֵי דִּמְחָמַע לָא תֵיכְלוּן בְּכָל אֲתַר מוֹתְבָנֵיכוֹן תֵּיכְלוּן פַּטִירֵי


Any mixture of leaven you shall not eat; in every place of your habitation you shall eat unleavened bread.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:21)


וּקְרָא משֶׁה לְכָל סָבֵי יִשְרָאֵל וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן נְגוּדוּ יְדֵיכוֹן מִטַּעֲוַות מִצְרָאֵי וּסְבוּ לְכוֹן מִן בְּנֵי עָנָא לְיִיחוּסֵיכוֹן וְכוּסוּ אִימַר פִּסְחָא


And Mosheh called all the elders of Israel, and said to them, Withdraw your hands from the idols of the Mizraee, and take to you from the offspring of the flock, according to your houses, and kill the paschal lamb.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:22)


וְתִסְבוּן אֵיסָרַת אֵזוֹבָא וְתִטְמְשׁוּן בְּדָמָא דִבְמַן פַּחֲרָא וְתַדוּן לְאִיסְקוֹפָא עִלָּאָה מִלְּבַר וּלְתַרְתֵּין סִפַּיָא מִן דְּמָא דִבְמַן פַּחֲרָא וְאַתּוּן לָא תִפְקוּן אֱנַשׁ מִן תְּרַע בֵּיתֵיהּ עַד צַפְרָא


And you shall take a bunch of hyssop, and dip it in the blood that is in the earthen vessel, and upon the upper bar without and upon the two posts you shall sprinkle of the blood which is in the earthen vessel, and not a man of you must come forth from the door of his hour till the morning.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:23)


וְיִתְגְּלֵי יְקָרָא דַיְיָ לְמִימְחֵי יַת מִצְרָאֵי וְיֶחֱמֵי יַת אַדְמָא דְעַל אִסְקוֹפָא וְעַל תַּרְתֵּין סִיפַיָא וְיָגִין מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ עַל תַּרְעָא וְלָא יִשְׁבּוֹק מַלְאָכָא מְחַבְּלָא לְמֵיעוֹל לְבָתֵּיכוֹן לְמִימְחֵי


For the Glory of the Lord will be manifested in striking the Mizraee, and He will see the blood upon the lintel and upon the too posts, and the Word of the Lord will spread His protection over the door, and the destroying angel will not be permitted to enter your houses to smite.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:24)


וְתִיטְרוּן יַת פִּתְגָמָא הָדֵין לִקְיַם לָךְ וְלִבְנָךְ דְּכוּרַיָא עַד עַלְמָא


And you shall observe this thing for a statute to thee and to thy sons for a memorial for ever.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:25)


וִיהֵי אֲרוּם תַּעֲלוּן לְאַרְעָא דְּעָתִיד לְמִתַּן לְכוֹן יְיָ הֵיכְמָא דְמַלֵּיל וְתִיטְרוּן מִזְמַן דְּתִמְטוֹן לְתַמָּן יַת פּוּלְחָנָא הֲדָא


And it shall be when you are come into the land that the Lord will give to you, as He hath spoken, that from the time of your coming you shall observe this service.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:26)


וִיהֵי אֲרוּם יֵימְרוּן לְכוֹן בְּנֵיכוֹן בְּזִמְנָא הַהוּא מַה פּוּלְחָנָא הָדָא לְכוֹן


And it shall be that when at that time your children shall say to you, What is this your service?


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:27)


וְתֵימְרוּן נִכְסַת חַיְיסָא הוּא קֳדָם יְיָ דְחַס בְּמֵימְרֵיהּ עַל בָּתֵּי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל בְּמִצְרַיִם בְּחַבְּלוּתֵיהּ יַת מִצְרָאֵי וְיַת בָּתָּנָא שֵׁיזִיב וְכַד שָׁמְעוּ בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל יַת פִּתְגָמָא הָדֵין מִפּוּם משֶׁה גָּחְנוּ וּסְגִידוּ


You shall say, It is the sacrifice of mercy before the Lord, who had mercy in His Word upon the houses of the sons of Israel in Mizraim, when He destroyed the Mizraee, and spared our houses. And when the house of Israel heard this word from the mouth of Mosheh, they bowed and worshipped.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:28)


וַאֲזָלוּ וַעֲבָדוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל הֵיכְמָא דְפַקֵּיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה וְיַת אַהֲרן הֵיכְדֵין אִיזְדַרְזוּ וַעֲבָדוּ


And the sons of Israel went and did as the Lord com-manded Mosheh and Aharon, so did they hasten and do.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:29)


וַהֲוָה בְּפַלְגוּת לֵילְיָא דַּחֲמֵיסַר וּמֵימְרָא דַיְיָ קָטַל כָּל בּוּכְרָא בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם מִבִּיר בּוּכְרָא דְפַרְעה דְּעָתִיד לְמִיתַּב עַל כּוּרְסֵי מַלְכוּתֵיהּ עַד בּוּכְרַיָיא בְּנֵי מַלְכַיָא דְאִשְׁתְּבִין וְאִינוּן בֵּי גוֹבָא מִתְמַשְׁכְּנִין בְּיַד פַּרְעה וְעַל דַּהֲווֹ חָדָן בְּשִׁעְבּוּדְהוֹן דְּיִשְרָאֵל לָקוּ אוּף הִינוּן וְכָל בּוּכְרֵי בְּעִירָא מֵתוּ דְּמִצְרָאֵי פַּלְחִין לְהוֹן


And it was in the dividing, of the night of the fifteenth, that the Word of the Lord slew all the firstborn in the land of Mizraim, from the firstborn son of Pharoh, who would have sat upon the throne of his kingdom, unto the firstborn sons of the kings who were captives in the dungeon as hostages under Pharoh's hand; and who, for having rejoiced at the servitude of Israel, were punished as (the Mizraee): and all the firstborn of the cattle that did the work of the Mizraee died also.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:30)


וְקָם פַּרְעה בְּלֵילְיָא הוּא וְכָל שְׁאָר עַבְדוֹי וְכָל שְׁאָר מִצְרָאֵי וַהֲוָה צְוַוחְתָּא רַבְּתָא אֲרוּם לָא הֲוָה תַּמָּן בֵּיתָא דְמִצְרָאֵי דְלָא הֲוָה תַמָּן בְּכוֹר מָאִית


And Pharoh rose up in that night, and all the rest of his servants, and all the rest of the Mizraee; and there was a great cry, because there was no house of the Mizraee where the firstborn was not dead.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:31)


וּתְחוּם אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם מַהֲלָךְ אַרְבַּע מְאָה פַּרְסֵי הֲוַת וְאַרְעָא דְגוֹשֶׁן דְּתַמָּן משֶׁה וּבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל בִּמְצִיעוּת אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם הֲוַת וּפַלְטְרִין דְּבֵית מַלְכוּתָא דְפַרְעה בְּרֵישׁ אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם הֲוָה וְכַד קְרָא לְמשֶׁה וּלְאַהֲרן בְּלֵילְיָא דְפִסְחָא אִשְׁתְּמַא קָלֵיהּ עַד אַרְעָא דְגשֶׁן מִתְחַנֵּן הֲוָה פַּרְעה בְּקַל עָצִיב וְכֵן אָמַר קוּמוּ פּוּקוּ מִגּוֹ עַמִּי אוּף אַתּוּן אוּף בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְזִילוּ פְלָחוּ קֳדָם יְיָ הֵיכְמָא דַאֲמַרְתּוּן


And the border of the land of Mizraim extended four hundred pharsee; but the land of Goshen, where Mosheh and the sons of Israel were, was in the midst of the land of Mizraim; and the royal palace of Pharoh was at the entrance of the land of Mizraim. But when he cried to Mosheh and to Aharon in the night of the Pascha, his voice was heard unto the land of Goshen; Pharoh crying with a voice of woe, and saying thus: Arise, Go forth from among my people, both you and the sons of Israel; and go, worship before the Lord, as you have said;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:32)


אוּף עַנְכוֹן אוּף תּוֹרֵיכוֹן דְּבָרוּ מִן דִּילִי הֵיכְמָא דְמַלֵּילְתּוּן וְזִילוּ וְלֵית אֲנָא בָּעֵי מִנְכוֹן אֱלָהֵין דְּתַצְלוּן עָלַי דְלָא אֵימוּת


your sheep also take, and whatever of mine you have spoken about, and go; and nothing ask I of you except that you pray for me that I may not die.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:33)


וְכַד שְׁמָעוּ משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן וּבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל קָל בְּכוּתָא דְפַרְעה וְלָא אַשְׁגָחוּ עַד דְּאָזַל הוּא וְכָל עַבְדוֹי וְכָל מִצְרָאֵי וּתְקִיפוּ לְכָל עַמָּא בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל לְמִפְטְרִינוּן מִן אַרְעָא אֲרוּם אָמְרִין אִי שַׁהְיַין הִינוּן הָכָא שַׁעְתָּא חָדָא הָא כּוּלָנָא מַיְיתוּן


When Mosheh and Aharon, and the sons of Israel, heard the voice of Pharoh's weeping, they were not mindful, until he came himself, and all his servants, and all the Mizraee, and urged all the people of the house of Israel, that they might hasten to send them forth from the land; For, said they, if they prolong here one hour more, behold, we are all dead.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:34)


וּנְטַל עַמָּא יַת לֵישֵׁיהוֹן עֲלוֹי רֵישֵׁיהוֹן עַד דְּלָא אַחְמָע וּמִן דְּמִשְׁתַּיֵּיר לְהוֹן מִן פַּטִּירֵי וּמְרִירִי סוֹבָרוּ צְרִיר בִּלְבוּשֵׁיהוֹן עַל כַּתְפֵיהוֹן


And the people carried their dough upon their heads, being unleavened, and what remained to them of the paschal cakes and bitter things they carried, bound up with their raiment, upon their shoulders.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:35)


וּבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל עָבָדוּ כְּפִתְגָמָא דְמשֶׁה וְשַׁיְילוּ מִן מִצְרָאֵי מָנִין דִּכְסַף וּמָנִין דִּדְהַב


And the sons of Israel did according to the word of Mosheh, and asked of the Mizraee vessels of silver and vessels of gold.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:36)


וַיְיָ יְהַב יַת עַמָּא לְחֵן וָחֶסֶד קֳדָם מִצְרָאֵי וְשַׁיְילִינוּן וְרוֹקִינוּן יַת מִצְרָאֵי מִנִכְסֵיהוֹן


And the Lord gave the people favour and compassion before the Mizraee, and they brought forth to them, and they emptied the Mizraee of their riches.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:37)


וּנְטָלוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מִן פִּילוּסִין לְסוּכּוֹת מְאָה וּתְלָתִין מִילִין תַּמָּן אִיתְחַפִּיאוּ שִׁבְעַת עֲנָנֵי יְקָרָא אַרְבָּעָה מֵאַרְבַּע צִיטְרֵיהוֹן וְחַד מֵעִילַוֵּיהוֹן דְּלָא יֵיחוֹת עֲלֵיהוֹן מִטְרָא וּבַרְדָא וְלָא יִתְחַרְכוּן בִּשְׁרָבֵי שִׁמְשָׁא וְחַד מִלְּרַע לְהוֹן דְּלָא יְהַנְזְקוּן לְהוֹן כּוּבִין וְלָא חֵיוִין וְעַקְרַבִּין וְחָד מְטַיָּיל קוּמֵיהוֹן לְאַשְׁוָאָה עוּמְקַיָא וּלְמֵימַךְ טוּרַיָא לְאַתְקָנָא לְהוֹן בֵּית מֵשְׁרוֹי וְהִינוּן כְּשִׁית מֵאָה אַלְפִין גּוּבְרַיָא וּמְטַיְילִין עַל רִיגְלֵיהוֹן וְלָא רַכְבִין עַל סוּסְוָון בַּר מִטַּפְלָא חַמְשָׁא לְכָל גַּבְרָא


And the sons of Israel moved forth from Pilusin towards Succoth, a hundred and thirty thousand, protected there by seven clouds of glory on their four sides: one above them, that neither hail nor rain might fall upon them, nor that they should be burned by the heat of the sun; one beneath them, that they might not be hurt by thorns, serpents, or scorpions; and one went before them, to make the valleys even, and the mountains low, and to prepare them a place of habitation. And they were about six hundred thousand men, journeying on foot, none riding on horses except the children five to every man;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:38)


וְאוּף נוּכְרָאִין סַגִּיאִין מִנְהוֹן מָאתָן וְאַרְבְּעִין רִבְוָון סַלִּיקוּ עִמְהוֹן וְעָאָן וְתוֹרֵי וְגֵיתֵי סַגִּי לַחֲדָא


and a multitude of strangers, two hundred and forty myriads, went up with them, and sheep, and oxen, and cattle, very many.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:39)


וַהֲווֹ קַטְעִין מִן לִישָׁא דְאַפִּיקוּ מִמִּצְרַיִם וְסַדְרִין עַל רֵישֵׁיהוֹן וּמִתְאַפֵּי לְהוֹן מְחוּמְתָּא דְשִׁימְשָׁא חֲרִירַן פַּטִירַן אֲרוּם לָא חָמִיעַ אֲרוּם אִיתְרִיכוּ מִמִּצְרַיִם וְלָא יָכִילוּ לְמִישְׁהֵי וְסַפִּיקָא לְהוֹן לְמֵיכַל עַד חֲמֵיסַר יוֹמִין לְיַרְחָא דְאִיָּיר מְטוֹל דִּזְוָודִין לָא עֲבָדוּ לְהוֹן


And they divided the dough which they brought out of Mizraim, which they had carried on their heads, and it was baked for them by the heat of the sun, (into) unleavened cakes, because it had not fermented; for the Mizraee had thrust them out, neither could they delay; and it was sufficient for them to eat until the fifteenth of the month Ijar; because they had not prepared provision for the way.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:40)


וְיוֹמַיָא דְיָתִיבוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל בְּמִצְרַיִם תְּלָתִין שְׁמִיטִין דִּשְׁנִין דִּסְכוּמְהוֹן מָאתָן וְעֶשֶר שְׁנִין וּמִנְיַן אַרְבַּע מְאָה וּתְלָתִין שְׁנִין מִן דְּמַלֵּיל יְיָ לְאַבְרָהָם מִן שַׁעְתָּא דְמַלֵּיל עִמֵּיהּ בַּחֲמֵיסַר בְּנִיסָן בֵּינֵי פְּסוּגַיָיא עַד יוֹמָא דִּנְפָקוּ מִמִּצְרַיִם


And the days of the dwelling of the sons of Israel in Mizraim were thirty weeks of years, (thirty times seven years,) which is the sum of two hundred and ten years. But the number of four hundred and thirty years (had passed away since) the Lord spake to Abraham, in the hour that He spake with him on the fifteenth of Nisan, between the divided parts, until the day that they went out of Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:41)


וַהֲוָה מִסּוֹף תְּלָתִין שְׁנִין מִדְאִיתְגַּזְרַת גְּזֵירְתָּא הָדָא עַד דְּאִיתְיְלִיד יִצְחָק עַד דִנְפָקוּ פְּרִיקִין מִמִּצְרַיִם אַרְבַּע מְאָה שְׁנִין וַהֲוָה בִּכְרַן יוֹמָא הָדֵין נְפָקוּ כָּל חֵילַיָא דַיְיָ פְּרִיקִין מֵאַרְעָא דְּמִצְרָיִם


And it was at the end of thirty years from the making of this covenant, that Izhak was born; and thence until they went out of Mizraim four hundred (years), on the selfsame day it was that all the hosts of the Lord went forth made free from the land of Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:42)


אַרְבָּעָה לֵילְוָון כְּתִיבִין בְּסֵפֶר דּוּכְרָנַיָיא קֳדָם רִבּוֹן עַלְמָא לֵילְיָא קַדְמָאָה כַּד אִיתְגְּלֵי לְמִבְרֵי עַלְמָא תִּנְיָינָא כַּד אִיתְגְּלֵי עַל אַבְרָהָם תְּלִיתָאָה כַּד אִיתְגְּלֵי בְּמִצְרַיִם וַהֲוָת יְדֵיהּ מְקַטְלָא כָּל בּוּכְרָא דְמִצְרַיִם וִימִינֵיהּ מְשֵׁיזְבָא בְּכוֹרֵיהֶן דְּיִשְרָאֵל רְבִיעָאָה כַּד אִתְגְּלֵי לְמִפְרוֹק עַמָּא בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל מִבֵּינֵי עַמְמַיָא וְכוּלְהוֹן קָרָא לֵילֵי נְטִיר בְּגִין כֵּן פָּרִישׁ משֶׁה וַאֲמַר לֵיל נְטִיר לְפוּרְקַן הוּא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ לְמַפְקָא יַת עַמָּא בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם הוּא לֵילְיָא הָדֵין נְטִיר מִמַּלְאָכָה מְחַבְּלָא לְכָל בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל דִּבְמִצְרַיִם וְכֵן לְמִיפְרַקְהוֹן מִגַּלְוַותְהוֹן לְדָרֵיהוֹן:


Four nights are there written in the Book of Memorials before the Lord of the world. Night the first,--when He was revealed in creating the world; the second,--when He was revealed to Abraham; the third,--when He was revealed in Mizraim, His hand killing all the firstborn of Mizraim, and His right hand saving the firstborn of Israel; the fourth,--when He will yet be revealed to liberate the people of the house of Israel from among the nations. And all these are called Nights to be observed; for so explained Mosheh, and said thereof, It is to be observed on account of the liberation which is from the Lord, to lead forth the people of the sons of Israel from the land of Mizraim. This is that Night of preservation from the destroying angel for all the sons of Israel who were in Mizraim, and of redemption of their generations from their captivity.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:43)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה וְאַהֲרן דָּא הִיא גְזֵירַת פִּסְחָא כָּל בַּר עַמְמִין אוֹ בַּר יִשְרָאֵל דְּאִיסְתְּלַק וְלָא הָדַר לָא יֵיכוּל בֵּיהּ


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:44)


וְכָל גּוּבְרָאֵי דְּאִזְדַּבַּן לְעֶבֶד לְגַבְרָא בַּר יִשְרָאֵל זַבִּין כַּסְפָּא וְתִגְזוֹר יָתֵיהּ וְתִטְבְּלִינֵיהּ בְּכֵן יֵיכוּל בֵּיהּ


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:45)


דַּיוֹר תּוֹתַב וַאֲגִירָא נוּכְרָאָה לָא יֵיכוֹל בֵּיהּ


A sojourner or a hired stranger shall not eat thereof.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:46)


בַּחֲבוּרָא חָדָא יִתְאָכֵל לָא תִפְקוּן מִן בֵּיתָא מִן בִּשְרָא בַּר מֵחֲבוּרָתָא וְלָא לִמְשַׁדְרָא דוֹרוֹנִין גְּבַר לְחַבְרֵיהּ וּגְרָמָא לָא תְתַבְּרִין בֵּיהּ בְּדִיל לְמֵיכוּל מַה דִּבְגַוֵּיהּ


In his own company he shall eat. Thou shalt not carry any of the flesh out of the house from (thy) company, nor send a gift one mail to his neighbour; and a bone of him shall not be broken for the sake of eating that which is within it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:47

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:47](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:47)


כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא דְיִשְרָאֵל מִתְעַרְבִין דֵּין גְנִיסָא עִם דֵּין גְּנִיסְתָּא אוֹחֲרִי לְמֶעֱבַד יָתֵיהּ


All the congregation of Israel shall mix together, this one with that, one family with another, that they may perform it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:48

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:48](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:48)


וַאֲרוּם יִתְגַּיַּיר עִמְכוֹן גִּיּוֹרָא וְיַעֲבֵד פִּסְחָא קֳדָם יְיָ יִגְזוֹר לֵיהּ כָּל דְּכוּרָא וּבְכֵן יְהֵי כָּשֵׁר לְמֶעֱבְדֵיהּ וִיהֵי כְּיַצִיבָא דְאַרְעָא וְכָל עַרְלָאֵי בַּר יִשְרָאֵל לָא יֵיכוּל בֵּיהּ


And if a proselyte sojourn with you, and would perform the pascha before the Lord, let every male belonging to him be circumcised, and so be made fit to perform it; and he shall be as the native of the land: but no uncircumcised one of the sons of Israel shall eat thereof.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:49

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:49](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:49)


אוֹרַיְיתָא חָדָא תְּהֵא לְכָל מִצְוָותָא לְיַצִּיבָא וּלְגִיּוֹרָא דִי מִתְגַּיֵּיר בֵּינֵיכוֹן


One law shall there be as to appointments for the native and for the proselyte who sojourneth among you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:50

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:50](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:50)


וַעֲבָדוּ כָּל בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל הֵיכְמָא דְפַּקִּיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה וְיַת אַהֲרן הֵיכְדֵין עָבָדוּ


And all the sons of Israel did as the Lord had commanded Mosheh and Aharon, so did they.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 12:51

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:51](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/12:51)


וַהֲוָה בִּכְרַן יוֹמָא הָדֵין אַפֵּיק יְיָ יַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם עַל חֵילֵיהוֹן


And it was on that same day that the Lord brought forth the sons of Israel from the land of Mizraim, with their hosts.



## Chapter 13

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:1)


וּמַלֵּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake unto Mosheh, saying,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:2)


אַקְדֵישׁ קֳדָמַי כָּל בּוּכְרָא דְכוּרַיָא פְּתַח כָּל וַולְדָא בִּבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל בְּאֵינָשָׁא וּבִבְעִירָא דִילִי הוּא


Sanctify before Me every firstborn male. Whatsoever openeth the womb of all the sons of Israel among men, and (also) among beasts, is Mine.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:3)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְעַמָּא הֲווֹן דְּכִירִין יַת יוֹמָא הָדֵין דִּי נְפַקְתּוּן פְּרִיקִין מִמִצְרַיִם מִבֵּית שִׁעְבּוּד עֲבָדַיָיא אֲרוּם בִּתְקוֹף גְּבוּרַת יְדָא אַפֵּיק יְיָ יַתְכוֹן מִיכָּא וְלָא יִתְאָכֵל חָמִיעַ


And Mosheh said to the people, Remember this the day in which you went out free from Mizraim from the house of the bondage of slaves; for by great strength of hand did the Lord bring you forth from thence; and you shall not eat leaven.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:4)


יוֹמָא דֵין אַתּוּן נַפְקוּן פְּרִיקִין בַּחֲמֵיסַר בְּנִיסָן הוּא יַרְחָא דְּאַבִּיבָא


This day you are come out free; on the fifteenth of Nisan, which is the month of Abiba.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:5)


וִיהֵי אֲרוּם יֵיעָלִינָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְאַרַע כְּנַעֲנָאֵי חִיתָּאֵי וְאֵימוֹרָאֵי וְחִיוָאֵי וִיבוּסָאֵי דְּקַיֵּים בְּמֵימְרֵיהּ וּלְאַבְהָתָךְ לְמִיתַּן לָךְ אַרְעָא עַבְדָא חֲלָב וּדְבָשׁ וְתִפְלַח יַת פּוּלְחָנָא הָדָא בְּיַרְחָא הָדֵין


And it shall be, when the Lord your God shall have brought you into the land of the Kenaanaee, and Hittaee, and Amoraee, and Hivaee, and Jebusaee, which He sware by His Word unto Abraham to give thee, a land producing milk and honey, that thou shalt keep this service in this month.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:6)


שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין תֵּיכוּל פַּטִּירָא וּבְיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה חַגָּא קֳדָם יְיָ


Seven days shalt thou eat unleavened cakes, and on the seventh day shall be a feast before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 13:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:7)


פַּטִּירֵי יִתְאָכֵיל יַת שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין וְלָא יִתְחָמֵי לָךְ חָמִיעַ וְלָא יִתְחָמֵי לָךְ חֲמִיר בְּכָל תְּחוּמָךְ


Unleavened cakes shall be eaten seven days, and nothing leavened shall be seen with thee, nor leaven itself be seen with thee in all thy borders.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 13:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:8)


וְתִתְנֵי לִבְרָךְ בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא לְמֵימָר מִן בִּגְלַל מִצְוָותָא דָא עֲבַד מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ לִי נִיסִין וּפְרִישִׁין בְּמִפְקֵי מִמִּצְרָיִם


And thou shalt instruct thy son on that day, saying, This precept is on account of what the Word of the Lord did for me in miracles and wonders, in bringing me forth from Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 13:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:9)


וִיהֵי לָךְ נִיסָא הָדֵין חֲקִיק וּמְפָרַשׁ עַל תְּפִילַת יְדָא בְּגוּבְהָא דִשְמָאלָךְ וּלְדוּכְרָן חֲקִיק וּמְפָרַשׁ עַל תְּפִלַּת רֵישָׁא קְבִיעָא כָּל קָבֵיל עֵינָךְ בְּגוּבְהָא לְרֵישָׁךְ מִן בִּגְלַל דְּתִיהֲוֵי אוֹרַיְיתָא דַיְיָ בְּפוּמָךְ אֲרוּם בְּחֵיל יְדָא תַקִיפְתָּא הַנְפָקָךְ יְיָ מִמִּצְרָיִם


And this miracle shall be inscribed and set forth upon the tephilla of the hand, on the top of thy left (arm,) and for a memorial inscribed and set forth upon the tephilla of thy head, set between thine eyes on thy forehead; that the law of the Lord may be in thy mouth, because in strength, with a mighty hand, the Lord brought thee forth from Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 13:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:10)


וְתִנְטוֹר יַת קְיָימָא הָדָא דִתְפִילֵי לְזִימְנָא דְחָזֵי לָהּ בְּיוֹמֵי עוֹבָדָא וְלָא בְּשַׁבַּיָיא וּבְמוֹעֲדַיָא וּבִימָמָא וְלָא בְּלֵילְיָא


Thou shalt therefore keep this statute of the Tephillin in the season to which it belongs, on work days, not on sabbaths or solemnities; and by day, not by night.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 13:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:11)


וִיהֵי אֲרוּם יָעֵלִינָךְ יְיָ לְאַרְעָא דְכְנַעֲנָאֵי הֵיכְמָא דְקַיֵּים לָךְ וּלְאַבְהָתָךְ וְיִתְּנִינָהּ לָךְ


And when I the Lord have brought thee into the land of the Kenaanaee, which I have sworn to thee and to thy fathers to give thee,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 13:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:12)


וְתַפְרֵשׁ כָּל פְּתַח וַולְדָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְכָל פְּתַח וַולְדָא בְּעִירָא דְּמֵשַׁגְרָא אִמֵּיהּ דִּיהוֹן לִיךְ דִּכְרִין תַּקְדֵּישׁ קֳדָם יְיָ


thou shalt set apart before the Lord every one that openeth the womb; and every animal that its dam beareth and that openeth the womb if it be to thee a male thou shalt sanctify before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 13:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:13)


וְכָל פְּתַח וַולְדָא דְחַמְרָא תִּפְרוֹק בְּאִימְרָא וְאִין לָא תִפְרוֹק וְתִנְקוֹף יָתֵיהּ וְכָל בּוּכְרָא דְּאֵינָשָׁא בִּבְרָךְ תִּפְרוֹק בְּכַסְפָּא וְלָא בְּעַבְדָךְ תִּפְרוֹק


And every ass that openeth the womb thou shalt redeem with a lamb; and if thou redeem him not, thou shalt cut him off; and every firstborn man (child) among thy sons thou shalt redeem; but thy servant thou mayest not redeem with money.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 13:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:14)


וִיהֵי אֲרוּם יְשַׁיְילִינָךְ בְּרָךְ מְחַר לְמֵימַר מַה דָּא מִצְוָותָא דְבוּכְרַיָיא וְתֵימַר לֵיהּ בִּתְקוֹף יְדָא אַפְקָנָא יְיָ מִמִּצְרַיִם פְּרִיקִין מִבֵּית שִׁעֲבּוּד עַבְדַיָא


And when in future thy son shall ask thee, saying, What is this ordinance of the firstborn? thou shalt tell him: By the power of a mighty hand the Lord delivered us from Mizraim, redeeming us from the house of the servitude of slaves.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 13:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:15)


וַהֲוָה כַּד אַקְשֵׁי מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ יַת לִבָּא דְפַרְעה לְמִפְטְרָנָא וְקָטַל יְיָ כָּל בּוּכְרָא בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם מִן בּוּכְרָא דְּאֵינָשָׁא וְעַד בּוּכְרָא דִבְעִירָא בְּגִין כֵּן אֲנָא דַבַּח קֳדָם יְיָ כָּל פְּתַח וַולְדָא דוּכְרַיָיא וְכָל בּוּכְרָא דִבְרַיי אֶפְרוֹק בְּכַסְפָּא


And when the Word of the Lord had hardened the heart of Pharoh (that be would) not deliver us, he killed all the firstborn in the land of Mizraim, from the firstborn of man to the firstborn of cattle; therefore do I sacrifice before the Lord every male that openeth the womb, and every firstborn of my sons I redeem with silver.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 13:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:16)


וִיהֵי לְאַת חֲקִיק וּמְפָרֵשׁ עַל יַד שְמָאלָךְ וְלִתְפִילִין בֵּין רִיסֵי עֵינָךְ אֲרוּם בִּתְקוֹף גְּבוּרַת יְדָא הַנְפָקָנָא יְיָ מִמִּצְרָיִם


And it shall be inscribed and set forth upon thy left land, and on the tephilla between thine eyebrows; because by mighty strength of hand the Lord brought us out of Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 13:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/13:17)
