Now have I known that the Lord is stronger than all gods; for by the very thing by which the Mizraee wickedly would have punished Israel by (drowning them in) the sea, upon themselves came the punishment, in being punished in the sea.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 18:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:12)


וּנְסַב יִתְרוֹ עַלְוָון וְנִכְסַת קוּדְשִׁין קֳדָם יְיָ וְאָתָא אַהֲרן וְכָל סָבֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְמֵיכוֹל לַחֲמָא עִם חָמוֹי דְמשֶׁה קֳדָם יְיָ וּמשֶׁה הֲוָה קָאִי וּמְשַׁמֵּשׁ קֳדָמֵיהוֹן


And Jethro took burnt offerings and holy sacrifices before the Lord, and Aharon and all the elders of Israel came to eat bread with the father-in-law of Mosheh before the Lord; and Mosheh stood and ministered before them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 18:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:13)


וַהֲוָה בְּיוֹמָא דְבָתַר יוֹמָא דְכִפּוּרֵי וִיתֵיב משֶׁה לְמֵידַן יַת עַמָּא וְקָם עַמָּא קֳדָם משֶׁה מִן צַפְרָא עַד רַמְשָׁא


And the day after, the day of reconciliation, Mosheh sat to judge the people: and the people stood before Mosheh from morning till evening.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 18:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:14)


וַחֲמָא חָמוֹי דְמשֶׁה יַת כָּל דְּהוּא טָרַח וְעָבֵיד לְעַמֵּיהּ וַאֲמַר מַה פִּתְגָמָא הָדֵין דְּאַנְתְּ עָבֵיד לְעַמָּא מַה דֵּין אַנְתְּ יָתֵב לְבִלְחוֹדָךְ לְמִידוּן וְכָל עַמָּא קַיְימִין קֳדָמָךְ מִן צַפְרָא עַד רַמְשָׁא


And the father-in-law of Mosheh saw how much he toiled and laboured for his people; and he said, What thing is this that thou art doing to the people? Why dost thou sit alone to judge, and all the people stand before thee from morning until evening?


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 18:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:15)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְחָמוֹי אֲרוּם אַתְיָין לְוָותִי עַמָא לְמִתְבּוֹעַ אוּלְפַן מִן קֳדָם יְיָ


And Mosheh said to his father-in-law, Because the people come to me to inquire for instruction from before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 18:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:16)


אֲרוּם יְהֵי לְהוֹן דִּינָא אָתָאָן לְוָותִי וְדַיֵּינָא בֵּין גַּבְרָא וּבֵין חַבְרֵיהּ וּמְהוֹדַעְנָא לְהוֹן יַת קְיָמַיָא דַיְיָ וְיַת אוֹרַיְיתֵיהּ


When they have a matter for judgment, they come to me, and I judge between a man and his fellow, and make them to know the statutes and the law of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 18:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:17)


וַאֲמַר חָמוּי דְמשֶׁה לֵיהּ לָא תַקִין פִּתְגָמָא דְאַנְתְּ עָבִיד


And the father-in-law of Mosheh said to him, This thing that thou art doing is not well ordered;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 18:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:18)


מִיתַר תִּינְתַר אוּף אַנְתְּ אוּף אַהֲרן וּבְנוֹי וְסָבַיָא דְעִמָּךְ אֲרוּם יַקִּיר מִינָךְ פִּתְגָּמָא לָא תֵיכוֹל לְמֶעְבְּדֵיהּ בִּלְחוֹדָךְ


thou wilt verily wear thyself away. Aharon also, and his sons, and the elders of thy people, because the thing is heavier than thou art, able to do by thyseIf, (should take part in it.)


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 18:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:19)


כְּדוֹן קַבֵּיל מִינִי אִימְלְכִינָךְ וִיהֵי מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ בְּסַעֲדָךְ הֲוֵי אַנְתְּ לְעַמָּא תְּבַע אוּלְפַן מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וְתַיְתֵי אַנְתְּ יַת פִּתְגָמָא דִילְהוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ


Now hearken to me and I will advise thee; and may the Word of the Lord be thy helper! When thou art with the people who seek instruction from before the Lord, thou shouldst take their affair before the Lord,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 18:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:20)


וְתַזְהַר יַתְהוֹן יַת קְיָימַיָא וְיַת אוֹרַיְיתָא וּתְהוֹדַע לְהוֹן יַת צְלוֹתָא דְיֵיצְלוּן בְּבֵית כְּנִשְׁתְּהוֹן וְיַת אוֹרְחָא דְּיַבְקְרוּן לְמָרִיעִין וּדְיַהֲכוּן לְמִיקְבּוֹר מֵתַיָא וּלְמִגְמוֹל בָּהּ חִיסְדָא וְיַת עוּבְדָא וְשׁוּרַת דִּינָא וּדְיַעַבְדוּן מִלְגַו לְשׁוּרָתָא לְרַשִׁיעַיָין


and give them counsel about the statutes and laws, make them understand the prayer they are to offer in the house of congregation, the manner of visiting the sick, of burying the dead, of being fruitful In doing good, and in the work and process of justice, and how to conduct themselves among the wicked.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 18:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:21)


וְאַנְתְּ בְּרוֹר מִכָּל עַמָא גִיבְרֵי חֵילָא דַחֲלַיָא דַיְיָ גּוּבְרִין דִּקְשׁוֹט דְּסָנַן לְקַבְּלָא מָמוֹן שִׁיקְרָא וּתְמַנֵי עֲלֵיהוֹן רַבָּנֵי אַלְפִין רַבָּנֵי מַאֲוָותָא רַבָּנֵי חוּמְשִׁין רַבָּנֵי עִשּׂוּרְיָיתָא


But thou shouldst elect from all the people men of ability who fear the Lord, upright men who hate to receive the mammon of dishonesty, and superappoint them to be heads of thousands, of hundreds, of fifties, and of tens.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 18:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:22)


וִידִינוּן יַת עַמָּא בְּכָל עִידַן וִיהֵי כָּל פִּתְגַּם רַב יֵיתוּן לְוָותָךְ וְכָל פִּתְגַּם קָלִיל יִדוֹנוּן הִינוּן וִיקִילוּן מִן מְטוֹל דְּעָלָךְ וִיסוֹבְרוּן עִמָךְ


And let them judge the people at all times, and every great matter bring to thee, but every little thing let them judge themselves, that they may lighten the burden that is upon thee, and bear it with thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 18:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:23)


אִין יַת פִּתְגָמָא הָדֵין תַּעֲבֵיד דְּתֵיהֵי פָנֵי מִן דִּינָא וְיַפְקְדִינָךְ יְיָ פְּקוּדַיָא וְתֵיכוּל לְמֵיקָם לְמִשְׁמָעֵהוֹן וְאוּף אַהֲרן וּבְנוֹי וְכָל סָבַיָא דְעַמָא הָדֵין עַל אֲתַר בֵּי דִינְהוֹן יֵיתוּן בִּשְׁלָם


If thou wilt do this, and exempt thyself from judging (every case) as the Lord shall give thee instruction, thou wilt be able to continue to hear them; and Aharon also and his sons, and all the elders of this people, will resort to the place of Judgment in peace.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 18:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:24)


וְקַבֵּל משֶׁה לְמֵימָר חָמוֹי וְעָבַד כָּל דְּאָמַר


And Mosheh hearkened to his father-in-law, and did all that he had said.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 18:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:25)


וּבְחַר משֶׁה גִיבָּרֵי חֵילָא מִכָּל יִשְרָאֵל וּמַנֵי יַתְהוֹן רֵישִׁין עַל עַמָא רַבָּנֵי אַלְפִין שִׁית מְאָה רַבָּנֵי מַאֲוָותָא שִׁית אַלְפִין רַבָּנֵי חוּמְשִׁין תְּרֵיסַר אַלְפִין וְרַבָּנֵי עִישׁוֹרְיָיתָא שִׁית רִבְוָון


And Mosheh selected able men from all Israel, and appointed them chief, over the people, rabbans of thousands, six hundred; rabbans of hundreds, six thousand; rabbans of fifties, twelve thousand; and rabbans of tens, six Myriads.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 18:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:26)


וַהֲווֹן דַּיְינִין יַת עַמָא בְּכָל עִידַן יַת פִּתְגַם קְשֵׁי מַיְיתוּן לְוַת משֶׁה וְכָל פִּתְגַם קָלִיל דַּיְינִין הִינוּן


And they judged the people at all times; a bard case they, brought to Mosheh; but every light matter they judged it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 18:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/18:27)


וּפְטַר משֶׁה יַת חָמוֹי וְאָזַל לֵיהּ לְגַיְירָא כָּל בְּנֵי אַרְעֵיהּ


And Mosheh parted from his father-in-law, and he went, and himself made proselytes of all the children of his land.



## Chapter 19

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:1)


בְּיַרְחָא תְּלִיתָאָה לְאַפְקוּת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם בְּיוֹמָא הָדֵין בְּחַד לְיַרְחָא אָתוּ לְמַדְבְּרָא דְסִינָי


In the third month of the Exodus of the sons of Israel from the land of Mizraim, on that day, the first of the month, came they to the desert;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:2)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵרְפִידִים וְאָתוֹ לְמַדְבְּרָא דְסִינַי וּשְׁרוֹ בְּמַדְבְּרָא וּשְׁרָא תַּמָן יִשְרָאֵל בְּלֵב מְיַיחֵד כָּל קְבֵיל טַוְורָא


for they had journeyed from Rephidim, and had come to the desert of Sinai and Israel encamped there in the desert, of one heart, nigh to the mountain.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:3)


וּמשֶׁה סְלִיק בְּיוֹמָא תִנְיָנָא לְרֵישׁ טַוְורָא וּקְרָא לֵיהּ יְיָ מִן טַוְורָא לְמֵימָר כִּדְנָא תֵימַר לִנְשַׁיָא דְבֵית יַעֲקב וְתַתְנֵי לְבֵית יִשְרָאֵל


And Mosheh on the second day went up to the summit of the mount; and the Lord called to him from the mount, saying, This shalt thou speak to the [women] of the house of Jakob, and instruct the house of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:4)


אַתּוּן חֲמִיתוּן מַה דִּי עֲבָדִית לְמִצְרָאֵי וּטְעָנִית יַתְכוֹן עַל עֲנָנִין הֵי כְּעַל גַּדְפֵי נִשְׁרִין מִן פִּילוּסִין וְאוֹבֵילִית יַתְכוֹן לְאַתַר בֵּית מוּקְדָּשָׁא לְמֶעֱבַד תַּמָּן פִּסְחָא וּבְהַהוּא לֵילְיָא אֲתֵיבִית יַתְכוֹן לְפִילוּסִין וּמִתַּמָּן קְרִיבִית יַתְכוֹן לְאוּלְפַן אוֹרַיְיתִי


Ye have seen what I did to the Mizraee; and how I bare you upon the clouds as upon eagles' wings from Pelusin, to take you to the place of the sanctuary, there to solemnize the Pascha; and in the same night brought you back to Pelusin, and from thence have brought you nigh, to (receive) the doctrine of My law.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:5)


וּכְדוּן אִין קַבָּלָא תְּקַבְּלוּן לְמֵימְרִי וְתִנְטְרוּן יַת קְיָימַי וּתְהוֹן קֳדָמַי חֲבִיבִין מִכָּל עַמְמַיָא דְעַל אַפֵּי אַרְעָא


And now, if you will truly hearken to My Word and keep My covenant, you shall be more beloved before Me than all the peoples on the face of the earth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:6)


וְאַתּוּן תְּהוֹן קֳדָמַי מַלְכִין קְטִירֵי כְּלִילָא וְכַהֲנִין מְשַׁמְשִׁין וְעַם קַדִּישׁ אִלֵין פִּתְגָמַיָא דִּתְמַלֵּיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


And before Me you shall be crowned kings, and sanctified priests, and a holy people. These are the words thou shalt speak to the sons of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:7)


וְאָתָא משֶׁה בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא וּקְרָא לְסָבֵי עַמָא וּסְדַר קֳדָמֵיהוֹן יַת כָּל פִּתְגָמַיָא הָאִלֵּין דְּפַקְדֵיהּ יְיָ


And Mosheh came that day, and called the elders of the people, and set in order before them all these words which the Lord had commanded.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:8)


וְאָתִיבוּ כָּל עַמָּא כַּחֲדָא וַאֲמָרוּ כָּל דְּמַלֵּיל יְיָ נַעֲבֵיד וְאָתֵיב משֶׁה יַת פִּתְגָמֵי עַמָּא קֳדָם יְיָ


And all the people responded together, and said, All that the Lord hath spoken we will do. And Mosheh carried back the words of the people before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:9)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה בְּיוֹמָא תְּלִיתָאָה הָא אֲנָא מִתְגְּלֵי עֲלָךְ בְּעֵיבָא דַּעֲנַן יְקָרָא מִן בִּגְלַל דְּיִשְׁמְעוּן עַמָּא בְּמַלָּלוּתִי עִמָּךְ וְאוּף בָּךְ יְהֵימְנוּן לְעָלַם וְתַנִי משֶׁה יַת פִּתְגָּמֵי עַמָּא קֳדָם יְיָ


And the Lord said to Mosheh, Behold, on the third day I will reveal Myself to thee in the depth of the cloud of glory, that the people may hear while I speak with thee, and may believe in thee forever. And Mosheh delivered the words of the people before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:10)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה בְּיוֹמָא רְבִיעַאָה אִיזֵיל לְוַת עַמָּא וְתִזְמְנִינוּן יוֹמָא דֵין וְיוֹם מָחְרָא וִיחַוְורוּן לְבוּשֵׁיהוֹן


And the Lord said to Mosheh on the fourth day, Go unto the people, and prepare them today and tomorrow; let them wash their raiment,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:11)


וִיהוֹן זְמִינִין לְיוֹמָא תְּלִיתָאָה אֲרוּם בְּיוֹמָא תְּלִיתָאָה יִתְגְּלֵי יְיָ לְעֵינֵי כָל עַמָּא עַל טוּרָא דְסִינָי


and be prepared On the third day; for on the third day the Lord will reveal Himself to the eyes of all the people, upon the Mount of Sinai.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:12)


וְתַתְחֵם יַת עַמָּא וִיקוּמוּן חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר לְטוּרָא לְמֵימָר הֲווֹ זְהִירִין מִלְמֵיסַק בְּהַר וּלְמִקְרַב בְּסַיְיפֵיהּ כָּל דְּיִקְרַב בְּטַוְורָא אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵיל


And thou shalt set limits for the people that they may stand round about the mountain, and shalt say, Beware that you ascend not the mount, nor come near its confines; whoever cometh nigh the mount will be surely put to death.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:13)


לָא תִקְרַב בֵּיהּ יְדָא אֲרוּם יִתְרְגָמָא יִתְרְגֵם בְּאַבְנָא בַרְדָא אוֹ גִירִין דְּאֵשָׁא יִדְרְקוּן בֵּיהּ אִין בְּעִירָא אִין אֵינָשָׁא לָא יִתְקַיֵּים בְּרַם בְּמֵיגַד קַל שׁוֹפְרָא הִינוּן מַרְשַׁן לְמֵיסַק בְּטַוְורָא


Touch it not with the hand; for he will be stoned with hailstone, or be pierced with arrows of fire; whether beast or man, he will not live. But when the voice of the trumpet is heard, they may go up (forwards) towards the mount.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:14)


וּנְחַת משֶׁה בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא לְוַת עַמָּא וְזַמִּין יַת עַמָּא וְחַוָּרוּ לְבוּשֵׁיהוֹן


And Mosheh went down that day to the people, and prepared the people, and they blanched their clothes.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:15)


וַאֲמַר לְעַמָּא הֲווֹ זְמִינִין לִתְלָתֵי יוֹמִין לָא תִקְרְבוּן לְתַשְׁמִישׁ דְעָרִיס


And he said to the people, Be ready for the third day; abstain from the marriage-bed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:16)


וַהֲוָה בְּיוֹמָא תְּלִיתָאָה בְּשִׁיתָּא בְּיַרְחָא בְּעִידוֹנֵי צַפְרָא וַהֲוָה קָלִין דִּרְעֵים וּבְרָקִים וַעֲנָנָא תַּקִּיף קְטִיר עַל טַוְורָא וְקַל שׁוֹפָרָא תַקִּיף לַחֲדָא וְזַע כָּל עַמָּא דִי בְּמַשְׁרִיתָא


And it was on the third day, on the sixth of the month, in the time of the morning, that on the mountain there were voices of thunders, and lightnings, and mighty clouds of smoke, and a voice of a trumpet exceeding loud; and all the people in the camp trembled.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:17)


וְאַנְפֵּיק משֶׁה יַת עַמָּא לִקְדָמוּת שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ מִן מַשְׁרִיתָא וּמִן יַד תָּלִישׁ מָארֵי עַלְמָא יַת טַוְורָא וְזַקְפֵיהּ בְּאַוִירָא וַהֲוָה זַיֵיג הֵי כְּאִסְפַּקְלַרְיָא וְאִתְעַתָּדוּ תְּחוֹתֵי טַוְורָא


And Mosheh brought forth the people from the camp to meet the glorious Presence of the Lord; and suddenly the Lord of the world uprooted the mountain, and lifted it in the air, and it became luminous as a beacon, and they stood beneath the mountain.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:18)


וְטַוְורָא דְסִינַי תָנֵין כּוּלֵיהּ מִן בִּגְלַל דְּאַרְכִין לֵיהּ שְׁמַיָא וְאִתְגְּלֵי עֲלוֹי בְּאֵישָׁא מְצַלְהֲבָא וְסָלֵיק קוּטְרֵיהּ הֵי כְקוּטְרָא דְאַתּוּנָא וְזַע כָּל טַוְורָא לַחֲדָא


And all the mount of Sinai was in flame; for the heavens had overspread it, and He was revealed over it in flaming fire, and the smoke went up as the smoke of a furnace, and all the mountain quaked greatly.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:19)


וְהַוָּה קַל שׁוֹפָרָא אָזִיל וְתַקִּיף לַחֲדָא משֶׁה הֲוָה מְמַלֵּיל וּמִן קֳדָם יְיָ הֲוָה מִתְעַנֵי בְקַל נָעִים וּמְשַׁבַּח וּנְעִימָתָא מַלְיָא


And the voice of the trumpet went forth, and grew stronger: (then) Mosheh spake, and was answered from before the Lord with a gracious and majestic voice, and with pleasant and gracious words.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:20)


וְאִתְגְּלֵי יְיָ עַל טַוְורָא דְסִינַי עַל רֵישׁ טַוְורָא וּקְרָא יְיָ לְמשֶׁה לְרֵישׁ טַוְורָא וּסְלֵיק משֶׁה


And the Lord revealed Himself on mount Sinai upon the summit of the mountain, and the Lord called unto Mosheh from the summit of the mount, and Mosheh went up.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:21)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה חוּת אַסְהִיד בְּעַמָּא דִּלְמָא יְכַוְּונוּן קֳדָם יְיָ לְאִסְתַּכְּלָא וְיַפִּיל מִנְהוֹן רַב דִּבְהוֹן


And the Lord said to Mosheh, Descend, and warn the people, lest they come directly before the Lord to gaze, and many of them fall.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:22)


וְאוּף כַּהֲנַיָא דִּקְרִיבִין לְשַׁמְשָׁא קֳדָם יְיָ יִתְקַדְּשׁוּן דִּלְמָא יִקְטוֹל בְּהוֹן יְיָ


The priests, also, who approach to minister before the Lord, must be sanctified, lest the Lord destroy them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:23)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה קֳדָם יְיָ לָא יִכְלוּן עַמָּא לְמֵיסַק לְטַוְורָא דְסִינַי אֲרוּם אַנְתְּ אַסְהֵידְתָּא בָּנָא לְמֵימָר תָּחִים יַת טַוְורָא וְקַדְשׁוֹהִי


And Mosheh said before the Lord, The people cannot come up to mount Sinai, because Thou didst instruct us, saying, Make limits to the mount, and sanctify it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:24)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ יְיָ אִיזֵל חוֹת וְתִיסַק אַנְתְּ וְאַהֲרן עִמָּךְ וְכַהֲנַיָא וְעַמָּא לָא יְכַוְונוּן לְמִיסוּק לְמִסְתַּכְּלָא קֳדָם יְיָ דִּילְמָא יִקְטוֹל בְּהוֹן


And the Lord said to him, Go down, and then ascend, thou and Aharon with thee; but let not the, priests or the people directly come up to gaze before the Lord, lest He slay them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 19:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/19:25)


וּנְחַת משֶׁה מִן טַוְורָא לְוַת עַמָּא וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן קְרִיבוּ קַבִּילוּ אוֹרַיְיתָא עִם עֲשַרְתֵּי דִבְּרַיָא


And Mosheh went down from the mountain to the people, and said to them, Draw nigh and hear the Law with Ten Words.



## Chapter 20

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:1)


וּמַלֵּיל יְיָ יַת כָּל דִּבְּרַיָא הָאִילֵין לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake all these words, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:2)


דְּבִירָא קַדְמָאָה כַּד הֲוָה נָפִיק מִן פּוּם קוּדְשָׁא יְהֵי שְׁמֵיהּ מְבָרֵךְ הֵי כְזִיקִין וְהֵי כְבַרְקִין וְהֵי כְשַׁלְהוֹבְיָין לַמְפַּד דִּינוּר מִן יְמִנֵיהּ וְלַמְפַּד דְּאֵישָׁא מִן שְמָאלֵיהּ פָּרַח וְטַיֵּיס בְּאַוִיר שְׁמַיָא וְחָזַר וּמִתְחַמֵּי עַל מַשִּׁירְיַתְהוֹן דְּיִשְרָאֵל וְחָזַר וּמִתְחֲקֵק עַל לוּחֵי קְיָימָא דַהֲווֹן יְהִיבִין בְּכַף יְדוֹי דְמשֶׁה וּמִתְהֲפִיךְ בְּהוֹן מִן סְטַר לִסְטָר וּבְכֵן צָוַוח וְאָמַר עַמִּי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל אֲנָא הוּא אֱלָהָכוֹן דִּי פְּרָקִית וְאַפֵּיקִית יַתְכוֹן פְּרִיקִין מִן אַרְעָא דְּמִצְרָאֵי מִבֵּית שִׁעְבוּד עַבְדַיָּא


The first word, as it came forth from the mouth of the Holy One, whose Name be blessed, was like storms, and lightnings, and flames of fire, with a burning light on His right hand and on His left. It winged its way through the air of the heavens, and was made manifest unto the camp of Israel, and returned, and was engraven on the tables of the covenant that were given by the hand of Mosheh, and were turned in them from side to side: and then called He, and said: Sons of Israel My people, I am the Lord your God, who brought you out free from the land of Mizraim, from the house of the bondage of slaves.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:3)


דְּבִירָא תִּנְיָנָא כַּד הֲוָה נָפִיק מִן פּוּם קוּדְשָׁא יְהֵי שְׁמֵיהּ מְבָרֵךְ הֵי כְּזִיקִין וְהֵי כְבַרְקִין וְהֵי כְּשַׁלְהוֹבִין דִּינוּר לַמְפַּד דִּינוּר מִן יְמִינֵיהּ וְלַמְפַּד דְּאֵישָׁא מִן שְמָאלֵיהּ פָּרַח וְטַיֵּיס בְּאַוִיר שְׁמַיָא חָזַר וּמִתְחַמֵּי עַל מַשִׁירְיַתְהוֹן דְּיִשְרָאֵל וְחָזַר וּמִתְחַקֵּק עַל לוּחֵי קְיָימָא וּמִתְהַפִּיךְ בְּהוֹן מִן סְטַר לִסְטַר וּבְכֵן הֲוָה צָוַוח וְאָמַר עַמִּי בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל לָא יֶהֱוֵי לָךְ אֱלָהָא אוֹחֲרָן בַּר מִינִי


The second word which came forth from the mouth of the Holy One, whose name be blessed, was like storms, and lightnings, and flames of fire. A burning light was on His right hand and on His left and was borne through the air of the heavens, returned, and was made manifest unto the camp of Israel; it returned, and was engraven on the tables of the covenant, and was turned in them from side to side. Then called He, and said, House of Israel, My people, Thou shalt have no other God beside Me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:4)


לָא תַעַבְדוּן לְכוֹן צְלַם וְצוּרָא וְכָל דְּמוֹי דְּבִשְׁמַיָא מִלְּעֵיל וְדִי בְאַרְעָא מִלְּרַע וְדִי בְמַיָא מִלְּרַע לְאַרְעָא


You shall not make to yourselves image or figure, or any similitude of what is in the heavens above, or on the earth beneath, or in the waters under the earth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:5)


לָא תִסְגְּדוּן לְהוֹן וְלָא תִפְלְחוּן קֳדָמֵיהוֹן אֲרוּם אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן אֱלָהּ קַנָאַן וּפוּרְעַן וּמִתְפְּרַע בְּקִינָאָה מַדְכַּר חוֹבֵי אַבְהַת רַשִׁיעַן עַל בְּנִין מְרָדִין עַל דַּר תְּלִיתָאֵי וְעַל דַּר רְבִיעָאֵי לְשׁוֹנְאָי


You shall not bow down to them, or worship before them; for I the Lord your God am a jealous God and an avenger, punishing with vengeance, recording the guilt of wicked fathers upon rebellious children unto the third and unto the fourth generation of them who hate Me;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:6)


וּנְטִיר חֶסֶד וְטִיבוּ לְאַלְפִין דָרִין לְרַחֲמַי צַדִּיקַיָא וּלְנַטְרֵי פִּיקוּדָי וְאוֹרַיְיתָי


but keeping mercy and goodness for thousands of generations of the righteous who love Me, and who keep My commandments and My laws.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:7)


עַמִּי בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל לָא יִשְׁתְּבַּע חַד מִנְכוֹן בְּשׁוּם מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן עַל מַגָּן אֲרוּם לָא מִזַכֵּי יְיָ בְּיוֹם דִּינָא רַבָּא יַת כָּל מַאן דְּמִשְׁתְּבַע בִּשְׁמֵיהּ עַל מַגָּן


My people of the house of Israel, Let no one of you swear by the name of the Word of the Lord your God in vain; for in the day of the great judgment the Lord will not hold guiltless any one who sweareth by His name in vain.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:8)


עַמִּי בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל הֲווֹן דְּכִירִין יוֹמָא דְשַׁבַּתָּא לְמַקְדְּשָׁא יָתֵיהּ


My people of the house of Israel, Remember the day of Shabbatha, to sanctify it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:9)


שִׁיתָּא יוֹמִין תִּפְלְחוּן וְתַעַבְדוּן כָּל עֲבִדְתֵּיכוֹן


Six days you shall labour, and do all your service:


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:10)


וְיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה שַׁבָּת וְנַיַּיח קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן לָא תַעְבְדוּן כָּל עִבִידְתָּא אַתּוּן וּבְנֵיכוֹן וּבְנָתֵיכוֹן וְעַבְדֵיכוֹן וְאַמְהוֹתֵיכוֹן וְגִיּוֹרֵיכוֹן בְּקִירְוֵיכוֹן


but the seventh day is (for) rest and quietude before the Lord your God: you shall not perform any work, you, and your sons, and your daughters, and your servants, and your handmaids, and your sojourners who are in your cities.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:11)


אֲרוּם בְּשִׁתָּא יוֹמִין בָּרָא יְיָ יַת שְׁמַיָא וְיַת אַרְעָא יַת יַמָא וְיַת כָּל מַה דְּאִית בְּהוֹן וְנַח בְּיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה בְּגִין כֵּן בְּרִיךְ יְיָ יַת יוֹמָא דְשַׁבְּתָא וְקַדִּישׁ יָתֵיהּ


For in six days the Lord created the heavens, and the earth, and the sea, and whatever is therein, and rested on the seventh day: therefore the Lord hath blessed the day of Shabbatha and sanctified it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:12)


עַמִּי בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל הֲווֹ זְהִירִין גְּבַר בִּיקָרָא דְאָבוּהִי וּבִיקָרָא דְאִמֵיהּ מִן בִּגְלַל דְּיִסְגוּן יוֹמֵיכוֹן עַל אַרְעָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן יָהִיב לְכוֹן


My people, the house of Israel, Let every man be instructed in the honour of his father and in the honour of his mother: that your days may be multiplied upon the land which the Lord your God giveth you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:13)


עַמִּי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לָא תֶהֱווּן קְטוֹלִין לָא חַבְרִין וְלָא שׁוּתְּפִין עִם קְטוֹלִין וְלָא יִתְחָמֵי בִּכְנִשְׁתְּהוֹן דְּיִשְרָאֵל עִם קְטוֹלִין וְלָא יְקוּמוּן בְּנֵיכוֹן מִן בַּתְרֵיכוֹן וְיִלְפוּן לְחוֹד הִינוּן לְמֶהֱוֵי עִם קְטוֹלִין אֲרוּם בְּחוֹבֵי קְטוֹלַיָא חַרְבָּא נָפִיק עַל עַלְמָא: לא תנאף עַמִּי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לָא תֶהֱווּן גִּיּוּרִין לָא חַבְרִין וְלָא שׁוּתָּפִין עִם גִּיּוּרִין וְלָא יִתְחָמֵי בִּכְנִשְׁתְּהוֹן דְּיִשְרָאֵל עִם גִּיּוּרִין דְּלָא יְקוּמוּן בְּנֵיכוֹן מִן בַּתְרֵיכוֹן וְיִלְפוּן לְחוֹד הִינוּן לְמֶהֱוֵי עִם גִּיּוּרִין אֲרוּם בְּחוֹבֵי גִיּוּרָא מוֹתָא נָפִיק עַל עַלְמָא: לא תגנוב עַמִּי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לָא תֶהֱווּן גַּנָּבִין לָא חַבְרִין וְלָא שׁוּתָּפִין עִם גַּנָּבִין וְלָא יִתְחָמֵי בִּכְנִשְׁתְּהוֹן דְּיִשְרָאֵל עִם גַּנָּבִין דְּלָא יְקוּמוּן בְּנֵיכוֹן מִן בַּתְרֵיכוֹן וְיִלְפוּן לְחוֹד הִינוּן לְמֶהֱוֵי עִם גַּנָּבִין אֲרוּם בְּחוֹבֵי גַנָבַיָא כַּפְנָא נָפִיק עַל עַלְמָא: לא תענה עַמִּי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לָא תֶהֱווּן מְסַהֲדִין בְּחַבְרֵיכוֹן סַהֲדֵי שִׁיקְרָא לָא חַבְרִין וְלָא שׁוּתָּפִין עִם מְסַהְדִין סַהֲדֵי שִׁקְרָא וְלָא יִתְחָמֵי בִּכְנִשְׁתְּהוֹן דְּיִשְרָאֵל עִם מְסַהֲדִין סַהֲדֵי שִׁיקְרָא דְלָא יְקוּמוּן בְּנֵיכוֹן בַּתְרֵיכוֹן וְיִלְפוּן לְחוֹד הִינוּן לְמֶהֱוֵי עִם מְסַהֲדִין סַהֲדֵי שִׁיקְרָא אֲרוּם בְּחוֹבֵי סַהֲדֵי שִׁיקְרָא עֲנָנָן סַלְקִין וּמִטְרָא לָא נָחִית וּבְצוֹרָתָא אַתְיָא עַל עַלְמָא


My people, the sons of Israel, You. shall not be murderers; you shall not be companions of or partakers with murderers: in the congregations of Israel there shall not be seen a murderous people; neither shall your sons rise up after you and teach one another to take part with murderers: for on account of the guilt of murder the sword cometh forth upon the world: My people of the house of Israel, Be ye not adulterers, nor companions nor partakers with adulterers: nor in the congregations of Israel shall there be seen an adulterous people, that your sons may not arise after you to teach one another to have part with adulterers: for through the guilt of adultery death cometh forth upon the world: Sons of Israel My people, Ye shall not be thieves, nor companions nor partakers with thieves: there shall not be seen in the congregations of Israel a thievish people; that your sons may not arise after you to teach one another to have part with thieves: for on account of the guilt of theft famine cometh forth upon the world: Sons of Israel My people, Ye shall not testify against your neighbours a testimony of falsehood, nor be companions or partakers with those who bear false witness nor shall there be seen in the congregations of Israel a people who testify a testimony of falsehood; neither shall your sons arise after you to teach one another to have part with those who testify falsehood: for because of the guilt of false testimony the clouds go up and the rain cometh not down, and dryness cometh upon the world.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:14)


עַמִּי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לָא תֶהֱווּן חֲמִידִין לָא חַבְרִין וְלָא שׁוּתָּפִין עִם חֲמִידִין וְלָא יִתְחֲמוּן בִּכְנִשְׁתְּהוֹן דְּיִשְרָאֵל עִם חֲמוּדִין דְּלָא יְקוּמוּן בְּנֵיכוֹן מִן בַּתְרֵיכוֹן וְיִלְפוּן לְחוֹד הִינוּן לְמֶהֱוֵי עִם חֲמוּדִין וְלָא יַחְמִיד חַד מִנְכוֹן יַת אִנְתְּתֵיהּ דְּחַבְרֵיהּ וְלָא לְעַבְדֵיהּ וְלָא לְאַמְתֵיהּ וְלָא לְתוֹרֵיהּ וְלָא לְחַמְרֵיהּ וְלָא לְכָל מַאן דְּאִית לְחַבְרֵיהּ אֲרוּם בְחוֹבֵי חִמּוּדַיָא מַלְכוּתָא מִתְגַרְיָא בְּנִכְסֵיהוֹן דִּבְנֵי נְשָׁא וּלְמֵיסַב יַתְהוֹן וְעַתִּירֵי נִכְסִין מִתְמַסְכְּנִין וְגָלוּתָא אַתְיָא עַל עַלְמָא


Sons of Israel My people, Ye shall not be covetous companions or partakers with the covetous: nor shall there be seen in the congregations of Israel a covetous people; that your sons may not arise after you to teach one another to have part with the covetous: neither shall any among you covet the wife of his neighbour, nor his servant, nor his handmaid, nor his ox, nor his ass nor anything that belongeth to his neighbour; because through the guilt of covetousness the government breaketh in upon the possessions of men to take them, and the wealthy are made poor, and slavery cometh upon the world.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:15)


וְכָל עַמָּא חַמְיַין יַת קָלַיָיא הֵיךְ הֲווֹ מִתְהַפְּכִין בְּשָׁמְעֲהוֹן דְּכָל חַד וְחַד וְהֵיךְ הֲווֹ נַפְקִין מִן גּוֹ בְּעוּרַיָא וְיַת קַל שׁוֹפְרָא הֵיךְ הֲוָה מְחֵי מֵיתַיָא וְיַת טוּרָא תָּנֵין וְחָמוּן כָּל עַמָּא וּרְתָעַן וְקָמוּ תְּרֵיסַר מִילִין מֵרָחִיק


And all the people saw the thunders, and were turned back, every one as he heard them coming forth from the midst of the lights, and the voice of the trumpet as it will raise the dead, and the mountain smoking; and all the people saw and drew back, and stood twelve miles off.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:16)


וַאֲמָרוּ לְמשֶׁה מַלֵּיל אַנְתְּ עִמָּנָא וּנְקַבֵּיל וְלָא יִתְמַלֵּל עִמָּנָא תּוּב מִן קֳדָם יְיָ דִלְמָא נְמוּת


And they said to Mosheh, Speak thou with us, and we can hear; but let it not be spoken with us any more from before the Lord, lest we die.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:17)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְעַמָּא לָא תְדַחֲלוּן אֲרוּם מִן בִּגְלַל לְנַסָּיוּתְכוֹן אִתְגְּלֵי לְכוֹן יְקָרָא דַיְיָ וּמִן בִּגְלַל דְּתֶהֱוֵי דַחַלְתֵּיהּ עַל אַפֵּיכוֹן בְּגִין דְּלָא תְחוּבוּן


And Mosheh said to the people, Fear not; for the glory of the Lord is revealed to try you, whether His fear is before your faces, that ye may not sin.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:18)


וְקָם עַמָּא תְּרֵיסַר מִילִין מֵרָחִיק וּמשֶׁה קָרֵיב לְצֵית אַמִיטְתָא דְתַּמָּן יְקַר שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ


And the people stood twelve miles off; but Mosheh drew near to the height of the darkness where was the glory of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:19)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה כִּדְנָא תֵימַר לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל אַתּוּן חֲמִיתּוּן אֲרוּם מִן שְׁמַיָא מַלֵּילִית עִמְכוֹן


And the Lord said to Mosheh, Speak thus to the sons of Israel: You have seen that from the heavens I have spoken with you;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:20)


עַמִּי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לָא תַעַבְדוּן לְמִסְגוֹד דְּמוּת שִׁמְשָׁא וְסִיהֲרָא וְכוֹכְבַיָא וּמַזְלַיָא וּמַלְאָכַיָא דִמְשַׁמְשִׁין קֳדָמוֹי דַּחֲלָן דִּכְסַף וְדַחֲלָן דִּדְהַב לָא תַעַבְדוּן לְכוֹן


sons of Israel, My people, you shall not make, that you may worship, the likeness of the sun or the moon or the stars, or the planets, or the angels who minister before Me; idols of silver, nor idols of gold, ye shall not make to you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:21)


מַדְבַּח אַדְמְתָא תַעֲבֵיד לִשְׁמִי וּתְהֵי דַבַּח עֲלוֹי יַת עַלְוָותָךְ וְיַת נִכְסַת קוּדְשָׁךְ מִן עָנָךְ וּמִן תּוֹרָךְ בְּכָל אַתְרָא דְאַשְׁרֵי שְׁכִינְתִּי וְאַנְתְּ פְּלַח קֳדָמַי תַּמָּן אֲשַׁלַח עֲלָךְ בִּרְכָתִי וְאֵיבָרֵיכִינָךְ


An altar of earth ye shall make to My Name, and sacrifice upon it thy burnt offerings and thy sanctified oblations from thy sheep and from thy oxen. And in every place where My Shekinah shall dwell, and thou worship before Me, there will I send My blessing upon thee, and will bless thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:22)


וְאִם מַדְבַּח אַבְנִין תַּעֲבֵיד לִשְׁמִי לָא תִבְנֵי יַתְהוֹן חֲצִיבִין דְּאִין אֲרֵימַת פַּרְזְלָא דְמִנֵיהּ מִתְעֲבֵיד סְיָיפָא עַל אַבְנָא אַפִיסְתָּא יָתָהּ


But if thou wilt make an altar of stones unto My Name, thou shalt not build them sculptured; for if thou lift up iron, from which the sword is made, upon the stone, thou wilt profane it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 20:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/20:23)


וְאַתּוּן כַּהֲנַיָא דְקַיְימִין לְמִשַׁמְשָׁא קֳדָמַי לָא תִסְקוּן בְּמַסוּקְיָין עַל מַדְבְּחִי אֱלָהֵן בְּגִשְׁרַיָא דְלָא תִּתְחַמֵי עִירְיָתָךְ עֲלוֹי


And you, the priests, who stand to minister before Me, shall not ascend to My altar by steps, but by (sloping) bridges; that thy shame may not be seen thereupon.



## Chapter 21

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:1)


ואלה וְאִלֵּין הִינוּן סִדְרֵי דִינַיָא דִי תְסַדֵּר קֳדָמֵיהוֹן


AND these are the orders of judgments which thou shalt order before them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:2)


אֲרוּם תִּיזְבוּן בִּגְנֵיבוּתֵיהּ לְעַבְדָא בַּר יִשְרָאֵל שִׁית שְׁנִין יִפְלַח וּבְמַעֲלֵי שְׁבִיעָתָא יִפּוֹק לְבַר חוֹרִין מַגָּן


If thou shalt have bought a son of Israel, on account of his theft, six years he shall serve, and at the incoming of the seventh he shall go out free without price.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:3)


אִין בִּלְחוֹדוֹי יֵיעוֹל בִּלְחוֹדוֹי יִפּוֹק וְאִין מָרֵי אִינְתְּתָא בַּת יִשְרָאֵל הוּא וְתִיפּוֹק אִינְתְּתֵיהּ עִמֵּיהּ


If he came in alone, he shall go out alone: but if (he be) the husband of a wife, a daughter of Israel, his wife shall go out with him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:4)


אִין רִבּוֹנֵיהּ יִתֵּן לֵיהּ אִתְּתָא אַמְתָא וְתֵילִיד לֵיהּ בְּנִין אוֹ בְּנָן אִתְּתָא וּבְנָהָא תְּהֵא לְרִבּוֹנָהּ וְהוּא יִפּוֹק בִּלְחוֹדוֹי


If his master give him a wife, an handmaid, and she bear him sons or daughters, the wife and her children shall belong to his master, and he may go out alone.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:5)


וְאִין יַתְנֵי וְיֵימַר עַבְדָא רְחֵימְנָא יַת רִבּוֹנִי יַת אִנְתְּתִי וְיַת בְּנַי לָא אֵיפּוֹק לְבַר חוֹרִין


But if the servant shall affirm and say, I love my master, my wife, and my children, (and) I will not go out free,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:6)


וִיקַרְבִינֵיהּ רִבּוֹנֵיהּ לִקְדָם דַּיָּינַיָא וְיִסַּב מִנְהוֹן רְשׁוּתָא וִיקַרְבִינֵיהּ לְוַת דָּשָׁא דְּלוּחַ מְזוּזְתָא וִיחַיֵּיט רִבּוֹנֵיהּ יַת אוּדְנֵיהּ יַמִּינָא בְּמַחְטָא וִיהֵי לֵיהּ עָבַד פְּלַח עַד יוּבְלָא


then his master shall bring him before the judges, and shall receive from them the power, and bring him to the door that hath posts; and his master shall pierce his right ear with an awl; and he shall be a servant to serve him until the jubela.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:7)


וַאֲרוּם יִזְבּוֹן גְּבַר בַּר יִשְרָאֵל יַת בְּרַתֵּיהּ זְעֵירְתָּא לְאַמְהוּ לָא תִיפּוֹק כְּמַפְקָנוּת עַבְדַיָא כְּנַעֲנָאֵי דְּמִשְׁתַּחְרְרִין בְּשִׁינָא וְעֵינָא אֱלָהֵן בִּשְׁנִין דִּשְׁמִיטָתָא וּבְסִימָנַיָא וּבְיוּבְלָא וּבְמוֹתַת רִבּוֹנָהָא וּפוּרְעַן כַּסְפָּא


And if a man of Israel sell his daughter, a little handmaid, she shall not go forth according to the going forth of the servants of the Kenaanaee, who are set at liberty on account of the tooth or the eye; but in the years of remission, and with tokens, and at the jubela, and on the death of her master, and by redemption with money.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:8)


אִין לָא אַשְׁכַּחַת חִינָא קֳדָם רִבּוֹנָהָא דְּזַבִּין יָתָהּ וְיִפְרוֹק יָתָהּ אָבוּהָא לִגְבַר אוֹחֲרָן לֵית לֵיהּ רְשׁוּ לִזְבּוֹנָהּ חֲלַף דְּמַנִי מָרָהּ רְשׁוּתֵיהּ עֲלָהּ


If she hath not found favour before her master who bought her, then her father may redeem her; but to a foreigner he shall not have power to sell her; for as a vessel of her Lord he hath power over her.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:9)


וְאִין לְצֵיד בְּרֵיהּ זַמִּין יָתָהּ כְּהִלְכַת בְּנַת יִשְרָאֵל יַעֲבִיד לָהּ


And if he had intended her for the side of his son, he shall do by her after the manner of the daughters of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:10)


אִין אוֹחֲרַנְתָּא בַּת יִשְרָאֵל יִסַב לֵיהּ עֲלָהּ מְזוֹנָהּ וְתַכְשִׁיטָהּ וּמְעַיֵּל לָהּ לָא יִמְנַע לָהּ


If he take another daughter of Israel to him beside her, her food, her adorning, and her conjugal rights, he shall not withhold from her.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:11)


אִין תְּלַת מִילַיָיא הָאִלֵּין לָא יַעֲבֵיד לָהּ לִמְזַמְנָא יָתָהּ לֵיהּ אוֹ לִבְרֵיהּ אוֹ לְמִפְרְקָא לְבַר אָבוּהָא וְתִיפּוֹק מַגָּן בְּלָא כְסַף בְּרַם גֵּט פְּטוּרִין יָהִיב לָהּ


And if these three things he doth not for her, to covenant her to himself, or to his son, or to release her into the hand of her father, she shall go free without payment, and a writing of release he shall give her.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:12)


דְּיִמְחֵי לְבַר יִשְרָאֵל אוֹ לְבַת יִשְרָאֵל וְיִקְטְלִינֵיהּ אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵיל בִּסְיָיפָא


Whosoever smiteth a son or a daughter of Israel, so as to cause death, shall be put to death with the sword.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:13)


וְדִי לָא אִיזְדַוֵּיג לֵיהּ וּמִן קֳדָם יְיָ אֲרַע עִיקְתֵיהּ לִידוֹי וַאֲזַמֵן לָךְ אֲתַר דְּיַעֲרוֹק לְתַמָּן


But he who did not attack him, but mischance from before the Lord befell him at his hand, I will appoint thee a place where he may flee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:14)


וַאֲרוּם יִרְשַׁע גְּבַר עַל חַבְרֵיהּ לְמִקְטְלֵיהּ בִּנְכִילוּ אֲפִילּוּ כַּהֲנָא הוּא וּמְשַׁמֵּשׁ עַל גַּבֵּי מַדְבְּחִי מִתַּמָּן תִּסְבוּנֵיהּ וְתִקְטְלִינֵיהּ בְּסַיָּיפָא


But if a man come maliciously upon his neighbour to kill him with craft, though the priests are ministering at My altar, thence thou shalt take him, and slay him with the sword.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:15)


וּדְחַבֵּל בְּאָבוֹי וּבְאִימֵיהּ אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵיל בְּשִׁינוּקָא דְסוּדְרָא


And he who woundeth his father or his mother shall die by strangling.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:16)


וּדְיִגְנוֹב נֶפֶשׁ מִבְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וִיזַבְּנִינֵיהּ וְיִשְׁתַּכַּח בִּרְשׁוּתֵיהּ אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵיל בְּשִׁינוּקָא דְסוּדְרָא


And he who stealeth a soul of the children of Israel, and selleth him, or if he be found in his possession, shall die by strangling.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:17)


וּדְיֵילוּט לְאָבוּי וּלְאִימֵיהּ בִּשְׁמָא מְפַרְשָׁא אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵיל בְּאַטְלוּת אַבְנִין


And he who curseth his father or his mother by the Great Name, dying he shall die by being stoned with stones.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:18)


וַאֲרוּם יִנְצוּן גּוּבְרִין וְיִמְחוּן חַד לְחַבְרֵיהּ בְּאַבְנָא אוֹ בְּמַרְתּוֹקָא (נ"א מוֹרְנִיתָא) וְלָא יְמוּת וְיִפּוֹל לְמֵירַע


And when men strive together, and one smite his neighbour with a stone, or with his fist, so that he die not, but fall ill,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:19)


אִין יְקוּם מִמַּרְעֵיהּ וּמְהֲלֵךְ בְּשׁוּקָא עַל מוֹרְנִיתֵיהּ וִיהֵי זַכְיַי מַחְיָיא מִדִּין קְטוֹל לְחוֹד בּוּטְלִין עִיבִידְתֵּיהּ וְצַעֲרֵיהּ וְנִזְקֵיהּ וּבַהֲתֵיהּ יִתֵּן וַאֲגַר אַסְיָא יְשַׁלֵּם עַד דְּמִתְּסֵי


if he rise again from his illness, and walk in the street upon his staff, he who smote him shall be acquitted from the penalty of death; only for his cessation from labour, his affliction, his injury, his disgrace, and the hire of the physician, he shall make good until he be cured.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:20)


וַאֲרוּם יִמְחֵי גְבַר יַת עַבְדֵיהּ כְּנַעֲנָאָה אוֹ יַת אַמְתֵיהּ כְּנַעֲנִיתָא בְּשַׁרְבִיטָא וִימוּת בְּהַהוּא יוֹמָא תְּחוֹת יְדֵיהּ יִתְדְּנָא יִתְדַן דִּין קְטִילַת סַיְיפָא


And when a man hath smitten his Kenaanite man-servant or maid-servant with a staff, and he die the same day under his hand, he shall be judged with the judgment of death by the sword.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:21)


בְּרַם אִין יוֹמָא חַד מֵעִידַן לְעִידַן אוֹ תְּרֵין יוֹמִין קְטִיעִין יִתְקַיֵּים לָא יִתְדַן מְטוֹל דִּכְסַף זְבִינֵיהּ הוּא


But if the wounded person continue one or two days from time to time, he shall not be (so) judged; because with money he had bought him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:22)


וַאֲרוּם יִנְצוּן גַּבְרִין וְיִמְחוּן אִתְּתָא מְעַבְּרָא וְאַפִּילַת יַת וַולְדָהָא וְלָא יְהֵי בָהּ מוֹתָא מִתְקַנְסָא יִתְקְנֵיס דְּמֵי וַולְדָא הֵיכְמָא דִמְשַׁוֵי עֲלֵיהּ בַּעֲלֵיהּ דְּאִינְתְּתָא וְיִתֵּן עַל מֵימַר דַּיָינַיָא


If men when striving strike a woman with child, and cause her to miscarry, but not to lose her life, the fine on account of the infant which the husband of the woman shall lay upon him, he shall pay according to the sentence of the judges.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:23)


וְאִין מוֹתָא יְהֵי בָהּ וּתְדִינוּן נַפְשָׁא דִקְטוֹלָא חוֹלַף נַפְשָׁא דְאִתְּתָא


But if death befall her, then thou shalt judge the life of the killer for the life of the woman.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:24)


דְּמֵי עֵינָא חוֹלַף עֵינָא דְּמֵי שִׁינָא חוֹלַף שִׁינָא דְּמֵי יְדָא חוֹלַף יְדָא דְּמֵי רִיגְלָא חוֹלַף רִיגְלָא


The value of an eye for an eye, the value of a tooth for a tooth, the value of a hand for a hand, the value of a foot for a foot,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:25)


דְּמֵי צַעַר מֵחֲרוֹךְ חוֹלַף מֵחֲרוֹךְ דְּמֵי פּוּדְעָא חוֹלַף פּוּדְעָא דְּמֵי הַלְכְשִׁישׁ חוֹלַף הַלְכְשִׁישׁ


all equivalent of the pain of burning for burning, and of wounding for wounding, and of blow for blow.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:26)


וַאֲרוּם יִמְחֵי גְּבַר יַת עֵינָא דְעַבְדֵיהּ כְּנַעֲנָאֵי אוֹ יַת עֵינָא דְאַמְתֵיהּ כְּנַעֲנִיתָא וְסָמִינָהּ לְבַר חוֹרִין יִפְטְרִינֵיהּ חוֹלַף עֵינֵיהּ


And when a man strikes the eye of his Kenaanite servant or handmaid, and causeth blindness, he shall let him go free, on account of the eye.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:27)


וְאִין שִׁינָא דְעַבְדֵיהּ כְּנַעֲנָאֵי אוֹ שִׁינָא דְאַמְתֵיהּ כְּנַעֲנִיתָא יַפִּיל לְבַר חוֹרִין יִפְטְרִינֵיהּ חוֹלַף שִׁינֵיהּ


And if he strike out the tooth of his Kenaanite man or maid-servant, he shall make the servant free on account of the tooth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:28)


וַאֲרוּם יִנְגַשׁ תּוֹר יַת גְּבַר אוֹ יַת אִיתָא וִימוּת יִתְרְגָמָא יִתְרְגֵם תּוֹרָא וְלָא יִתְנְכֵס לְמֵיכוּל יַת בִּשְרֵיהּ וּמָרֵיהּ דְּתוֹרָא יְהֵי זַכְּאַי מִדִּין קְטוֹל וְאוּף מִדְמֵי עַבְדָא וְאַמְהוּ


And if an ox goreth a man or woman to cause death, the ox must be stoned, but shall not be killed that his flesh may be eaten; and the owner of the ox shall be exempt from the condemnation of death, and also from the price of the servant or handmaid.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:29)


וְאִין תּוֹר נַגְשָׁן הוּא מֵאִתְמְלֵי וּמִדְקֳדָמוֹי וְאִתַּסְהַד עַל אַנְפֵּי מָרֵיהּ תְּלָתֵי זִמְנֵי וְלָא נַטְרֵיהּ וּמִבָּתַר כֵּן קָטֵל גַּבְרָא אוֹ אִיתָא תּוֹרָא יִתְרְגֵם וְאוּף מָרֵיהּ יִתְקְטֵיל בְּמִיתוּתָא דְמִשְׁתַּלְחָא עֲלוֹי מִן שְׁמַיָא


But if the ox (had been wont) to gore yesterday and before, and it had been attested before his owner three times, and he (had neglected) to restrain him, the ox, when he killeth man or woman, shall be stoned, and his master also shall die with a death sent upon him from heaven.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:30)


בְּרַם אִין קְנָסָא דְמָמוֹנָא יִתְשַׁוֵּי עֲלוֹי וְיִתֵּן פּוּרְקַן נַפְשֵׁיהּ כְּכָל מַה דִּישַׁווּן עֲלוֹי סַנְהֶדְרִין דְּיִשְרָאֵל


Yet if a fine of money be laid upon him, he may give a ransom for his life, according to what shall be imposed on him by the sanhedrin of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:31)


אִין בַּר יִשְרָאֵל יְנַגַּח תּוֹרָא אוֹ לְבַת יִשְרָאֵל יְנַגַּח כְּדִינָא הָדֵין יִתְעֲבֵד לֵיהּ


Whether the ox hath gored a son or a daughter of Israel, according to that judgment it shall be done to him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:32)


בְּרַם אִין עֶבֶד כְּנַעֲנָאֵי נְגַשׁ תּוֹרָא אוֹ אַמְתָא כְּנַעֲנִיתָא כְּסַף תְּלָתִין סִלְעִין יִתֵּן לְמָרֵיהּ דְּעַבְדָא אוֹ דְאַמְתָא וְתוֹרָא יִתְרְגָם


If an ox goreth a Kenaanite man-servant or handmaid, the master of the man or woman-servant shall give thirty sileen of silver, and the ox shall be stoned.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:33)


וַאֲרוּם יִפְתָּח אֵינַשׁ גּוּב אוֹ אֲרוּם יַחְפֵּס אֵינָשׁ גּוּב בְּשׁוּקָא וְלָא יְכַסִּינֵיהּ וּנְפוֹל תַּמָּן תּוֹרָא אוֹ חַמָרָא


And if a man openeth a pit in the street, and doth not cover it, and an ox or an ass fall therein;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:34)


מָרֵיהּ דְּגוֹבָא יְשַׁלֵּם כְּסַף יָתֵיב לְמָרֵיהּ דְּמֵי תּוֹרֵיהּ וְחַמְרֵיהּ וּנְבֵילְתָּא יְהֵי דִילֵיהּ


the master of the pit shall deliver silver to give to its owner the price of the ox or the ass, and the dead body shall be his.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:35)


וַאֲרוּם יִנְגוֹף תּוֹר דִּגְבַר יַת תּוֹרָא דְּחַבְרֵיהּ וִימוּת וִיזַבְּנוּן יַת תּוֹרָה חַיָיא וְיִפְלְגוּן יַת דְּמֵיהּ וְאוּף יַת דְּמֵי מוֹתָא יִפְלְגוּן


And when an ox woundeth his neighbour's ox, and he die, they shall sell the living ox, and divide the price, and the price of the dead one shall they also divide.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:36)


אִין אִישְׁתְּמוֹדַע אֲרוּם תּוֹר נַגְחָן הוּא מֵאִתְמְלֵי וּמִדְקֳדָמוֹי וְלָא נַטְרֵיהּ מָרֵיהּ שַׁלְמָא יְשַׁלֵם תּוֹרָא חוֹלַף תּוֹרָא וּנְבֵילְתָּא וּמוֹשְׁכָא יְהֵי דִילֵיהּ


But if it hath been known that the ox was wont to gore in time past, and his master did not restrain him, he shall surely deliver ox for ox; but the carcase and the skin shall be his.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 21:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/21:37)


אֲרוּם יִגְנוֹב גְּבַר תּוֹר אוֹ אִימַר וְיִכְסִינֵיהּ אוֹ זַבִּינֵיהּ חַמְשָׁא תּוֹרָא יְשַׁלֵם חוֹלַף תּוֹר חַד מִן בִּגְלַל דְּבַטְלֵי מִן רַדְיֵיה וְאַרְבַּע עָנָא חוֹלַף אִימַר חַד מִן בִּגְלַל דְּאַקְטֵיהּ בִּגְנֵבוּתֵיהּ וְלֵית בָּהּ עִיבִידְתָּא


When a man stealeth an ox or a sheep, and killeth or selleth it, five oxen shall he make good for one ox, because he hath hindered him from his ploughing; and four sheep for one, because he hath impoverished him by his theft, and not done service by it.



## Chapter 22

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:1)


אִין בַּחְרַכָּא דְכוֹתְלָא מִשְׁתְּכַח גַּנָבָא וְיִתְמְחֵי וִימוּת לֵית לֵיהּ חוֹבַת שְׁפִיכוּת אֲדַם זַכְּאַי


If a thief be found in a window of the wall, and be smitten and die, there shall not be on his account the guilt of the shedding of innocent blood.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:2)


אִין בָּרִיר פִּתְגָּמָא כְשִׁמְשָׁא דְּלָא לְמִיקְטוֹל נְפַשׁ עָאל וְקַטְלֵיהּ חוֹבַת שְׁפִיכוּת אֲדַם זַכְּאַי עֲלוֹי וְאִין אִישְׁתְּזִיב מִן יְדוֹי שַׁלָמָא יְשַׁלֵם אִין לֵית לֵיהּ מַה דִּמְשַׁלֵם וְיִזְדַּבֵּן לֵיהּ בֵּית דִּינָא מִן גְּנִיבוּתֵיהּ וְעַד שַׁתָּא דִשְׁמִיטְתָא


If the thing be as clear as the sun that he was not entering to destroy life, and one hath killed him, the guilt of the shedding of innocent blood is upon him; and if spared from his hand, restoring he shall restore. If he have not wherewith to restore, the beth din shall sell him for his theft until the year of release.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:3)


אִין בְּסַהֲדַיָא מִשְׁתַּכְּחָא בִּרְשׁוּתֵיהּ גְּנִיבוּתֵיהּ מִן תּוֹר עַד חֲמוֹר עַד אִימַר כַּד הִינוּן קַיְימִין עַל חַד תְּרֵין יְשַׁלֵּם


If before witnesses, the thing stolen was found in his possession, from an ox or an ass, unto a sheep alive, he shall restore two for one.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:4)


אֲרוּם יִפְקַר גְּבַר חֲקִיל אוֹ כַּרְמָא וִישַׁלַח יַת בְּעִירֵיהּ וְיֵיכוּל בַּחֲקַל גְּבַר אוֹחֲרָן שְׁפַר חַקְלֵיהּ וּשְׁפַר כַּרְמֵיהּ יְשַׁלֵּם


If a man break in upon a field or a vineyard, and send in his beast to feed in another man's field, the best of his field and the best of his vineyard he shall restore.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:5)


אֲרוּם תִּתְפּוֹק נוּר וְתַשְׁכַּח כּוּבִין וְתִגְמַר גָּדִישׁ אוֹ מִדַּעַם דְּקָאִי אוֹ חֲקִיל שַׁלָמָא יְשַׁלִים מַאן דְּאַדְלִיק יַת דְּלֵיקְתָּא


If fire break out, and catch thorns, and consume the sheaves, or whatever is standing, or the field, whoever kindled the fire shall surely restore.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:6)


אֲרוּם יִתֵּן גְּבַר לְחַבְרֵיהּ כְּסַף אוֹ מָאנִין לְמִנְטַר בְּלָא אֲגַר נְטִיר וּמִתְגַנְבִין מִן בֵּית גְּבַר אִין מִשְׁתְּכַּח גַּנְבָא אִיהוּ מְשַׁלֵּם עַל חַד תְּרֵין


When a man confideth to his neighbour silver, or vessels to keep, without recompense for the care, and they be stolen from the man's house,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:7)


אִין לָא אִישְׁתַּכַּח גַּנְבָא וְיִתְקְרֵיב מָרֵיהּ בֵּיתָא לִקְדָם דַּיָינַיָא וְיוֹמֵי דְלָא אוֹשִׁיט יְדֵיהּ בְּעִיסְקָא דְחַבְרֵיהּ


if the thief be found, he shall restore two for one. If the thief be not found, the master of the house shall be brought before the judges, and shall swear that he hath not put forth his own hand upon the property of his neighbour.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:8)


עַל כָּל מִדַּעַם דְּאִתְּבִיר בְּכוּסְיָא עַל תּוֹר עַל חֲמוֹר עַל אִימַר עַל כְּסוּי עַל כָּל אֲבִידְתָּא יוֹמֵי כַּד יֵימַר אֲרוּם הוּא דֵין וְכַד מִשְׁתַּכְּחָא גְנִיבוּתָא בָּתַר כֵּן בְּיַד גַּנָבָא קֳדָם דַּיָינַיָא יוֹעִיל דִּין תַּרְוֵויהוֹם דִּין מָרֵיהּ דְּבֵיתָא וְדִין גַּנְבָא וּלְמַאן דִּמְחַיְיבִין דַּיָינַיָא יְשַׁלֵּם גַּנְבָא עַל חַד תְּרֵין לְחַבְרֵיהּ


And about whatever is injured covertly, whether ox, or ass, or sheep, or raiment, of whatever is (so) lost, he shall make oath when he saith that so it is; and when the thing stolen shall be afterward found in the hand of the thief, the cause of both shall be brought before the judges, the cause of the householder and the cause of the thief; and whom the judges shall condemn, the thief shall restore twofold to his neighbour.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:9)


אֲרוּם יִתֵּן גְּבַר לְחַבְרֵיהּ חֲמוֹר אוֹ תּוֹר אוֹ אִימַר וְכָל בְּעִירָא לְמִנְטוֹר בְּלָא אֲגַר נְטִיר וִימוּת אוֹ אִיתְבִיר מִן חֵיוָא אוֹ אִישְׁתְּבֵי וְלֵית סָהִיד חָמֵי וּמַסְהִיד


If a man deliver to his neighbour all ox, or a sheep, or any animal to keep, (if) he is to keep it without recompense, and it die, or be torn by wild beast, or be carried off, and no witness seeing who can testify it;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:10)


מוֹמָתָא דַיְיָ תְּהֵי בֵּין תַּרְוֵיהוֹם יוֹמֵי דְלָא אוֹשִׁיט יְדֵיהּ בְּעִיסְקָא דְחַבְרֵיהּ וִיקַבֵּל מָארֵיהּ מִנֵיהּ מוֹמָתָא וְלָא יְשַׁלֵּם


an oath of the Lord shall be between them both, that he hath not put forth his hand upon the property of his neighbour; and the owner of the thing shall accept his oath, and he shall not (be required to) make it good.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:11)


וְאִין יִתְגַנְבָא יִתְגַנֵּב מִנֵיהּ דַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ עִמֵּיהּ אֲגַר נְטִיר יְשַׁלֵם לְמָרוֹי


But if it be stolen from him who was to receive recompense for the care, he shall make it good to its owner.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:12)


אִין אִתְבְּרָא יִתְבַּר מִן חֵיוַת בְּרָא מַיְיתֵי לֵיהּ סַהֲדִין אוֹ יַמְטִינֵיהּ עֵד גּוּפָא דִתְבִיר לָא יְשַׁלֵּם


If it hath been torn by a wild beast, let him bring witnesses, or bring him to the carcase: because for that which is (so) torn he shall not make restitution.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:13)


וַאֲרוּם יִשְׁאַל גְּבַר מִדַּעַם מִן חַבְרֵיהּ וְיִתְבַּר מָנָא אוֹ מִית בְּעִירָא מָרֵיהּ לֵית עִמֵּיהּ שַׁלָּמָא יְשַׁלֵּם


And if a man borrow anything of his neighbour, and the vessel be broken, or the animal die, and the owner be not with it, lie shall certainly make it good.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:14)


אִין מָרֵיהּ עִמֵּיהּ לָא יְשַׁלֵּם אִין אֲגִירָא הוּא עָאֵל פְּסֵידֵיהּ בְּאַגְרֵיהּ


If the owner be with it, he shall not make it good: if it had been lent for profit, its loss came on account of its hire.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:15)


וַאֲרוּם יְשַׁרְגֵג גְּבַר בְּתוּלְתָּא דְלָא מְאַרְסָא וִישַׁמֵּשׁ עִמָּהּ מַפְרָנָא יַפְרִין יָתָהּ לֵיהּ לְאִינְתּוּ


If a man seduce a virgin unbetrothed, and have criminal conduct with her, endowing, he shall endow her to be his wife.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:16)


אִין לָא חַמְיָא לֵיהּ אוֹ דְלָא יִצְבֵי אָבוּהָא לְמִתְבָהּ לֵיהּ כְּסַף חַמְשִׁין סַלְעִין יִתְקְנֵס הֵי כְמִפְרוֹנִי בְּתוּלְתָּא


If this doth not appear to him (to be desirable), or if her father be not willing to give her to him fifty sileen of silver shall be laid upon him, according to the endowment of a virgin.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:17)


עַמִּי בְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל כָּל עֲבִיד חַרְשִׁיּוּתָא לָא תְקַיְימוּן


Sons of My people Israel, whosoever practiseth witchcraft you shall not suffer to live.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:18)


כָּל דְּיִשְׁכּוּב עִם בְּעִירָא בְאַטְלוּת אַבְנִין יִתְקַטְלוּן


Whosoever lieth with a beast shall be stoned to death.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:19)


כָּל מַאן דְּדַבַּח לְטַעֲוַות עַמְמַיָא יִתְקְטֵל בְּסַיְיפָא וְנִכְסוֹי יִתְגַמְרוּן בְּגִין כֵּן לָא תְהוֹן פַּלְחִין אֱלָהֵן לִשְׁמָא דַיְיָ בִּלְחוֹדוֹי


Whosoever sacrificeth to the idols of the Gentiles shall be slain with the sword, and his goods be destroyed; for ye shall worship only the Name of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:20)


וְגִיּוֹרָא לָא תְקַנְטוּן בְּמִילִין וְלָא תְעִיקוּן לְמִיסַב לֵיהּ נִכְסוֹי עַמִּי בְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל הֲווֹ דְכִירִין אֲרוּם דַּיְירִין הֲוֵיתוּן בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם


And the stranger you shall not vex with words, nor distress him by taking his goods: Remember, sons of Israel, My people, that you were strangers in the land of Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:21)


כָּל אַרְמְלָא וְיָתוֹם לָא תְסַגְפוּן


You shall not impoverish the widow or the orphan.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:22)


אִין סַגָּפָא תְסַגֵּף יָתֵיהּ אִזְדַהֲרוּן לְכוֹן אֲרוּם אִין יְקוּם וְיִצְוַוח עֲלֵיכוֹן בִּצְלוֹי קֳדָמַי שְׁמַע אֲנָא בְּקַל צְלוֹתֵיהּ וּפָרַע לֵיהּ


If thou impoverish her, beware; for if they rise up and cry against you in prayer before Me, I will hear the voice of their prayer, and will avenge them,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:23)


וְיִתְקַף רוּגְזִי וְאֶקְטוֹל יַתְכוֹן בְּחַרְבָּא דְמוֹתָא וִיהוֹן נְשֵׁיכוֹן אַרְמְלָן וּבְנֵיכוֹן יַתְמִין


and My anger will be kindled, and I will slay you with the sword, and your wives shall be widows, and your children be orphans.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:24)


אִין כַּסְפָּא תּוֹזִיף לְעַמִּי יַת עַנְיָיא דְעִמָּךְ לָא תְהֵי לֵיהּ הֵי כְּרַשְׁיָא לָא תְשַׁוּוּן דְּלֶהֱוֵי עֲלוֹי סַהֲדִין וּמִידִי עַרְבְנוּתָא לָא עַל שְׁעָרִין וְלָא רִיבְיָין


If thou lend money to (one of) My people, to (one of) the humble of My people, thou shalt not be to him as an usurer, neither lay it upon him that there shall be witnesses against him, or that he give pledges, or equivalents, or usury.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:25)


אִין מְמַשְׁכְּנָא תְמַשְׁכַּן כְּסוּתָא דְחַבְרָךְ עַד לָא יִטְמוֹעַ שִׁמְשָׁא תְּתָבִינֵיהּ לֵיהּ


If thou take (at all) for a pledge the garment of thy neighbour, thou shalt restore it to him before sunset;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:26)


אֲרוּם הִיא טָלֵית דְּמִתְכַּסֵּי בָהּ בִּלְחוֹדָהּ הִיא תּוֹתְבֵיהּ דְּנַפְלָא לְמוֹשְׁכֵיהּ וְאִין תִּיסַב מַצַּע עַרְסֵיהּ בַּמֶּה יִשְׁכּוּב וִיהֵי אִין יִקְבּוֹל קֳדָמַי וַאֲקַבֵּיל צְלוֹתֵיהּ אֲרוּם אֱלָהָא חֲנָנָא אֲנָא


for it may be his taleth which alone covereth him; (or) it is his only garment in which he rests, which falleth upon his skin; and if thou take the coverlet of the bed whereon he lies, and he be heard before Me, I will hearken to his prayer; for I am Eloah the Merciful.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:27)


עַמִּי בְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל דַּיָּינֵיכוֹן לָא תְקִילִין וְרַבָּנִין דִּמִתְמַנִין נְגוֹדִין בְּעַמָּךְ לָא תְלוּטִין


Sons of Israel My people, ye shall not revile your judges, nor curse the rabbans who are appointed rulers among thy people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:28)


בִּכּוּרֵי פֵּירָךְ וּבִכּוּרֵי חֲמַר נַעֲוָוךְ לָא תַשְׁהֵי עַל זִמָנֵיהוֹן מִן לְאַסְקוּתְהוֹן לַאֲתַר שְׁכִינְתִּי בּוּכְרֵי דִבְרָךְ תַּפְרֵישׁ קֳדָמַי


The firsts of thy fruits, and the firsts of thy wine-press, thou shalt not delay to bring up in their time to the place of My habitation. The firstlings of thy males thou shalt separate before Me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:29)


הֵיכְדֵין תַּעֲבֵד לְבוּכְרֵי תוֹרָךְ וְעָנָךְ שַׁבְעָא יוֹמִין יְהֵי יֵינִיק בָּתַר אִימֵיהּ וּבְיוֹמָא תְּמִינָאָה תַּפְרִישִׁינֵיהּ קֳדָמַי


So shalt thou do with the firstlings of thy oxen and sheep; seven days it shall be suckled by its mother, and on the eighth day thou shalt separate it before Me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 22:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/22:30)


וְאַנְשִׁין קַדִּישִׁין טְעָמִין חוּלִין בִּדְכוּתָא תְּהוֹן קֳדָמַי בְּרַם בְּשַר תְּלִישׁ מִן חֵיוָותָא חַיָיא לָא תֵיכְלוּן לְכַלְבָּא תֵירְמוּן יָתֵיהּ בְּסִיטְרֵיהּ


And holy men, tasting unconsecrated things innocently, shall you be before Me; but flesh torn by wild beasts alive you may not eat, but throw it to the dog as his portion.



## Chapter 23

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:1)


עַמִּי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לָא תְקַבְּלוּן מִילֵי שִׁיקְרָא מִגַּבְרָא דְּיֵיכוּל קוֹרְצִין בְּחַבְרֵיהּ קֳדָמָךְ וְלָא תְשַׁוֵּי יְדָךְ עִם רַשִׁיעָא דִיהֵי סָהִיד שְׁקָר


Sons of Israel My people, take not up lying words from a man who accuseth his neighbour before thee, nor put thine hand with the wicked to become a false witness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:2)


עַמִּי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לָא תֶהֱווֹן בָּתַר סַגִּיאִין לְמַבְאֲשָׁא אֱלָהֵן לִמְטַיְיבָא וְלָא יִתְמְנַע חַד מִנְכוֹן לְמַלְפָא זְכוּ עַל חַבְרֵיהּ בְּדִינָא לְמֵימַר הָא בָּתַר סַגִּיאֵי דִינָא סָטֵי


Sons of Israel My people, you shall not go after the many to do evil, but to do good; and no one among you shall restrain himself from affirming justly concerning his neighbour in the judgment, by saying, Behold, the judgment sides with the many.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:3)


וּמִסְכְּנָא דְאִתְחַיֵּיב בְּדִינֵיהּ לָא תִיסַב לֵיהּ אַפִּין לִמְרַחֲמָא עֲלוֹי אֲרוּם לֵית מִיסַב אַפִּין בְּדִינָא


And to the poor man who is guilty in his cause, thou shalt not be partial in having compassion upon him; for there must not be respect of persons in judgment.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:4)


אִין תֶּאֱרַע תּוֹרָא דְסַנְאָךְ דְּאַתְּ סָנֵי לֵיהּ עַל חוֹבָתָא דְאַנְתְּ יְדַע בֵּיהּ בִּלְחוֹדָךְ אוֹ חַמְרָא דְטָעֵי מִן אָרְחָא אָתָבָא תִתְבִינֵיהּ לֵיהּ


If thou meet the ox of thine enemy whom thou dislikest on account of the wickedness which thou only knowest is in him, or an ass that wandereth from the way, thou shalt surely bring it to him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:5)


אִין תֶּחֱמֵי חַמְרָא דְסַנְאָךְ דְּאַנְתְּ סָנֵי לֵיהּ עַל חוֹבָתָא דְאַנְתְּ יְדַע בֵּיהּ בִּלְחוֹדָךְ רְבִיעַ תְּחוֹת טוֹנֵיהּ וְתִמְנוֹעַ לְנַפְשָׁךְ מִלְמִקְרוֹב לֵיהּ מִשְׁבּוֹק תִּשְׁבּוֹק בְּהַהִיא שַׁעְתָּא יַת סָנָא דִבְלִבָּךְ עֲלוֹי וְתִפְרוֹק וְתִטְעוֹן עִמֵּיהּ


If thou seest the ass of thy enemy whom thou dislikest on account of the wickedness which thou only knowest to be in him, lying under his burden, and thou wouldst refrain thyself from going near him, thou shalt relinquish at once the dislike of thy heart against (thy enemy), and release and take care of the ass (or, charge thyself with him).


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:6)


עַמִּי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לָא תַצְלוּן דִּין מִסְכֵּינָא בְּדִינֵיהּ


Sons of Israel My people, ye shall not warp the judgment of the poor in his cause.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:7)


מִפִּתְגַם שִׁיקְרָא הֲוֵי רָחִיק וְדִי נְפַק זַכְּאַי מִבֵּי דִינָךְ וְאַשְׁכָּחוּ לֵיהּ חוֹבָתֵיהּ וְדִי נְפַק חַיָּיב וְאַשְׁכָּחוּ לֵיהּ זְכוּ לָא תִקְטֵיל אֲרוּם לָא הֲוֵינָא מְזַכֵּי לֵיהּ אִין הוּא חַיָּיבָא


From a false matter keep distant. And when one hath gone forth from thy house of justice acquitted, and they (afterwards) find out his guilt; or one hath been brought out condemned, and they (afterward) find out his innocence,-­thou shalt not put him to death; for I will not hold (the former) innocent, nor the latter guilty.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:8)


וְשׁוֹחֲדָא לָא תְקַבֵּיל אֲרוּם שׁוֹחֲדָא מְסַמֵּי עֵינוֹי נַסְבוֹי וּמְטַלְטֵל חַכִּימַיָא מִמוֹתְבָנֵיהוֹן וּמְקַלְקֵל פִּתְגָּמִין תְּרִיצִין דְּמִכְתָּבִין בְּאוֹרַיְיתָא וּמְעַרְבֵב מִילֵי זַכָּאִין בְּפוּמְהוֹן בְּשַׁעַת דִּינָא


And thou mayest not receive a bribe; for a bribe blindeth their eyes who have taken it, and casteth down the wise from their seats, and perverteth the right words which are written in the law, and confoundeth the words that are in the mouth of the innocent in the hour of judgment.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:9)


וּלְגִיּוּרָא לָא תְעִיקוּן וְאַתּוּן חַכִּימְתּוּן יַת אֲנִיק נְפַשׁ גִּיּוֹרָא אֲרוּם דַּיְירִין הֲוֵיתוּן בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם


Thou shalt not oppress the stranger; for ye know the sigh of a stranger's soul; because ye were sojourners in the land of Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:10)


וְשִׁית שְׁנִין תִּדְרַע יַת אַרְעָךְ וְתִכְנוֹשׁ יַת עֲלַלְתָּא


Six years thou shalt sow thy land, and gather the produce;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:11)


וּשְׁבִיעִיתָא תִּשְׁמְטִינָא מִפּוּלְחָנָא וְתַפְקַר פֵּירָהָא וְיֵיכְלוּן מִסְכֵּינֵי עַמָּא וְשִׁיּוּרְהוֹן תֵּיכוּל חֵיוַת בָּרָא כְּדֵין תַּעֲבֵיד לְכַרְמָךְ לְזֵיתָךְ


but the seventh year thou shalt exempt it from labour, and give up the fruit of it to be eaten by the poor of My people; and what they leave shall be eaten by the beasts of the field. And in like manner shalt thou do with thy vine and olive grounds.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:12)


שִׁיתָּא יוֹמִין תַּעֲבֵד עוּבְדָךְ וּבְיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה תִּנּוּחַ מִן בִּגְלַל דִּינוּחוּן תּוֹרָךְ וְחַמְרָךְ וְיִשְׁקוֹט בַּר אַמְתָךְ עַרְלָאָה וְגִיּוֹרָא


Six days do thy work, and on the seventh day repose, that thy ox and thy ass may rest, and that the uncircumcised son of thy handmaid, and the stranger, may rest.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:13)


וּבְכָל פִּקּוּדַיָא דְאָמְרִית לְכוֹן תִּסְתַּמְּרוּן וְשׁוּם טַעֲוַות עַמְמַיָא לָא תִדְכְּרוּן לָא יִשְׁתְּמַע עַל פּוּמְכוֹן


And of all the precepts that I have spoken to you, be carefull; and the names of the idols of the Gentiles remember not, nor let them be heard upon your lips.
