###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:14)


תְּלָתָא זִמְנִין תְּחַגּוּן קֳדָמַי בְּשַׁתָּא


Three times in the year thou shalt keep festival before Me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:15)


יַת חַגָּא דְפַטִירַיָיא תִּנְטוֹר שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין תֵּיכוֹל פַּטִּירָא הֵיכְמָא דְפַקֵידְתָּךְ לִזְמַן יַרְחָא דְאַבִּיבָא אֲרוּם בֵּיהּ נְפַקְתְּ מִמִּצְרַיִם וְלָא יִתְחַמּוּן קֳדָמַי רֵיקָנִין


The feast of unleavened cakes thou shalt keep. Seven days thou art to eat unleavened bread, as I have instructed thee, in the time of the month of Abiba, because in it thou camest forth from Mizraim; and you shall not appear before Me empty.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:16)


וְחַגָא דְחַצְדָא בִּכּוּרֵי עוּבְדָךְ דְּתִזְרַע בְּחַקְלָא וְחַגָא דִּכְנָשָׁא בְּמִפְקָא דְשַׁתָּא בְּמִכְנְשָׁךְ יַת עוּבְדָךְ מִן חַקְלָא


And the feast of the harvest first-fruits of the work thou didst sow in the field; and the feast of gathering, at the end of the year, when thou hast gathered in thy work from the field.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:17)


תְּלָתָא זִמְנִין בְּשַׁתָּא יִתְחַמּוּן כָּל דְּכוּרָךְ קֳדָם רִבּוֹן עַלְמָא יְיָ


Three times in the year shall all thy males appear before the Lord the Ruler of the world.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:18)


עַמִּי בְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לָא תֵכְסוּן כַּד דְּחָמִיעַ בְּבָתֵּיכוֹן דָּם נִכְסַת פִּיסְחִי וְלָא יְבִית בַּר מִן מַדְבְּחָא תַּרְבֵּי נִכְסַת פִּסְחִי עַד צַפְרָא לָא מִן בִּשְרָא דְתֵיכְלוּן בְּרַמְשָׁא


Sons of Israel My people, while there is leaven in your houses you may not immolate the bloody sacrifice of My Pascha; nor shall the fat of the sacrifice of My Pascha remain without the altar until morning, nor of the flesh that you eat in the evening.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:19)


שֵׁירוּי בִּיכוּרֵי פֵּירֵי אַרְעָךְ תַּיְתֵי לְבֵית מַקְדְּשָׁא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ עַמִּי בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל לֵית אַתּוּן רְשָׁאִין לָא לִמְבַשְׁלָא וְלָא לְמֵיכוּל בְּשַר וַחֲלַב מְעַרְבִין כַּחֲדָא דְלָא יִתְקַף רוּגְזִי וְאִיבְשֵׁיל עִיבּוּרֵיכוֹן דְּגָנָא וְקַשָׁא תְּרֵיהוֹן כַּחֲדָא


The first of the choice fruits of thy ground thou shalt bring to the sanctuary of the Lord thy God. My people of the house of Israel, you are not permitted to dress or to eat of flesh and milk mingled together, lest I be greatly displeased; and I prepare you the wheat and the straw together for your food.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:20)


הָא אֲנָא מְשַׁגֵּר מַלְאָכָא קֳדָמָךְ לְמִטְרָךְ בְּאָרְחָא וּלְאַעֲלוּתָךְ לַאֲתַר שְׁכִינְתִּי דְאִיתְקֵינִית


Behold, I will send an Angel before thee, to keep thee in the way, and to bring thee in to the place of My habitation which I have prepared.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:21)


אִיזְדַהַר מִן קֳדָמוֹי וְקַבֵּיל לְמֵימְרֵיהּ לָא תְסָרֵיב עַל מִילוֹי אֲרוּם לָא יִשְׁבּוֹק לְחוֹבֵיכוֹן אֲרוּם בִּשְׁמִי מֵימְרֵיהּ


Be circumspect before Him, and obey His word, and be not rebellious against His words; for He will not forgive your sins, because His word is in My Name.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:22)


אֲרוּם אִין קַבָּלָא תְקַבֵּיל לְמֵימְרֵיהּ וְתַעֲבֵיד כָּל דַּאֲמַלֵּיל עַל יְדוֹי וְאַסְגֵי יַת סַנְאָךְ וְאָעִיק לְמֵיעִיקְיָךְ


For if thou wilt indeed hearken to His Word, and do all that I speak by Him, I will be the enemy of thy enemy, and will trouble them who trouble thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:23)


אֲרוּם יְטַיֵיל מַלְאָכִי קֳדָמָךְ וְיֵעָלִינָךְ לְוַת אֱמוֹרָאֵי וְחִיתָּאֵי וּפְרִיזָאֵי וּכְנַעֲנָאֵי חִיוָאֵי וִיבוּסָאֵי וְאֵישֵׁיצִינוּן


For My Angel shall go before thee, and bring thee to the Amoraee, and Pherizaee, and Kenaanaee, Hivaee, and Jebusaee; and I will destroy them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:24)


לָא תִסְגְּדוּן לְטַעֲוַותְהוֹן וְלָא תִפְלְחִינוּן וְלָא תַעַבְדוּן כְּעוֹבְדֵיהוֹן בִּישַׁיָּיא אֲרוּם מְפַכְרָא תִּפְכַר בֵּית סְגוּדְהוֹן וְתִבְרָא תְּתַבֵּר קָמְתֵי צִילְמֵיהוֹן


Thou shalt not worship their idols, nor serve them, nor do after their evil works; but thou shalt utterly demolish the house of their worship, and break the statues of their images.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:25)


וְתִפְלְחוּן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן וִיבָרֵךְ יַת מְזוֹנֵי מֵיכְלָךְ וּמִשְׁתַּיָּיךְ וְאַעֲדֵי מְחַת מָרִירְתָּא מִגַּוָּוךְ


And you shall do service before the Lord our God and He will bless the provision of thy food and thy drinks, and remove the bitter plague from among thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:26)


לָא תְהֵי תַּכְלָא וְעַקְרָא בְּאַרְעָךְ יַת מִנְיַין יוֹמֵי חַיָּיךְ אֲשַׁלֵּם מִיּוֹמָא לְיוֹמָא


None shall be abortive or barren in thy land; the number of the days of thy life I will fulfil from day to day.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:27)


יַת אֵימְתִי אֲשַׁדֵּר קֳדָמָךְ וָאֶשְׁגַשׁ יַת כָּל עַמְמַיָא דְאַנְתְּ אָתֵי לְסַדְרָא בְּהוֹן סִדְרֵי קְרָבָא וְאִיתֵּין יַת כָּל בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבָךְ קֳדָמָךְ מְחַזְרֵי קְדָל


My terror will I send before thee, and will perturb all the peoples to whom thou comest, that thou mayest wage battle against them; and I will make all thy enemies turn back before thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:28)


וְאֵשַׁדַּר יַת אוּרְעִיתָא קֳדָמָךְ וּתְתָרֵךְ יַת חִיוָאֵי וְיַת כְּנַעֲנָאֵי וְיַת חִיתָּאֵי מִן קֳדָמָךְ


And I will send the hornet before thee to drive out the Hivaee, and Kenaanaee, and Hitaee, from before thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:29)


לָא אֵיתָרֵיכִינוּן מִן קֳדָמָךְ בְּשַׁתָּא חֲדָא דִלְמָא תְהֵי אַרְעָא צַדְיָא וְיִסְגוּן עֲלָךְ חֵיוַת בָּרָא כַּד יֵיתוּן לְמֵיכוּל פִּיגְרֵיהוֹן וְיִנְזְקוּן בָּךְ


I will not expel them before thee in one year, lest the land become a wilderness, and the beasts of the field multiply upon thee, when they come to eat their carcasses, and injure thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:30)


קָלִיל קָלִיל אֵיתָרְכִינוּן מִן קֳדָמָךְ עַד דִּי תִסְגֵי וְתֶחֱסִין יַת אַרְעָא


By little and little I will drive them out before thee, until thou art increased, and inherit the land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:31)


וַאֲשַׁוֵי יַת תְּחוּמָךְ מִן יַמָא דְסוּף עַד יַמָא דִפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי וּמִן מַדְבְּרָא וְעַד פְּרָת אֲרוּם אֶמְסוֹר בְּיֶדְכוֹן יַת כָּל יַתְבֵי אַרְעָא וְאַנְתְּ תְּתָרֵיכִינוּן מִן קֳדָמָךְ


And I will set thy boundary from the sea of Suph, to the sea of the Philistaee, and from the desert unto the Pherat; for I will deliver into your hand all the inhabitants of the land, and thou shalt drive them out from before thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:32)


לָא תִגְזְרוּן לְהוֹן וּלְטַעֲוַותְהוֹן קְיָים


Thou shalt make no covenant with them, nor with their idols.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 23:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/23:33)


לָא תַעֲבֵיד לְהוֹן שְׁכוּנַן בְּאַרְעָךְ דִּלְמָא יַטְעִינָךְ וִיחַיְיבוּן יָתָךְ קֳדָמַי אֲרוּם תִּפְלַח יַת טַעֲוַותְהוֹן אֲרוּם יְהוֹן לָךְ לְתוּקְלָא


Thou shalt not let them dwell in thy land, lest they cause thee to err, and to sin before Me, when thou dost worship their idols; for they will be a stumbling-block to thee.



## Chapter 24

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:1)


וּלְוַת משֶׁה אָמַר מִיכָאֵל סַרְכַן חָכְמְתָא בְּיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה לְיַרְחָא סַק קֳדָם יְיָ אַנְתְּ וְאַהֲרן נָדָב וַאֲבִיהוּא וְשׁוּבְעִין מִסָּבֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְתִסְגְּדוּן מֵרָחִיק


And Michael, the Prince of Wisdom, said to Mosheh on the seventh day of the month, Come up before the Lord, thou and Aharon, Nadab and Abihu, and seventy of the elders of Israel, and worship at a distance.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:2)


וְיִתְקָרֵב משֶׁה בִּלְחוֹדוֹי קֳדָם יְיָ וְהִינוּן לָא יִתְקַרְבוּן וְעַמָּא לָא יִסְקוּן עִמֵּיהּ


And Mosheh alone shall approach before the Lord; but they shall not draw nigh, nor may the people come up with him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:3)


וְאָתָא משֶׁה וְאִשְׁתָּעֵי לְעַמָּא יַת כָּל פִּתְגָּמַיָא דַיְיָ וְיַת כָּל דִּינַיָיא וְאָתֵיב כָּל עַמָּא קָלָא חָד וַאֲמָרוּ כָּל דְּמַלֵּיל יְיָ נַעֲבֵיד


And Mosheh came and set before the people all the words of the Lord, and all the judgments. And all the people answered with one voice, and said, All that the Lord hath spoken we will do.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:4)


וּכְתַב משֶׁה יַת כָּל פִּתְגָמַיָא דַיְיָ וְאַקְדֵים בְּצַפְרָא וּבְנָא מַדְבְּחָא בְּשִׁיפּוֹלֵי טוּרָא וּתְרֵי סְרֵי קָמָן לִתְרֵי סְרֵי שִׁבְטַיָא דְיִשְרָאֵל


And Mosheh wrote the words of the Lord, and arose in the morning and builded an altar at the lower part of the mountain; and twelve pillars for the twelve tribes of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 24:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:5)


וְשַׁדַּר יַת בּוּכְרֵי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל אֲרוּם עַד הַהִיא שַׁעְתָּא הֲוַות פּוּלְחָנָא בְּבוּכְרַיָא דְעַד כְּדוֹן לָא אִיתְעֲבֵיד מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְעַד כְּדוֹן לָא אִתְיְיהִיבַת כְּהוּנְתָּא לְאַהֲרן וְאַסִיקוּ עֲלַוָון וְנִכְסַת קוּדְשִׁין קֳדָם יְיָ תּוֹרִין


And he sent the firstborn of the sons of Israel,-for until that hour had the firstborn had the (office of performing) worship, the tabernacle of ordinance not (as yet) being made, nor the priesthood given unto Aharon; and they offered burnt offerings and consecrated oblations of oxen before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 24:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:6)


וּנְסֵיב משֶׁה פַּלְגוּת אֲדָם נִכְסָא וְשַׁוִי בְּמִזְרְקַיָא וּפַלְגוּת אֲדָם נִכְסָא דָרַק עַל מַדְבְּחָא


And Mosheh took half of the blood of the offering, and put it in basins, and half of the blood of the offering he sprinkled upon the altar.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 24:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:7)


וּנְסֵיב סִפְרָא דְקַיָימָא דְאוֹרַיְיתָא וּקְרָא קֳדָם עַמָא וְאָמָרוּ כָּל פִּתְגָמַיָא דְמַלֵּיל יְיָ נַעֲבֵיד וּנְקַבֵּיל


And he took the Book of the Covenant of the Law and read before the people; and they said, All the words which the Lord hath spoken we will perform and obey.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 24:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:8)


וּנְסֵיב משֶׁה יַת פַּלְגוּת דְּמָא דִבְמִזְרְקַיָא וְדָרֵיק עַל מַדְבְּחָא לְכַפָּרָא עַל עַמָּא וַאֲמַר הָא דֵין אֲדַם קְיָימָא דִגְזַר יְיָ עִמְכוֹן עַל כָּל פִּתְגָמַיָא הָאִילֵן


And Mosheh took half of the blood which was in the basins, and sprinkled upon the altar, to expiate the people, and said, Behold, this is the blood of the Covenant which the Lord hath made with you upon all these words.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 24:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:9)


וּסְלֵיק משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן נָדָב וַאֲבִיהוּא וְשִׁבְעִין מִסָּבֵי יִשְרָאֵל


And Mosheh and Aharon, Nadab and Abihu, and seventy of the elders of Israel, went up.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 24:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:10)


וּזְקָפוּ נָדָב וַאֲבִיהוּא יַת עֵינֵיהוֹן וְחָמוּן יַת אִיקַר אֱלָהָא דְיִשְרָאֵל וּתְחוֹת אַפִּיפּוּרִין דְּרִיגְלוֹי דְמַיְצַע תְּחוֹת כּוּרְסֵיהּ הֵי כְעוֹבַד אַבַן סַפִּירִינוּן מִידְכַּר שִׁעֲבּוּדָא דְשַׁעְבִּידוּ מִצְרָאֵי יַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל בְּטִינָא וּבְלִיבְנִין וַהֲווֹן נְשַׁיָא בָּטְשָׁן יַת טִינָא עִם גּוּבְרֵיהוֹן הֲוַת תַּמָּן רִיבָא מְפַנְקָתָא מַעְבַּרְתָּא וְאַפִּילַת יַת עוּבְרָא וְאִיתְבַּטַּשׁ עִם טִינָא נָחָת גַּבְרִיאֵל וְעָבַד מִנֵיהּ לְבֵינְתָא וְאַסְקֵיהּ לִשְׁמֵי מְרוֹמָא וְאַתְקְנֵיהּ גִּילוּגְדַק תְּחוֹת אַפִּיפּוּרִין דְּמָרֵי עַלְמָא זִיוֵיהּ הֵי כְּעוֹבַד אֲבַן טָבָא וְהֵי כִּתְקוּף שְׁפַר שְׁמַיָא כַּד הִינוּן בְּרִירִין מִן עֲנָנַיָא


And Nadab and Abihu lifted up their eyes, and saw the glory of the God of Israel; and under the footstool of His feet which was placed beneath His throne, was like the work of sapphire stone a memorial of the servitude with which the Mizraee had made the children of Israel to serve in clay and bricks, (what time) there were women treading clay with their husbands; the delicate young woman with child was also there, and made abortive by being beaten down with the clay. And thereof did Gabriel, descending, make brick, and, going up to the heavens on high, set it, a footstool under the cathedra of the Lord of the world whose splendour was as the work of a precious stone, and as the power of the beauty of the heavens when they are clear from clouds.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 24:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:11)


וּלְוַת נָדָב וַאֲבִיהוּא עוּלֵמַיָא שַׁפִּירַיָא לָא שָׁדַר מַחְתֵּיהּ בְּהַהוּא שַׁעְתָּא בְּרַם אִתְנַטְרָא לְהוֹן לְיוֹם תְּמִינַיָא לְאַשְׁלָמוּתָא לְאַסְעָרָא עֲלֵיהוֹן וְחָמוֹן יַת אִיקַר שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ וַהֲווֹן חָדָן בְּקוּרְבָּנֵיהוֹן דְּאִתְקַבָּלוּ בְּרַעֲוָא הֵי כְאָכְלִין וְהֵי כְשַׁתְיָין


But upon Nadab and Abihu, the comely young men, was the stroke not sent in that hour, but it awaited them on the eighth day for a retribution to destroy them; but they saw the glory of the Shekinah of the Lord, and rejoiced that their oblations were received with favour, [as if they] did eat and drink.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 24:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:12)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה סוֹק קֳדָמַי לְטַוְורָא וַהֲוֵי תַמָּן וְאֵיתַּן לָךְ יַת לוּחֵי אַבְנָא דִּבְּהוֹן רָמוּז שְׁאָר פִּתְגָמֵי אוֹרַיְיתָא וְשִׁית מְאָה וּתְלֵיסְרֵי פִקּוּדַיָיא דִכְתָבִית לְאַלּוּפֵיהוֹן


And the Lord said to Mosheh, Ascend before Me at the mount, and I will there give thee the tables of stone on which I have set forth the rest of the words of the Law, and the six hundred and thirteen precepts which I have written for their instruction.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 24:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:13)


וְקָם משֶׁה וִיהוֹשֻׁעַ מְשׁוּמְשָׁנֵיהּ וּסְלֵיק משֶׁה לְטַוְורָא דְאִיתְגְּלֵי עֲלוֹי יְקַר שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ


And Mosheh arose and Jehoshua his minister; and Mosheh went up to the mountain on which was revealed the glory of the Shekinah of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 24:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:14)


וּלְחַכִּימַיָא אָמַר אַמְתִּינוּן לָנָא הָכָא עַד זְמַן דִּנְתוֹב לְוַותְכוֹן וְהָא אַהֲרן וְחוּר עִמְכוֹן מַאן דְּאִית לֵיהּ עֵיסַק דִּינָא יִתְקְרֵב לְוַותְהוֹן


And to the sages he had said, Expect us here, at the time of our return to you; and, behold, Aharon and Hur are with you; if there be any matter of judgment, bring it to them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 24:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:15)


וּסְלֵיק משֶׁה לְטַוְורָא וַחֲפָא עֲנַן יְקָרָא יַת טַוְורָא


And Mosheh went up into the mount, and the Cloud of Glory covered the mount.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 24:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:16)


וּשְׁרָא אִיקַר שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ עַל טַוְורָא דְסִינַי וַחֲפָהִי עֲנַן יְקָרָא שִׁיתָּא יוֹמִין וּקְרָא לְמשֶׁה בְּיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה מִגּוֹ עֲנָנָא


And the glory of the Lord's Shekinah abode upon the mountain of Sinai, and the Cloud of Glory covered it six days. And on the seventh day He called to Mosheh from the midst of the Cloud.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 24:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:17)


וְחֵיזוּ זִיו יִקְרָא דַיְיָ הֵי כְאֵשָׁא בָעֲרָא וְזִיקוּקֵי אֵשָׁא אָכְלָא אֵשָׁא וַהֲווֹ חָמָן וּתְמֵהִין בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


And the appearance of the splendour of the glory of the Lord was as burning fire with flashes of devouring fire; and the sons of Israel beheld and were awe-struck.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 24:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/24:18)


וְעָאל משֶׁה בְּגוֹ עֲנָנָא וּסְלֵיק לְטַוְורָא וַהֲוָה משֶׁה בְּטַוְורָא אַלִיף פִּתְגָמֵי אוֹרַיְיתָא מִן פּוּם קוּדְשָׁא יְהֵי שְׁמֵיהּ מְשַׁבַּח אַרְבְּעִין יְמָמִין וְאַרְבָּעִין לֵילְוָון


And Mosheh entered into the midst of the Cloud, and ascended the mountain; and Mosheh was upon the mountain forty days and forty nights, learning the words of the Law from the mouth of the Holy One, whose Name be praised.



## Chapter 25

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:1)

And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:2)


מַלֵּיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְיִסְבוּן קֳדָמַי אַפְרְשׁוּתָא מִן כָּל דְּיִתְרְעֵי לִבֵּיהּ וְלָא בְּאַלְמוּתָא תִסְבוּן יַת אַפְרָשׁוּתִי


Speak unto the children of Israel, that they shall set apart (take) before me a Separation: of every one whose heart is willing, but not by constraint, ye shall take my separation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:3)


וְדָא אַפְרָשׁוּתָא דְתִסְבוּן מִנְהוֹן דַּהֲבָא וְכַסְפָּא וּנְחָשָׁא


And this is the separation which you shall take of them: gold, and silver, and brass;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:4)


וְתִכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָונָא וּצְבַא זְהוֹרֵי וּבוּץ וּמֵעַזֵּי


and hyacinth, and purple, and crimson, and fine linen; and goats' skins,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:5)


וּמַשְׁכֵי דְדִכְרֵי מְסַמְקֵי וּמַשְׁכֵי דְסַסְגוֹנָא וְקַיְסִין דְּשִׁיטִין


and skins of rams dyed red, and purpled skins, and woods of sittin,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:6)


וּמְשַׁח זֵיתָא לְאַנְהָרָא וּבוּסְמַיָא לְפִיטוּמָא דִמְשַׁח רְבוּתָא וּלְפִיטוּמָא דִקְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמַיָא


and olive oil for the light, and aromatics for the confection of the pure anointing oil, and of the fragrant incense;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:7)

gems of beryl that are gems of perfection, for engraving and insertion in the ephoda and in the breastplate.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:8)

And they shall make a Sanctuary to My Name, that My Shekinah may dwell among them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:9)


כְּכָל מַה דַּאֲנָא מַחֲמֵי יָתָךְ יַת צוּרַת מַשְׁכְּנָא וְיַת צוּרַת כָּל מָנוֹי וְהֵיכְדֵין תַּעַבְדוּן


According to all that I show thee, the likeness of the tabernacle and the likeness of all its vessels, so shalt thou make.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:10)


וִיעַבְּדוּן אֲרוֹנָא דְקַיְסֵי שִׁיטָא תַרְתֵּין אַמִין וּפַלְגָא אוּרְכֵּיהּ וְאַמְתָא וּפַלְגָא פּוּתְיֵהּ וְאַמְתָא וּפַלְגָא רוּמֵיהּ


And they shall make an ark of sitta wood; two cubits and a half its length, a cubit and a half its breadth, and a cubit and a half its height.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:11)


וְתַחְפֵּי יָתֵיהּ דְּהַב דְּכֵי מִגֵּיו וּמִבָּרָא תַחֲפִינֵיהּ וְתַעֲבֵיד עֲלוֹי דֵּיר דִּדְהַב חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר


And thou shalt cover it with pure gold within and without, and shalt make upon it a crown of gold round about.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:12)


וְתַתִּיךְ לֵיהּ אַרְבַּע עִזְקַן דִּדְהַב וְתִתֵּן עַל אַרְבַּע אִיזְתַוְודוֹי וְתַרְתֵּין עִזְקַן עַל צִיטְרֵיהּ חָד וְתַרְתֵּין עִיזְקַן עַל צִיטְרֵיהּ תִּנְיָינָא


And thou shalt cast for it four rings of gold, and set them upon its four corners; two rings for one side, and two rings for the second side.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:13)


וְתַעֲבֵיד אֲרִיחֵי דְקֵיסֵי שִׁיטָא וְתַחֲפֵי יַתְהוֹן דַּהֲבָא


And thou shalt make staves of sitta wood, and cover them with gold;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:14)


וּתְהַנְעַל יַת אֲרִיחַיָא בְּעִזְקָתָא עַל צִיטְרֵיהּ אֲרוֹנָא לְמֵיטוֹל יַת אֲרוֹנָא בְּהוֹן


and thou shalt introduce the staves into the rings on the sides of the ark, that the ark may be carried upon them.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:15)


בְּעִיזְקָתָא דַאֲרוֹנָא יְהוֹן מַחְתָן אֲרִיחַיָא לָא יַעֲדוּן מִנֵּיהּ


The staves shall be inlaid in the rings of the ark, and not be removed from it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:16)


וְתִתֵּן בְּגוֹ אֲרוֹנָא יַת סַהֲדוּתָא דְאֵיתֵּן לָךְ


And thou shalt put within the ark the Testament that I will give thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:17)


וְתַעֲבֵיד כַּפּוּרְתָּא דִדְהַב דְּכֵי תַרְתֵּין אַמִּין וּפַלְגָא אוּרְכָּהּ וְאַמְתָא וּפַלְגָא פּוּתְיָהּ וְסוּמְכָהּ יְהֵא פּוּשְׁכָה


And thou shalt make a (kaphortha) mercy-seat of pure gold; two cubits and a half the length, and a cubit and a half the breadth, and its depth shall be a handbreadth (pusheka).


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:18)


וְתַעֲבֵיד תְּרֵין כְּרוּבִין דִּדְהַב דְּכֵי נְגִיד תַּעֲבֵיד יַתְהוֹי מִתְּרֵין צִיטְרֵי כְפּוּרְתָּא


And thou shalt make two kerubin, of pure beaten gold shalt thou make them on the two sides of the mercy-seat.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:19)


וְעַבֵיד כְּרוּבָא חָד מִצִּיטְרָא מִכָּא וּכְרוּבָא חָד מִצִּיטְרָא מִכָּא מִן כַּפּוּרְתָּא תַעֲבֵיד יַת כְּרוּבַיָא מִתְּרֵין צִיטְרוֹי


Thou shalt make one keruba on this side, and one keruba on that side of the mercy-seat; you shall make the kerubaia on its two sides.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:20)


וִיהוֹן כְּרוּבַיָא פְּרִיסַן גַּדְפֵיהוֹן לְעֵילָא כָּל קְבֵיל רֵישֵׁיהוֹם מְטַלְלִין בְּגַדְפֵיהוֹן עַל כַּפּוּרְתָּא וְאַפֵּיהוֹן חָד כָּל קְבֵיל חַד לִקְבֵיל כַּפּוּרְתָּא יְהוֹן אַפֵּי כְרוּבַיָא


And the kerubaia shall stretch forth their wings above, their heads over against each other, their wings overshadowing the mercy-seat, and their faces over against each other; towards the mercy-seat shall be the faces of the kerubaia.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:21)


וְתִתֵּן יַת כַּפּוּרְתָּא עַל אֲרוֹנָא מִלְעֵילָא וּבְגוֹ אֲרוֹנָא תִּתֵּן יַת לוּחֵי סַהֲדוּתָא דְאֵיתֵּן לָךְ


And thou shalt put the mercy-seat above upon the ark, and within the ark thou shalt lay the Tables of the Testament that I will give thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:22)


וְאֵיזַמֵן מֵימְרִי לָךְ תַּמָּן וְאֵימַלֵּיל עִמָּךְ מֵעִילַוֵי כַּפּוּרְתָּא מִבֵּין תְּרֵין כְּרוּבַיָא דְעַל אֲרוֹנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא יַת כָּל מַה דַּאֲפַקֵּיד יָתָךְ לְוַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


And I will appoint My Word with thee there, and will speak with thee from above the mercy-seat, between the two kerubaia that are over the ark of the testament, concerning all that I may command thee for the sons of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:23)


וְתַעֲבֵיד פְּתוֹרָא דְקֵיסֵי שִׁיטִין תַּרְתֵּין אַמִּין אוּרְכֵּיהּ וְאַמְתָא פּוּתְיֵהּ וְאַמְתָא וּפַלְגָא רוּמֵיהּ


And thou shalt make a table of sitta wood; two cubits its length, and a cubit its breadth, and a cubit and a half its height.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:24)


וְתַחְפֵי יָתֵיהּ דְּהַב דְּכֵי וְתַעֲבֵיד לֵיהּ דֵּיר דִּדְהַב חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר


And thou shalt overlay it with pure gold, and make for it a rim of gold round about.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:25)


וְתַעֲבֵיד לֵיהּ גַּפוּף רוּמָא פּוּשְׁכָא חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר וְתַעֲבֵיד דֵּיר דִּדְהַב לְגִיפוּפֵיהּ חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר


And thou shalt make to it a border, a handbreadth high round about, and make a golden wreath for its border round about.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:26)


וְתַעֲבֵיד אַרְבַּע עִזְקַן דִּדְהַב וְתִתֵּן יַת עִזְקָתָא עַל אַרְבַּע זִיוְיַין דִּי לְאַרְבַּע רִיגְלוֹי


And thou shalt make for it four golden rings, and set the rings in its four corners which are at its four feet.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:27)


כָּל קְבֵיל גָּפוּף תֶּהֶוְיַין עִיזְקָתָא לְאַתְרָא לַאֲרִיחַיָיא לְמִיטוֹל יַת פָּתוֹרָא


Over against the border shall the rings be, for the place of the staves, to carry the table.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:28)


וְתַעֲבֵיד יַת אֲרִיחַיָיא דְקֵיסֵי שִׁיטָא וְתַחְפֵי יַתְהוֹן דַּהֲבָא וִיהוֹן נַטְלִין בְּהוֹן יַת פְּתוֹרָא


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:29)


וְתַעֲבֵיד פַּיַילְתּוֹי וּבָזִיכוֹי וְקַשְוָותוֹי וּמְכִילְתוֹי דִי יִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ וְיִתְרוֹק בְּהוֹן דִּדְהַב דְּכֵי תַעֲבֵיד יַתְהוֹן


And thou shalt make its dishes and its spoons, its tankards and its measures, which are for the service of libation; of pure gold shalt thou make them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:30)


וּתְסַדֵּר עַל פְּתוֹרָא לַחֲמָא גַוָואָה קֳדָמַי תְּדִירָא


And upon the table thou shalt set in order the interior bread before Me continually.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:31)


וְתַעֲבֵיד מְנַרְתָּא דִדְהַב דְּכֵי נְגִיד תַּעֲבֵיד מְנַרְתָּא בָּסִיס דִּידָהּ וּקְנָאָה כַּלִידָהָא וְחִיזוּרְיָיהּ וְשׁוֹשַׁנְיָיהּ מִינָהּ יְהוֹן


And thou shalt make a Candelabrum; of pure beaten gold shalt thou make the candelabrum; its base and shaft, its cups and apples and lilies, shall be of the same.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:32)


וְשִׁיתָּא קְנִין נַפְקִין מִצִּיטְרָהָא תְּלָתָא קְנֵי מְנַרְתָּא מִצִּטְרָה חָד וּתְלָתָא קְנֵי מְנַרְתָּא מִצִּיטְרָה תִּנְיָינָא


Six branches shall spread out from its sides; three branches of the candelabrum from one side, and three branches of the candelabrum on the second side.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:33)


תְּלָתָא כַּלִּידִין מְשַׁקְעִין בְּצִיּוּרֵיהוֹן בְּקַנְיָא חָד חֵיזוּר וְשׁוֹשַׁן וּתְלָתָא כַּלִּידִין מְשַׁקְעִין בְּצִיּוּרֵיהוֹן בְּקַנְיָא חָד חֵיזוּר וְשׁוֹשַׁן הֵיכְדֵין לְשִׁיתָּא קְנִין דְּנַפְקִין מִן מְנַרְתָּא


Three calyxes adorned with their figurations on one branch, with apple and lily; and three calyxes adorned with their figurations on the other branch, with apple and lily: so for the six branches that spread out from the candelabrum.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:34)


וּבִמְנַרְתָּא אַרְבְּעָה כַּלִּידִין מְשַׁקְעִין בְּצִיּוּרֵיהוֹן חִיזוּרְיָהָא וְשׁוֹשַׁנְיָהָא


And upon the candelabrum there shall be four calyxes adorned with their figurations, their apples and lilies.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:35)


וְחֵיזוֹר תְּחוֹת תְּרֵין קְנִין דְּמִינָהּ וְחֵיזוּר תְּחוֹת תְּרֵין קְנִין דְמִינָהּ וְחֵיזוּר תְּחוֹת תְּרֵין קְנִין דְּמִינָהּ לְשִׁיתָּא קְנִין דְּנַפְקִין מִן מְנַרְתָּא


And there shall be an apple under two branches of it, and an apple under two branches of it, and an apple under two branches of it, for the six branches which extend from the candelabrum.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:36)


חֵיזוּרֵיהוֹן וּקְנֵיהוֹן מִינָהּ יְהוֹן כּוּלָהּ נְגִידָא חֲדָא דִדְהַב דְּכֵי


Their apples and their branches shall be of the same; all of it one beaten work of pure gold.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:37)


וְתַעֲבֵיד יַת בּוֹצִינָהָא שַׁבְעָא וּדְלֵיק כַּהֲנָא דְמִתְמַנֵּי יַת בּוֹצִינָהָא וִיהוֹן מְנַהֲרִין כָּל קְבֵיל אַפָּהָא


And thou shalt make its seven lights, and the priest who ministers shall kindle the lights, that they may shine over upon its face.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:38)


וּמַלְקָטַיָיהָא וּמַחְתְּיָיתָא דִדְהַב דְּכֵי


And its snuffers and its shovels of pure gold.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:39)


קַנְטְנַר דִּדְהַב דְּכֵי יַעֲבֵיד יָתָהּ יַת כָּל מָנַיָיא דִידָהּ הָאִלֵּין


Of a talent of pure gold shall he make it and all these its vessels.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 25:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/25:40)


וַחֲמֵי וַעֲבַד בְּצִיּוּרֵיהוֹן דְּאַנְתְּ מִתְחֲמֵי בְּטַוְורָא


And look thou, and make according to their forms which thou hast seen in the mount.



## Chapter 26

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:1)


וְיַת מַשְׁכְּנָא תַעֲבֵיד עֲשַר יְרִיעָן דְּבוּץ שְׁזִיר וְתִכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָונָא וּצְבַע זְהוֹרֵי צִיּוּר כְּרוּבִין עוֹבֵד אוֹמַן תַּעֲבֵיד יַתְהוֹם


And the Tabernacle thou shalt make with ten curtains of fine linen twined, and hyacinth, and purple, and crimson, with figures of kerubin; with the work of the artificer shalt thou make them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:2)


אוּרְכָּא דִירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא עַשְרִין וְתַמְנֵי אַמִּין וּפוּתְיָה אַרְבַּע אַמִּין דִּירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא מִשְׁחֲתָא חֲדָא לְכָל יְרִיעֲתָא


The length of one curtain twenty and eight cubits, and the breadth of one curtain four cubits; the measure of one shall be that of all the curtains.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:3)


חֲמֵשׁ יְרִיעַן תֶּהֶוְיַן מְלַפְּפָן חֲדָא עִם חֲדָא וַחֲמֵשׁ יְרִיעַן חוֹרַנְיָין מְלַפְּפָן חֲדָא עִם חֲדָא


Five curtains shall be coupled one with another, and five other curtains coupled one with another.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:4)


וְתַעֲבֵיד עֲנוּבִין דְּתִיכְלָא עַל אִימְרָא דִירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא מִן צִיטְרֵיהּ בְּבֵית לִיפוּפָא וְכֵן תַּעֲבֵיד בְּאִימְרָא דִירִיעֲתָא בְּבֵית לִיפוּפָא תִּנְיָין


And thou shalt make loops of hyacinth upon the edge of one curtain at the side in the place of coupling, and so shalt thou do in the edge of the second curtain in the place of conjoinment.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:5)


חַמְשִׁין עֲנוּבִין תַּעֲבֵיד בִּירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא וְחַמְשִׁין עֲנוּבִין תַּעֲבֵיד בְּצִיטְרָא דִירִיעֲתָא דִּבְּבֵית לִיפוּפָא תִנְיָינָא מְכַוְונַן עֲנוּבַיָא חָד כָּל קְבֵיל חָד


Fifty loops shalt thou make in one curtain, and fifty loops shalt thou make in the side of the second curtain in the place of conjoinment, so that the loops may answer one to the other.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:6)


וְתַעֲבֵיד חַמְשִׁין פּוּרְפִין דִּדְהַב וּתְלַפֵיף יַת יְרִיעֲתָא חֲדָא עִם חֲדָא בְּפוּרְפַּיָיא וְיִתְחַבֵּר מַשְׁכְּנָא לְמֶהֱוֵי חָד


And thou shalt make fifty taches of gold, and couple the curtains one with another with the taches, and the Tabernacle shall be conjoined to be one.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:7)


וְתַעֲבֵיד יְרִיעַן דְּמֵעִיזֵי לְמִפְרַס עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא חַדְסְרִי יְרִיעַן תַּעֲבֵיד יַתְהֵין


And thou shalt make curtains of goats' hair to extend over the tabernacle: eleven curtains thou shalt make them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:8)


אוּרְכָּא דִירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא תְּלָתִין אַמִּין וּפוּתְיָא אַרְבַּע אַמִּין דִּירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא מִשְׁחֲתָא חֲדָא לְחַדְסְרִי יְרִיעַן


The length of one curtain thirty cubits, and the breadth of one curtain four cubits; and the measure of one (shall be that of each) of the eleven (other) curtains.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:9)


וּתְלָפִיף יַת חֲמֵשׁ יְרִיעַן לְחוֹד כָּל קְבֵיל חַמְשָׁא סִפְרֵי אוֹרַיְיתָא וְיַת שִׁית יְרִיעַן לְחוֹד כָּל קְבֵיל שִׁית סִדְרֵי מַתְנִיתָּא וְתֵיעוֹף יַת יְרִיעֲתָא שָׁתִיתֵיתָא כָּל קְבֵיל אַפֵּי מַשְׁכְּנָא


And thou shalt conjoin five curtains together, corresponding with the five books of the Law; and six curtains together, corresponding with the six orders of the Mishna; and shalt fold the sixth curtain over the front of the tabernacle.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:10)


וְתַעֲבֵיד חַמְשִׁין עֲנוּבִין עַל אִימְרַת דִּירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא מִצִּיטְרָא בְּבֵית לִיפּוּפָא וְחַמְשִׁין עֲנוּבִין עַל אִימְרָא דִירִיעֲתָא בְּבֵית לִיפּוּפָא תִּנְיָינָא


And thou shalt make fifty loops upon the edge of one curtain at the side of the place of coupling; and fifty loops in the edge of the second curtain at the place of coupling.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:11)


וְתַעֲבֵיד פּוּרְפִּין דִּנְחַשׁ חַמְשִׁין וְתֵעוּל יַת פּוּרְפַּיָא בַּעֲנוּבַיָיא וּתְלַפֵף יַת מַשְׁכְּנָא וִיהֵי חָד


And thou shalt make taches of brass, fifty, and put the taches into the loops, and conjoin the tabernacle, that it may be one.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:12)

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:14)

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:15)

And thou shalt make the boards of the tabernacle of sittin woods; they shall stand up, after the manner of their plantation.

Ten cubits the length of the board, and a cubit and a half the breadth of one board.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:17)

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:19)


וְאַרְבְּעִין חוֹמְרִין דִּכְסָף תַּעֲבֵיד תְּחוֹת עַשְרִין לוּחִין תְּרֵין חוֹמְרִין תְּחוֹת לוּחָא חָד לִתְרֵין צִירוֹי וּתְרֵין חוֹמְרִין תְּחוֹת לוּחָא חָד לִתְרֵין צִירוֹי


And thou shalt make forty bases of silver beneath the twenty boards; two bases beneath one board with its two tenons, and two bases under the other board with its two tenons.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:20)


וְלִסְטָר מַשְׁכְּנָא תִנְיָנָא לְרוּחַ צִיפוּנָא עֶשְרִין לוּחִין


And for the second side of the tabernacle towards the north wind twenty boards,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:21)

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:24)


וִיהוֹן מִזַוְוגִין מִלְּרַע וְכַחֲדָא יְהוֹן מְזַוְוגִין עַל רֵישֵׁיהוֹן בְּעִזְקָתָא חֲדָא הֵיכְדֵין יְהֵי לְתַרְוֵיהוֹן לִתְּרֵין זִיוְויָן יְהוֹן

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:25)

And thou shalt make bars of sittin woods, five for the boards of one side of the tabernacle,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:27)


וְחַמְשָׁא נַגְרִין לְלוּחֵי צְטַר מַשְׁכְּנָא תִּנְיָנָא וְחַמְשָׁא נַגְרִין לְלוּחֵי צְטַר מַשְׁכְּנָא לְסוֹפֵיהוֹן מַעֲרָבָא


and five bars for the boards of the second side of the tabernacle, and five bars for the side of the tabernacle at their extremity towards the west,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:28)


וְנַגְרָא מְצִיעָאָה בְּגוֹ לוּחַיָיא מְשַׁלְבִישׁ מִן סְיָיפֵי לִסְיָיפֵי מִן אִילָנָא דִנְצִיב אַבְרָהָם בִּבְאֵרָה דְשָׁבַע וְכַד עֲבָרוּ יִשְרָאֵל יַת יַמָּא קְטָעוּ מַלְאָכַיָא יַת אִילָנָא וְטַלְקוּהוּ לְיַמָא וַהֲוַת טָפֵי עַל אַנְפֵּי מַיָא וַהֲוָה מַלְאָכַיָא מַכְרִיז וַאֲמַר דֵּין הוּא אִילָנָא דִנְצִיב אַבְרָהָם בְּבֵירָא דְשָׁבַע וְצַלִּי תַמָּן בְּשׁוּם מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ וְנַסְבוֹהִי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וַעֲבָדוּ מִנֵיהּ נַגְרָא מְצִיעָאָה אוּרְכֵּיהּ שׁוּבְעִין אַמִּין וּפְרִישָׁן מִתְעַבְדָן בֵּיהּ דְּכַד הֲווֹ מוֹקְמִין יַת מַשְׁכְּנָא הֲוָה מִתְגַּלְגֵּל הֵי כְּעַכְנָא חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר מִלְגֵיו לְלוּחֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא וְכַד הֲוָה מִתְפָּרֵק הֲוָה פַּשִׁיט הֵי כְּחוּטְרָא


And the middle bar in the midst of the boards passing from end to end shall be from the tree which Abraham planted in Beara of Sheba: for when Israel had crossed the sea, the angels cut down the tree and cast it into the sea, and it floated on the face of the waters. And an angel proclaimed, and said, This is the tree which Abraham planted in Beara of Sheba, and prayed there in the name of the Word of the Lord. And the sons of Israel shall take and make thereof the middle bar, seventy cubits in length, and with it shall wondrous things be done: for when they have reared up the tabernacle, it shall go round it like a serpent among the boards of the tabernacle and when they take it down, it shall become straight as a rod.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:29)


וְיַת לוּחַיָא תַחֲפֵי דַהֲבָא וְיַת עִזְקַתְהוֹן תַּעֲבֵיד דַהֲבָא אַתְרָא לְנַגְרִין וְתַחֲפֵי יַת נַגְרַיָא דַהֲבָא


And the boards thou shalt overlay with gold, and make of gold their rings for the place of the bars, and shalt overlay the bars with gold.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:30)


וְתָקִים יַת מַשְׁכְּנָא כְּהִילְכָתָא דְאִתְחֲמִיתָא בְּטַוְורָא


And thou shalt rear the tabernacle according to the manner showed thee in the mountain.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:31)


וְתַעֲבֵיד פַּרְגוֹדָא דְתִכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָונָא וּצְבַא זְהוֹרֵי וּבוּץ שְׁזִיר עוֹבַד אוּמַן יַעֲבֵיד יָתָהּ צוּרִין כְּרוּבִין


And thou shalt make a veil of hyacinth, and purple, and crimson, and fine linen entwined; the work of the artificer shalt thou make it, with figures of kerubin.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:32)


וּתְסַדַּר יָתָהּ עַל אַרְבָּעָה עַמּוּדֵי שִׁיטָא מְחַפִּין דַּהֲבָא וָוֵיהוֹן דַּהֲבָא עַל אַרְבְּעָה חוֹמְרִין דִּכְסַף


And thou shalt range it upon four pillars of sitta, covered with gold, their hooks of gold, upon four bases of silver.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:33)


וְתִתֵּן יַת פַּרְגוֹדָא תְּחוֹת פּוּרְפַּיָא וְתֵיעוֹל לְתַמָּן מִלְגֵיו לְפַרְגוֹדָא יַת אֲרוֹנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא וְתַפְרֵישׁ פַּרְגוֹדָא לְכוֹן בֵּינֵי קוּדְשָׁא וּבֵינֵי קוֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשָׁא

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:34)


וְתִתֵּן יַת כַּפּוּרְתָּא בַּהֲדֵי כְּרוּבַיָא דְּנַפְקִין נְגִיד מִינָהּ בַּקּדֶשׁ קוּדְשַׁיָא

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:35)


וּתְשַׁוֵּי יַת פָּתוֹרָא מִבָּרָא לְפַרְגוֹדָא וְיַת מְנַרְתָּא כָּל קְבֵיל פָּתוֹרָא עַל סְטַר מַשְׁכְּנָא דָרוֹמָא וּפְתוֹרָא תְּסַדֵּר עַל סְטַר צִיפוּנָא


And thou shalt set the Table outside of the veil, and the Candelabrum over against the table on the southern side of the tabernacle; but the table thou shalt arrange on the northern side.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 26:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:36)


וְתַעֲבֵיד פְּרָסָא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכְּנָא תִיכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָונָא וּצְבַע זְהוֹרֵי וּבוּץ שְׁזִיר עוֹבַד צִיּוּר מַחְטָא
