[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/26:37)


וְתַעֲבֵיד לִפְרָסָא חַמְשָׁא עַמּוּדֵי שִׁיטָא וְתַחֲפֵי יַתְהוֹן דַּהֲבָא וָוֵיהוֹן דַּהֲבָא וְתַתִּיךְ לְהוֹן חַמְשָׁא חוֹמְרִין דִּנְחָשָׁא


And thou shalt make for the curtain five pillars of sitta, and cover them with gold, and their nails shall be of gold; and thou shalt cast for them five bases of brass.



## Chapter 27

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:1)


וְתַעֲבֵיד יַת מַדְבְּחָא דְקֵיסֵי שִׁיטָא חָמֵשׁ אַמִּין אוּרְכָּא וְחָמֵשׁ אַמִּין פּוּתְיָא מְרַבַּע יְהֵי מַדְבְּחָא וּתְלַת אַמִּין רוּמֵיהּ


And thou shalt make the Altar of woods of sitta; five cubits the length, and five cubits the breadth: square shall be the altar, and its height three cubits.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:2)


וְתַעֲבֵיד קַרְנוֹהִי עַל אַרְבַּע זִיוְיָתֵיהּ מִנֵיהּ יֶהֶוְיַן קַרְנוֹי זְקִיפִין לְעֵיל וְתַחְפֵי יָתֵיהּ נְחָשָׁא


And thou shalt make its horns upon its four corners: the, horns shall be of it, they shall rise upward, and thou shalt cover it with brass.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:3)


וְתַעֲבֵיד דּוּדְוָתֵיהּ לְמֵירְדָדָא יָתֵיהּ וּמִגְרִפַתֵּיהּ וּמִזִירִקוֹי וּמְשִׁילְיָיתֵיהּ וּמַחְתְּיָיתֵיהּ כָּל מָנוֹי תַּעֲבֵיד נְחָשָׁא


And thou shalt make its pots to carry away its ashes and its shovels, and its basins, and its thuribles; all its vessels thou shalt make of brass.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:4)


וְתַעֲבֵיד לֵיהּ קַנְקַל עוֹבַד מְצַרְתָּא דְנַחָשָׁא וְתַעֲבֵיד עַל מְצַרְתָּא אַרְבַּע עִזְקַן דִּנְחָשָׁא עַל אַרְבַּע צִיטְרוֹי


And thou shalt make for it a grate of network of brass, and upon the network four brass rings upon its four corners.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:5)


וְתִיתֵּן יָתָהּ תְּחוֹת סוֹבְבֵי מַדְבְּחָא מִלְרַע וּתְהֵי מְצַרְתָּא עַד פַּלְגוּת מַדְבְּחָא וְאִין נָפִיל גּוֹמָא אוֹ גוּמְרָא דְאֵשָׁא מֵעִילַוֵי מַדְבְּחָא נָפִיל עִילַוֵי קַנְקַל וְלָא יִתְמְטֵי לְאַרְעָא וְנַסְבִין יָתֵיהּ כַהֲנַיָא מֵעִילַוֵי קַנְקַל וּמְהַדְרִין יָתֵיהּ עַל מַדְבְּחָא


And thou shalt place it under the surroundings of the altar, beneath, that the network may be to the middle of the altar, that, if any fragment or fiery coal fall from the altar, it may fall upon the grate, and not touch the ground, and that the priests may take it from the grate, and replace it upon the altar.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:6)


וְתַעֲבֵיד אֲרִיחִין דְּקֵיסֵי שִׁיטָא וְתַחְפֵּי יַתְהוֹן נְחָשָׁא


And thou shalt make staves of sittin woods, and overlay them with brass;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:7)


וִיהַנְעַל יַת אֲרִיחוֹי בְּעִזְקָתָא וִיהוֹן אֲרִיחַיָא עַל תְּרֵין צִטְרֵי מַדְבְּחָא בְּמֵיטַל מַדְבְּחָא


and thou shalt place the staves within the rings, and the staves shall be on the two sides of the altar in carrying the altar,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:8)


חַלִיל לוּחִין מְלֵי עַפְרָא תַעֲבֵיד יָתֵיהּ הֵיכְמָא דְאַחְמִיתָךְ בְּטַוְורָא הֵיכְדֵין יַעַבְדוּן


hollow: (with) boards filled with dust shalt thou make it; according to what showed thee in the mountain, so shall they make.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:9)


וְתַעֲבֵיד יַת דָּרַת מַשְׁכְּנָא לְרוּחַ עִיבַר דָּרוֹמָא וִילַוָון לְדַרְתָּא דְּבוּץ שְׁזִיר מְאָה אַמִּין אוּרְכָּא לְרוּחַ חֲדָא


And thou shalt make the court of the tabernacle. For the southern side shall be hangings of fine linen twined; a hundred cubits the length for one side.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:10)


וְעַמּוּדוֹי עַשְרִין וְחוּמְרֵיהוֹן עַשְרִין דִּנְחָשָׁא וָוֵי עַמּוּדַיָא וְכִיבּוּשֵׁיהוֹן כְּסָף


And its pillars twenty, and their foundations twenty, of brass; the looks of the pillars, and their rods, of silver.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:11)


וְהֵיכְדֵין לְרוּחַ צִפוּנָא בְּאוּרְכָּא וִילַוָון מְאָה אוּרְכָּא וְעַמּוּדֵיהוֹן עַשְרִין וְחוּמְרֵיהוֹן עַשְרִין דִּנְחָשָׁא וָוֵי עַמּוּדַיָא וְכִיבּוּשֵׁיהוֹן כְּסָף


And so for the northern side, for length, the hangings a hundred (cubits) long, and their pillars twenty, and their foundations twenty, of brass; the hooks of the pillars, and their rods, of silver.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:12)


וּפוּתְיָא דְדַרְתָּא לְרוּחַ מַעַרְבָא וִילַוָון חַמְשִׁין אַמִּין עַמּוּדֵיהוֹן עַשְרָא וְחוּמְרֵיהוֹן עַשְרָא


And for the breadth of the court on the western side, the hangings shall be fifty cubits; their columns ten and their foundations ten.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:13)


וּפוּתְיָא דְדַרְתָּא לְרוּחַ קִידוּמָא מַדִּינְחָא חַמְשִׁין אַמִּין


And for the breadth of the eastern side eastward fifty cubits;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:14)


וַחֲמֵיסְרֵי אַמִּין וִילַוָון לְעִיבְרָא עַמּוּדֵיהוֹן תְּלָתָא וְחוּמְרֵיהוֹן תְּלָתָא


and fifteen cubits the hangings of the side, their pillars three and the their foundations three.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:15)


וּלְעִיבְרָא תִּנְיָנָא חֲמֵשׁ עַסְרֵי סְרָדִין עַמּוּדֵיהוֹן תְּלָתָא וְחוּמְרֵיהוֹן תְּלָתָא


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:16)


וְלִתְרַע דַּרְתָּא פְרָסָא עַשְרִין אַמִּין דְּתִיכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָונָא וּצְבַע זְהוֹרֵי וּבוּץ שְׁזִיר עוֹבַד צִיּוּר מַחְטָא עַמּוּדֵיהוֹן אַרְבָּעָה וְחוּמְרֵיהוֹן אַרְבָּעָה


And for the door of the court shall be a veil of ten cubits of hyacinth, and purple, and crimson, and fine linen twined, of embroidered work; their pillars four, and their foundations four.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:17)


כָּל עַמּוּדֵי דַרְתָּא חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר מְכֻבָּשִׁין כְּסַף וָוֵיהוֹן כְּסַף וְחוּמְרֵיהוֹן דִּנְחָשָׁא


All the pillars of the court round about shall be united with silver rods; their hooks of silver, and their foundations of brass.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:18)


אוּרְכָּא דְדַרְתָּא מְאָה אַמִּין וּפוּתְיָא חַמְשִׁין לְמַעַרְבָא וְחַמְשִׁין לְמַדִינְחָא וְרוּמָא חֲמֵשׁ אַמִּין דְּבוּץ שְׁזִיר וְחוּמְרֵיהוֹן דִּנְחָשָׁא


The length of the court one hundred cubits, and the breadth fifty on the west and fifty on the east, and the height five cubits, of fine linen twined, and their foundations of brass.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:19)


לְכָל מָאנֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא בְּכָל פּוּלְחָנֵיהּ וְכָל מִתְחוֹי וְכָל מִתְחֵי דַרְתָּא חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר דִּנְחָשָׁא


All the vessels of the tabernacle in all its service, and all the pins of the court around, of brass.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:20)


ואתה וְאַנְתְּ תְּפַקֵיד יַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְיִסְבוּן לָךְ מִישְׁחָא דְזֵיתָא דַכְיָא כְּתִישָׁא לְאַנְהוֹרֵי לְאַדְלָקָא בּוֹצִינַיָא תְּדִירָא


And thou shalt instruct the sons of Israel to bring the pure olive oil, beaten, for illumination, that the lamps may burn continually.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 27:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/27:21)


בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא מִבָּרָא לְפַרְגוֹדָא דְעַל סַהֲדוּתָא יְסַדֵּר יָתֵיהּ אַהֲרן וּבְנוֹי מִן רַמְשָׁא עַד צַפְרָא קֳדָם יְיָ קְיַים עֲלָם לְדָרֵיהוֹן מִן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


In the tabernacle of the covenant, without the veil that is before the testimony, Aharon and his sons shall set it in order from evening until morning before the Lord, an everlasting statute to your generations of the house of Israel.



## Chapter 28

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:1)


וְאַנְתְּ קְרַב לְוָותָךְ יַת אַהֲרן אָחוּךְ וְיַת בְּנוֹי עִמֵּיהּ מִגּוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְשַׁמָּשָׁא קֳדָמַי אַהֲרן נָדָב וַאֲבִיהוּא אֶלְעָזָר וְאִיתָמָר בְּנוֹי דְאַהֲרן


And thou, bring near to thee Aharon thy brother, and his sons with him, from among the sons of Israel, to minister before Me: Aharon, Nadab, and Abihu, Elazar and Ithamar, the sons of Aharon.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:2)


וְתַעֲבֵיד לְבוּשָׁא דְקוּדְשָׁא לְאַהֲרן אָחוּךְ לִיְקַר וּלְתוּשְׁבְּחָא


And thou shalt make garments of holiness for Aharon thy brother, for glory and for praise.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:3)


וְאַנְתְּ תְּמַלֵּיל עִם כָּל חַכִּימֵי לִבָּא דְאַשְׁלֵימִית עִמְהוֹן רוּחָא דְחָכְמְתָא וְעָבָדוּ יַת לְבוּשֵׁי אַהֲרן לְקַדְשׁוּתֵיהּ לְשַׁמָּשָׁא קֳדָמַי


And thou shalt speak with all who are wise of heart, and whom I have filled with the spirit of wisdom, that they make the vestments of Aharon to consecrate him to minister before Me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:4)


וְאִלֵּין לְבוּשַׁיָא דְיַעַבְדוּן חוּשְׁנָא וְאֵיפוֹדָא וּמְעִילָא וְכִיתּוּנוּן מְרַמְצִין מַצִינְפַן וְקִימוֹרִין וְיַעַבְדוּן לְבוּשֵׁי קוּדְשָׁא לְאַהֲרן אָחוּךְ וְלִבְנוֹי לְשַׁמָּשָׁא קֳדָמַי


And these are the vestments they shall make: the breastplate and ephoda, and the robe, and the embroidered tunics, the mitres and girdles; and they shall make the holy garments for Aharon thy brother, and for his sons, to minister before Me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:5)


וְהִינוּן יִסְבוּן מִן מָמוֹנְהוֹן יַת דַּהֲבָא וְיַת תִּכְלָא וְיַת אַרְגְּוָונָא וְיַת צְבַע זְהוֹרֵי וְיַת בּוּצָא


And they shall take from their treasures gold, and hyacinth, and purple, and crimson, and fine linen.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:6)


וְיַעַבְדוּן יַת אֵיפוֹדָא תִּכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָונָא צְבַע זְהוֹרֵי וּבוּץ שְׁזִיר עוֹבַד אוּמָן


And they shall make the ephod of hyacinth, and purple, and crimson, and fine linen, twined, the work of the artificer.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:7)


תַּרְתֵּין כַּתְפִין מְלַפְפִין יֶהֱוָן לֵיהּ לִתְרֵין סִטְרוֹי וְיִתְלָפֵף


Two shoulder (pieces) shall it have, conjoined to its two sides, and (so) it shall be united.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:8)


וְהֶמְיַן טִיכוּתֵיהּ דְּעָלוֹי הֵי כְּעוּבְדֵיהּ מִנֵיהּ יְהֵי דַהֲבָא תִּיכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָונָא וּצְבַע זְהוֹרֵי וּבוּץ שְׁזִיר


And the ornamented girdle which is to be upon it shall be of the same work; of gold, hyacinth, purple, and crimson, and fine twined linen, shall it be.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:9)


וְתִיסַב יַת תַּרְתֵּין מַרְגַלְיַין דְּבוּרְלָא וְתִגְלוֹף עֲלֵיהוֹן שְׁמָהַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


And thou shalt take two gems of onyx, and engrave upon them the names of the sons of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:10)


שִׁיתָּא מִן קְצַת שְׁמָהַתְהוֹן עַל מַרְגַלְיָיא חֲדָא וְיַת שְׁמָהַת שִׁיתָּא דְמִשְׁתַּיְירִין עַל מַרְגְּלִיתָא תִּנְיָתָא מְסַדְרִין הֵי כְּיַלְדוּתְהוֹן


Six of their names shall be engraven upon one gem, and the six remaining names upon the second gem; they shall be set in the order of their nativity.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:11)


עוֹבַד אוּמַן יֶהֶוְיַין מַרְגְּלַיְיתָא גְּלוּף חָקִיק וּמְפָרֵשׁ הֵי כִּגְלוֹף דְּעִזְקָא תִּגְלוֹף יַת תַּרְתֵּין מַרְגְּלִיָיתָא עַל שְׁמָהַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מַקְפַן בְּאוּמַנְוַתְהוֹן מְרַמְצָן דִּדְהַב תַּעֲבֵיד יַתְהוֹן


The engraved gems shall be the work of the artificer, engraven, and the engraving be distinct as the engraving of a ring; thou shalt engrave the two gems according to the names of the sons of Israel in their work round about; inset in gold shalt thou make them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:12)


וּתְסַדֵּר יַת תַּרְתֵּין מַרְגָלִיָתָא עַל כִּתְפֵי אֵפוֹדָא מַרְגַלְיַית מְדַכְרַן זְכוּתָא לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְיִטּוֹל אַהֲרן יַת שְׁמָהַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל עַל תְּרֵין כַּתְפוֹי לְדוּכְרָנָא


And thou shalt fix the two gems upon the shoulders of the ephod, to be set for a memorial of righteousness for the sons, of Israel; and Aharon shall bear the names of the sons of Israel upon his two shoulders for a memorial.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:13)


וְתַעֲבֵיד מְרַמְצָן דִּדְהַב


And thou shalt make the sockets of gold.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:14)


וְתַרְתֵּין שִׁישְׁלַן דִּדְהַב דְּכֵי מְתַחְמָן תַּעֲבֵיד יַתְהוֹן עוֹבַד קְלִיעָא וְתִתֵּן יַת שׁוֹשִׁילְתָּא קְלִיעֲתָא עַל מְרַמְצָתָא


And two chains of pure gold, measured shalt thou make them, of twisted work, and insert the twisted chains in the sockets.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:15)


וְתַעֲבֵיד חוֹשֶׁן דִּינָא דְּבֵיהּ מְהוֹדַע דִּינֵיהוֹן דְּיִשְרָאֵל דְּאִתְכַּסֵּי מִן דַּיָינַיָא וְסִדְרֵי נַצְחַן קְרָבֵיהוֹן וּלְמִכַפְּרָא עַל דַּיָינַיָא עוֹבֵד אוּמַן הֵי עוֹבַד אֵפוֹדָא תַעַבְדִינֵיהּ דַּהֲבָא תִּיכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָונָא וּצְבַע זְהוֹרֵי וּבוּץ שְׁזִיר תַּעֲבֵיד יָתֵיהּ


And thou shalt make the BREASTPLATE OF JUDGMENT, by which are made known the judgments that are hidden from the judges; and the order of the victories of their battles, and amends that are made for the judges; the work of the artificer; as the work of the ephod, thou shalt make it; of gold, hyacinth, and purple, and crimson, and fine linen twined, thou shalt make it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:16)


מְרַבַּע יְהֵי עֵיף זַרְתָּא אוּרְכֵּיהּ וְזַרְתָּא פּוּתְיֵיהּ


Square shall it be, doubled; a span its length, and a span its breadth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:17)


וְתַשְׁלֵים בֵּיהּ אַשְׁלָמוּת דְּמַרְגַּלְיָין טַבָאָן אַרְבְּעָא סִדְרִין דִּמַרְגַלְיָין טַבָאָן כָּל קָבֵיל אַרְבַּע טְרִיגוֹנִין דְּעַלְמָא סִדְרָא קַדְמָאָה סִמוּקְתָּא יַרְקְתָא וּבַרְקְתָא סִדְרָא חָד וַעֲלֵיהוֹן חָקִיק וּמְפָרַשׁ שְׁמָהַת שִׁבְטַיָא רְאוּבֵן שִׁמְעוֹן וְלֵוִי


And thou shalt fill it with a fulness of precious stones: four rows of precious gems, answering to the four regions of the world. The first row, carnelian, topaz, carbuncle; and on them shall be engraven and expressed the names of the tribes Reuben, Shemeon, and Levi.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:18)


וְשׁוּם סִדְרָא תִּנְיָנָא אִיזְמוֹרַד וְסַפִּירִינוּן וְכַדְכוֹדֵי וַעֲלֵיהוֹן חָקִיק וּמְפָרַשׁ שְׁמָהַת תְּלָתָא שִׁבְטַיָא יְהוּדָה דָּן וְנַפְתָּלִי


And the name of the second row, smaragd, and sapphire and chalcedony; and upon them shall be engraven and expressed the names of the three tribes, Jehuda, Dan, and Naphtali.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:19)


וְשׁוּם סִדְרָא תְּלִיתָאָה קַנְכִּירִינוּן וְעַרְקִין וְעֵין עֵיגָל וַעֲלֵיהוֹן חָקִיק וּמְפָרַשׁ שְׁמָהַת תְּלָתָא שִׁבְטַיָא גָּד וְאָשֵׁר וְיִשָשׁכָר


And the name of the third row, ligure, and agate, and amethyst; and upon them shall be written and expressed the names of three tribes, Gad, and Asher, and Issakar.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:20)


וְשׁוּם סִדְרָא רְבִיעָאָה כְּרוּם יַמָא רַבָּא וּבִירְלֵיוַות חַלָא וּמַרְגְּנִיַית אַפַּנְטוּרִין וַעֲלֵיהוֹן חָקִיק וּמְפָרַשׁ שְׁמָהַת תְּלָתָא שִׁבְטַיָא זְבוּלוּן יוֹסֵף וּבִנְיָמִין מְשַׁקְעִין בִּדְהַב יְהוֹן בְּאַשְׁלָמוּתְהוֹן


And the name of the fourth row, chrysolite, onyx, and jasper; and upon them shall be written and expressed the names of three tribes, Zebulon, Joseph, and Benjamin. Set in gold shall they be, in their completeness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:21)


וּמַרְגַּלְיָיתָא תֶהֶוְיָין מִתְנַסְבָן עַל שְׁמָהַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל הִינוּן תַּרְתֵּיסְרֵי עַל שְׁמָהַתְהוֹן גְּלִיף חָקִיק וּפָרַשׁ הֵי כִּגְלוֹף דְּעִזְקָא גְּבַר מַרְגָּלִיתֵיהּ עַל שְׁמֵיהּ תֶּהֶוְיַין לִתְרֵיסַר שִׁבְטִין


And the jewels shall be taken according to the names of the sons of Israel, twelve are they according to their names, engraven and set forth as the engraving of a ring; the gem of each man according to his name: (so) shall they be for the twelve tribes.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:22)


וְתַעֲבֵיד עַל חוּשְׁנָא שִׁישְׁלַן מְתַחְמָן עוֹבַד קְלִיעָא דִּדְהַב דְּכֵי


And thou shalt make upon the breastplate measured chains of twisted work of pure gold,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:23)


וְתַעֲבֵיד עַל חוּשְׁנָא תַּרְתֵּין עִזְקַן דִּדְהַב דְּכֵי וְתִתֵּן יַת תַּרְתֵּין עִזְקָתָא עַל תַּרְתֵּין סִטְרֵי חוּשְׁנָא


and shalt make upon the breastplate two rings of pure gold, and put the two rings upon the two sides of the breastplate.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:24)


וְתִתֵּן יַת תַּרְתֵּין קְלִיעַן דִּדְהַב עַל תַּרְתֵּין עִיזְקָתָא בְּסִיטְרֵי חוּשְׁנָא


And thou shalt put the two golden chains upon the two rings in the sides of the breastplate,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:25)


וְיַת תַּרְתֵּין קְלִיעַן דְּעַל תְּרֵין סִיטְרוֹי תִּתֵּן עַל תַּרְתֵּין מְרַמְצָתָא וְתִתֵּן עַל כִּתְפֵי אֵפוֹדָא כָּל קְבֵיל אַנְפּוֹי


and the two chains which are upon the two sides thou shalt put upon the two sockets, and set on the shoulders of the ephod against its front.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:26)


וְתַעֲבֵיד תַּרְתֵּין עִזְקַן דִּדְהַב וְתִתֵּן יַתְהוֹן עַל תְּרֵין סִיטְרֵי חוּשְׁנָא עַל שִיפְמֵיהּ דִּלְעַבְרָא לְאֵפוֹדָא לְגֵיו


And thou shalt make two golden rings, and fix them upon the two sides of the breastplate, in the border of it which is on the edge of the ephod inwardly.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:27)


וְתַעֲבֵיד תַּרְתֵּין עִזְקַן דִּדְהַב וְתִתֵּן יַתְהוֹן עַל תְּרֵין כִּתְפֵי אֵפוֹדָא מִלְרַע מִקְבֵיל אַפּוֹי כָּל קְבֵיל בֵּית לוֹפֵי מֵעִילַוֵי לְהֶמְיַין אֵפוֹדָא


And thou shalt make (likewise) two golden chains, and fix them upon the two shoulders of the ephod beneath, towards its front, towards the place of its conjoinment above the girdle of the ephod;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:28)


וִיטַכְּסוּן יַת חוּשְׁנָא מֵעִזְקָתֵיהּ לְעִזְקַת אֵיפוֹדָא בִּשְׁזִיר חוּטָא דְתִיכְלָא לְמֶהֱוֵי אֲדִיק עַל הֶמְיַין אֵפוֹדָא וְלָא יִתְפְּרֵק חוּשְׁנָא מֵעִילַוֵי אֵפוֹדָא


and they shall unite the breastplate with its rings to the rings of the ephod, with twined ribbon, of hyacinth, to be joined upon the girdle of the ephod, that the breastplate may not be removed from the ephod.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:29)


וְיִטוֹל אַהֲרן יַת שְׁמָהַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל בְּחֹשֶׁן דִּינָא עַל לִבֵּיהּ בִּזְמַן מַעֲלֵיהּ לְקוּדְשָׁא לְדוּכְרָן טַב קֳדָם יְיָ תְּדִירָא


And Aharon shall bear the names of the sons of Israel upon his heart what time he entereth into the sanctuary for a good memorial before the Lord continually.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:30)


וְתִתֵּן בְּחֹשֶׁן דִּינָא יַת אוּרַיָא דִּמְנַהֲרִין מִלֵּיהוֹן וּמְפַרְסְמִין טְמִירָן דְּבֵית יִשְרָאֵל וְיַת תּוּמַיָא דְמַשְׁלִימִין בְּעוֹבָדֵיהוֹן לְכַהֲנָא רַבָּא דְתָבַע אוּלְפַן מִן קֳדָם יְיָ בְּהוֹן דִּבְהוֹן חָקִיק וּמְפָרַשׁ שְׁמָא רַבָּא וְקַדִּישָׁא דְבֵיהּ אִתְבַּרְיַין תְּלַת מְאָה וְעִישַרְתֵּי עַלְמַיָא וְחָקִיק וּמְפָרֵשׁ בְּאֶבֶן שְׁתִיָה דְבָהּ חָתַם מָארֵי עַלְמָא פּוּם תְּהוֹמָא רַבָּא מִן שֵׁירוּיָיא וְכָל מַאן דְּמַדְכַּר הַהוּא שְׁמָא קַדִּישָׁא בְּשַׁעַת אֲנִיקֵי מִשְׁתְּזֵיב וּטְמִירַן מְגַלְיַין וִיהוֹן עַל לִבָּא דְאַהֲרן בִּזְמַן מֵיעֲלֵיהּ קֳדָם יְיָ וְיִטוֹל אַהֲרן יַת דִּין בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל עַל לִבֵּיהּ קֳדָם יְיָ תְּדִירָא


And thou shalt put upon the breastplate of judgment the Uraia, which illuminate their words, and manifest the hidden things of the house of Israel, and the Tumaia, which fulfil (or perfect) their work to the high priest, who seeketh instruction by them before the Lord; because in them is engraven and expressed the Great and Holy Name by which were created the three hundred and ten worlds, and which was engraven and expressed in the foundation stone wherewith the Lord of the world sealed up the mouth of the great deep at the beginning. Whosoever remembereth that holy name in the hour of necessity shall be delivered. And they shall be upon Aharon's heart in the time that he entereth before the Lord; and Aharon shall bear the judgment of the sons of Israel before the Lord continually.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:31)


וְתַעֲבֵיד יַת מִנְטַר מְעִילָא דְאֵפוֹדָא שְׁזִיר חוּטָא דְתִיכְלָא


And thou shalt make the mantle-robe (mintar meila) of the ephod, of twined thread of hyacinth;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:32)


וִיהֵי פּוּם רֵישֵׁיהּ בְּמִיצְעֵיהּ תּוֹרָא בְּשִיפְמֵיהּ יְהֵי מַקִּיף לְפוּמֵיהּ חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר עוֹבַד גִרְדָאֵי הֵי כְפוּם לַשִּׁירַיָא יְהֵי לֵיהּ לָא יִתְבְּזָע


and an orifice shall be in the middle of its upper part; a border shall be upon its opening round about its orifice, the work of the sewer; as the orifice of a coat of mail it shall be, that it may not be rent.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:33)


וְתַעֲבֵיד עַל שִׁיפּוּלוֹי רוּמַנְיַין דְּתִיכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָונָא וּצְבַע זְהוֹרֵי עַל שִׁיפּוּלוֹי חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר וְחִיזוּרְיַין דְּדַהֲבָא בְּמִצְעֵיהוֹן חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר


And thou shalt make upon the hem of it pomegranates of hyacinth, and purple, and crimson, upon its hem, round about, and bells of gold among them round about.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:34)


חִיזוּרָא דְדַהֲבָא וְרִמוֹנָא דִתְכֶלְתָּא וְדִצְבַע זְהוֹרֵי חִיזוּרָא דְדַהֲבָא וְרִימוֹנָא דִתְכֶלְתָּא וְדִצְבַע זְהוֹרֵי עַל שִׁיפּוּלֵי מִינְטַר מְעִילָא חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר סְכוּמְהוֹן שַׁבְעִין וְחַד


A golden bell, and a pomegranate of hyacinth and crimson; a golden bell, and a pomegranate of hyacinth and crimson upon the border of the robe round about; their number, seventy and one.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:35)


וִיהֵי עָטִיף עַל אַהֲרן לְשַׁמָשָׁא וְיִשְׁתְּמַע קָלֵיהּ בִּזְמַן מֵיעֲלֵיהּ לְקוּדְשָׁא קֳדָם יְיָ וּבִזְמַן מִיפְקֵיהּ וְלָא יְמוּת בְּאֵשָׁא מְצַלְהֲבָא


And it shall be a vestment upon Aharon to minister, and its voice shall be heard at the time that he hath entered the holy place before the Lord, and at the time that he cometh out, that he die not by the flaming fire.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:36)


וְתַעֲבֵיד צִיצָא דִדְהַב דְּכֵי וְתִגְלוֹף עֲלוֹי חָקִיק וּמְפָרַשׁ קוֹדֶשׁ לַיְיָ


And thou shalt make a plate (or crown) of pure gold, and engrave upon it with distinct engraving, HOLINESS TO THE LORD.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:37)


וּתְשַׁוֵּי יָתֵיהּ עַל שְׁזִיר חוּטָא דִתְכֶלְתָּא לִמְכַפְּרָא עַל חֲצִיפֵי אַפַּיָא וִיהֵי עַל מַצְנַפְתָּא מֵעִילַוֵי תְּפִילַת רֵישָׁא כָּל קְבֵיל אַפֵּי מִצְנַפְתָּא יְהֵי


And thou shalt put it on a twined ribbon of hyacinth, to make amends for boldness of face; and it shall be on the mitre above the tephillin of the head in front of the mitre shall it be.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:38)


וִיהֵי עַל פְּדַחְתָּא דְבֵית אַפּוֹי דְאַהֲרן מִן צִידְעָא לְצִידְעָא יִתְמְטֵי וְיִטוֹל אַהֲרן יַת עֲוָויַית קוּדְשַׁיָא דִיקַדְשׁוּן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְכָל מַתְּנַת קוּדְשֵׁיהוֹן דִּמְשַׁקְרִין בְּהוֹן וִיהֵי עַל פְּדַחְתֵּיהּ תְּדִירָא לְרַעֲוָא לְהוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ


And it shall be in the front of Aharon's forehead, from time to time as it comes; and Aharon shall bear the iniquity of the consecrated things which the sons of Israel may consecrate; even of all their sacred gifts in which they have been insincere. And it shall be upon the front continually, for their reconciliation before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:39)


וּתְרַמֵץ כִּיתּוּנָא דְבוּצָא לִמְכַפְּרָא עַל שִׁידֵי אַדְמָא זַכָּאָה וְתַעֲבֵיד מִצְנַפְתָּא דְבוּצָא לִמְכַפְּרָא עַל מַגִּיסֵי רַעֲיוֹנֵיהוֹן וְקָמוּר תַּעֲבֵיד עוֹבַד צִיּוּר


And thou shalt weave the tunic of fine linen to expiate for the shedding of innocent blood; and shalt make the tiara of fine linen to expiate for the pride of their thoughts; and the girdle shalt thou make of the work of the embroiderer.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:40)


וְלִבְנֵי אַהֲרן תַּעֲבֵיד כִּתּוּנִין וְתַעֲבֵיד לְהוֹן קְמוּרִין וְכוֹבְעִין תַּעֲבֵיד לְהוֹן לִיקַר וּלְתוּשְׁבְּחָא


And for the sons of Aharon thou shalt make tunics, and girdles, and mitres, for glory and for praise.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:41)


וְתַלְבֵּשׁ יַתְהוֹן יַת אַהֲרן אָחוּךְ וְיַת בְּנוֹי עִמֵיהּ וּתְרַבֵּי יַתְהוֹן וּתְקָרֵב יַת קוּרְבָּנֵיהוֹן וּתְקַדֵּשׁ יַתְהוֹן וִישַׁמְשׁוּן קֳדָמַי


And with them thou shalt invest Aharon thy brother, and his sons with him, and anoint them, that they may offer their oblations; and sanctify them to minister before Me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:42)


וַעֲבֵיד לְהוֹן אֲוַורְסְקִין דְּבוּץ לְכַסָּאָה בְּשַר עִירְיָא מִן אֲתַר קָטוֹר חַרְצֵיהוֹן וְעַד יַרְכֵיהוֹן יְהוֹן


And thou shalt make for them under-garments of fine linen to cover the flesh of their shame; from the girt of the girdle of their loins unto their thighs they shall be.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 28:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/28:43)


וִיהוֹן עַל אַהֲרן וְעַל בְּנוֹי בִּזְמַן מֵעַלְהוֹן לְמַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא אוֹ בְּמִקְרָבֵיהוֹן לְמַדְבְּחָא לְשַׁמָשָׁא בְּקוּדְשָׁא וְלָא יְקַבְּלוּן חוֹבָא וְלָא יְמוּתוּן בְּאֵשָׁא מִצַלְהֲבָא קְיַים עֲלַם לֵיהּ וְלִבְנוֹי בַּתְרוֹי


And they shall be upon Aharon and upon his sons at the time when they enter the tabernacle of testimony, or when they approach the altar to minister in the sanctuary, that they may not receive the punishment of flaming fire. This is an everlasting statute for him and for his sons after him.



## Chapter 29

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:1)


וְדֵין פִּתְגָמָא דְתַעֲבֵיד לְהוֹן לְקַדְשָׁא יַתְהוֹן לְשַׁמָשָׁא קֳדָמַי סַב תּוֹר חַד בַּר תּוֹרֵי דְלָא עֵירוּבִין וְדִכְרִין תְּרֵין שַׁלְמִין


And this is the thing that thou shalt do to them to sanctify them, that they may serve before Me. Take one bullock, the young of a bullock, without spot; and two rams, unblemished (perfect);


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:2)


וּלְחֵים פַּטִּיר וּגְרִיצַן פַּטִּירִין דִּפְּתִיכִין בִּמְשַׁח זֵיתָא וְעֵירוּבִין דִּלְחֵם פַּטִּיר דִּמְשִׁיחִין בִּמְשַׁח זֵיתָא סְמִידָא דְחִינְטִין תַּעֲבֵיד יַתְהוֹן


and unleavened bread, and unleavened cakes, mingled with olive oil; and wafers of unleavened bread anointed with olive oil, and with flour of wheat shalt thou make them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:3)


וְתִתֵּן יַתְהוֹן עַל סַלָא חָד וּתְקָרֵב יַתְהוֹן בְּסַלָא וְיַת תּוֹרָא וְיַת תְּרֵין דִּכְרִין יְסוֹבְרוּן בְּאַסְלָא


And thou shalt put them upon one basket, and offer them in the basket, and the bullock and the two rams they shall bring in a vehicle.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:4)


וְיַת אַהֲרן וְיַת בְּנוֹי תִּקְרַב לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְתִּטְבּוֹל יַתְהוֹן בְּאַרְבְּעִין סְאִין דְּמַיָין חַיָין


And Aharon and his two sons thou shalt bring near to the door of the tabernacle of testimony, and wash them, in four measures of living water.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:5)


וְתִסַב יַת לְבוּשַׁיָא וְתַלְבֵּישׁ יַת אַהֲרן יַת כִּיתּוּנָא וְיַת מִנְטַר מְעִיל אֵפוֹדָא וְיַת אֵפוֹדָא וְיַת חוּשְׁנָא וּתְטַכֵּס יָתֵיהּ בְּהֶמְיַין אֵפוֹדָא


And thou shalt take the vestments, and clothe Aharon with the tunic, and the robe of the ephod, and the ephod, and the breastplate, and gird him (or ordain him) with the girdle of the ephod.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:6)


וּתְשַׁוֵּי מַצְנַפְתָּא עַל רֵישֵׁיהּ וְתִתֵּן יַת כְּלִילָא דְבֵיהּ חֲקִיק שְׁמָא דְקוּדְשָׁא עַל מִצְנַפְתָּא


And thou shalt set the mitre on his head, and put the diadem upon which is engraven the Name of Holiness upon the mitre.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:7)


וְתִיסַב יַת מִשְׁחָא דִרְבוּתָא וְתָרִיק עַל רֵישֵׁיהּ וּתְרַבֵּי יָתֵיהּ


And thou shalt take the anointing oil, and pour it upon his head and anoint him.

וְיַת בְּנוֹי תַקְרֵב וְתַלְבְּשִׁינוּן כִּיתּוּנִין


And thou shalt bring his sons near, and dress them in the tunics,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:9)


וְתִּטְכַס יַתְהוֹן בְּקַמּוּרִין לְאַהֲרן וּבְנוֹי וְתִכְבּוֹשׁ לְהוֹן כּוּבְעִין וּתְהֵי לְהוֹן כְּהוּנָתָא לִקְיַים עֲלָם וּתְקָרֵיב קוּרְבָּנָא דְאַהֲרן וְקוּרְבָּנָא דִבְנוֹי


and indue (ordain) them with the girdles, Aharon and his sons, and wrap on them the mitres; and the priesthood shall be theirs by an everlasting statute. And thou shalt offer the oblation of Aharon, and the oblation of his sons.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:10)


וְתַקְרֵיב יַת תּוֹרָא קֳדָם מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְיִסְמוֹךְ אַהֲרן וּבְנוֹי יַת יְדֵיהוֹן עַל רֵישׁ תּוֹרָא


And thou shalt bring the bullock before the tabernacle of ordinance, and Aharon and his sons shall lay their hands upon the head of the bullock,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:11)


וְתִיכּוֹס יַת תּוֹרָא קֳדָם יְיָ לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא


and thou shalt kill the bullock before the door of the tabernacle of ordinance;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:12)


וְתִיסַב מֵאַדְמָא דְתוֹרָא וְתִתֵּן עַל קַרְנַת מַדְבְּחָא בְּאַדְבָּעָךְ וְיַת כָּל דְּמָא תֵשׁוֹד לִיסוֹדָא דְמַדְבְּחָא


and take of the blood of the bullock, and put (it) on the horns of the altar with thy finger, and all the (remaining) blood thou shalt pour out at the foot of the altar.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:13)


וְתִיסַב יַת כָּל תַּרְבָּא דְחָפֵי עַל בְּנֵי גַוָא וְיַת דְּמִשְׁתְּאַר עַל חֲצַר כַּבְדָא וְיַת תַּרְתֵּין כּוֹלְיַין וְיַת תַּרְבָּא דִעֲלֵיהוֹן וּתְסַדֵּר עַל מַדְבְּחָא


And thou shalt take all the fat that covereth the inwards, and what remaineth upon the caul of the liver, and the two kidneys, and the fat which is upon them, and set them in order upon the altar.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:14)


וְיַת בִּשְרָא דְתוֹרָא וְיַת מוֹשְׁכֵיהּ וְיַת רֵיעֵיהּ תּוֹקַד בְּנוּרָא מִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא חַטָּאתָא הוּא


And the flesh of the bullock, and his skin, and his dung, thou shalt burn with fire without the camp; it is a sin offering.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:15)


וְיַת דִּכְרָא חָד תִּסַב וְיִסְמְכוּן אַהֲרן וּבְנוֹי יַת יְדֵיהוֹן עַל רֵישׁ דִּכְרָא


And the one ram thou shalt take, and Aharon and his sons shall lay their hands on the head of the ram.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:16)


וְתִכּוֹס יַת דִּכְרָא וְתִסַּב יַת אַדְמֵיהּ וְתִדְרוֹק עַל מַדְבְּחָא חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר


And thou shalt kill the ram, and take his blood and sprinkle on the altar round about.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:17)


וְיַת דִּכְרָא תַפְסִיג לְפִסְגוֹי וּתְחַלֵּיל בְּנֵי גַוָאָה וּכְרָעוֹי וּתְסַדֵּר עַל אֵיבָרוֹי וְעַל רֵישֵׁיהּ


And the ram shalt thou divide according to his members (dividings), and wash his inwards and his legs, and set them in order upon his members, and upon his head.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:18)


וְתַסִיק יַת כָּל דִּכְרָא לְמַדְבְּחָא עֲלָתָא הוּא קֳדָם יְיָ לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְּרַעֲוָא קוּרְבָּנָא קֳדָם יְיָ הוּא


And thou shalt offer the whole ram upon the altar, it is a holocaust before the Lord to be accepted with favour, an oblation it is before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:19)


וְתִיסַב יַת דִּכְרָא תִנְיָנָא וְיִסְמוֹךְ אַהֲרן וּבְנוֹי יַת יְדֵיהוֹן עַל רֵישׁ דִּכְרָא


And thou shalt take the second ram, and Aharon and his sons shall lay their hands upon the head of the ram.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:20)


וְתִיכּוֹס יַת דִּכְרָא וְתִיסַב מִן אַדְמֵיהּ וְתִתֵּן עַל חַסְחוּס אוּדְנָא דְאַהֲרן דְּיַמִינָא וְעַל חַסְחוּס אוּדְנָא דִבְנוֹי דְיַמִינָא וְעַל אִילְיוֹן יְדֵיהוֹן דְּיַמִינָא וְעַל אִילְיוֹן רִיגְלֵיהוֹן דְּיַמִינָא וְתִדְרוֹק יַת מוֹתַר אַדְמָא עַל מַדְבְּחָא חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר


And thou shalt kill the ram, and take of his blood, and put upon the tip of Aharon's right ear, and upon the tip of the right ear of his sons, and upon the thumb of their right hands, and upon the toe of their right feet, and pour the rest of the blood upon the altar round about.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:21)


וְתִיסַב מִן אַדְמָא דְעַל מַדְבְּחָא וּמִמִּשְׁחָא דִרְבוּתָא וְתַדִי עַל אַהֲרן וְעַל לְבוּשׁוֹי וְעַל בְּנוֹי וְעַל לְבוּשֵׁי בְּנוֹי עִמֵּיהּ וְיִתְקַדֵּשׁ הוּא וּלְבוּשׁוֹהִי וּבְנוֹהִי וּלְבוּשֵׁי בְּנוֹהִי עִמֵּיהּ


And thou shalt take of the blood which is upon the altar, and of the anointing oil, and drop it upon Aharon, and upon his vestments, and on his sons, and on his sons' vestments with him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:22)


וְתִסַב מִן דִּכְרָא תַּרְבָּא וְאַלְיָתָא וְיַת תַּרְבָּא דְחָפֵי יַת בְּנֵי גַוָא וְיַת דְּמִשְׁתְּאַר עַל חֲצַר כַּבְדָא וְיַת תַּרְתֵּין כּוּלְּיַין וְיַת תַּרְבָּא דַּעֲלֵיהוֹן וְיַת שׁוֹקָא דְיַמִּינָא אֲרוּם דְּכַר קוּרְבָּנַיָא הוּא


And thou shalt take of the ram, the fat and the tail, and the fat that covereth the inwards, and which remaineth upon the caul of the liver, and the two kidneys, and the fat that is upon them, and the right shoulder, for it is the ram of the oblation;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:23)


וַעֲגוֹל דִּלְחֵים חָד וּגְרֵיץ דִּלְחֵים פָּתִיךְ בִּמְשַׁח חַד וְעָרִיךְ חַד מִסַּלָא דְפַטִּירָא דִקְדָם יְיָ


and one round of bread, and one cake of bread mingled with oil, and one wafer from the basket of unleavened bread which is before the Lord;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:24)


וּתְשַׁוֵּי כוֹלָא עַל יְדֵי אַהֲרן וְעַל יְדֵי בְנוֹי וְתָרִים יַתְהוֹן אֲרָמָא קֳדָם יְיָ


and thou shalt put all upon the hands of Aharon and upon the hands of his sons, and shalt uplift them for an elevation before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:25)


וְתִיסַב יַתְהוֹן מִידֵיהוֹן וּתְסַדֵּר בְּמַדְבְּחָא עַל עֲלָתָא לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם יְיָ קוּרְבָּנָא הוּא קֳדָם יְיָ


And thou shalt take them from their hands, and set them in order upon the altar upon the burnt offering, to be received with acceptance before the Lord: it is an oblation before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:26)


וְתִיסַב יַת חַדְיָא מִדְכַר קוּרְבָּנַיָא דִלְאַהֲרן וְתָרֵים יָתָהּ אֲרָמָא קֳדָם יְיָ וִיהֵי לָךְ לְחוּלָק


And thou shalt take the breast of the ram of Aharon's oblation, and uplift it, an elevation before the Lord, and it shall be thy portion.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:27)


וּתְקַדֵּשׁ יַת חַדְיָא דְאַרָמוּתָא וְיַת שׁוֹקָא דְאַפְרָשׁוּתָא דְאִיתְרַם וּדְאִיתְפָּרֵשׁ מִדְכַר קוּרְבָּנַיָא מִידֵי לְאַהֲרן וּמִידֵי לִבְנוֹהִי


And thou shalt consecrate the breast of the elevation and the shoulder of the separation, which have been uplifted and separated from the ram of the oblation from the hand of Aharon and from the hand of his sons.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:28)


וִיהֵי לְאַהֲרן וְלִבְנוֹי לִקְיַים עֲלָם מִן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל אֲרֵי אַפְרָשׁוּתָא הוּא וְאַפְרְשׁוּתָא יְהֵי מִן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מִנִּכְסַת קוּדְשֵׁיהוֹן אַפְרָשׁוּתְהוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ


And it shall be for Aharon and for his sons by a perpetual statute for the sons of Israel; because it is a separation, and a separation it shall be from the sons of Israel from the offerings of their consecration, their separation before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:29)


וּלְבוּשֵׁי קוּדְשָׁא דִלְאַהֲרן יְהוֹן לִבְנוֹי בַּתְרוֹי לְרַבָּאָה בְּהוֹן וּלְקָרָבָא בְּהוֹן יַת קוּרְבָּנֵיהוֹן


And the holy vestments of Aharon shall be to his sons after him, to be anointed in them, and in them to offer their oblations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:30)


שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין יַלְבְּשִׁינוּן כַּהֲנָא דִיקוּם בַּתְרוֹי מִן בְּנוֹי וְלָא מִן לֵיוָאֵי בִּזְמַן דִּיעוֹלִין לְמַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא לְשַׁמָּשָׁא בְּקוּדְשָׁא


Seven days shall the priest wear them, who ariseth after him from his sons, but not from the Levites, at the time when he entereth into the tabernacle of ordinance to minister in the sanctuary.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:31)


וְיַת דְּכַר קוּרְבָּנַיָא תִיסַב וּתְבַשֵּׁל יַת בִּשְרֵיהּ בְּאָתַר קַדִּישׁ


And thou shalt take the ram of the oblation, and boil its flesh in the holy place;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:32)


וְיֵיכוֹל אַהֲרן וּבְנוֹי יַת בִּישְרָא דְדִיכְרָא וְיַת לַחֲמָא דִי בְסַלָא בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא


and Aharon and his sons shall eat the flesh of the ram, and the bread that is in the basket at the door of the tabernacle of ordinance.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:33)


וְיֵיכְלוּן יַתְהוֹן דְּאִתְכַּפֵּר בְּהוֹן לְקָרְבָא יַת קוּרְבַּנְהוֹן לְמַקְדְּשָׁא יַתְהוֹן לְשַׁמָּשָׁא קֳדָמַי וְחִילוֹנֵי לָא יֵיכוּל אֲרוּם קוּדְשָׁא הִינוּן


And they shall eat those things by which atonemen, was made for them in offering their oblations to sanctify them to minister before Me: but the profane shall not eat; for they are holy.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:34)


וְאִין יִשְׁתְּיַיר מִבְּשַר קוּרְבָּנַיָא וּמִן לַחְמָא עַד צַפְרָא וְתוֹקֵיד יַת דְּמִשְׁתְּיַיר בְּנוּרָא לָא יִתְאָכֵיל אֲרוּם קוּדְשָׁא הוּא


And if any of the flesh of the oblation and of the bread remain until the morning, thou shalt burn that which remaineth with fire; it shall not be eaten; for it is sacred.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:35)


וְתַעֲבֵיד לְאַהֲרן וְלִבְנוֹהִי הֵיכְדֵין כְּכָל דִּי פַקֵידִית יָתָךְ שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין תִּקְרַב קוּרְבַּנְהוֹן


And thus thou shalt do to Aharon and to his sons according to all that I have prescribed to thee; seven days thou shalt offer their oblation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:36)


וְתוֹרָא דְחַטָּאתָא תַּעֲבֵיד לְיוֹמָא עַל כִּיפּוּרַיָא וּתְרַבֵּי עַל מַדְבְּחָא בְּכַפָּרוּתָךְ עֲלוֹי וּתְרַבֵּי עֲלֵיהּ לְקַדָּשׁוּתֵיהּ


And a bullock for a sin offering thou shalt offer daily for atonement, and shalt anoint the altar in offering the atonement upon it; thou shalt anoint it, to consecrate it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:37)


שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין תְּכַפֵּר עַל מַדְבְּחָא וּתְקַדֵּישׁ יָתֵיהּ וִיהֵי מַדְבְּחָא קוֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשִׁין כָּל דְּיִקְרַב בְּמַדְבְּחָא יִתְקַדֵּשׁ מִן בְּנֵי אַהֲרן בְּרַם מִן שְׁאַר עַמָּא לֵית אֶפְשַׁר לְהוֹן לְמִקְרַב דִּלְמָא יִתּוֹקְדוּן בְּאֵשָׁא מְצַלְהֲבָא דְנָפִיק מִן קוּדְשַׁיָא


Seven days make thou atonement upon the altar to consecrate it; and it shall be the altar of the Holy of Holies. Every one of the sons of Aharon who approacheth to the altar must be holy; to the rest of the people it is not lawful to approach, lest they be burned with the fiery flame which cometh from the holy place.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:38)


וְדֵין קוּרְבָּנָא דְתַעֲבֵיד עַל מַדְבְּחָא אִמְרִין בְּנֵי שְׁנָה תְּרֵין לְיוֹמָא תְדִירָא


And this is the oblation which thou shalt perform upon the altar; two lambs of one year, daily, evermore.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:39)


יַת אִימְרָא חַד תַּעֲבֵיד בְּצַפְרָא וְיַת אִימְרָא תִנְיָנָא תַעֲבֵיד בֵּינֵי שִׁימְשָׁתָא


The one lamb thou shalt perform in the morning; and the second lamb thou shalt perform between the evenings.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:40)


וְעִשְרוֹנָא סְמִידָא פָּתִיךְ בִּמְשַׁח זֵיתָא כְּתִישָׁא רַבְעוּת הִינָא וְנִיסוּכָא רַבְעוּת הִינָא חַמְרָא לְאִימְרָא חָד


And the tenth of flour mingled with oil of olives beaten; (with) the fourth of a hina, and the libation of a fourth of a hina for the one lamb.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:41)


וְיַת אִימְרָא תִנְיָנָא תַּעֲבֵיד בֵּינֵי שִׁמְשְׁתָא הֵי כְּמִנְחַת צַפְרָא וְהֵי כְנִיסוּכָהּ תַּעֲבֵיד לָהּ לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְּרַעֲוָא קוּרְבָּנָא קֳדָם יְיָ


And the second lamb thou shalt perform between the evenings: it shall be as the mincha of the morning, and as the libation thou shalt do it, to be received with acceptance, an oblation before the Lord;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:42)


עֲלַת תְּדִירָא לְדָרֵיכוֹן בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא קֳדָם יְיָ דְּאַזְמִין מֵימְרִי לְכוֹן תַּמָּן לְמַלָּלָא עִמָךְ תַּמָּן


a perpetual holocaust for your generations at the door of the tabernacle of ordinance before the Lord; where I will appoint My Word to (meet) thee there, to speak with thee there.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:43)


וְאֵיזַמֵן מֵימְרִי תַמָּן לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְאֵקַדֵשׁ בְּרַבָּנֵיהוֹן בְּגִין יְקָרִי


And there I will appoint My Word (to meet) with the sons of Israel, and I will be sanctified in their rulers for My glory.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:44)


וְאֵיקַדֵּשׁ יַת מַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא וְיַת מַדְבְּחָא וְיַת אַהֲרן וְיַת בְּנוֹי אֵיקַדֵּשׁ לְשַׁמָּשָׁא קֳדָמָי


And I will sanctify the tabernacle of ordinance and the altar; and Aharon and his sons will I sanctify to minister before Me:


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:45)


וְאַשְׁרֵי שְׁכִינְתִּי בְּגוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְאֶהֱוֵי לְהוֹן לֵאלָהָא


and My Shekinah shall dwell in the midst of the sons of Israel, and I will be their God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 29:46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/29:46)


וְיֵדְעוּן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל אֲרוּם אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָהוֹן דִּי הַנְפֵיקִית יַתְהוֹן פְּרִיקִין מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם לְאַשְׁרָאָה שְׁכִינְתִּי בֵּינֵיהוֹן אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ אֱלָהָהוֹן


And the sons of Israel shall know that I am their God, who led them out free from the land of Mizraim to make My Shekinah dwell among them. I am the Lord their God.



## Chapter 30

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:1)


וְתַעֲבֵיד מַדְבְּחָא לְאַסָקָא עֲלוֹי קְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמַיָא דְקִיסֵי שִׁיטָא תַעֲבֵיד יָתֵיהּ


And thou shalt make an altar on which to burn incense of perfumes: of sitta wood shalt thou make it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:2)


אַמְתָא אוּרְכֵּיהּ וְאַמְתָא פּוּתְיֵהּ מְרַבַּע יְהֵא וְתַרְתֵּין אַמִּין רוּמֵיהּ מִינֵיהּ יְהוֹן קַרְנוֹי זְקִיפוּן


A cubit its length, and a cubit its breadth; foursquare shall it be: and two cubits its height: and its upright horns shall be of it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:3)


וְתַחְפֵי יָתֵיהּ דְּהַב דְּכֵי יַת אִיגְרֵיהּ וְיַת כּוֹתְלוֹי חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר וְיַת קַרְנוֹי וְתַעֲבֵיד לֵיהּ זֵיר דִּדְהַב חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר


And thou shalt overlay it with pure gold, its top, and its wall round about, and its horns; and make for it a border of gold round about.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 30:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:4)


וְתַרְתֵּין עִיזְקַן דִּדְהַב תַּעֲבֵיד לֵיהּ מִלְרַע לְזֵירֵיהּ עַל תְּרֵין זִיוְיָיתֵיהּ תַּעֲבֵיד עַל תְּרֵין צִיטְרוֹי וִיהֵי לְאַתְרָא לַאֲרִיחַיָא לְמֵיטַל יָתֵיהּ בְּהוֹם


And two golden rings make thou for it beneath its border at the two corners, thou shalt make upon its two sides, to be the place for the staves by which it may be carried.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 30:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:5)


וְתַעֲבֵיד יַת אֲרִיחַיָא דְקִיסֵי שִׁיטָא וְתַחֲפֵי יַתְהוֹן דַּהֲבָא


And thou shalt make the staves of sitta wood, and cover them with gold.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 30:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:6)


וְתִתֵּן יָתֵיהּ קֳדָם פַּרְגּוֹדָא דְעַל אֲרוֹנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא קֳדָם כַּפּוּרְתָּא דְעַל סַהֲדוּתָא דְּאֵיזַמֵּן מֵימְרִי לָךְ תַּמָּן


And thou shalt place it before the veil which is over the ark of the testimony, before the mercy seat that is upon the testimony, where I will appoint My Word to be with thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 30:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:7)


וְיַקְטֵר עֲלוֹי אַהֲרן קְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמִין בִּצְפַר בִּצְפַר בְּאַתְקָנוּתֵיהּ יַת בּוֹצִינַיָא יַקְטְרִינָהּ


And Aharon shall burn sweet incense upon it from morning to morning: when he ordereth the lamps, be shall burn it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 30:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:8)


וּבְאַדְלָקוּת אַהֲרן יַת בּוֹצִינַיָא בֵּינֵי שִׁמְשְׁתָא יַקְטְרִינָהּ קְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמִין תְּדִירָא קֳדָם יְיָ לְדָרֵיכוֹן


And when Aharon kindleth the lamps between the evenings, he shall burn sweet incense perpetually before the Lord in your generations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 30:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:9)


לָא תִסְקוּן עֲלוֹי קְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמִין דְּעַמְמִין נוּכְרָאִין וְעָלָתָא וּמִנְחָתָא וְנִיסוּכִין לָא תְנַסְכוּן עֲלוֹי


Thou shalt not offer thereon the sweet incense of strange peoples, nor offer upon it burnt offerings, or minchas, nor pour libations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 30:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:10)


וִיכַפֵּר אַהֲרן עַל קַרְנוֹי חֲדָא בְּשַׁתָּא מִן אֲדָם חַטָּאתָא דְכִפּוּרַיָא חֲדָא זִמְנָא בְּשַׁתָּא יְכַפֵּר עֲלוֹי בְּיוֹמָא דְכִפּוּרֵי לְדָרֵיכוֹן קוֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשִׁין הוּא קֳדָם יְיָ


And Aharon shall expiate upon its horns once in the year with the blood of the sin offering for an expiation: once in the year shall he make atonement upon it on the day of atonement in your generations: it shall be most holy before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 30:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:11)


וידבר וּמַלֵּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake unto Mosheh, saying,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 30:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:12)


אֲרוּם תְּקַבֵּל יַת חוּשְׁבַּן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְמִנְיָינֵיהוֹן וְיִתְּנוּן גְּבַר פּוּרְקַן נַפְשֵׁיהּ קֳדָם יְיָ כַּד תִּמְנֵי יַתְהוֹן וְלָא יְהֵי בְּהוֹן נִזְקָא דְמוֹתָא כַּד תִּמְנֵי יַתְהוֹן


When thou takest the sum of the sons of Israel according to their number, they shall give every man the ransom of their souls before the Lord when thou numberest them; that there may not be among them the calamity of death when thou dost number them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 30:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:13)


דֵּין שִׁיעוּרָא אִיתְחַמֵי לְמשֶׁה בְּטַוְורָא הֵי כְדִינְרָא דְאֵשָׁא וְהֵיכְדֵין אָמַר לֵיהּ כְּדֵין יִנָּתְנוּן כָּל מַאן דְּעָבַר עַל מִנְיָנַיָא פַּלְגוּת סִילְעָא בְּסִילְעָא דְטַבְעָא דְקוּדְשָׁא עַשְרִין מָעִין סִילְעָא פַּלְגוּת סִילְעָא אַפְרָשׁוּתָא קֳדָם יְיָ


This valuation was shown to Mosheh in the mountain as with a denarius of fire, and thus spake He to him: So shall every one who passeth to the numberment give a half shekel of the coin of the sanctuary: (a half shekel is twenty manin:) the half shekel is to be the separation before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 30:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:14)


כָּל מַאן דְּעָבַר עַל מִנְיָינַיָא מִבַּר עַשְרִין שְׁנִין וּלְעֵילָא יִתֵּן אַפְרָשׁוּתָא קֳדָם יְיָ


Every one who passeth to the numbering, from a son of twenty years and upwards, shall give the seperation before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 30:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:15)


דְּעַתִּיר לָא יִסְגֵי וּדְמִסְכֵּן לָא יַזְעַר מִפַּלְגוּת סִלְעָא לְמִתַּן יַת אַפְרָשׁוּתָא קֳדָם יְיָ לְכַפָּרָא עַל נַפְשָׁתֵיכוֹן


He who is rich shall not add to, and he who is poor shall not diminish from, the half shekel in giving the seperation before the Lord, to atone for your souls.

וְתִיסַב יַת כַּסְפָּא דְכִיפּוּרַיָא מִן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְתִתֵּן יָתֵיהּ עַל עֲבִידַת מַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא וִיהֵי לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְדוּכְרַן טַב קֳדָם יְיָ לְכַפָּרָא עַל נַפְשָׁתֵיכוֹן


And thou shalt take the silver of the ransom from the sons of Israel, and apply it to the work of the tabernacle of ordinance; that it may be for the sons of Israel for a good memorial before the Lord, as a ransom for your souls.

And thou shalt make a laver of brass, and its foundation of brass, for purification; and shalt set it between the tabernacle of ordinance and the altar, and put water therein.

וְאַנְתְּ סַב לָךְ בּוּסְמִין בִּשְׁרוּיָיא מוֹר בְּחִיר מַתְקַל חֲמֵשׁ מְאָה מָנִין וְקִינְמוֹן בּוֹשֶם פַּלְגוּתֵיהּ מַתְקַל מָאתָן וְחַמְשִׁין מָנִין וּקְנֵהּ בּוּשְמָא מַתְקַל מָאתָן וְחַמְשִׁין מָנִין

and the altar of burnt offering and all its vessels, and the laver and its foundation,

And the Lord said to Mosheh, Take to thee spices, balsam, and onycha, and galbanum, choice spices, and pure frankincense, weight for weight shall it be.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:35)

And confect therewith a fragrant incense, the work of the compounder, a pure and sacred mixture.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:36)


וְתִכְתּוֹשׁ מִינָהּ וְתָדִיק וְתִתֵּן מִינָהּ קֳדָם סַהֲדוּתָא בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא דְּאֵיזַמִין מֵימְרִי לָךְ תַּמָּן קוֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשִׁין תְּהֵא לְכוֹן


And beat, and make it small, and of it some shalt thou put before the testimony in the tabernacle of ordinance, where I will appoint My Word to be with thee. Most sacred shall it be to you.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/30:37)

And of the sweet incense thou shalt make, the like shall not be made among you; it shall be sacred to you before the Lord:

the man who maketh the like of it to smell thereto shall be destroyed from his people.

And the Lord Spake with Mosheh, saying,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/31:2)

Look, Mosheh, I have called by name the good Bezalel: bar Uri bar Hur, of the tribe of Jehudah,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/31:3)


וְאַשְׁלֵמִית עִמֵּיהּ רוּחַ קוּדְשָׁא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ בְּחָכְמָתָא בְּסוּכְלְתָנוּ וּבְמַנְדְּעָא וּבְכָל עִיבִידְתָּא


and have fulfilled him with the Spirit of holiness from before the Lord, in wisdom and in intelligence, in knowledge, and in all workmanship;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/31:4)


לְמֵיחְשַׁב בְּרַעֲיוֹנֵיהוֹן הֵיךְ לְמֶעֱבַד בְּדַהֲבָא וּבְכַסְפָּא וּבִנְחָשָׁא


to think in their thoughts so as to work (perfectly) in gold, and in silver, and in brass;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 31:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/31:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/31:5)


וּבְאַגְלָפוּת מַרְגְּלִיתָא לְאַשְׁלָמָא וּבְנַגָרוֹת קִיסָא לְמֶעֱבַד כָּל עִיבִידְתָּא


and in the cutting of jewels for their insetting, and in the carving of woods, to make all manner of work.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 31:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/31:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/31:6)


וַאֲנָא הָא מַנְיַית עִמֵּיהּ יַת אָהֳלִיאָב בַר אֲחִיסָמָךְ לְשִׁבְטָא דְדָן וּבְלִבָּא דְּכָל חַכִּימֵי לִיבָּא אוֹסִיפִית רוּחַ חוּכְמָתָא וְיַעַבְדוּן יַת כָּל מַה דְּאַפְקֵדִיתָךְ


And, behold, I have appointed with him Ahaliab bar Achisamah, of the tribe of Dan; and in the heart of every one wise hearted I have added the Spirit of wisdom, that they may perform all whatever I have commanded thee.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/31:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/31:13)


וְאַנְתְּ תְּמַלֵּיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְמֵימַר בְּרַם יַת יוֹמֵי שַׁבַּיָא דִי לִי תִנְטְרוּן אֲרוּם אָת הוּא בֵּין מֵימְרִי וּבֵינֵיכוֹן לְדָרֵיכוֹן לְמִידוֹעַ אֲרוּם אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ מְקַדִּישְׁכוֹן


Also, speak thou with the sons of Israel, saying, Ye shall keep the day of My Sabbaths indeed; for it is a sign between My Word and you, that you may know that I am the Lord who sanctify you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 31:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/31:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/31:14)


וְתִנְטְרוּן יַת שַׁבְּתָא אֲרוּם קוּדְשָׁא הִיא לְכוֹן דְּיַפְסִינָהּ אִיתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵיל אֲרוּם כָּל מַאן דְּיַעֲבֵיד בָּהּ עִיבִידְתָּא וְיִשְׁתֵּיצֵי בַּר נְשָׁא הַהוּא מֵעַמֵּיהּ


Ye shall keep the Sabbath, because it is holy to you; whosoever profaneth it, dying he shall die; whoso doeth work therein, that man shall be destroyed from his people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 31:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/31:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/31:15)


שִׁיתָּא יוֹמִין יִתְעֲבֵיד עִיבִידְתָּא וּבְיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה שַׁבָּת שַׁבְּתָא קוּדְשָׁא קֳדָם יְיָ כָּל מַאן דְּיַעֲבֵיד עִיבִידְתָּא בְּיוֹמָא דְשַׁבְּתָא אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵיל בְּמַטְלוּת אַבְנִין


Six days ye shall do work; but the seventh day is Sabbath, the holy Sabbath before the Lord. Whoso doeth work upon the Sabbath, dying he shall die, by the casting of stones.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/31:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/31:17)


בֵּין מֵימְרִי וּבֵין בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל אָת הִיא לְעַלָם אֲרוּם בְּשִׁיתָּא יוֹמִין בְּרָא יְיָ וְשַׁכְלֵיל יַת שְׁמַיָא וְיַת אַרְעָא וּבְיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה שְׁבָת וְנָח


between My Word and the sons of Israel it is a sign for ever. For in six days the Lord created and perfected the heavens and the earth; and in the seventh day He rested and refreshed.

וִיהַב לְמשֶׁה כַּד פָּסַק לְמַלָּלָא עִמֵּיהּ בְּטַוְורָא דְסִינַי תְּרֵין לוּחֵי סַהֲדוּתָא לוּחֵי דְאֶבֶן סַפִּירִינוּן מִכּוּרְסֵי יְקָרָא מַתְקַלְהוֹן אַרְבְּעִין סְאִין כְּתִיבִין בְּאֶצְבְּעָא דַיְיָ


And He gave to Mosheh, when He had finished to speak with him in Mount Sinai, the two tablets of the testimony, tablets of sapphire-stone from the throne of glory, weighing forty sein, inscribed by the finger of the Lord.



## Chapter 32

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:1)


וַחֲמָא עַמָּא אֲרוּם אִשְׁתְּהֵי משֶׁה לְמֵיחוּת מִן טַוְורָא וְאִתְכְּנֵישׁ עַמָּא עַל אַהֲרן כַּד חֲמוּן דְּעָבַר זִימְנָא דְקָבַע לְהוֹן וַאֲזַל סַטָנָא וְאַטְעִינוּן וְהָדַר לִבְּהוֹן זְחוֹחִין וַאֲמַרוּ לֵיהּ קוּם עִיבַד לָנָא דַחֲלַן דִּי טַיְילִן קָדָמָנָא אֲרוּם דֵּין משֶׁה גַבְרָא דְאַסְקָנָא מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם אִשְׁתַּלְהַב בְּטַוְורָא דְאֵשָׁא מְצַלְהֲבָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ לָא אִשְׁתְּמוֹדְעָנָא מַה הֲוָה לֵיהּ בְּסֵיפֵיהּ


But the people saw that Mosheh delayed to come down from the mount, and the people gathered together unto Aharon, when they saw that the time he had appointed to them had passed; and Satana had come, and caused them to err, and perverted their hearts with pride. And they said to him, Arise, make us gods that shall go before us; for as for this Mosheh the man who brought us up from the land of Mizraim, he may have been consumed in the mountain by the fire which flameth from before the Lord, (and) we know not what hath befallen him in his end.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:2)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן אַהֲרן פְּרִיקוּ קָדָשֵׁי דְדַהֲבָא דִבְאוּדְנֵי נְשֵׁיכוֹן בְּנֵיכוֹן וּבְנָתֵיכוֹן וְאַיְיתוּ לְוָתִי


And Aharon said to them, Deliver the golden rings that are in the ears of your wives, your sons, and your daughters, and bring them to me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:3)


וְסָרִיבוּ נְשַׁיָא לְמִיתַּן תַּכְשִׁיטֵיהוֹן לְגַבְרֵיהוֹן וּמִן יַד פְּרִיקוּ כָּל עַמָּא יַת קָדָשֵׁי דְדַהֲבָא דִי בְאוּדְנֵיהוֹן וְאַיְיתִיוּ לְאַהֲרן


And their wives denied themselves to give their ornaments to their husbands; and all the people at once delivered up the golden rings which were in their ears, and brought them to Aharon.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:4)


וּנְסֵיב מִידֵיהוֹן וְצַר יָתֵיהּ בְּשׁוֹשִׁיפָא וּרְמָא יָתֵיהּ בְּטוּפְרָא וְעַבְדֵיהּ עֵיגַל מַתְּכָא וַאֲמָרוּ אִלֵּין דַּחַלְתָּךְ יִשְרָאֵל דְּהַנְפָקוּךְ מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם


And he took them from their hands, and bound them in a wrapper, and wrought it with a tool, having made a molten calf; and he said, These, Israel, are thy gods, which brought thee forth from the land of Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:5)


וַחֲמָא אַהֲרן יַת חוּר נְכִיס קֳדָמוֹי וּדְחִיל וּבְנָא מַדְבְּחָא קֳדָמוֹי וּקְרָא בְּקַל עָצִיב וַאֲמַר חַגָּא דַיְיָ מְחַר מִנִּכְסַת קְטוֹל בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבוֹי אִלֵּין דְּכָּפְרִין בְּמָרֵיהוֹן וּפָרְגוּ עִיקַר שְׁכִינְתֵּיהּ יְיָ בְּעִיגְלָא הָדֵין


For Aharon had seen Hur slain before him, and was afraid; and he builded an altar before him, and Aharon cried with doleful voice, and said, Let there be a feast before the Lord tomorrow, of the sacrifice of the slain of these adversaries who have denied their Lord, and have changed the glory of the Shekinah of the Lord for this calf.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:6)


וְאַקְדִימוּ מִיּוֹמָא מָחְרָא וְאַסִיקוּ עֲלָוָון וּקְרִיבוּ נִיכְסִין וְאַסְחַר עַמָא לְמֵיכוֹל וּלְמִישְׁתֵּי וְקָמוּ לְהוֹן לִמְגַחֲכָא בְּפוּלְחָנָא נוּכְרָאָה


And on the day following, they arose, and sacrificed burnt-offerings; and the people sat around to eat and to drink, and rose up to disport themselves with strange service.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:7)


וּמַלֵּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה אִיזֵיל חוּת מִן רְבוּת יְקָרָךְ דְּלָא יְהָבִית לָךְ רְבוּתָא אֱלָהֵן בְּגִין יִשְרָאֵל וּכְדוּן חַבִּילוּ עוֹבָדֵיהוֹן עַמָּךְ דְּאַפֵּיקְתָּא מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם


And the Lord said to Mosheh, Descend from the greatness of thine honour; for I have not given thee greatness except on account of Israel. But now thy people, whom thou didst bring up from the land of Mizraim, have corrupted their works;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:8)


סָטוּ בִּפְרִיעַ מִן אוֹרְחָא דְפַקֵידְתִּינוּן בְּסִינַי לָא תַעַבְּדוּן לְכוֹן צְלַם וְצוּרָה וְכָל דְּמוּ וּכְדוֹן עָבְדוּ לְהוֹן עֵיגַל מַתְּכָא וּסְגִידוּ לֵיהּ וְדַבְּחוּ לֵיהּ וְאַכְרִיזוּ קֳדָמוֹי אִלֵּין דַּחַלְתָּךְ יִשְרָאֵל דְאַסְקוּךְ מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם


quickly have they declined from the way which I taught them in Sinai, (that) ye shall not make yourselves image, or figure, or any similitude; for now have they made to them a molten calf, and have worshipped it, and sacrificed to it, and proclaimed before it, These are thy gods, Israel, which brought thee up from the land of Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:9)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה גְּלֵי קֳדָמָי זְדוֹנְהוֹן דְּעַמָּא הָדֵין וְהָא עַם קְשֵׁי קְדַל הוּא


And the Lord said to Mosheh, The pride of this people is manifest before Me, and, behold, it is a people of hard neck.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:10)


וּכְדוּן אַנַח בְּעוּתָךְ וְלָא תַפְגִין עֲלֵיהוֹן קֳדָמַי וְאַרְתַח רְגַז אֵשָׁתָא תַקִיפְתָּא בְּהוֹן וְאֵשֵׁיצִינוּן וְאֶעְבַּד יָתָךְ לְעַם סַגִי


And now, cease from thy prayer, and cry not for them before Me; for I will let My anger burn like strong fire against them, and consume them, and I will make thee a great people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:11)


וְאִתְחַלְחַל משֶׁה מִן רְתִיתָא וּשְׁרֵי לְצַלָּאָה קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהֵיהּ וַאֲמַר לְמָא יְיָ יִתְקוֹף רוּגְזָךְ בְּעַמָּךְ דְּהַנְפָּקַת מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם בְּחֵיל רַב וּבִידָא תַקִיפָא


And Mosheh was shaken with fear, and began to pray before the Lord his God; and he said, Wherefore should Thy wrath, O Lord, prevail against Thy people whom Thou didst bring up from the land of Mizraim, with great power and with a mighty hand.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:12)


לְמָא דֵין יֵימְרוּן מִצְרָאֵי דְמִשְׁתַּיְירִין לְמֵימָר בְּבִישָׁא אַנְפֵּיקִינוּן לְקַטְלָא יַתְהוֹן בֵּינֵי טַוְורַיָא תָּבוֹר וְחֶרְמוֹן וְשִירְיוֹן וְסִינַי וּבְגִין לְשֵׁיצָאָה יַתְהוֹן מֵעַל אַנְפֵּי אַרְעָא תּוּב מִתְקוֹף רוּגְזָךְ וִיהֱוֵי תוֹהוּ קֳדָמָךְ עַל בִּישְׁתָּא דְמַלֵּילַת לְמֶעְבַּד לְעַמָּךְ


Why should the Mizraee who are remaining say, It was for evil that He led them out, to kill them among the mountains of Tabor and Hermon, and Sirion and Sinai, and to destroy them from the face of the earth? Turn from Thy strong anger, and let there be relenting before Thee over the evil that Thou hast threatened to do unto Thy people.

Remember Abraham, and Izhak, and Israel, Thy servants, to whom Thou didst swear in Thy Word and didst say to them, I will multiply your children as the stars of the heavens, and all this land of which I have told you will I give to your sons, and they shall inherit for ever.

וּשְׁמַע יְהוֹשֻׁעַ יַת קַל עַמָּא כַּד מְיַבְּבִין בְּחֶדְוָא קֳדָם עִיגְלָא וַאֲמַר לְמשֶׁה קַל סִידְרֵי קְרָבָא בְּמַשְׁרִיתָא
