###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:18)


וַאֲמַר לָא קַל גִּיבָּרִין דְּנַצְחִין בְּסִידְרֵי קְרָבָא וְלָא קַל חֲלָשִׁין דְּמִתְנַצְחִין מִן בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבֵיהוֹן בְּסִדְרֵי קְרָבָא קַל פַּלְחֵי פּוּלְחָנָא נוּכְרָאָה וּמְגַחֲכָן קַדְמָאָה אֲנָא שְׁמַע


But he said, It is not the voice of the strong, who are victorious in battle, nor the voice of the weak, who are overcome by their adversaries in the fight; but the voice of them who serve with strange service, and who make merriment before it, that I hear.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:19)


וַהֲוָה כַּד קָרֵיב משֶׁה לְמַשְׁרִיתָא וַחֲמָא יַת עִיגְלָא וְחִינְגִין בִּידֵיהוֹן דְּרַשִׁיעַיָא מְחַנְגִין וּמְגַחֲנִין קֳדָמוֹי וְסִטְנָא הֲוָה בְּגַוֵּיהּ מְטַפֵּז וּמְשַׁוֵּור קֳדָם עַמָּא וּמִן יַד תְּקַף רָתַח רוּגְזֵיהּ דְּמשֶׁה וּטְלַק מִן יְדוֹי יַת לוּחַיָא וּתְבַר יַתְהוֹן בְּשִׁיפּוּלֵי טַוְורָא בְּרַם כְּתָבָא קַדִּישָׁא דִי בְּהוֹן הֲוָה פָּרַח וְטַיַיס לְאַוֵיר שְׁמַיָיא וַהֲוָה צָוַוח וַאֲמַר חֲבַל עַל עַמָּא דְשָׁמְעוּ בְּסִינַי מִן פּוּם קוּדְשָׁא לָא תַעַבְדוּן לְכוֹן צְלַם וְצוּרָה וְכָל דְּמוּי וּלְסוֹף אַרְבְּעִין יוֹמִין עֲבָדוּ עֵיגַל מַתְּכָא דְלֵית בֵּיהּ מַשָׁשָׁא


And it was when Mosheh came near the camp, and saw the calf, and the instruments of music in the hands of the wicked, who were dancing and bowing before it, and Satana among them dancing and leaping before the people, the wrath of Mosheh was suddenly kindled, and he cast the tables from his hands, and brake them at the foot of the mountain;- the holy writing that was on them, however, flew, and was carried away into the air of the heavens;- and he cried, and said, Woe upon the people who heard at Sinai from the mouth of the Holy One, Thou shalt not make to thyself an image, or figure, or any likeness,- and yet, at the end of forty days, make a useless molten calf!

And he took the calf which they had made, and burned it in fire, and bruised it into powder, and cast (it) upon the face of the water of the stream, and made the sons of Israel drink; and whoever had given thereto any trinket of gold, the sign of it came forth upon his nostrils.

And Mosheh said unto Aharon, What did this people to thee, that thou hast brought upon them a great sin?

and they said to me, Make us gods that may go before us; for this Mosheh, the man who brought us up from the land of Mizraim, is consumed in the mountain, by the flaming fire from before the Lord, and we know not what hath been done to him in his end.

And Mosheh saw that the people were naked; for they had been stripped by the hand of Aharon of the holy crown which was upon their head, inscribed and beautified with the great and glorious Name; and that their evil report would go forth among the nations of the earth, and they would get to them an evil name unto their generations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:26)

And Mosheh stood in the sanhedrin gate of the camp, and said, Who feareth the Lord, let him come to me. And there gathered to him all the sons of Levi.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:27)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן כִּדְנַן אָמַר יְיָ אֱלָהָא דְיִשְרָאֵל כָּל מַאן דְּדָבַח לְטַעֲוַת עַמְמַיָא יִתְקְטֵל בְּסַיְיפָא וּכְדוּן עִיבְרוּ וְתוּבוּ מִתְּרַע סַנְהֶדְרִין לִתְרַע בֵּי דִינָא בְּמַשְׁרִיתָא וּבְעוֹי מִן קֳדָם יְיָ דְיִשְׁתְּבֵיק לְכוֹן חוֹבָא דָא וְאִתְפְּרָעוּ מִן רַשִׁיעַיָא פַּלְחֵי פּוּלְחָנָא נוּכְרָאָה וּקְטִילוּ אֲפִלּוּ גְבַר יַת אָחוֹי וּגְבַר יַת חַבְרֵיהּ וְאֵינַשׁ יַת קָרִיבֵיהּ


And he said to them, Thus hath said the Lord, the God of Israel, Whosoever hath sacrificed to the idols of the Gentiles, let him be slain with the sword. And now, go, pass through from the gate of the sanhedrin to the gate of the house of judgment, in the camp, and with prayer before the Lord that He will forgive you this sin, take vengeance upon the wicked workers of strange worship and slay, even a man his brother, and a man his companion, and a man his neighbour.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:28)

And the sons of Levi did according to the word of Mosheh; and of the people who had the mark in their nostrils there fell that day by the slaughter of the sword about the number of three thousand men.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:29)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה קְרִיבוּ קוּרְבָּנֵכוֹן עַל שְׁפִיכוּת אַדְמָא דִי בִידֵיכוֹן וְיִתְכַּפֵּר לְכוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ אֲרוּם נְגַעֲתּוּן גְּבַר בִּבְרֵיהּ וּבְאָחוֹי וּלְאַיְיתָאָה עֲלֵיכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין בִּרְכָתָא


And Mosheh said, Offer your oblation for the shedding of the blood that is upon your hands, and make atonement for yourselves before the Lord, because you have smitten a man his son or his brother, and that you may bring a blessing upon you this day.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:30)


וַהֲוָה בְּיוֹמָא אָחְרִי וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְעַמָּא אַתּוּן חַבְתּוּן חוֹבָא רַבָּא וּכְדוּן אֵיסַק וְאַצְלֵי קֳדָם יְיָ הַלְוַאי אֵיכַפֵּר עַל חוֹבֵיכוֹן


And it came to pass on the day following, that Mosheh said to the people, You have sinned a great sin; but now I will go up and will pray before the Lord, if haply I may obtain forgiveness of your sin.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:31)


וְתַב משֶׁה וְצַלִּי קֳדָם יְיָ וַאֲמַר בְּמָטוּ מִינָךְ רִבּוֹן כָּל עָלְמַיָא גְּלֵי קֳדָמָךְ חֲשׁוֹכָא הֵי כִנְהוֹרָא וּכְדוֹן חַב עַמָּא הָדֵין חוֹבָא רַבָּא וְעָבָדוּ לְהוֹן דַּחֲלָן דִּדְהַב


And Mosheh returned, and prayed before the Lord, and said, I supplicate of Thee, Thou Lord of all the world, before whom the darkness is as the light! Now have this people sinned a great sin, and have made to them gods of gold;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:32)


וּכְדוֹן אִין תִּשְׁבּוֹק לְחוֹבֵיהוֹן שְׁבוֹק וְאִין לָא מְחֵינִי כְּדוֹן מִן סֵפֶר צַדִּיקַיָא דְּכָתַבְתָּא לִשְׁמִי בְּגַוֵּיהּ


but now, if Thou wilt forgive their sin, forgive; but if not, blot me, I pray, from the book of the just, in the midst of which Thou hast written my name.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:33)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה לָא חֲמֵי לְמִימְחֵי שְׁמָךְ אֱלָהֵן מַאן דְּחַב קֳדָמַי אַמְחִינֵיהּ מִסִּפְרִי


And the Lord said to Mosheh, It is not right that I should blot out thy name; but whosoever sinneth before Me, him will I blot from My book.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:34)


וּכְדוּן אִיזֵיל דְּבַר יַת עַמָּא לַאֲתַר דְּמַלֵּילִית לָךְ הָא מַלְאָכִי יְטַיֵּיל קֳדָמָךְ וּבְיוֹם אַסְעָרוּתִי וְאַסְעַר עֲלֵיהוֹן חוֹבֵיהוֹן


But now, go lead the people to the place of which I have told thee; behold, My angel shall proceed before thee; but in the day of My visitation I will visit upon them their sin.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 32:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/32:35)


וַחֲבַל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ יַת עַמָּא עַל דְּגָחְנָן לְעִגְלָא דְעָבַד אַהֲרן


And the Word of the Lord plagued the people, because they had bowed themselves to the calf that Aharon had made.



## Chapter 33

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:1)


וּמַלֵּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה אִיזֵיל אִיסְתַּלֵּק מִכָּא דִלְמָא יִתְקֵף רְתַח רוּגְזִי בְּעַמָא וְאֵישֵׁיצִינוּן בְּגִין כֵּן טַיֵּיל אַנְתְּ וְעַמָּא דְאַסֵיקְתָּא מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם לְאַרְעָא דְקַיֵּימִית לְאַבְרָהָם לְיִצְחָק וּלְיַעֲקב לְמֵימַר לִבְנָךְ אֵיתְּנִינָהּ


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, Go, remove thee hence, lest My anger grow hot against the people, and I consume them. Therefore proceed thou, and the people whom thou didst bring up out of the land of Mizraim, (to that land) which I have covenanted unto Abraham, to Izhak, and to Jakob, saying, Unto thy sons will I give it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:2)


וְאֵיזַמֵּן קֳדָמָךְ מַלְאָךְ וְאֵיתָרֵךְ עַל יְדוֹי יַת כְּנַעֲנָאֵי אֱמוֹרָאֵי חִיתָּאֵי וּפְרִיזָאֵי חִיוָאֵי וִיבוּסָאֵי


And I will appoint before thee an angel, and by his hand will cast out the Kenaanaee, Amoraee, Hittaee, and Pherizaee, Hivaee, and Jebusaee;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:3)


לְאַרַע עַבְדָא חֲלָב וּדְבָשׁ אֲרוּם לֵית אֶפְשַׁר דְּאֵיסַלֵק שְׁכִינַת יְקָרִי מִבֵּינֵיכוֹן בְּרַם לָא יְהֵי יְקָרִי שְׁרֵי בְּמָדוֹר מַשְׁרִיתֵיהוֹן אֲרוּם עַם קְשֵׁי קְדַל אַנְתְּ דִּילְמָא אֵישֵׁיצִינְכוֹן בְּאָרְחָא


to the land producing milk and honey. For the Shekinah of My Glory cannot go up among you, nor My Majesty dwell in the habitation of their camp, because thou art a hard-necked people, lest I destroy you in the way.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:4)


וּשְׁמַע עַמָּא יַת פִּתְגָּמָא בִּישָׁא הָדֵין וְאִתְאַבָּלוּ וְלָא שַׁוִּיוּ גְבַר יַת תִּיקוּן זֵינֵיהּ דְּאִתְיַהַב לְהוֹן בְּסִינַי דְבֵיהּ שְׁמָא רַבָּא וְקַדִּישָׁא חָקִיק וּמְפָרַשׁ עֲלוֹי


And the people heard this evil word, and mourned; and no man put on his accustomed ornaments, which had been given them at Mount Sinai, and on which was inscribed and set forth the great and holy Name.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:5)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אֵימַר לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל אַתּוּן עַם קְשֵׁי קְדַל שְׁעָא חֲדָא קְלִילָא אֵיסַלֵּק אִיקַר שְׁכִינְתִּי מִבֵּינָךְ וְאֵישֵׁיצִינָךְ וּכְדוֹן אַעֲדֵי תִיקוּן זֵינָךְ מִינָךְ דְּאִתְגְּלֵי קֳדָמַי מַאן אֶעֱבַד לָךְ


And the Lord said to Mosheh, Speak to the sons of Israel, Ye are a hard-necked people: were the glory of My Shekinah to go up with you, in one little hour I should destroy you. And now put thy accustomed ornaments from thee, that it may be manifest before Me what I may do to thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:6)


וְאִתְרוֹקְנוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מִן תִּיקוּן זֵינֵיהוֹן דִּשְׁמָא רַבָּא מְפָרַשׁ כְּתִיב בְּהוֹן דְּאִתְיַהֲבוּ לְהוֹן לְמַתָּנָה מִן טַוָור חוֹרֵב


And the sons of Israel were deprived of their usual adornments, on which was written and set forth the great Name; and which had been given them, a gift from Mount Horeb. And Mosheh took and hid them in his tabernacle of instruction.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:7)


וּמשֶׁה נְסִיבוּנוּן וּטְמִירִינוּן בְּמַשְׁכַּן אוּלְפַן אוֹרַיְיתָא דִּילֵיהּ בְּרַם יַת מַשְׁכְּנָא נָסַב מִתַּמָּן וּפַרְסֵיהּ לֵיהּ מִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא אַרְחִיק יָתֵיהּ מִן מַשִׁירְיַת עַמָּא דְּאִתְנְדוּן תְּרֵין אַלְפִין אַמִּין וַהֲוָה קָרֵי לֵיהּ מַשְׁכַּן בֵּית אוּלְפָנָא וַהֲוֵי כָּל מַאן דַּהֲדַר בִּתְתוּבָא בְּלֵב שְׁלִים קֳדָם יְיָ הֲוָה נָפִיק לְמַשְׁכַּן בֵּית אוּלְפָנָא דְמִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא מוֹדֵי עַל חוֹבֵיהּ וּמַצְלֵי עַל חוֹבֵיהּ וּמַצְלֵי וּמִשְׁתְּבֵק לֵיהּ


But the tabernacle he took away from thence, and spread it without the camp, and removed it from the camp of the people to the distance of two thousand cubits; and it was called the Tabernacle of the House of Instruction: and it was that when any one turned by repentance with a true heart before the Lord, he went forth to the Tabernacle of the House of Instruction that was without the camp, to confess and pray for the pardon of his sins; and praying he was forgiven.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:8)


וַהֲוָה כַּד הֲוָה נָפִיק משֶׁה מִן מַשְׁרִיתָא וְאָזֵיל לְמַשְׁכְּנָא קַיְימִין כָּל רַשִׁיעֵי עַמָּא וּמִתְעַתְּדִין גְּבַר בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכְּנֵיהּ וּמִסְתַּכְּלִין בְּעֵינָא בִּישָׁא אֲחוּרֵי משֶׁה עַד זְמַן מֵיעֲלֵיהּ לְמַשְׁכְּנָא


And it was when Mosheh passed forth from the camp to go to the tabernacle that all the wicked people arose, and stood, every man at the door of his tent, and looked with the evil eye after Mosheh, when he entered the tabernacle.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:9)


וַהֲוָה כַּד אָעֵיל משֶׁה לְמַשְׁכְּנָא נָחִית עַמּוּדָא דַּעֲנָן יְקָרָא וְקָאֵי בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכְּנָא וּמִתְמַלֵּיל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ עִם משֶׁה


And it came to pass when Mosheh had gone into the tabernacle, the column of the glorious Cloud descended and stood at the door of the tabernacle; and the Word of the Lord spake with Mosheh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:10)


וְחָמַן כָּל עַמָּא יַת עַמּוּדָא דַעֲנָנָא קָאִי בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכְּנָא וּמִן יַד קַיְימִין כָּל עַמָּא וְסַגְדִין כָּל קְבֵל מַשְׁכְּנָא כִּדְקַיְימִין גְּבַר בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכְּנֵיהּ


And all the people beheld the column of the Cloud standing at the door of the tabernacle, and the whole people at once rose up and worshipped towards the tabernacle, standing every man at the door of his tent.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:11)


וּמִתְמַלֵּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה מַמְלֵל קְבֵל מַמְלֵל קַל דִּבּוּרָא הֲוָה שָׁמַע בְּרַם זִיו אַפִּין לָא הֲוָה חָמֵי הֵיכְמָא דִמְמַלֵּיל גְּבַר עִם חַבְרֵיהּ וּמִן בָּתַר דְּאִסְתַּלֵּק קַל דִּבּוּרָא תָּאִיב לְמַשְׁרִיתָא וּמִתְנֵי פִּתְגָמַיָא לִכְנִישְׁתְּהוֹן דְּיִשְרָאֵל בְּרַם מְשׁוּמְשָׁנֵיהּ יְהוֹשֻׁעַ בַּר נוּן הֲוָה טְלֵי לָא הֲוַת זֵייעַ מִגּוֹ מַשְׁכְּנֵיהּ


And the Lord spake with Mosheh word for word,- the voice of the Word (dibbura) was heard, but the Majesty of the Presence was not seen,- in the way that a man converseth with his companion: and after the speaking voice had ascended, he returned to the camp, and delivered the word to the congregation of Israel. But his minister, Jehoshua bar Nun, a young man, removed not from the tabernacle.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:12)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה קֳדָם יְיָ חָמֵי מַה דְּאַנְתְּ אָמַר לִי סַלֵיק יַת עַמָּא הָדֵין וְאַנְתְּ לָא אוֹדַעְתָּנִי יַת מַאן דְּאַנְתְּ שְׁלַח עִמִּי וְאַנְתְּ בְּמֵימְרָךְ אָמַרְתְּ מַנֵית יָתָךְ בְּשׁוּם טַב וְאוּף אַשְׁכַּחְתְּ רַחֲמִין קֳדָמָי


And Mosheh said before the Lord, Lo, what hast Thou said to me, Take this people up? but Thou hast not made me to know whom Thou wilt send with me. By Thy Word Thou hast said, I have ordained thee with a goodly name, and thou hast found favour before Me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:13)


וּכְדוּן אִין בְּבָעוּ אַשְׁכָּחִית רַחֲמִין קֳדָמָךְ אוֹדְעַנִי כְּדוּן יַת אוֹרַח טוּבָךְ וְאִנְדַע רַחֲמָךְ הֵיךְ אַנְתְּ מְדַבֵּר עִם בְּנֵי נְשָׁא זַכָּאִין מָטֵי לְהוֹן הֵי כְחַיְיבִין וּלְחַיְיבִין הֵי כְזַכָּאִין וְתוּב זַכָּאִין מָטֵי לְהוֹן הֵי כְזָכְוַותְהוֹן וְחַיְיבִין מָטֵי לְהוֹן הֵי כְחוֹבֵיהֶן מִן בִּגְלַל דְּאַשְׁכַּח רַחֲמִין קֳדָמָךְ וּגְלֵי קֳדָמָךְ אֲרוּם עַמָּךְ עַמָּא הָדֵין


But now I pray, if I have found mercy before Thee, make me to know the way of Thy goodness, to understand Thy mercy when in Thy dealing with just men it falleth to them as it (falleth) to the guilty, and to the guilty as to the just; but, on the contrary how it (indeed) befalleth the just according to their righteousness and the guilty according to their guilt: that I may find mercy before Thee, and it be made manifest by Thee that this people is Thy people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:14)


וַאֲמַר אַמְתֵּן עַד דִּיהֲכוּן סְבַר אַפִּין דְּרוּגְזָא וּמִן בָּתַר כְּדוֹן אָנוּחַ לָךְ


And He said, Await, until the face of My displeasure shall have gone away, and afterward I will give thee rest.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:15)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ אִין לֵית אַפִּין מְהַלְכָא מִינָנָא לָא תְסַלְקִינָנָא מִיכָּא בִּסְבַר אַפִּין דְּרוּגְזָא


And he said to Him, If Thy wrath go not from us, suffer us not to go up from hence under the frown of Thy displeasure.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:16)


וּבְמָה יִתְיְדַע כְּדוּן אֲרוּם אַשְׁכָּחִית רַחֲמִין קֳדָמָךְ אֲנָא וְעַמָּךְ אֱלָהֵן בְּמַלָלוּת שְׁכִינְתָּךְ עִמָּן וְיִתְעַבְדָן לָנָא פְּרִישָׁן בְּסַלְקוּתָךְ רוּחַ נְבוּאָה מֵיעִילַוֵי אוּמַיָא וּתְהֵי מִתְמַלֵּיל בְּרוּחַ קוּדְשָׁא לִי וּלְעַמָּךְ מִן בִּגְלַל דְּנֵיהֵי מְשַׁנְיֵי מִכָּל עַמְמַיָא דְעַל אַנְפֵּי אַרְעָא


In what will it be known that I have found mercy before Thee but in the converse of Thy Shekinah with us, that distinguishing signs may be wrought for us, in the withholdment of the Spirit of prophecy from the nations, and by Thy speaking by the Holy Spirit to me and to Thy people, that we may be distinguished from all the peoples upon the face of the earth?


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:17)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אוּף יַת פִּתְגָמָא הָדֵין דְּמַלֵּילְתָּא אֶעֱבַד אֲרוּם אַשְׁכַּחְתָּא רַחֲמִין קֳדָמַי וּמַנֵּית יָתָךְ בְּשׁוּם טַב


And the Lord said to Mosheh, This thing also which thou hast spoken of, will I do; for thou hast found mercy before Me, and I have ordained thee with a goodly name.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:18)


וַאֲמַר אַחְמֵי כְדוּן יָתִי יַת יְקָרָךְ


And he said, Show now unto me Thy glory:


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:19)


וַאֲמַר אֲנָא מַעֲבֵר כָּל מְכִילַת טוּבִי קֳדָמָךְ וְאֵיקְרֵי בְּשׁוּם מֵימְרֵיהּ דַּיְיָ קֳדָמָךְ וְאֵיחוּס עַל מַאן דְּחָמֵי לֵיהּ לְמֵיחוּס וְאֵירְחֵים עַל מַאן דְּחָמֵי לֵיהּ לְמִתְרַחֲמָא


but He said, Behold, I will make all the measure of My goodness pass before thee, and I will give utterance in the good name of the Word of the Lord before thee; and I will have compassion upon whom I see it right to have compassion, and will be merciful to whom I see it right to have mercy.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:20)


וַאֲמַר לֵית אֶפְשַׁר לָךְ לְמֵיחְמֵי יַת סְבַר אַפַּי אֲרוּם לָא יַחְמִינַנִי אֵינָשָׁא וְיִתְקַיֵּים


And He said, Thou canst not see the visage of My face; for no man can see Me and abide alive. And the Lord said,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:21)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ הָא אֲתַר מְתַקַּן קֳדָמַי וּתְהֵי מְעַתַּד עַל טִינָרָא


Behold, a place is prepared before Me, and thou shalt stand upon the rock.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:22)


וִיהֵי בְּמֵיעִיבַר יְקַר שְׁכִינְתִּי וְאֵישַׁוִינָךְ בְּאַסְפְּלִידָא דְטִינְרָא וְאַגִין בְּמֵימְרִי עֲלָךְ עַד זְמַן דְּאַעִיבַּר


And it shall be that when the glory of My Shekinah passeth before thee, I will put thee in a cavern of the rock, and will overshadow thee with My Word until the time that I have passed by.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 33:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/33:23)


וְאַעֲבַּר יַת כִּיתֵּי מַלְאָכַיָא דְּקַיְימִין וּמְשַׁמְשִׁין קֳדָמַי וְתֶחְמֵי יַת קְטוֹר דְּבִידָא דִתְפִילֵי אִיקַר שְׁכִינְתִּי וְאַפֵּי אִיקַר שְׁכִינְתִּי לֵית אֶפְשָׁר לָךְ לְמֵיחְמֵי


And I will make the host of angels who stand and minister before Me to pass by, and thou shalt see the handborder of the tephilla of My glorious Shekinah; but the face of the glory of My Shekinah thou canst not be able to see.



## Chapter 34

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:1)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה פְּסָל לָךְ תְּרֵין לוּחֵי אַבְנִין הֵי כְּקַדְמָאֵי וְאֶכְתּוֹב עַל לוּחַיָא יַת פִּתְגָמַיָא דַהֲווֹ עַל לוּחַיָא קַדְמָאֵי דִי תְבַרְתָּ


And the Lord said to Mosheh, Hew thee two tables of stone, as the former, and write upon the tables the words that were upon the former tables which thou didst break;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:2)


וַהֲוֵי זַמִין לְצַפְרָא וְתִיסוֹק לְצַפְרָא לְטַוְורָא דְסִינַי וְתִתְעַתַּד קֳדָמַי תַּמָּן עַל רֵישׁ טוּרָא


and be ready in the morn; and at morning ascend thou Mount Sinai and stand there before Me on the summit of the mountain.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:3)


וְאֵינַשׁ לָא יִסַק עִמָּךְ וְאוּף אֵינַשׁ לָא יִתְחָמֵי בְּכָל טַוְורָא אוּף עָנָא וְתוֹרֵי לָא יַרְעוּן כָּל קְבֵיל טַוְורָא הַהוּא


No man shall ascend with thee, nor any man be seen on all the mountain, nor sheep, nor oxen grazing on the side of the mount.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:4)


וּפְסַל תְּרֵין לוּחֵי אַבְנִין הֵי כְקַדְמָאֵי וְאַקְדִים משֶׁה בְּצַפְרָא וּסְלֵיק לְטַוְורָא דְסִינַי הֵיכְמָא דְפַקֵּיד יְיָ יָתֵיהּ וּנְסֵיב בִּידֵיהּ תְּרֵין לוּחֵי אַבְנַיָא


And he hewed two tables of stone like the former: and Mosheh arose in the morning and ascended Mount Sinai, as the Lord had instructed him, and took in his hand the two tables of stone.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:5)


וְאִתְגְּלֵי יְיָ בְּעַנָנֵי אִיקַר שְׁכִינְתֵּיהּ וְאִתְעַתַּד משֶׁה עִמֵּיהּ תַּמָּן וּקְרָא משֶׁה בְּשׁוּם מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ


And the Lord revealed Himself in the cloud of the glory of His Shekinah, and Mosheh stood with Him there; and Mosheh called on the Name of the Word of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:6)


וְאַעֲבֵיר יְיָ שְׁכִינְתֵּיהּ עַל אַפּוֹי וּקְרָא יְיָ יְיָ אֱלָהָא רַחֲמָנָא וְחַנָנָא אֶרֶךְ רוּחַ וּקְרִיב רַחֲמִין וּמַסְגֵי לְמֶעֱבַד חֶסֶד וּקְשׁוֹט


And the Lord made His Shekinah to pass by before his face, and proclaimed, The Lord, the Lord God, merciful and gracious, long-suffering, and nigh in mercies, abounding to exercise compassion and truth;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:7)


נְטִיר חֶסֶד וְטִיבוּ לְאַלְפֵי דָרִין שָׁרֵי וְשָׁבִיק עַל חוֹבִין וּמַעֲבַר עַל מְרוֹדִין וּמְכַפֵּר עַל חַטָּאִין סְלַח לִדְתַיְיבִין לְאוֹרַיְיתָא וְלִדְלָא תַיְיבִין לָא מְזַכֵּי בְּיוֹם דִּינָא רַבָּא מַסְעַר חוֹבֵי אַבְהָן עַל בְּנִין מְרוֹדִין עַל דַּר תְּלִיתָאֵי וְעַל דַּר רְבִיעָאֵי


keeping mercy and bounty for thousands of generations, absolving and remitting guilt, passing by rebellions, and covering sins; pardoning them who convert unto the law, but holding not guiltless in the great day of judgment those who will not convert; visiting the sins of fathers upon rebellious children upon the third and upon the fourth generation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:8)


וְאוֹחִי משֶׁה וְגָחַן עַל אַרְעָא וּסְגִיד


And Mosheh made haste and bowed himself upon the earth and worshipped.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:9)


וַאֲמַר אִין בְּבָעוּ אַשְׁכָּחִית רַחֲמִין קֳדָמָךְ יְיָ תְּהַךְ כְּדוֹן שְׁכִינַת יְקָרָךְ יְיָ בֵּינָנָא אֲרוּם עַם קְשֵׁי קְדַל הוּא וְתִשְׁבּוֹק לְחוֹבָנָא וּלְחַטָּאנָא וְתַחְסְנִינָנָא יַת אַרְעָא דְקַיְימִית לְאַבְהָתָנָא דְּלָא תְשַׁלְחְפִינָנָא בְּעַם אוֹחֲרָן


And he said, If now I have found mercy before the Lord let the Shekinah of the Glory of the Lord go among us; for it is a people of hard neck; but pardon Thou our guilt and our sin, and give us the inheritance of the land which Thou didst covenant unto our fathers, and change us not to become an alien people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:10)


וַאֲמַר הָא אֲנָא גְזַר קְיַים דְּלָא אַשַׁלְחִיף עַמָּא הָדֵין בְּעַם אוֹחְרַן בְּרַם מִינָךְ יִפְקוּן אוֹכְלוֹסִין דְּצַדִּיקִין קְבֵיל כָּל עַמָּךְ אַעֲבֵּיד פְּרִישַׁן לְהוֹן בִּזְמַן דִּיהָכוּן בְּשִׁבְיָיתָא עַל נַהַרְוַות בָּבֶל וְאֵסֵילֵיקִינוּן מִתַּמָּן וְאֵשְׁרִינוּן מִן לְגֵיו לִנְהַר סַמְבַּטְיוֹן וּכְהִינוּן פְּרִישַׁן לָא אִתְבְּרִיוּ בְּכָל דַּיְירֵי אַרְעָא וּבְכָל עַמְמַיָא וְיֶחֱמוּן כָּל עַמָּא דְאַנְתְּ שָׁרֵי בֵּינֵיהוֹן בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא יַת עוּבְדָא דַיְיָ אֲרוּם דָּחִיל הוּא דַאֲנָא עָבִיד עִמָךְ


And He said, Behold, I make covenant that I will not change this people to become an alien people; nevertheless from thee shall proceed a multitude of the righteous; and with all thy people will I do wondrous things in the time when they go into captivity by the rivers of Bavel: for I will bring them up from thence, and make them dwell from within the river Sambation; and like wonders shall not be created among all the inhabitants of the earth, nor among any nation. And all the people among whom thou wilt dwell shall see in that day the work of the Lord; for terrible is the thing that I will do with thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:11)


טוֹר לָךְ יַת מַה דַּאֲנָא מְפַקֵיד לָךְ יוֹמָא דֵין הָאֲנָא מְתָרֵיךְ מִן קֳדָמָךְ יַת אֱמוֹרָאֵי וּכְנַעֲנָאֵי וְחִתָּאֵי וּפְרִיזָאֵי וְחִיוָאֵי וִיבוּסָאֵי


Observe thou that which I command thee this day: behold, I drive out from before thee the Amoraee, and Kenaanaee, and Hittaee, and Pherizaee, and Hivaee, and Jebusaee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:12)


אִיסְתַּמַּר לָךְ דִּילְמָא תִגְזוֹר קְיַים לְיָתֵיב אַרְעָא דְאַנְתְּ עָלֵיל עֲלָהּ דִּלְמָא יְהֵי לְתַקָּלָא בֵּינָךְ


Take heed to thee, lest thou strike covenants with the inhabitants of that land into which thou art to enter; that it may not be a stumbling-block unto thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:13)


אֲרוּם יַת אֵיגוֹרֵיהוֹן תְּתַרְעוּן וְיַת קָמַתְהוֹן תְּתַבְּרוּן וְיַת אֲשֵׁרֵיהוֹן תְּקַצְּצוּן


But thou shalt rather destroy their high places, and break their statues, and cut down their groves;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:14)


אֲרוּם לֵית לְכוֹן רְשׁוּ לְמִסְגוֹד לֶאֱלָהָא אוֹחֲרָן אֲרוּם יְיָ קַנַּאי וּפוּרְעַן שְׁמֵיהּ אֱלָהּ קַנְּאַי וּפוּרְעַן הוּא


for it is not lawful for thee to worship other gods; for the Lord is zealous and avengeful; His Name is God, the Zealous and the Avenger.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:15)


דִּילְמָא תִגְזוֹר קְיַים לְיָתֵיב אַרְעָא וְיִטְעוּן בָּתַר טַעֲוַותְהוֹן וְיִדְבְּחוּן לְטַעֲוַותְהוֹן וִיזַמְנוּן לָךְ וְתֵיכוֹל מִן דִּיבְחֵי טַעֲוַותְהוֹן


Lest thou strike covenant with the dwellers in the land, and they draw thee astray after their idols, and they sacrifice to their idols, and invite thee, and thou eat of the sacrifices of their idols


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:16)


וְתִיסַב מִבְּנַתְהוֹן לִבְנָךְ וְכַד טַעֲיָין בְּנַתְהוֹן בָּתַר טַעֲוַותְהֵין מַטְעֲיַין אוּף יַת בְּנָךְ בָּתַר טַעֲוַותְהֵין


and thou take of their daughters for thy sons, and when their daughters wander after their idols they make thy sons also go astray after their idols.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:17)


דַּחֲלַן דְּמַתְּכָא לָא תַעְבְּדוּן לְכוֹן


Molten gods you shall not make to you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:18)


יַת חַגָא דְפַטִירַיָא תִנְטְרוּן שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין תֵּיכוֹל פַּטִּירֵי הֵיכְמָא דְפַקֵידְתָּךְ לִזְמַן יַרְחָא דְאַבִּיבָא אֲרוּם בְּיַרְחָא דְאַבִּיבָא נְפַקְתּוּן פְּרִיקִין מִמִּצְרָיִם


You shall observe the feast of the unleavened. Seven days thou shalt eat unleavened (cakes), as I have commanded thee, in the time of the month Abiba; for in the month of Abiba you came out free from Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:19)


כָּל פְּתַח וַולְדָא דִילִי הוּא וְכָל בְּעִירָךְ תַּקְדִישׁ מִנְהוֹן דִּכְרַיָא דְתוֹרֵי וְאִמְרֵי


Whatever openeth the womb is Mine; and of all cattle thou art to consecrate the males, of oxen, and of sheep.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:20)


וּבוּכְרָא דְחַמְרָא תִּפְרוֹק בְּאִימְרָא וְאִין לָא תִפְרוֹק וְתִנְקְפֵיהּ בְּקוּפִּיץ וְכָל בּוּכְרָא דִבְרָךְ תִּפְרוֹק וְלָא יִתְחֲמוּן קֳדָמַי רֵיקָנִין


But the firstling of an ass thou mayest redeem with a lamb; but if thou redeem him not, thou shalt cut him off with the blade. And each firstborn of thy sons thou must redeem; and they shall not appear before Me empty.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:21)


שִׁיתָּא יוֹמִין תִּפְלָח וּבְיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה תָנוּחַ בְּרִידַיָא וּבְחַצְדָא תָּנוּחַ


Six days shalt thou work, and in the seventh day have rest; in ploughing time and in harvest thou shalt rest.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:22)


וְחַגָא דִּשְׁבוּעַיָא תַּעֲבֵיד לָךְ בִּזְמַן בִּכּוּרֵי חֲצַד חִינְטִין וְחַגָא דִכְנָשָׁא בְּמִיפְקָא דְשַׁתָּא


The feast of weeks also shalt thou make to thee in the time of the firsts of the wheat harvest; and the feast of ingathering at the conclusion of the ear.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:23)


תְּלַת זִמְנִין בְּשַׁתָּא יִתְחַמְיוּן כָּל דְּכוּרָךְ קֳדָם רִבּוֹן עָלְמַיָא יְיָ אֱלָהָא דְיִשְרָאֵל


Three times in the year shall all thy males appear before the Master of the world, the Lord God of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:24)


אֲרוּם אֵיתָרֵיךְ עַמְמִין מִן קֳדָמָךְ וְאַפְתֵּי יַת תְּחוּמָךְ וְלָא יַחְמֵיד אֵינַשׁ יַת אַרְעָךְ בִּזְמַן מִיסְקָךְ לְאִתְחֲמָאָה קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ תְּלַת זִמְנִין בְּשַׁתָּא


For I will drive out the nations from before thee, and enlarge thy borders; and no man shall covet thy land at the time of thy going up to appear before the Lord thy God thrice in the year.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:25)


לָא תִכְסוּן קֳדָם עַד לָא תְבַטְלִין חֲמִיעַ נִכְסַת פִּסְחִי וְלָא יְבִיתוּן לְצַפְרָא בַּר מִמַּדְבְּחָא תַּרְבֵּי נִכְסַת פִּיסְחָא


You shall not sacrifice the victim of My passover before you have done away with leaven; nor suffer the fat of the paschal sacrifice to remain about the altar till the morning.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:26)


שֵׁירוּי בּוּכְרַת פֵּרֵי אַרְעֲכוֹן תַּיְיתוּן לְבֵית מוּקְדְּשָׁא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן לֵית אַתּוּן רְשָּׁאִין לָא לִמְבַשְׁלָא וְלָא לְמֵיכוּל בְּשַר וַחֲלַב תְּרֵיהוֹן מְעַרְבִין כַּחֲדָא דְּלָא יִתְקוֹף רוּגְזִי בְּכוֹן וְאֵירְשִׁין פֵּרֵי אֵילָנֵיכוֹן עִם בּוּסְרָא בְּלִבְלוּבֵיהוֹן וְטַרְפֵיהוֹן כַּחֲדָא


The best of the firstfruits of your land ye shall bring to the sanctuary of the Lord your God. You are not allowed to boil or to eat flesh and milk mixed together, lest My displeasure be kindled against you, and the fruit of your trees, with the grapes in their branches and their leaves, be laid waste together.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:27)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה כְּתוֹב לָךְ יַת פִּתְגָמַיָא הָאִילֵין אֲרוּם עַל מֵימַר פִּתְגָמַיָא הָאִילֵין גְּזָרִית עִמָּךְ קְיַים וְעִם יִשְרָאֵל


And the Lord said to Mosheh, Write thou these words; for upon the expression of these words have I stricken My covenant with thee and with the people of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:28)


וַהֲוָה תַּמָּן קֳדָם יְיָ אַרְבְּעִין יְמָמִין וְאַרְבְּעִין לֵילַוָן לַחֲמָא לָא אָכַל וּמוֹי לָא אִשְׁתֵּי וּכְתַב עַל לוּחַיָא חוֹרַנְיָיתָא יַת פִּתְגָמֵי קַיְימָא עֲשַרְתֵּי דִבּוּרַיָא דַהֲווֹ כְתִיבִין עַל לוּחַיָא קַדְמָאֵי


And he was there before the Lord forty days and forty nights; he ate no bread nor did he drink water; and he wrote upon the other tables the words of the covenant, the Ten Words which had been written upon the former tables.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:29)


וַהֲוָה בִזְמַן מֵיחַת משֶׁה מִן טַוְורָא דְסִינַי וּתְרֵין לוּחֵי דְסַהֲדוּתָא בִּידָא דְמשֶׁה בְּמֵיחְתֵיהּ מִן טַוְורָא וּמשֶׁה לָא חַכִּים אֲרוּם אִשְׁתַּבְהַר זִיו אִיקוּנִין דְּאַנְפּוֹי דַּהֲוַת לֵיהּ מִן זִיו אִיקַר שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ בִּזְמַן מַלֵלְיוּתֵיהּ עִמֵיהּ


And it was at the time when Mosheh came down from the mountain of Sinai, with the two tables of the testimony in the hand of Mosheh, in his descending from the mount, that Mosheh knew not that the visage (form) of his face shone with the splendour which had come upon him from the brightness of the glory of the Lord's Shekinah in the time of His speaking with him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:30)


וַחֲמָא אַהֲרן וְכָל בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל יַת משֶׁה וְהָא אִשְׁתַּבְהַר זִיו אִקּוּנִין דְּאַנְפּוֹי וּדְחִילוּ מִן לְמִקְרְבָא לְוָותֵיהּ


And Aharon and all the sons of Israel saw Mosheh, and, behold, the glory of the form of his face shined, and they were afraid to come near to him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:31)


וּקְרָא לְהוֹן משֶׁה וְתָבוּ לְוָותֵיהּ אַהֲרן וְכָל רַבְרְבַיָא דְאִתְמְנוּן נְגוֹדִין בִּכְנִישְׁתָּא וּמַלֵיל משֶׁה עִמְהוֹן


And Mosheh called to them and Aharon, and all the princes who had been appointed chiefs in the congregation returned, and Mosheh conversed with them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:32)


וּמִבָּתַר כְּדֵין אִיתְקְרִיבוּ כָּל בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וּפַקֵּידִינוּן יַת כָּל מַה דְּמַלֵּיל יְיָ עִמֵּיהּ בְּטַוְורָא דְסִינַי


And afterward drew nigh all the sons of Israel, and he taught them all that the Lord had spoken to him on Mount Sinai.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:33)


וּפְסַק משֶׁה מִן לְמַלָלָא עִמְהוֹן וִיהַב עַל אִיקוּנִין דְּבֵית אַנְפּוֹי סוּדָרָא


And when Mosheh had finished to speak with them, he put over the form of his face a veil.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:34)


וְכַד עָלֵיל משֶׁה לִקְדָם יְיָ לְמַלָלָא עִמֵּיהּ מַעְדֵי יַת סוּדְרָא דְעַל אִיקוּנִין דְּבֵית אַנְפּוֹי עַד מִיפְקֵיהּ וְנָפֵיק וּמְמַלֵּיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל יַת מַה דְּאִתְפַּקֵּד


And when Mosheh went in before the Lord to speak with Him, he removed the veil from his countenance until he came forth; and he came forth and spake to the sons of Israel what had been commanded.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 34:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/34:35)


וְחַמְיַין בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל יַת אִיקוּנִין דְּמשֶׁה אֲרוּם אִשְׁתַּבְהַר זִיו אִיקוּנִין דְּאַנְפֵּי משֶׁה וְאָתֵיב משֶׁה יַת סוּדְרָא עַל בֵּית אַפּוֹי עַד זְמַן מֵיעֲלֵיהּ לְמַלָלָא עִמֵּיהּ


And the sons of Israel saw the countenance of Mosheh, that the glory of the form of Mosheh's face was shining. And Mosheh replaced the veil upon his face until the time of his going in to speak with Him.



## Chapter 35

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:1)


ויקהל וּכְנַשׁ משֶׁה יַת כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן אִלֵּין פִּתְגָמַיָא דְפַקֵיד יְיָ לְמֶעֱבַּד יַתְהוֹן


And Mosheh gathered together all the congregation of the sons of Israel, and said to them: These are the things which the Lord hath commanded to be done.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:2)


שִׁיתָּא יוֹמִין תִּתְעֲבֵיד עִיבִידְתָּא וּבְיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה יְהֵי לְכוֹן קוּדְשָׁא שַׁבְּתָא נְיָיחָא קֳדָם יְיָ כָּל דְּיַעֲבֵיד עִיבִידְתָּא בְּיוֹמָא דְשַׁבַּתָּא אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵיל בְּמַטְלִית אַבְנִין


Six days thou shalt do work, and on the seventh day there shall be to you the holy Sabbath of repose before the Lord. Whoever doeth work on the Sabbath day, dying he shall die by the casting of stones.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:3)


עַמִּי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לָא תְבַעֲרוּן אֵישָׁתָא בְּכָל אֲתַר מוֹתְבָנֵיכוֹן בְּיוֹמָא דְשַׁבַּתָּא


My people of the sons of Israel, ye shall not kindle a fire in any place of your habitations on the day of the Sabbath.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:4)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְכָל כְּנִישְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְמֵימַר דֵּין פִּתְגָּמָא דְפַקֵּיד יְיָ לְמֵימָר


And Mosheh spake to all the assembly of the children of Israel, saying, This is the word which the Lord hath commanded, saying,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:5)


סָבוּ מִנְכוֹן אַפְרָשׁוּתָא קֳדָם יְיָ כָּל מַאן דְּאִתְרְעֵי לִבֵּיהּ יַיְתֵי יַת אַפְרָשׁוּתָא דַיְיָ דַהֲבָא וְכַסְפָּא וּנְחָשָׁא


Take of you a separation before the Lord: whosoever is moved in his heart, let him present the separation for the Lord; gold, or silver, or brass,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:6)


וְתִכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָונָא וּצְבַע זְהוֹרֵי וּבוּץ וּמֵעַזֵּי


or hyacinth, or purple, or scarlet, or fine linen, or goats' hair,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:7)


וּמַשְׁכֵי דְדִיכְרֵי מְסַמְקֵי וּמַשְׁכֵי דְסַסְגוֹנָא וְקֵיסֵי דְשִׁיטָא


or rams' skins dyed red, or skins of seals, (purple skins), and woods of sitta;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:8)


וּמִשְׁחָא לְאַנְהָרוּתָא וּבוּשְמַיָא לִמְשַׁח רְבוּתָא וְלִקְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמַיָא


or oil for the lights, aromatics for the anointing oil, and sweet incense;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:9)


מַרְגַלְיַין דְּבוּרְלִין הִינוּן וּמַרְגַּלְיַין דְּאַשְׁלָמוּתָא לִמְשַׁקְעָא בְּאֵפוֹדָא וּבְחוּשְׁנָא


the onyx stones, and stones for completing the enchasement of the ephoda and the breastplate.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:10)


וְכָל חַכִּימֵי לִבָּא דִבְכוֹן יֵיתוּן וְיַעַבְּדוּן יַת דְּפַקֵּיד יְיָ


And let all the wise-hearted among you give and make all that the Lord hath commanded:


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:11)


יַת מַשְׁכְּנָא יַת פְּרָסֵיהּ וְיַת חוֹפָאָה דִילֵיהּ יַת פּוּרְפוֹי וְיַת לוּחוֹי יַת נַגְרוֹי וְיַת עַמּוּדוֹי וְיַת חוֹמְרוֹי


The tabernacle, its tent, and its covering, its hasps, and its boards, its bars, and its pillars, and its bases.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:12)


יַת אֲרוֹנָא וְיַת אֲרִיחוֹי וְיַת כַּפּוּרְתָּא וְיַת פַּרְגוֹדָא דִפְרָסָא


The ark, with its staves, and the mercy seat, and the veil for the covering;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:13)


יַת פָּתוֹרָא וְיַת אֲרִיחוֹי וְיַת כָּל מָנוֹי וְיַת לְחֵם דְּאַפַּיָא


and the table, and its staves, and all its vessels, and the bread for the Presence;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:14)


וְיַת מְנַרְתָּא דְאַנְהוֹרֵי וְיַת מָנָהָא וְיַת בּוֹצִינָהָא וְיַת מִשְׁחָא דְאַנְהָרוּתָא


and the candelabrum for illumination, with the lamps, and the oil for the light;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:15)


וְיַת מַדְבְּחָא דִקְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמַיָא וְיַת אֲרִיחוֹי וְיַת מִישְׁחָא דִרְבוּתָא וְיַת קְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמַיָא וְיַת פָּרָסָא דְתַרְעָא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא


and the altar of sweet incense, and its staves, and the anointing oil, and the sweet incense, and the curtain for the door of the tabernacle of ordinance;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:16)


יַת מַדְבְּחָא דַעֲלָתָא וְיַת קַנְקַל דִּנְחָשָׁא דִילֵיהּ יַת אֲרִיחוֹי וְיַת כָּל מָנוֹי וְיַת כִּיּוֹרָא וְיַת בְּסִיסֵיהּ


the altar of burnt offering, and its brasen grate, with its staves, and its vessels, and the laver, with its base;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:17)


יַת וָוִילְוַת דַּרְתָּא יַת עַמּוּדוֹי וְיַת חוֹמַרְנָהָא וְיַת פְּרָסָא דִתְרַע דַּרְתָּא


and the curtains of the court, with its pillars, and bases, and the hanging for the gate of the court,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:18)


יַת מַחְתֵּי מַשְׁכְּנָא וְיַת מַחְתֵּי דַרְתָּא וְיַת אֲטוּנֵיהוֹן


and the pins of the tabernacle, and the pins of the court, and their cords;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:19)


יַת לְבוּשֵׁי שִׁמּוּשָׁא לְשַׁמָּשָׁא בְּקוּדְשָׁא יַת לְבוּשֵׁי קוּדְשָׁא דִּלְאַהֲרן כַּהֲנָא וְיַת לְבוּשֵׁי בְּנוֹי לְשַׁמָּשָׁא


the vestments of ministration, for ministering in the sanctuary, and the holy vestments for Aharon the priest, and the vestments of his sons for ministering.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:20)


וּנְפָקוּ כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מִן קֳדָם משֶׁה


And all the congregation of the sons of Israel went out from before Mosheh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:21)


וְאָתוֹ כָּל גְּבַר דְּאִתְרְעֵי לִבֵּיהּ וְכָל דְּאַשְׁלֵמִית רוּחֵיהּ בִּנְבוּאָתָא דְעִמֵּיהּ הַיְתִיוּ יַת אַפְרָשׁוּתָא לִקְדָם יְיָ לְעִיבִידַת מַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא וּלְכָל פּוּלְחָנֵיהּ וְלִלְבוּשֵׁיהּ קוּדְשָׁא


And every man whose heart moved him, and every one who was filled with the Spirit of prophecy, came, and brought what he had for a separation before the Lord for the work of the tabernacle of ordinance, and for all its service, and for the holy vestments.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:22)


וְאָתוּ גוּבְרַיָא עִם נְשַׁיָא כָּל מַאן דְּאִתְרְעֵי לִבֵּיהּ הַיְתִיוּ שִׁירִין וְשִׁיוְוִין עִזְקַן וּמְחוֹכִין וְכָל תַּכְשִׁיט דִּדְהַב וְכָל גְבַר דְּאֲרִים אֲרָמוּת דַּהֲבָא קֳדָם יְיָ


And with the men came the women, every one whose heart was moved, and brought chains, and necklaces, rings, bracelets, and every ornament of gold; every one offering up the offering of gold before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:23)


וְכָל גְּבַר דְּהִשְׁתַּכַּח עִמֵּיהּ תִּכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָונָא וּצְבַע זְהוֹרֵי וּבוּץ וּמְעִיזֵי וּמַשְׁכֵי דְדִכְרֵי מְסַמְקֵי וּמַשְׁכֵי סַסְגוֹנָא הַיְיתִיוּ


And every one with whom was found hyacinth, or purple, or crimson, or fine linen, goats' hair, or purple skins, brought the separation before the Lord; ___


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:24)


כָּל דְּאָרִים אֲרָמוּת כַּסְפָּא וּנְחָשָׁא הַיְתִיוּ יַת אַפְרָשׁוּתָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְכָל מַאן דְּהִשְׁתַּכַּח עִמֵּיהּ קִיסִין דְּשִׁיטָא לְכָל עִיבִידַת פּוּלְחָנָא הַיְתִיוּ


___ and all with whom were found woods of sitta for all the work of the service brought.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:25)


וְכָל אִיתְּתָא חַכִּימַת לִבָּהָא בִידָהָא הֲוַת עַזְלָא וּמַתְיָין כַּד עָזִיל יַת תִּכְלָא וְיַת אַרְגְּוָונָא וְיַת צְבַע זְהוֹרִי וְיַת בּוּצָא


And every woman whose heart was wise spun with her hands, and brought the spun work of hyacinth, and purple, and crimson, and fine linen.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:26)


וְכָל נְשַׁיָא דְאִתְרְעֵי לִבְּהוֹן עִמְהוֹן בְּחָכְמְתָא הֲווֹן עַזְלָן יַת מְעִיזַיָא עַל גְּוִיַיתְהוֹן וּמְנַפְסִין יַתְהֵן כַּד הִינוּן חַיִּין


And all the women whose hearts were moved in wisdom spun goats' hair (while) upon their bodies, and sheared them, being alive.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:27)


וַעֲנַנֵי שְׁמַיָא אָזְלִין לְפִישׁוֹן וְדַלְיַין מִתַּמָּן יַת אַבְנֵי בּוּרְלְוַת חֵילָא וְיַת אַבְנֵי אַשְׁלָמוּתָא לְשַׁקְעָא בְּאֵיפוֹדָא וּבְחוּשְׁנָא וּמַחְתַן יַתְהוֹן בְּאַנְפֵּי מַדְבְּרָא אָזְלִין רַבְרְבָנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וּמַיְיתַן יַתְהוֹן לִצְרוֹךְ עִיבִידְתָּא


And the clouds of heaven went to the Phison, and drew up from thence onyx stones, and stones for infilling, to enchase the ephod and the breastplate, and spread them upon the face of the wilderness; and the princes of Israel went, and brought them for the need of the work.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:28)


וְתַיְבִין עֲנָנֵי שְׁמַיָא וְאָזְלִין לְגַן עֵדֶן וְנַסְבִין מִתַּמָּן יַת בּוּשְמָא בְּחִירָא וְיַת מִשְׁחָא דְזֵיתָא לְאַנְהָרוּתָא וְיַת אַפַרְסְמָא דַכְיָא לִמְשַׁח רְבוּתָא וְלִקְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמַיָא


And the clouds of heaven returned, and went to the garden of Eden, and took from thence choice aromatics, and oil of olives for the light, and pure balsam for the anointing oil, and for the sweet incense.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:29)


כָּל גְּבַר בַּר יִשְרָאֵל וְאִתְּתָא בַת יִשְרָאֵל דְּאִתְרְעֵי לִבְּהוֹן עִמְהוֹן לְאַיְיתָאָה לְכָל עִיבִידְתָּא דְפַקֵּיד יְיָ לְמֶעֱבַד בִּידָא דְמשֶׁה הַיְתִיוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל נְדַבְתָּא קֳדָם יְיָ


Every man, a son of Israel, and (every) woman, a daughter of Israel, who was moved in heart, brought for all the work which the Lord by Mosheh had commanded to be made; so brought the children of Israel the votive gift before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:30)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל חָמוֹן דְּמַנֵי יְיָ בְּשׁוּם טַב בְּצַלְאֵל בַּר אוּרִי בַּר חוּר לְשִׁבְטָא דִיהוּדָא


And Mosheh said to the sons of Israel, See, the Lord hath ordained with a good name Bezalel bar Uri bar Hur, of the tribe of Jehudah,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:31)


וְאַשְׁלֵים עִמֵּיהּ רוּחַ נְבוּאָה מִן קֳדָם יְיָ בְּחוּכְמָתָא בְּסוּכְלְתָנוּ וּבְמַנְדְּעָא וּבְכָל עִיבִידְתָּא


and hath filled him with the Spirit of prophecy from before the Lord, in wisdom, in understanding, in knowledge, and in all handicraft;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:32)


וּלְאַלָפָא אוּמַנְוָון לְמֶעֱבַד בְּדַהֲבָא וּבְכַסְפָּא וּבִנְחָשָׁא


and to instruct artificers to work in gold, and in silver, and in brass,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:33)


וּבְאַגְלָפוּת מַרְגְּלִיתָא טַבְתָא לְאַשְׁלָמוּת בְּהוֹן יַת עִיבִידְתָּא וּבְנַגָּרוּת קִיסָא לְמֶעֱבַד בְּכָל עִיבִידַת אוּמַנְוָון


and in the cutting of precious stones, to perfect by them the work, and in the fabrication of wood, to work in all the work of artificers.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:34)


וּלְמֵילַף אוּמַנְוָותָא לְמִשְׁאַר אוּמַנְיָא יְהַב מַנְדְּעָא בְּלִבֵּיהּ הוּא וְאָהֳלִיאָב בַּר אֲחִיסָמָךְ לְשִׁבְטָא דְדָן


And to teach art-work to the rest of the artificers he imparted skill to his heart, and to (that of) Ahaliab bar Achisamak, of the tribe of Dan.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 35:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/35:35)


אַשְׁלִים עִמְהוֹן חַכִּימוּת לִבָּא לְמֶעֱבַד כָּל עִיבִידַת נַגַר וְאוּמָן וְצַיַיר בְּתִיכְלָא וּבְאַרְגְּוָונָא וּבִצְבַע זְהוֹרֵי וּבְבוּצָא וְגַרְדֵי עַבְדֵי כָּל עִיבִידְתָּא וּמַלְפֵי אוּמַנְוָון


He filled them with wisdom of heart to make all the work of the carpenter and the embroiderer, in hyacinth, and in purple, and in crimson, and in fine linen; and of the sewer, to fashion all the work, and to teach the workmen.



## Chapter 36

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:1)


וַעֲבַד בְּצַלְאֵל וְאָהֳלִיאָב וְכָל גְּבַר חַכִּים לִבָּא דִּיהַב יְיָ חוּכְמָתָא וְסוּכְלְתָנוּתָא בְּהוֹן לְמִידַע וּלְמֶעֱבַד יַת כָּל עִיבִידַת פּוּלְחַן קוּדְשָׁא לְכָל מַה דְּפַקֵּיד יְיָ


And Bezalel and Ahaliab wrought, and every man wise in heart, to whom the Lord had given wisdom and intelligence to understand and to make all the work for the service of the sanctuary, according to all that the Lord had commanded.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:2)


וּקְרָא משֶׁה לִבְצַלְאֵל וּלְאָהֳלִיאָב וּלְכָל גְּבַר חַכִּים לִבָּא דִיהַב יְיָ חָכְמְתָא בְּלִבֵּיהּ כָּל מַאן דְּאִתְרְעֵי לִבֵּיהּ לְמִקְרַב לְעִיבִידְתָּא לְמֶעֱבַד יָתָהּ


And Mosheh called Bezalel and Ahaliab, and every man wise in heart, to whose heart the Lord had given wisdom, every one whose heart was moved, to draw near, and do the work itself.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 36:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:3)


וּנְסִיבוּ מִן קֳדָם משֶׁה יַת כָּל אַפְרָשׁוּתָא דְאַיְיתִיוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְעִיבִידַת פּוּלְחַן קוּדְשָׁא לְמֶעֱבַד יָתָהּ וְהִינוּן מַיְיתִין לֵיהּ תּוּב נְדַבְתָּא בִּצְפַר בִּצְפָר מִן מָמוֹנְהוֹן


And they took from before Mosheh all the separation that the children of Israel had brought for the work of the service of the sanctuary, to make it. And they still brought to him the voluntary gift, morning after morning from their possessions.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 36:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:4)


וְאָתוּ כָּל חַכִּימַיָא דְעָבְדִין יַת כָּל עִיבִידַת קוּדְשָׁא גְּבַר גְּבַר מֵעִבִידְתֵּיהּ דְּהִינוּן עָבְדִין


And all the wise men who did all the work of the sanctuary came, each man from the work which he had done;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 36:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:5)


וַאֲמָרוּ לְמשֶׁה מַסְגְּיַין עַמָּא לְמַיְיתַיָא מִיסַת פּוּלְחָנָא לְעִיבִידְתָּא דְפַקֵּיד יְיָ לְמֶעֱבַד יָתָהּ


and they said to Mosheh, The people abound in bringing (more) than is enough for the service of the work, which the Lord hath ordained.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 36:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:6)


וּפַקֵּיד משֶׁה וְאַעֲבָרוּ כְרוֹזָא בְּמַשְׁרִיתָא לְמֵימָר גְּבַר וְאִתְּתָא לָא יַעַבְדוּן תּוּב עִיבִידְתָּא לְאַפְרָשׁוּת קוּדְשָׁא וּפְסַק עַמָּא מִלְאַיְתָאָה


And Mosheh commanded, and they made proclamation through the camp, saying, Neither man nor woman may make any more work for the holy separation: and the people ceased from bringing.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 36:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:7)


וְעִיבִידְתָּא הֲוַת כְּמֵיסַת לְכָל עִבִידְתָּא וַעֲבָדוּ יָתָהּ וּבְרַם שַׁיְירוּ


For what had been done was according to the sufficiency of all the work; and they did it, and had more than enough.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 36:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:8)


וַעֲבָדוּ כָּל חַכִּימֵי לִבָּא יַת מַשְׁכְּנָא עֲשַר יְרִיעַן דְּבוּץ שְׁזִיר וְתִכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָונָא וּצְבַע זְהוֹרִי צוּרַת כְּרוּבִין עוֹבַד אוּמַן עֲבַד יַתְהוֹן


And all the wise in heart made the TABERNACLE; ten curtains of fine linen, and hyacinth, and purple, and scarlet, figured with kerubin, the work of the embroiderer, he made them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 36:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:9)


אוּרְכָּא דִירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא עַשְרִין וְתַמְנֵי אַמִּין וּפוּתְיָא אַרְבַּע אַמִּין סְכוּם דִּירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא מְשַׁחְתָּא חֲדָא לְכָל יְרִיעָתָא


The length of one curtain twenty and eight cubits, the sum of one curtain ; the measure was one for all the curtains.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 36:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:10)


וְלָפֵיף יַת חָמֵשׁ יְרִיעַן חֲדָא עִם חֲדָא וְחָמֵשׁ יְרִיעַן לָפֵיף חֲדָא עִם חֲדָא


And he conjoined five curtains one with another, and (the other) five curtains conjoined he one with another.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 36:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:11)


וְעָבַד עֲנוּבִין דְּתִכְלָא עַל שִפְתָא דִירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא מִסִּטְרָא בֵּית לוֹפֵי כֵּן עָבַד בְּשִפְתָא דִירִיעֲתָא בֵּית לוֹפֵי תִּנְיָינָא


And he made loops of hyacinth upon the edge of one curtain, at the place of conjunction in the side; so made he in the side at the place of conjunction in the other curtain.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 36:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:12)


חַמְשִׁין עֲנוּבִין עָבַד בִּירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא וְחַמְשִׁין עֲנוּבִין עָבַד בְּסִטְרָא דִירִיעֲתָא דְבֵית לוֹפֵי תִנְיָינָא מְכַוְונַן עֲנוּבַיָא חֲדָא לְקָבֵל חֲדָא


Fifty loops he made in one curtain, and fifty loops made he at the place of juncture of the edge of the second curtain; the loops were arranged one over against the other.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 36:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:13)


וְעָבַד חַמְשִׁין פּוּרְפִין דִּדְהַב וְלָפֵיף יַת יְרִיעֲתָא חֲדָא עִם חֲדָא בְּפוּרְפַּיָא וַהֲוָה מַשְׁכְּנָא חָד


And he made fifty taches of gold, and conjoined one curtain with another with the taches, and there was one tabernacle.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 36:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:14)


וַעֲבַד יְרִיעַן דְּמֵעַזֵּי לִפְרָסָא עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא חַד סְרֵי יְרִיעַן עֲבַד יַתְהוֹן


And he made curtains of goats' hair to spread upon the tabernacle: eleven curtains he made them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 36:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:15)


אוּרְכָּא דִירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא תְּלָתִין אַמִּין וְאַרְבַּע אַמִּין פּוּתְיָא דִירִיעֲתָא חֲדָא מְשַׁחְתָּא חֲדָא לְחַד סְרֵי יְרִיעַן


The length of one curtain thirty cubits, and four cubits the breadth of one curtain; one measure for the eleven curtains.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 36:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:16)


וְלָפֵיף יַת חֲמֵשׁ יְרִיעַן לְחוֹד כָּל קְבֵיל חַמְשָׁא סִפְרֵי אוֹרַיְיתָא וְיַת שִׁית יְרִיעַן לְחוֹד כָּל קְבֵיל שִׁתָּא סִדְרֵי מַתְנְיָיתָא


And he joined five curtains together, corresponding with the five books of the law; and six curtains together, corresponding with the six orders of the Mishna.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 36:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:17)


וַעֲבַד עֲנוּבִין חַמְשִׁין עַל שִפְתָא דִירִיעֲתָא בְּצִטְרָא בֵּית לוֹפֵי וְחַמְשִׁין עֲנוּבִין עֲבַד עַל שִפְתָא דִירִיעֲתָא דְּבֵית לוֹפֵי תִּנְיָינָא


And he made fifty loops in the border of the curtain at the place of conjuncture, and fifty loops made he upon the border of the curtain at the second place of conjuncture.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 36:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:18)


וַעֲבַד פּוּרְפִּין דִּנְחָשָׁא לְלַפְּפָא יַת מַשְׁכְּנָא לְמֶהֱוֵי חָד


And he made taches of brass to compact the tabernacle, that it might become one.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 36:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:19)


וַעֲבַד חוֹפָאָה לְמַשְׁכְּנָא מַשְׁכֵי דְדִכְרֵי מְסַמְקֵי וּמַשְׁכֵי דְסַסְגוֹנָא לְטַלְלָא מִלְעֵילָא


And he made a covering for the tabernacle of rams' skins reddened, and of purple skins to protect it above.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 36:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/36:20)


וַעֲבַד יַת לוּחַיָא לְמַשְׁכְּנָא מִן קִיסֵי שִׁיטָא קַיְימִין הֵי כְאוֹרַח נְצִיבְהוֹן


And he made the boards of the tabernacle of sitta wood, standing up, after the way of their plantation;

## Chapter 37

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:1)


וְעָבַד בְּצַלְאֵל יַת אֲרוֹנָא דְקִיסֵי שִׁיטָא תַּרְתֵּין אַמִּין וּפַלְגָא אוּרְכֵּיהּ וְאַמְתָא וּפַלְגָא פּוּתְיֵהּ וְאַמְתָא וּפַלְגָא רוּמֵיהּ


And Bezalel made the ARK of sitta wood; two cubits and a half its length, and a cubit and half its breadth, and a cubit and half its height.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:2)

And he covered it with pure gold within and without, and made for it a crown of gold round about.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 37:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:3)

And he cast for it four golden rings upon its four corners; two rings upon one side of it, and two rings upon its second side.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 37:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:4)

And he made the staves of it of sitta wood, and covered them with gold,

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:5)

and put the staves into the rings upon the sides of the ark, to carry the ark.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 37:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:6)


וְעָבַד כַּפּוּרְתָּא דִדְהַב דְּכֵי תַּרְתֵּין אַמִּין וּפַלְגָא אוּרְכָּא וְאַמְתָא וּפַלְגָא פּוּתְיָא בְּרַם סוּמְכָא הֲוַת פּוּשְׁכָא


And he made the MERCY SEAT of pure gold; two cubits and a half the length, and a cubit and half its breadth; but its thickness was a span.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 37:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:7)

And he made two kerubin of pure gold, beaten made be them, on the two sides of the mercy seat.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 37:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:8)


כְּרוּבָא חָד מִסִטְרָא מִכָּא וּכְרוּבָא חָד מִסִטְרָא מִכָּא נְגִידִין וְאַדִּיקִין הֲווֹן כְּרוּבַיָא וְלָא מִתְפַּרְשִׁין מִן כַּפּוּרְתָּא אֲרוּם בְּחוּכְמַת רוּחַ נְבוּאָה עָבַד יַת כְּרוּבַיָא מִתְּרֵין סִטְרוֹי


One keruba on this side, and one keruba on that side, of beaten work; and the kerubin were face to face. They were not separated from the mercy seat; but by the wisdom of the Spirit of prophecy, he made the kerubin on its two sides.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 37:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:9)


וַהֲווֹן כְּרוּבַיָא פְּרִיסִין גַּדְפֵיהוֹן בַּהֲדֵי רֵישֵׁיהוֹן לְעֵילָא מְטַלְלִין בְּגַדְפֵיהוֹן עַל כַּפּוּרְתָּא וְאַפֵּיהוֹן חַד לִקְבֵיל חָד כָּל קֳבֵיל כַּפּוּרְתָּא הֲווֹן אַנְפֵּי כְּרוּבַיָא


And the kerubaia spread forth their wings, with their heads upward, overshadowing the mercy seat with their wings, and their faces were toward each other, over against the mercy seat were the faces of the kerubaia.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 37:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:10)


וְעָבַד יַת פָּתוֹרָא דְקִיסֵי שִׁיטָא תַּרְתֵּין אַמִּין אוּרְכֵּיהּ וְאַמְתָא פּוּתְיֵהּ וְאַמְתָא וּפַלְגָא רוּמֵיהּ


And he made the TABLE of sitta wood, two cubits its length, and a cubit its breadth, and a cubit and half its height.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 37:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:11)


וַחֲפָא יָתֵיהּ דְּהַב דְּכֵי וְעָבַד לֵיהּ דֵּיר דִּדְהַב חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר


And he covered it with pure gold, and made for it a golden crown round about.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 37:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:12)


וְעָבַד לֵיהּ גִּפּוּף רוּמֵיהּ פּוּשְׁכָא חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר וְעָבַד דֵּיר דִּדְהַב לְגַפוּפֵיהּ חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר


And he made a border for it, its height a span round about. And he made a crown of gold for the border round about.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:13)


וְאַתִּיךְ לֵיהּ אַרְבַּע עִזְקַן דִּדְהַב וִיהַב יַת עִזְקָתָא עַל אַרְבַּע זִוְיָתָא דִלְאַרְבַּע רִגְלוֹי


And he cast for it four golden rings, and set the rings upon the four corners of its four feet.

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 37:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:16)


וַעֲבַד יַת מָנַיָא דְעַל פָּתוֹרָא יַת פְּיַלְוָותֵיהּ וְיַת בָּזִיכוֹי וְיַת מְכִילָתֵיהּ וְיַת קַסְוָותָא דִמְחַפְיַין בְּהוֹן דִּדְהַב דְּכֵי

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 37:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:17)


וַעֲבַד יַת מְנַרְתָּא דִּדְהַב דְּכֵי נְגִיד עֲבַד יַת מְנַרְתָּא בָּסִיס דִּידָהּ וּקְנָהָא כַּלִידָהָא חֲזוֹרְיָיהָא וְשׁוֹשַׁנְהָא מִינָהּ הֲווֹן


And he made the CANDELABRUM of pure gold, beaten made he the candelabrum; its base and its shaft, its cups, its apples, and its lilies were of the same.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 37:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:18)


וְשִׁתָּא קְנִין נַפְקִין מִסִטְרָהָא תְּלָתָא קְנֵי מְנַרְתָּא מִסִּטְרָא חָד וּתְלָתָא קְנֵי מְנַרְתָּא מִסִּטְרָא תִּנְיָנָא


And six branches came forth from its sides; three branches of the candelabrum on one side, and three branches of the candelabrum on the second side.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 37:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:19)

Three embossed cups with their figurations on one branch, with the apple and lily, and three embossed cups with their figurations on the other branch, with the apple and lily; so the six branches which proceeded from the candelabrum.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:20)

And upon the candelabrum, four embossed cups with their figurations of apples and lilies.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:21)


וְחֵיזוּר תְּחוֹת תְּרֵין קְנִין דְּמִינָהּ וְחֵיזוּר תְּחוֹת תְּרֵין קְנִין דְּמִינָהּ וְחֵיזוּר תְּחוֹת תְּרֵין קְנִין דְּמִינָהּ לְשִׁתָּא קְנִין דְּנַפְקִין מִינָהּ

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:22)

of a talent of pure gold made he it, and all its vessels.

And he made the ALTAR OF SWEET INCENSE of sitta wood, a cubit its length, and a cubit its breadth, foursquare, and two cubits was its height; its two upright horns were of the same.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/37:26)

## Chapter 38

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:1)


וַעֲבַד יַת מַדְבְּחָא דְעָלָתָא דְקִיסֵי שִׁיטָא חֲמֵשׁ אַמִּין אוּרְכֵּיהּ וַחֲמֵשׁ אַמִּין פּוּתְיֵהּ מְרַבַּע וּתְלַת אַמִּין רוּמֵיהּ


And he made the ALTAR OF BURNT OFFERING of Sitta wood; five cubits its length, and five cubits its breadth, four-square, and three cubits its height.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:2)


וְעָבַד קַרְנוֹי עַל אַרְבַּע זִוְיָיתֵיהּ מִנֵיהּ הֲוָואָה קַרְנוֹי זְקִיפִין לְעֵיל וַחֲפָא יָתֵיהּ נְחָשָׁא


And he made horns upon its four corners; of the same were its horns stretching upward; and he covered it with brass.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:3)


וְעָבַד יַת כָּל מָנֵי מַדְבְּחָא יַת דּוּדְוָתַיָא וְיַת מִגְרוֹפְיָתָא וְיַת מִזְרְקַיָא וְיַת מַשִׁילְיָתָא וְיַת מַחְתְּיָתָא לְכָל מָנוֹי עָבַד נְחָשָׁא


And he made all the vessels of the altar; the pots, and the cleaners, and the basins, and the fleshhooks, and the pans, all its vessels made he of brass.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:4)


וַעֲבַד לְמַדְבְּחָא קַנְקַל עוֹבַד מְצַרְתָּא דִנְחָשָׁא תְּחוֹת סוֹבְבֵיהּ מִלְרַע עַד פַּלְגֵיהּ מְטוֹל לְקַבָּלָא גוּמְרַיָא וְגַרְמַיָא דְנָפְלִין מִן מַדְבְּחָא


And he made the grate of the altar, of brasen network under the border beneath, reaching to the middle of it, to receive the cinders and bones that fell from the altar.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:5)


וְאַתֵּיךְ אַרְבַּע עִזְקַן בְּאַרְבַּע זִוְיָתֵיהּ לְקַנְקַל דִּנְחָשָׁא אַתְרָא לַאֲרִיחַיָא


And he cast four rings, for the four corners of the grate, of brass, (to be) places for the staves.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:6)


וַעֲבַד יַת אֲרִיחַיָא דְקִיסֵי שִׁיטָא וַחֲפָא יַתְהוֹן נְחָשָׁא


And he made the staves of sitta wood, and coated them with brass.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:7)


וְהַנְעַל יַת אֲרִיחַיָא בְּעִזְקָתָא עַל סְטַר מַדְבְּחָא לְמִטַּל יָתֵיהּ בְּהוֹן חֲלִיל לוּחִין מְלֵי עַפְרָא עֲבַד יָתֵיהּ


And he put the staves into the rings, upon the sides of the altar, to carry it by them: hollow with boards, and filled with earth made he it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:8)


וַעֲבַד יַת כִּיּוֹרָא דִנְחָשָׁא וְיַת בְּסִיסֵיהּ דִּנְחָשָׁא מִן אַסְפַּקְלִירֵי נְחָשָׁא נְשַׁיָא צְנִיעוּתָא וּבְעִידַן דְּאָתוּן לְצַלָּאָה בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא הֲוָאָן קַיְימִין עַל קָרְבַּן אַדְמוּתְהוֹן וּמְשַׁבְּחָן וּמוֹדָן וְתַיְיבִין לְגוּבְרֵיהוֹן וְיָלְדַן בְּנִין צַדִּיקִין בִּזְמַן דְּמִדְכַן מִן סוֹאָבוֹת דִּמְהוֹן


And he made the brasen Laver, and its foundation of brass, from the brasen mirrors of the pious women, who, at the season, came to pray at the door of the tabernacle of appointment, standing with their oblations, giving thanks and confession, and returning to their husbands, the mothers of righteous children, who had been purified from the uncleanness of their blood.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:9)


וַעֲבַד יַת דָּרְתָא לְרוּחַ עִיבַר דָּרוֹמָא וִילַוֵי דָרְתָא דְבוּץ שְׁזִיר מְאָה אַמִּין


And he made the court; on the southern side, the curtains of the court (made he) with fine linen, a hundred cubits,
