[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:10)


עַמּוּדֵיהוֹן עַשְרִין וְחוֹמְרֵיהוֹן עַשְרִין דִּנְחָשָׁא וָוֵי עַמּוּדַיָא וְכִבּוּשֵׁיהוֹן דִּכְסָף


their pillars twenty, and their bases twenty, of brass; the hooks of the pillars and their rods were of silver.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:11)


וּלְרוּחַ צִפּוּנָא מְאָה אַמִּין עַמּוּדֵיהוֹן עַשְרִין וְחוֹמְרֵיהוֹן עַשְרִין דִּנְחָשָׁא וָוֵי עַמּוּדַיָא וְכִבּוּשֵׁיהוֹן דִּכְסָף


And on the north side, a hundred cubits, their pillars twenty, and their bases twenty, of brass; the hooks of the pillars and their rods were of silver.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:12)


וּלְרוּחַ מַעַרְבָא וִילַוַן חַמְשִׁין אַמִּין עַמּוּדֵיהוֹן עַשְרָא וְחוֹמְרֵיהוֹן עַשְרָא וָוֵי עַמּוּדַיָא וְכִבּוּשֵׁיהוֹן דִּכְסָף


And the curtains of the western side, fifty cubits, their pillars ten, and their bases ten; the hooks of the pillars, and their rods, were of silver.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:13)


וּלְרוּחַ קִידוּמָא מַדִנְחָא חַמְשִׁין אַמִּין


And on the east side, eastward, fifty cubits.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:14)


וִילַוַן חֲמֵיסְרֵי אַמִּין לְעִבְרָא עַמּוּדֵיהוֹן תְּלָתָא וְחוֹמְרֵיהוֹן תְּלָתָא


And the curtains were fifteen cubits on a side; their pillars three, and their bases three.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:15)


וּלְעִבְרָא תִּנְיָינָא מִכָּא וּמִכָּא לִתְרַע דָּרְתָּא וִילַוַן חֲמֵיסְרֵי אַמִּין עַמּוּדֵיהוֹן תְּלָתָא וְחוֹמְרֵיהוֹן תְּלָתָא


And for the second side of the door of the court, here and there, at the gate of the court, curtains fifteen cubits, their pillars three and their bases three.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:16)


כָּל וִילַוֵי דָרְתָא חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר דְּבוּץ שְׁזִיר


All the curtains of the court round about were of fine linen twined.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:17)


וְחוֹמְרַיָא לַעֲמוּדַיָא דִנְחָשָׁא וָוֵי עַמּוּדַיָא וְכִבּוּשֵׁיהוֹן כְּסַף וְחִפּוּי רֵישֵׁיהוֹן כְּסָף וְהִינוּן מְכַבְּשִׁין כְּסַף הֵיכְדֵין הֲווֹ מִתְעַבְּדִין כָּל עַמּוּדֵי דָרְתָא


And the bases of the pillars were of brass, the hooks of the pillars and their rods of silver, and the overlaying of their capitals silver, and the rods silver; so were made all the pillars of the court.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:18)


וּפְרָסָא דִתְרַע דָּרְתָא עוֹבַד צִיּוּר מַחְטָא דְתִכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָונָא וּצְבַע זְהוֹרֵי וּבוּץ שְׁזִיר וְעֶשְרִין אַמִּין אוּרְכָּא וְרוּמֵיהּ בְּפוּתְיָהּ חֲמֵשׁ אַמִּין כָּל קֳבֵיל וִילַוֵי דָרְתָא


And the hanging for the gate of the court was made of embroidered work in hyacinth, and purple, and crimson, and fine linen twined; and twenty cubits (was) the length, and the height on its breadth five cubits, corresponding with the curtains of the court.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:19)


וְעַמּוּדֵיהוֹן אַרְבְּעָא וְחוֹמְרֵיהוֹן אַרְבְּעָא דִנְחָשָׁא וָוֵיהוֹן כְּסַף וְחִפּוּי רֵישֵׁיהוֹן וְכִבּוּשֵׁיהוֹן כְּסָף


And their pillars four, and their bases four, of brass; their hooks silver, and the overlaying of their capitals, and their rods, silver.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:20)


וְכָל מַתַּחְיָא לְמַשְׁכְּנָא וּלְדָרְתָא חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר דִּנְחָשָׁא


But all the pins of the tabernacle, and of the court round about, were of brass.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:21)


אלה אִלֵין מִנְיָנֵי מַתְקַלְוָן וּסְכוּמְהוֹן דְּמַשְׁכְּנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא דְאִתְמְנִיוּ עַל פּוּם מֵימְרָא דְמשֶׁה בְּרַם פּוּלְחַן לֵיוָאֵי הֲוַת בִּידָא דְאִיתָמָר בַּר אַהֲרן כַּהֲנָא


These are the sums, weights, and numbers of the Tabernacle of the Testimony, which were counted by the word of the mouth of Mosheh. But the service of the Levites was by the hand of Ithamar bar Aharon the priest.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:22)


וּבְצַלְאֵל בַּר אוּרִי בַּר חוּר לְשִׁבְטָא דִיהוּדָה עֲבַד יַת כָּל דְּפַקֵּיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה


And Bezalel bar Uri bar Hur, of the tribe of Jehudah, made all that the Lord had commanded Mosheh;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:23)


וְעִמֵּיהּ אָהֳלִיאָב בַּר אֲחִיסָמָךְ לְשִׁבְטָא דְדָן נַגַר וְאוֹמַן וְצַיַיר בְּתִכְלָא וּבְאַרְגְוָונָא וּבִצְבַע זְהוֹרֵי וּבְבוּצָא


and with him, Ahaliab bar Achisamak, of the tribe of Dan, a worker in wood, and an artificer, and an embroiderer in hyacinth, and in purple, and in crimson, and in fine linen.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:24)


כָּל דַּהֲבָא דְּאִתְעֲבֵיד לְעִיבִידְתָּא בְּכָל עִיבִידַת קוּדְשָׁא וַהֲוָה סְכוּם דְּהַב אֲרָמוּתָא עַשְרִין וּתְשַׁע קַנְטְרִין וּשְׁבַע מְאָה וּתְלָתִין סִלְעִין בְּסִלְעֵי קוּדְשָׁא דֵין הוּא דְהַב אֲרָמוּתָא דְאַרִימוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל כָּל גְּבַר דְּאִתְרְעֵי לִבֵּיהּ לְאַפְרָשָׁא


All the gold that was used in making the whole work of the sanctuary, and it was the amount of the gold of the oblation, (was) twenty and nine talents, and seven hundred and thirty shekels, in the shekel of the sanc­tuary. This was the gold of the oblation, which every man of the sons of Israel, whose heart was willing, had offered as a separation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:25)


וּכְסַף מִנְיָינֵי דִּבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל דִּיהָבוּ בִּזְמַן דִּמְמַנּוּן משֶׁה גְּבַר פּוּרְקַן נַפְשֵׁיהּ מְאָה קַנְטְרִין וְאַלְפָא וּשְׁבַע מְאָה וְשׁוּבְעִין וְחָמֵשׁ סַלְעִין בְּסִלְעֵי קוּדְשָׁא


And the silver of them who were numbered of the sons of Israel, who gave at the time when Mosheh numbered them, every one for the redemption of his soul, a hundred talents, and a thousand and seven hundred and seventy and five shekels, of the shekel of the sanctuary.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:26)


דַּרְכְּמוֹנָא לְגֻלְגַלְתָּא פַּלְגוּת סִלְעָא בְּסִלְעָא דְקוּדְשָׁא לְכָל מַאן דְּעָבַר עַל מִנְיָינַיָא מִבַּר עֶשְרִין שְׁנִין וּלְעֵילָא לְשִׁית מְאָה וּתְלָתָא אַלְפִין וַחֲמֵשׁ מְאָה וְחַמְשִׁין


A drachma for (each) head, a half shekel of the shekel of the sanctuary, for every one who passed to the numberments, from twenty years and upwards, for six hundred and three thousand and five hundred and fifty (men).


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:27)


וַהֲוָאָה מְאַת קַנְטִינְרִין דְּכַסְפָּא לַאֲתָבָא יַת חוֹמְרֵי קוּדְשָׁא וְיַת חוֹמְרֵי פַּרְגוֹדָא מְאָה חוֹמְרִין כָּל קְבֵיל קַנְטִינְרִין קַנְטִינַר לְחוֹמְרָא


And there were a hundred talents of silver for casting the bases of the sanctuary, and the bases of the veil, a hundred bases, answering to the talents, a talent for a base.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:28)


וְיַת אַלְפָא וּשְׁבַע מְאָה וְשַׁבְעִין וַחֲמֵשׁ סַלְעִין עֲבַד וָוִין לַעֲמוּדַיָא וַחֲפָא רֵישֵׁיהוֹן וּכְבַשׁ יַתְהוֹן


And with the thousand and seven hundred and seventy and five shekels, he made hooks for the pillars, and the overlaying of their capitals and their rods.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:29)


וּנְחָשָׁא דְאַרָמוּתָא שַׁבְעִין קַנְטִירִין וּתְרֵין אַלְפִין וְאַרְבַּע מְאָה סִלְעִין


And the brass of the oblation was seventy talents, and two thousand and four hundred shekels.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:30)


וְעָבַד בָּהּ יַת חוֹמְרֵי תְּרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא וְיַת מַדְבְּחָא דִנְחָשָׁא וְיַת קַנְקַלֵי דִנְחָשָׁא דִילֵיהּ וְיַת כָּל מָנֵי מַדְבְּחָא


And he made with it the bases of the gate of the tabernacle of ordinance, and the brasen altar, and the brasen grate for it, and all the vessels of the altar.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 38:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/38:31)


וְיַת חוֹמְרֵי דָרְתָא חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר וְיַת חוֹמְרֵי תְרַע דָּרְתָא וְיַת כָּל מַתְחֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא וְיַת כָּל מַתְחֵי דָרְתָא חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר


And the bases of the court round about, and the bases of the gate of the court, and all the pins of the tabernacle, and all the pins of the court round about.



## Chapter 39

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:1)


וּמִן תִּכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָונָא וּצְבַע זְהוֹרֵי עֲבָדוּ לְבוּשֵׁי שִׁמּוּשָׁא לְשַׁמָּשָׁא בְּקוּדְשָׁא וַעֲבָדוּ יַת לְבוּשֵׁי קוּדְשָׁא דִי לְאַהֲרן כַּהֲנָא הֵי כְּמָא דְפַקֵּיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה


And of the hyacinth, and purple, and crimson, they made the vestments of ministration, to minister in the sanctuary. And they made the holy vestments of Aharon the priest, as the Lord had commanded Mosheh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:2)


וְעָבַד יַת אֵיפוֹדָא דְדַהֲבָא תִּכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָונָא וּצְבַע זְהוֹרֵי וּבוּץ שְׁזִיר


And he made the ephoda of gold, hyacinth, and purple, and fine linen twined.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 39:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:3)


וְרַדִּידוּ יַת טַסֵי דְדַהֲבָא וְקָטָעוּ יַתְהוֹן שֶׁזְרִין לְמֶעְבַּד בְּגוֹ תִכְלָא וּבְגוֹ אַרְגְּוָונָא וּבְגוֹ צְבַע זְהוֹרֵי וּבְגוֹ בוּצָא עוֹבַד אוּמָן


And they beat out the plates of gold, and cut them into threads to inwork with the hyacinth, and the purple, and the crimson, and the fine linen, the work of the artificer.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 39:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:4)


כַּתְפִין עָבְדוּ לֵיהּ מְלַפְפָן עַל תְּרֵין צִטְרוֹי מִתְלָפֵף


Shoulderpieces made they for it conjoined; upon its two sides were they conjoined.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 39:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:5)


וְהֵמְיַן טִיכוּסֵיהּ דַּעֲלוֹי מִנֵּיהּ הוּא הֵי כְּעוֹבָדוֹי דַהֲבָא תִּכְלָא וְאַרְגְּוָונָא וּצְבַע זְהוֹרֵי וּבוּץ שְׁזִיר הֵיכְמָא דְפַקֵיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה


And its ordered band that was upon it was of the same; it was according to its work, of gold, hyacinth, and purple, and crimson, and fine linen twined, even as the Lord had commanded Mosheh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 39:6

וּשְׁלִימַת כָּל עִיבִידַת מַשְׁכְּנָא מַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא וַעֲבָדוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל הֵיכְמָא דְפַקֵּיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה הֵיכְדֵין עֲבָדוּ


And all the work of the tabernacle, the tabernacle of ordinance, was completed: and the sons of Israel did as the Lord commanded Mosheh, so did they.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 39:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:33)


וְאַיְיתִיוּ יַת מַשְׁכְּנָא לְוַת משֶׁה לְבֵית מִדְרָשֵׁיהּ דְּהֲווּ תַּמָּן יַתְבִין משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן וּבְנוֹי וַהֲוָה מִתְרַץ לְהוֹן סֵדֶר כְּהוּנְתָּא וְתַמָּן יַתְבִין סָבֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְאַחְוִיאוּ לֵיהּ יַת מַשְׁכְּנָא וְיַת כָּל מָנוֹי פּוּרְפּוֹי לוּחוֹי נַגְרוֹי עֲמוּדוֹי וְחוֹמְרוֹי


And they brought the tabernacle to Mosheh at his house of instruction, (beth Midrash,) where sat Mosheh, and Aharon, and his sons, where he gave direction to them concerning the order of the priesthood; and there, and Aharon, (also) sat the elders of Israel. And they brought to him the tabernacle and all its vessels: its taches, its boards, its bars, its pillars, and its bases;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 39:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:34)


וְיַת חוֹפָאָה דְמַשְׁכֵי דְדִכְרֵי מְסַמְקֵי וְיַת חוֹפָאָה דְמַשְׁכֵי סַסְגְוָונָא וְיַת פַּרְגוֹדָא דִפְרָסָא


and the covering of reddened rams' skins, and the covering of purple skins, and the veil that was to be spread;

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:35)


וְיַת אֲרוֹנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא וְיַת אֲרִיחוֹי וְיַת כַּפּוּרְתָּא וּכְרוּבַיָא דְנַפְקִין נְגִיד מִינָהּ חָד מִכָּא וְחָד מִכָּא


and the ark of the testimony, and its staves, and the mercy-seat, and the kerubaia produced of beaten work of the same, the one here, and the other there;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 39:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:36)


וְיַת פָּתוֹרָא וְיַת כָּל מָנוֹי וְיַת לְחֵים אַפַּיָא


and the table, and all its vessels, and the bread of faces;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 39:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:37)


וְיַת מְנַרְתָּא וְיַת בּוֹצִינָהָא בּוֹצִינֵי סְדוּרָא דִמְסַדְרִין כָּל קְבֵיל שִׁבְעָתֵי כּוֹכְבַיָא דִנְהִיגִין בְּשִׁטְרֵיהוֹן בִּרְקִיעָא בִּימָמָא וּבְלֵילְיָא וְיַת מִשְׁחָא דְאַנְהָרוּתָא


and the candelabrum, and its lamps, the lamps of order, which were ordained to correspond to the seven stars, that rule in their prescribed places in the firmament by day and by night; and the oil for the lights,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 39:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:38)


וְיַת מַדְבְּחָא דְדַהֲבָא וְיַת מִשְׁחָא דִרְבוּתָא וְיַת קְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמַיָא וְיַת פְּרָסָא דְבִתְרַע מַשְׁכְּנָא


and the golden altar, and the consecration oil, and the sweet incense, and the hanging for the door of the tabernacle;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 39:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:39)


וְיַת מַדְבְּחָא דִנְחָשָׁא וְיַת קַנְקַל דִּנְחָשָׁא דִילֵיהּ יַת אֲרִיחוֹי וְיַת כָּל מָנוֹי יַת כִּיּוֹרָא וְיַת בְּסִיסֵיהּ


and the brasen altar, and its brasen grate, and its staves, and all its utensils; and the laver, and its base;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 39:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:40)


יַת וִילַוֵי דַרְתָּא וְיַת עַמּוּדָהָא יַת חוּמְרָהָא וְיַת פְּרָסָא לִתְרַע דַּרְתָּא וְיַת אֲטוּנוֹי וּמַחְתָּהָא וְיַת כָּל מָנֵי פּוּלְחַן מַשְׁכְּנָא לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא


the curtain-work of the court, and its pillars, and the bases and the veil of the gate of the court, its cords, and pins, and all the vessels for the service of the tabernacle, even the tabernacle of ordinance;


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 39:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:41)


יַת לְבוּשֵׁי שִׁמוּשָׁא לְשַׁמָשָׁא בְּקוּדְשָׁא יַת לְבוּשֵׁי קוּדְשָׁא לְאַהֲרן כַּהֲנָא וְיַת לְבוּשֵׁי בְּנוֹי לְשַׁמָּשָׁא


and the vestments of ministration for ministering in the sanctuary, the holy vestments of Aharon the priest, and the vestments of his sons, to minister.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 39:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:42)


הֵי כְּכָל מַה דְּפַקֵּיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה הֵיכְדֵין עָבָדוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל יַת כָּל פּוּלְחָנָא


According to all that the Lord had commanded Mosheh, so had the sons of Israel made all the service,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 39:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/39:43)


וַחֲמָא משֶׁה יַת כָּל פּוּלְחָנָא וְהָא עָבָדוּ יָתָהּ הֵיכְמָא דְפַקֵּיד יְיָ הֵיכְדֵין עָבָדוּ וּבְרִיךְ יַתְהוֹן משֶׁה וַאֲמַר תִּשְׁרֵי שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ בְּעוֹבָדֵי יְדֵיכוֹן


And Moshe surveyed all the service, and, behold, they had made it as the Lord had commanded, so had they made it. And Mosheh blessed them, and said, May the Shekinah of the Lord dwell within the work of your hands!



## Chapter 40

###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:1)


וּמַלֵּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 40:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:2)


בְּיוֹמָא דְיַרְחָא קַדְמָאָה הוּא יַרְחָא דְנִיסָן בְּחַד לְיַרְחָא תְּקִים יַת מַשְׁכְּנָא מַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא


On the day of the first month, that is the month of Nisan, on the first of the month, thou shalt rear up the tabernacle, the tabernacle of ordinance.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 40:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:3)


וּתְשַׁוֵּי תַּמָּן יַת אֲרוֹנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא וְתַטְלֵיל עַל אֲרוֹנָא יַת כַּפּוּרְתָּא


And thou shalt set there the ark of the testimony, and overlay the ark with the mercy seat.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 40:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:4)


וְהַנְעַל יַת פָּתוֹרָא בִּסְטַר צִפּוּנָא מְטוּל דְּמִתַּמָּן מִתְיָהֵיב עוּתְרָא דְמִתַּמָן זָלְחָן רְסִיסֵי מַלְקוֹשִׁין עַל עוּסְבִין לְמִתְפַּרְנְסָא בְּהוֹן דַּיְירֵי עַלְמָא וּתְסַדֵּר יַת סִדְרוֹי תְּרֵין סִדְרֵי לַחְמָא גַוָאָה שִׁית עֲגוּלִין בְּסִדְרָא כָּל קְבֵל שִׁבְטוֹי דְיַעֲקב וּתְהַנְעַל יַת מְנַרְתָּא בִּסְטַר דָּרוֹמָא מְטוֹל דְּמִתַּמָּן שְׁבִילֵי שִׁמְשָׁא וְסִיהֲרָא וּמִתַּמָּן אִיסְרַטְוָון דִּנְהוֹרֵי וְתַמָּן גִּנְזֵי חָכְמְתָא דִמְתִילִין לִנְהוֹרֵי וְתַדְלֵיק יַת בּוֹצִינָהָא שׁוּבְעָא כָּל קְבֵיל שִׁבְעָתֵי כּוֹכְבַיָא דִמְתִילִין לְצַדִּיקַיָא דִמְנַהֲרִין לְעַלְמָא בִּזְכוּתֵיהוֹן


And thou shalt bring in the table on the north side, because, from thence are given riches; for from thence distil the drops of the latter rain upon the herbs, for the food of the inhabiters of the world; and thou shalt arrange its orders, two rows of bread, comprising six cakes in a row, answering to the tribes of Jakob. And thou shalt bring in the candelabrum, on the south side, because there are the paths of the sun and of the moon, and the pathways of the luminaries; and thence are the treasures of the wisdom which resembleth the light. And thou shalt kindle the seven lamps, corresponding to the seven stars which resemble the just, who shine unto eternity in their righteousness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 40:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:5)


וְתִתֵּן יַת מַדְבְּחָא דְדַהֲבָא לִקְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמַיָא קֳדָם אֲרוֹנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא מְטוּל חַכִּימַיָא דַעֲסִיקִין בְּאוֹרַיְיתָא וְרֵחֵיהוֹן נָדִיף הֵי כִקְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמַיָא וּתְשַׁוֵּי יַת פְּרָסָא דְתַרְעָא לְמַשְׁכְּנָא מְטוּל צַדִּיקַיָא דַחֲפִין בִּזְכוּתְהוֹן עַל עַמָּא בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל


And thou shalt place the golden altar for sweet incense before the ark of the testimony; because the wise who are diligent in the law have a perfume fragrant as the sweet incense. And thou shalt set the veil at the gate of the tabernacle; because the righteous so covereth with their righteousness the people of the house of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 40:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:6)


וְתִתֵּן יַת מַדְבְּחָא דְעָלָתָא קֳדָם תְּרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא מְטוּל עֲתִירַיָא דִמְסַדְרִין פָּתוֹרָא קֳדָם תַּרְעֵיהוֹן וּמְפַרְנְסִין מִסְכֵּנַיָא וּמִשְׁתְּבֵיק לְהוֹן חוֹבֵיהוֹן כְּאִילוּ מְקַרְבִין עֲלָתָא עַל מַדְבְּחָא


And thou shalt place the altar of burnt offering before the door of the tabernacle of ordinance; because the rich, who spread the table before their doors and feed the poor, shall have their sins forgiven what time they make the offering upon the altar.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 40:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:7)


וְתִתֵּן יַת כִּיּוֹרָא בֵּינֵי מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וּבֵינֵי מַדְבְּחָא וְתִתֵּן תַּמָּן מוֹי מְטוּל חוֹבַיָא דְהַדְרִין בִּתְיוּבְתָּא וְשַׁדִיַין עַקְמוּמִיתְהוֹן הֵי כְמַיָא


And thou shalt place the laver between the tabernacle of ordinance and the altar, and put water therein for the sins of such as convert by repentance, and pour off their perversity like water.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 40:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:8)


וּתְשַׁוֵּי יַת דַּרְתָּא חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר מְטוֹל זַכְוַות אַבְהַת עַלְמָא דִמְחַזְרִין חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר לְעַמָּא בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל וְתִתֵּן יַת פְּרָסָא דִתְרַע דָּרְתָא מְטוּל זַכְוַת אַבְהַת עַלְמָא דְפָרִיס בִּתְרַע גֵּיהִנָּם מִן בִּגְלַל דְּלָא יֵעָלוּן תַּמָּן נַפְשַׁת דַרְדְּקֵי עַמָּא דְיִשְרָאֵל


And thou shalt place the court round about, because of the merit of the fathers of the world, which encompasseth the people of the house of Israel round about. And thou shalt set the hanging of the gate of the court on account of the merit of the mothers of the world, which spreadeth at the gate of Gehennam, that none may enter there of the souls of the children of the people of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 40:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:9)


וְתִסַּב יַת מִשְׁחָא דִרְבוּתָא וּתְרַבֵּי יַת מַשְׁכְּנָא וְיַת כָּל דְּבֵיהּ וּתְקַדֵּשׁ יָתֵיהּ מְטוּל כְּלִיל דְּמַלְכוּתָא דְבֵית יְהוּדָה וּמַלְכָּא מְשִׁיחָא דְעָתִיד לְמִפְרַק יַת יִשְרָאֵל בְּסוֹף יוֹמַיָא


And thou shalt take the consecration-oil, and anoint the tabernacle, and all that is therein, and shalt sanctify it, on account of the crown of the kingdom of the house of Jehudah, and of the King Meshiha, who is to redeem Israel at the end of the days.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 40:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:10)


וּתְרַבֵּי יַת מַדְבְּחָא דַעֲלָתָא וְיַת כָּל מָנוֹי וּתְקַדֵּשׁ יַת מַדְבְּחָא וִיהֵי מַדְבְּחָא קוֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשִׁין מְטוּל כְּלִילָא דִכְהוּנְתָּא דְאַהֲרן וּבְנוֹי וְאֵלִיָהוּ כַּהֲנָא רַבָּא דַּעֲתִיד לְמִשְׁתַּלְחָא בְּסוֹף גַּלְוָותָא


And thou shalt anoint the altar of burnt offering, and all its vessels, and consecrate the altar, that it may be an altar most holy, on account of the crown of the priesthood of Aharon, and his sons, and of Elijah, the great Priest who is to be sent at the end of the captivity.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 40:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:11)


וּתְרַבֵּי יַת כִּיּוֹרָא וְיַת בְּסִיסֵיהּ וּתְקַדֵּשׁ יָתֵיהּ מְטוּל יְהוֹשֻׁעַ מְשׁוּמְשָׁנָךְ רַבָּא דְסַנְהֶדְרִין דְּעִמֵּיהּ דְּעַל יְדוֹי עֲתִידָא אַרְעָא דְיִשְרָאֵל לְאִיתְפַּלְגָא וּמְשִׁיחָא בַּר אֶפְרַיִם דְּנָפִיק מִינֵיהּ דְּעַל יְדוֹי עֲתִידִין בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל לִמְנַצְחָא לְגוֹג וּלְסִיעָתֵיהּ בְּסוֹף יוֹמַיָא


And thou shalt anoint the laver, and its base, and consecrate it, on account of Jehoshua thy minister, chief of the sanhedrin of his people; by whose hand the land of Israel is to be partitioned: and of Meshiha bar Ephraim, who shall spring from him, by whose hand the house of Israel is to vanquish Gog and his confederates at the end of the days.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 40:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:12)


וּתְקָרֵיב יַת אַהֲרן וְיַת בְּנוֹי לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְתַסְחֵי יַתְהוֹן בְּמַיָא


And thou shalt bring Aharon and his sons to the door of the tabernacle of ordinance, and wash them with water,

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:13)


וְתַלְבֵּשׁ יַת אַהֲרן לְבוּשֵׁי קוּדְשָׁא וּתְרַבֵּי יָתֵיהּ וּתְקַדֵּשׁ יָתֵיהּ וִישַׁמֵּשׁ קֳדָמַי


and clothe Aharon with the holy vestments, and anoint him, and consecrate him; that he may minister before Me.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:14)


וְיַת בְּנוֹי תַּקְרִיב וְתַלְבְּשִׁינוּן כִּיתּוּנִין


And his sons thou shalt bring near, and dress them with tunics,


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 40:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:15)


וּתְרַבֵּי יַתְהוֹן הֵיכְמָא דְרָבִית יַת אֲבוּהוֹן וִישַׁמְשׁוּן קֳדָמַי וּתְהֵי לְמֶהֱוֵי לְהוֹן רְבוּתְהוֹן לִכְהוּנַת עֲלָם לְדָרֵיהוֹן


and anoint them, as thou didst anoint their father, that they may minister before Me; and their consecration shall be for a perpetual priesthood in their generations.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:16)


וְעָבַד משֶׁה הֵי כְּכָל מַה דְּפַקֵּיד יְיָ יָתֵיהּ הֵיכְדֵין עֲבָד


And Mosheh did all that the Lord commanded, so did he.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 40:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:17)


וַהֲוָה בְּיַרְחָא קַמָאָה הוּא יַרְחָא דְנִיסָן בְּשַׁתָּא תִּנְיֵינְתָא בְּחַד לְיַרְחָא אִיתָקַם מַשְׁכְּנָא


And it was in the first month, that is, the month Nisan, in the second year, in the first of the month, that he reared up the tabernacle.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:18)


וְאָקִים משֶׁה יַת מַשְׁכְּנָא וִיהַב יַת חוֹמְרוֹי וְשַׁוִּי יַת לוּחוֹי וִיהַב יַת נַגְרוֹי וְאָקִים יַת עַמּוּדוֹי


And Mosheh reared the tabernacle, and placed its bases, and set its boards, and placed its bars, and reared its pillars.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:19)


וּפְרַס יַת פְּרָסָא עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא וְשַׁוִּי יַת חוֹפָאָה לְמַשְׁכְּנָא עֲלוֹי מִן לְעֵילָא הֵיכְמָא דְפַקֵּיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה


And he spread the tent over the tabernacle, and placed the covering of the tabernacle upon it above, as the Lord commanded Mosheh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 40:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:20)


וּנְסֵיב יַת תְּרֵין לוּחֵי אַבְנָא לוּחֵי קְיָימָא דְאִתְיְהִיבוּ לֵיהּ בְּחוֹרֵב וַהֲווּ קַיְימִין עַל אָת בְּבֵית אוּלְפָנָא הִנּוּן לוּחֵי סַהֲדוּתָא וְתַבְרֵי לוּחַיָא בְּאָרוֹנָא וְשַׁוִי יַת אֲרִיחַיָא עַל אֲרוֹנָא וִיהַב יַת כַּפּוּרְתָּא בַּהֲדֵי כְּרוּבַיָא דְנַפְקִין נְגִיד מִינָהּ עַל אֲרוֹנָא מִלְעֵילָא


And he took the two tables of stone, the tables of the covenant which were given to him in Horeb, and set them up for a sign in the House of Instruction: they are the tables of the testimony. And the broken tables (he deposited) in the ark. And he set the staves in the ark, and placed the mercy seat, with the kerubaia that were produced for it of beaten work, upon the ark above.

And he set the laver upon its foundation between the tabernacle of ordinance and the altar, and put living water therein for purification, that it may not fail, nor become corrupt all the days.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:33)


וְאָקֵים יַת דַרְתָּא חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר לְמַשְׁכְּנָא וּלְמַדְבְּחָא וִיהַב יַת פְּרָסָא דְבִתְרַע מַשְׁכְּנָא וּגְמַר משֶׁה יַת עִיבִידְתָּא

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:34)

Then the Cloud of Glory overspread the tabernacle of ordinance, and the glory of the Shekinah of the Lord filled the tabernacle.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:35)


וְלָא הֲוָה אֶפְשַׁר לְמשֶׁה לְמֵיעַל לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא אֲרוּם שְׁרָא עֲלוֹי עֲנַן יְקָרָא וְאִיקַר שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ אִתְמְלֵי יַת מַשְׁכְּנָא


And Mosheh was not able to enter the tabernacle of ordinance, because the Cloud of Glory rested upon it, and the glory of the Lord's Shekinah filled the tabernacle.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:36)

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:37)

but if the Cloud of Glory went not up, they did not go forward until the day when it ascended.


###### Targum Jonathan on Exodus 40:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Exodus/r/40:38)


אֲרוּם עֲנַן יְקָרָא דַיְיָ הֲוָה מְטַלָל עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא בִּימָמָא וַעֲמוּדָא דְאֵישָׁתָא הֲוָה מִנְהַר בְּלֵילְיָא וְחַמְיָין כָּל בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל בְּכָל מַטְלָנֵיהוֹן


For the Cloud of the Glory of the Lord overspread the tabernacle by day, and (as) a column of fire it gave light in the night, that all the sons of Israel might see in all their journeys.
